### [1] [Intuition with Evidence: Adaptive Dual Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33019)
*Qiuhe Hong,Jingya Wang,Cuzhen Geng,Shenyao Peng,Munan Ning,Yuyang Liu,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双记忆框架，将模型原生的潜在记忆作为直觉先验，与多索引情景记忆通过检索条件联合对齐统一，推理时用基于JSD敏感度与置信度代理的无训练路由自适应组合，多模态基准上整体超越单范式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体长期交互中，长期记忆需同时承担细粒度证据召回与内化理解两类功能。情景记忆提供显式历史记录、潜在记忆提供内化经验，但二者单独均无法适应跨任务多样的记忆亲和性：情景记忆存在证据覆盖与整合不足，潜在记忆缺乏事实依据。

Method: 构建双记忆框架：以模型原生潜在记忆作为直觉先验，以多索引情景记忆实现精确证据重建，二者通过检索条件联合对齐（retrieval-conditioned joint alignment）统一。推理阶段采用免训练路由机制，由JSD敏感度与置信度代理驱动，按查询相关的记忆亲和性自适应组合两类记忆。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上取得SOTA，整体优于单范式基线（较原始情景记忆基线提升+4.67%）；分析显示情景记忆在证据定位类任务占优，潜在记忆有助于整合分散信息并补偿不完美的检索。

Conclusion: 融合情景与潜在记忆的双记忆范式可互补两类记忆的短板，配合查询级自适应路由，是提升多模态智能体长期记忆能力的有效路径。

Abstract: As multimodal agents engage in sustained interactions, long-term memory serves two distinct but complementary functions: fine-grained evidence recall and internalized interpretation for global understanding. Although episodic and latent memory address these demands through explicit historical records and internalized experience, neither paradigm alone can accommodate the diverse memory affinities across tasks, owing to limitations in episodic evidence coverage and integration, and in latent factual grounding. To bridge this divide, we propose a dual-memory framework, unifying a model-native latent memory as an intuitive prior with a multi-indexed episodic memory for exact evidence reconstruction via retrieval-conditioned joint alignment. At inference, the framework adaptively composes these memories through a training-free routing mechanism driven by Jensen-Shannon Divergence (JSD) sensitivity and confidence proxies for query-dependent memory affinity. Extensive evaluations across multimodal benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, outperforming single-paradigm baselines overall (+4.67\% over vanilla Episodic), with further analysis highlighting episodic strengths in evidence-localized tasks and latent contributions to integrating dispersed information and compensating for imperfect retrieval.

</details>


### [2] [Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B 24GB: A Hybrid Vision-Language Model for Local AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33039)
*Ganesh Ajjanagadde,Chris Alexiuk,Luke Alonso,Kumar Anshuman,Maor Ashkenazi,Nave Assaf,Gorkem Batmaz,Omer Belhasin,Shai Ben Nun,Akhiad Bercovich,Pritam Biswas,Caroline Blanco,Tijmen Blankevoort,Michael Boone,Christopher Borden,Kari Briski,Bryan Catanzaro,Zijia Chen,Aishwarya Chhabra,Hung-Yueh Chiang,Annamalai Chockalingam,Jonathan Cohen,Joey Conway,Olivier Delalleau,Gerardo Delgado Cabrera,Leon Derczynski,Li Ding,Will Efstratis,Michael Elad,Ellie Evans,Wes Feely,Michael Fukuyama,Gaurav Garg,Rishabh Garg,Arvind Gopalakrishnan,Shishir Goyal,Kartik Gulia,Hao Guo,Negar Habibi,Scott Hara,Ehsan Hosseini Asl,Rohit Jena,Udi Karpas,Bilal Kartal,Mayank Kaushik,Manoj Kilaru,Mackenzie Kosseim,Oleksii Kuchaiev,Matthieu Le,Raymond Lee,Jian Leong,Jonathan Li,Sheldon Liang,Mingjie Liu,Eileen Long,Katherine Luna,Chenjie Luo,Mingyuan Ma,Arsh Maknojia,Richard Mazzarese,Jessica Medina,Puhui Meng,Yoav Miron,Pavlo Molchanov,David Mosallanezhad,Chetan Mungekar,Marina Neseem,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Jason Paul,Bhavesh Pawar,Seth Poulos,Anav Prasad,Meredith Price,Sweta Priyadarshi,Haifeng Qian,Vinay Raman,Kaustubh Rao,Hayley Ross,Matan Rusanovsky,Sudeep Sabnis,Karan Sapra,Micah Schaffer,Schmitty,Alex Settle,Ashton Sharabiani,Haran Kumar Shiv Kumar,Rameshwar Shivbhakta,Guillermo Siman,Barnaby Simkin,Oliver Simons,Andrii Skliar,Padmavathy Subramanian,Yoshi Suhara,Ali Taghibakhshi,Nima Tajbakhsh,Andrew Tao,Roy Uziel,Prasoon Varshney,Jose Vergara,Huy Vu,Mengru Wang,Rochelle Woods,Pinky Xu,Dina Yared,Chenhan Yu,Chaosen Zhang,Jian Zhang,Yiheng Zhang,Ran Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B 24GB：一个混合 Mamba-Transformer 视觉语言模型，通过 Star Elastic 压缩与 2-bit 量化感知蒸馏，在 24GB 显存受限场景下实现高"每 GB 智能与速度"，并配套发布 LiLiCorr 投机解码草稿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向本地智能体（local agentic）负载，在内存受限条件下同时优化推理速度与模型智能密度（intelligence per GB of memory）。

Method: 基于 Nemotron 3.5 Super VL 120B-A12B 模型族派生；架构为混合 Mamba-Transformer（VLM）；采用 Star Elastic 压缩 + 2-bit 量化感知蒸馏（QAD）；另训练 LiLiCorr 投机解码草稿器以提升实际推理速度。

Result: 在 RTX Spark 上达 84 TPS，在 GDPVal v2、BrowseComp、Terminal-Bench 2.1 等通用智能体、网页浏览与终端任务基准上，准确率高于同等内存占用与速度的领先模型，并可与激活参数量低得多的模型速度相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明显存 24GB 级本地部署可支撑长时程智能体应用；该工作的核心贡献是将压缩、低位量化蒸馏与投机解码协同设计，以内存密度为优化目标。

Abstract: We introduce Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B 24GB, a hybrid Mamba-Transformer vision-language model that optimizes speed and intelligence per GB of memory used for local agentic workloads. Derived from the Nemotron 3.5 Super VL 120B-A12B model family, Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B 24GB combines Star Elastic compression with 2-bit Quantization-Aware Distillation for memory-constrained deployment. The model is optimized for reasoning, agentic tool use, and web & terminal interactions. We also trained a LiLiCorr speculative drafter that is released alongside the model to reach our target inference speed. The resulting model achieves a higher accuracy than leading models with similar memory footprints and speed across many benchmarks for general agentic capabilities (e.g., GDPVal v2), web browsing (e.g., BrowseComp), and terminal use (e.g., Terminal-Bench 2.1), with a relatively high TPS of 84 on RTX Spark that is comparable to models with much lower active parameter counts and more than sufficient for long-horizon agentic applications.

</details>


### [3] [EKT-Router: Expert-Evidence Alignment for Embodied Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33044)
*Weilin Ruan,Yingjie Xu,Yangkai Wei,Bohan Zhou,Shuaibo Li,Harold Haodong Chen,Kaiwen Zhou,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EKT-Router，联合选择能力专家、可选知识与感知工具，在调用预算下评分“专家-证据”完整路由，并经验证器过滤无效证据；在6个具身QA基准上均分61.4为最高，10个通用VLM基准均分74.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉-语言模型需在部分、演化的观测下作答，其表现既取决于所选推理能力，也取决于该能力可获得的外部证据。现有“专家路由”与“工具增强”各自独立进行，可能导致某专家被选中却缺乏所需证据，造成能力与证据不匹配。

Method: EKT-Router为有界联合路由框架：用结果导出的签名刻画General、Spatial Reasoning、Spatiotemporal三类专家能力的重叠优势；在调用预算约束下，以联合效用对完整的“专家-证据”路由打分并选择；获取证据后由packet verifier按“以专家为条件的预测增益为正”来接纳有效证据；最终所选专家仅作答一次，若全部证据包被拒则回退到未改动的观测。

Result: 在6个具身QA基准上平均得分61.4，为对比系统中最高；在10个通用视觉-语言基准上平均74.7。关闭全部外部证据时，仅凭问题条件化的专家选择即比最佳固定专家提升1.75分。消融显示学习式路由为最主要贡献组件，知识与感知呈互补性。

Conclusion: 能力选择与证据获取应联合决策而非分别优化；学习式专家路由带来主要增益，外部知识与感知工具作用互补，且验证器与预算约束保证资源有界与证据质量可控。

Abstract: Embodied vision-language models must answer questions from partial, evolving observations. Their success depends on both the reasoning capability selected and the evidence available to it. Expert routers adapt internal computation, while tool-augmented systems acquire external evidence; choosing these independently can leave a suitable expert without the evidence it needs. We introduce EKT-Router, a bounded framework that jointly selects a capability expert, optional knowledge, and perceptual tools. Outcome-derived signatures represent the overlapping strengths of General, Spatial Reasoning, and Spatiotemporal experts. A joint utility scores complete expert-evidence routes under a call budget. After acquisition, a packet verifier admits valid evidence with positive predicted expert-conditioned gain. The selected expert then answers once, reverting to the unchanged observation if all packets are rejected. Across 6 embodied QA benchmarks, EKT-Router achieves a mean score of 61.4, the highest among the compared systems, and averages 74.7 on 10 general vision-language benchmarks. With all external evidence disabled, question-conditioned expert selection alone improves over the best fixed expert by 1.75 points. Ablations show that learned routing is the dominant component and that knowledge and perception are complementary.

</details>


### [4] [Detection of false positives by human reviewers depends on the face recognition algorithm and its security configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33045)
*Esha Edakoth,John J. Howard,Isabelle M. Shuggi,Yevgeniy B. Sirotin,Robert Wood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究测量801名人类审核员在三种安全阈值下检测商业人脸识别(FR)算法假阳性错误的能力，发现人工审核可减少算法假阳性，但阈值越严格性能越差，且不同算法与人工监督的兼容性存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FR算法已达超人级规模与精度，但这反而使人工监督更困难，人类-AI团队的有效性存疑；需量化人类审核员对算法假阳性的检出能力，并考察安全配置与算法差异的影响。

Method: 构建新数据集DFICT（Difficult Facial Image Comparison Test），其素材取自四种可比商业FR算法的假阳性样本；以三种代表实际生物识别阈值的操作相关安全配置为条件，测量801名人类审核员检测算法假阳性的表现。

Result: 人类审核员能够减少算法假阳性，但在更严格的安全阈值下表现下降；审核员表现随算法不同而变化，说明部分算法与人工监督更兼容。

Conclusion: 生物识别系统设计应考虑人类审核员效能的可变性，尤其在高安全场景；结论可为算法选型及人工监督策略提供依据，以降低FR实际部署中的AI相关风险。

Abstract: Integration of artificial intelligence (AI) technology into identity workflows, such as automated face recognition (FR), has enhanced operational capabilities, with FR algorithms achieving super-human scale and accuracy. However, these gains can make human oversight of FR more difficult, raising questions about the effectiveness of these human-AI teams. We measured human reviewers' ability to detect false positive errors made by commercial FR algorithms across varying security configurations. Using a novel dataset, the Difficult Facial Image Comparison Test (DFICT), derived from false positives made by four comparable commercial face recognition algorithms, we measured the ability of 801human reviewers to detect algorithmic false positives across three security configurations, representing operationally relevant biometric thresholds. Our findings show that human reviewers can reduce algorithmic false positives, but performance declines at stricter security thresholds. Further, reviewer performance varied by algorithm indicating that some algorithms are more compatible with human oversight. We suggest that biometric systems should be designed to account for human reviewers' variable effectiveness, particularly in high-security applications. Our findings should be considered during algorithm selection and inform human oversight strategies to minimize AI-related risks in operational FR deployments.

</details>


### [5] [Saga: an in silico ontology for a multimodal patient world model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33054)
*Youcheng Li,Fa Tian,Binbin Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态"患者世界模型"Saga，从病史学习共享患者状态，用于预测临床事件并生成多模态图像，实现事件驱动的疾病轨迹推演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟个体疾病进程需将患者病史与未来事件、临床时序和疾病形态学联系起来；现有纵向预测与多模态表征虽利用这些信息，但在统一患者状态上组织未来读数、以及在缺乏真实中间观测时推进轨迹仍是核心挑战。

Method: Saga从患者历史中学习共享状态，据此预测临床事件并生成多模态图像；生成的事件与时间被追加回历史并重新编码以推进预测。研究纳入112个中心、12种癌症的461,370名患者，并采用模态特异的配对评估，将未来图像与观测随访对齐。

Result: 固定历史图像条件下，去除患者条件使事件F1由0.716降至0.591，病灶负荷误差由11.8%升至21.3%。自由rollout中八步事件F1为0.672（纵向Transformer为0.553），时序误差低8.7天。内部测试集一年进展AUROC为0.882，时间与地理外部验证分别为0.871和0.856。

Conclusion: 共享患者状态可跨观测尺度连接未来表型，并以事件驱动方式推进轨迹；在rollout中保持比较性预测优势，并在外部临床评估中维持判别能力，使患者级疾病模拟成为可与真实随访对照检验的多模态预测任务。

Abstract: Simulating an individual's disease course requires connecting patient history to future events, clinical timing and disease morphology. Longitudinal prediction and multimodal representations exploit this information, but organizing future readouts around a common patient state and advancing trajectories without real intermediate observations remain central challenges. Here we introduce Saga, a multimodal patient world model that learns a shared state from patient histories to predict clinical events and generate multimodal images. Generated events and times are appended to the history and re-encoded to advance prediction. We studied 461,370 patients with 12 cancer types across 112 centres. Modality-specific paired evaluations linked future images to observed follow-up. With historical images held fixed, removing patient conditioning reduced event F1 from 0.716 to 0.591 and increased lesion-burden error from 11.8% to 21.3%. In free rollouts, eight-step event F1 was 0.672, compared with 0.553 for a longitudinal Transformer evaluated from the same origins, and timing error was lower by 8.7 days. In the internal test set, one-year progression AUROC was 0.882, with values of 0.871 and 0.856 in temporal and geographical external evaluations. These results show that shared patient states connect future phenotypes across observational scales with event-driven trajectory progression, sustaining comparative predictive advantages during rollout and discrimination in external clinical evaluation. Saga makes patient-level disease simulation a multimodal prediction task that can be tested against observed follow-up.

</details>


### [6] [GMoE-FTI: A Universal Facial Template Inversion Method Adopting Genuine Mixture-of-Experts Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33055)
*Zixuan Shen,Zhihua Xia,Kaikai Gan,Peipeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通用型人脸模板反演框架GMoE-FTI，借助真实MoE机制与三阶段训练，可从多种异构模板重建身份一致的人脸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸模板反演（FTI）可威胁生物特征隐私，但现有FTI方法仅适配单一类型模板，无法对未见类型模板重建身份一致的人脸，通用性不足。

Method: 依据模板分布将主流模板提取器分为五类（unit-sphere、angle-linear、strict unit-sphere、cone-shaped、anuniform hypersphere）；提出GMoE-FTI，采用真正的MoE机制（区别于负载均衡的多分支替代方案），三阶段训练：(1) 独立训练五个映射专家（ME）与匹配模块（MM）；(2) 用MM输出加权各ME输出进行协同推理，生成与预训练StyleGAN3兼容的统一隐码；(3) 对所有模块端到端联合微调以增强身份一致性与跨专家协作。

Result: 在LFW基准上优于SOTA方法；在AdaFace、CosFace、CurricularFace、ElasticFace、SphereFace2、SphereFace-R、TransFace七种未见模板类型上平均TAR提升约40%，FAPD与FAPC各降低约2%。

Conclusion: GMoE-FTI实现了对异构模板的通用泛化，验证了基于模板分布分类的MoE设计在跨类型模板反演中的有效性。

Abstract: Face recognition systems have been ubiquitously deployed across daily scenarios. To protect user privacy, mainstream systems store facial templates rather than raw face images for identity verification. However, facial template inversion (FTI) technologies can reconstruct faces from templates, which endangers biometric privacy. Existing FTI methods only fit single type of templates, failing to reconstruct identity-consistent faces from unseen types. To tackle this limitation, we survey mainstream template extractors which can be classified into five categories according the template distribution: unit-sphere, angle-linear, strict unit-sphere, cone-shaped, anuniform hypersphere. Inspired by this observation, this paper proposes GMoE-FTI, a universal FTI framework powered by a genuine MoE mechanism that differs from load-balanced multi-branch alternatives. It follows a three-stage training strategy: (1) independently train five mapping experts (MEs) and a matching module (MM); (2) conduct collaborative inference by weighting each ME's output with MM's output, so as to generate unified latent codes compatible with pre-trained StyleGAN3; (3) perform end-to-end joint fine-tuning over all modules to identity consistency and boost cross-expert coordination. Extensive evaluations on the LFW benchmark validate the superiority of GMoE-FTI against SOTA methods. Evaluated on seven prevailing unseen template types including AdaFace, CosFace, CurricularFace, ElasticFace, SphereFace2, SphereFace-R, and TransFace, GMoE-FTI achieves an average 40% TAR improvement, with average FAPD and FAPC each reduced by 2%, verifying its universal generalization across heterogeneous templates.

</details>


### [7] [Aerial-R1: Reinforcing Aerial Reasoning and Segmentation in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33056)
*Jisheng Dang,Huaiyuan Peng,Yixuan Wang,Kun Wang,Zhang Li,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Aerial-R1，首个面向航空领域的 MLLM 强化学习框架，通过跨视角一致性作为视觉版思维链，并用 H-GAWR 将群组相对优势估计引入非自回归掩码生成，实现拒绝机制以抑制航空场景幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在航空域因极端尺度变化与视觉歧义而幻觉严重；SFT 范式迫使模型模仿真值、不建模不确定性，导致对微小或不可辨识目标盲目猜测。

Method: 受 DeepSeek-R1 推理激励机制启发，以高/低分辨率多视角一致性类比视觉 Chain-of-Thought；提出 Hybrid-View Group Advantage-Weighted Regression (H-GAWR)，动态构建多分辨率视角组，将群组相对优势估计与优势加权策略提取结合，以适配非自回归掩码生成的策略优化；对清晰输入强制一致、对模糊输入严格惩罚幻觉，形成显式激励的拒绝机制。

Result: 在首个联合评测密集推理与幻觉鲁棒性的航空 MLLM 基准 VSAI-Ref 上，Aerial-R1 的分割精度显著优于 SFT 基线，并大幅缓解低信息场景下的致命幻觉。

Conclusion: 跨视角一致性可作为视觉推理的可信感知信号，强化学习式策略优化能有效替代 SFT 以提升航空 MLLM 的可靠性与可信度，为构建可信航空智能体提供路径；代码已开源。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have revolutionized general visual perception, existing methods suffer from severe hallucination in the aerial domain, where extreme scale variations and visual ambiguity abound. Existing Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) paradigms force models to mimic ground truth without modeling uncertainty, often resulting in blind guessing on tiny or unrecognizable objects in aerial scenes. In this paper, we present Aerial-R1, the first aerial MLLM framework to reinforce visual reasoning capabilities for aerial reasoning and segmentation. Drawing inspiration from the reasoning-incentivizing mechanisms of DeepSeek-R1, we find that trustworthy perception stems from consistency across views, establishing a visual parallel to the Chain-of-Thought in logic. To effectively adapt policy optimization for non-autoregressive mask generation, we introduce Hybrid-View Group Advantage-Weighted Regression (H-GAWR). This strategy constructs dynamic groups of high-resolution and low-resolution views, seamlessly bridging group-relative advantage estimation with advantage-weighted policy extraction. By enforcing consensus on clear inputs and strictly penalizing hallucinations on ambiguous ones, Aerial-R1 develops an explicitly incentivized refusal mechanism. Extensive experiments on VSAI-Ref, the first aerial MLLM benchmark dedicated to jointly evaluating dense reasoning and hallucination robustness, demonstrate that Aerial-R1 significantly outperforms SFT baselines in segmentation precision while substantially mitigating fatal hallucinations in low-information scenarios, paving the way for more reliable and trustworthy aerial agents. Codes are available on https://github.com/phy709/Aerial-R1.

</details>


### [8] [The Paired Thermal Channel Is Two Orthogonal Axes: A Controlled Attribution Study of Calibration Cost and Detection Gain in Infrared Person Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33118)
*Tian Zhang,Yang Chen,Bing Bai,Weikai He,Xiang Gao,Zhirong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文以温度证据学习(TEL)为试验平台，通过五组受控消融解耦红外行人检测中"校准热通道"的配对轴与尺度轴、以及探测轴的贡献，并给出一项有限证据退化定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外行人检测中的校准ΔT通道与灰度伪ΔT通道常被混用，其价值来源（配对对应关系、绝对校准尺度、空间结构）未被解耦归因，导致增益来源不清、校准质量与检测精度关系不明。

Method: 五臂受控归因实验 + TEL测试平台：以真ΔT对比尺度匹配的灰度伪ΔT；构造2×2（配对×尺度）交互以定位ECE上升；采用相位随机化破坏空间结构而保留样本方差以检验探测轴；引入事后温度缩放检验校准代价可重校准性；理论上证明有限证据退化定理与闭式界。

Result: 真ΔT较灰度伪ΔT提升+0.0221 mAP（配对Wilcoxon p=0.0098, d=+1.08）；保持配对与绝对校准尺度使ECE由≈0.05升至0.142，2×2交互p=0.0059定位来源，破坏任一因子即回落（p=0.002）；温度缩放后配对代价仍存（p=0.002），尺度代价被吸收（p=0.16）。相位随机化在FLIR ADAS上较真ΔT提升+0.0185 mAP（p=0.014, d=+0.96），FLAME 3为+0.0064 mAP（p=0.078, n=8）；校准轴上FLIR ADAS不显著（p=0.47），FLAME 3显著（p=0.0078）。物理0.5°C阈值可跨数据集通用，灰度阈值20则发生反转且无物理解释。

Conclusion: 校准通道的检测增益主要来自结构去结构化而非标定尺度本身；配对与尺度两轴对ECE的影响可分离，配对代价不可被事后温度缩放消除而尺度代价可被吸收；有限证据退化定理说明证据路由标量在有限证据下退化为唯一标量并具闭式界，物理阈值较灰度阈值具备跨数据集可解释性。

Abstract: The calibrated thermal channel in infrared person detection comprises two structurally orthogonal axes. True calibrated $ΔT$ outperforms scale-matched grayscale pseudo-$ΔT$ by $+0.0221$ mAP (paired Wilcoxon $p=0.0098$, Cohen's $d=+1.08$), confirming that the calibrated channel carries information the grayscale channel lacks. The calibration axis is driven by the joint pairing-scale condition. Preserving both paired correspondence and absolute calibrated scale raises expected calibration error (ECE) from $\approx 0.05$ to $0.142$. The $2\times 2$ interaction ($p=0.0059$) localizes the rise to this condition. Breaking either factor alone returns ECE to the broken-factor level (true-vs-const $p=0.002$; true-vs-shuffled $p=0.002$). Under post-hoc temperature scaling, the pairing cost survives recalibration ($p=0.002$), while the scale cost is largely absorbed ($p=0.16$). As regards the detection axis, the gain stems from structural de-structuring. Phase randomization, which destroys spatial structure while preserving per-sample variance, yields $+0.0185$ mAP over true $ΔT$ on FLIR ADAS ($p=0.014$, $d=+0.96$). The FLAME~3 gain is $+0.0064$ mAP ($p=0.078$, n=8). On the calibration axis, the same intervention is insignificant on FLIR ADAS ($p=0.47$) but significant on FLAME~3 ($p=0.0078$). These findings rest on a five-arm controlled attribution with temperature-evidential learning (TEL) as the testbed. TEL obtains a single evidence-derived routing scalar together with a complementary diagnostic entropy, and establishes a finite-evidence degeneracy theorem proving that the acquisition product degenerates to this scalar with a closed-form bound. A physical $0.5^\circ$C threshold admits cross-dataset physical readings, whereas a gray threshold of $20$ inverts without physical interpretation.

</details>


### [9] [DRA-SAM: Disagreement-Driven Referential Active SAM for Low-Altitude Drone Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33057)
*Jisheng Dang,Huaiyuan Peng,Wencan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Jingwen Zhao,Wenjun Ma,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRA-SAM，一种基于"指称分歧"驱动的选择性局部再推理框架，用于无人机低空影像的指称分割，在RIS-LAD和UAVid-RIS上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无人机影像的指称分割需在复杂语言与空间约束下区分小而视觉相似的实例。全局推理可能产生多个可行掩码，或使目标指称物表征不足，因而有必要进行选择性局部再推理。

Method: 框架依据竞争性指称假设与"未决状态"分配额外一次推理：轻量EvidenceScorer估计已有候选及未决状态的置信；免训练的Referential Disagreement LOOK策略权衡信念加权候选竞争与未决区域覆盖来选择裁剪区域；由同一基准适配的SAM3-I骨干处理该区域，证据用于更新既有假设并引入新候选。

Result: 在RIS-LAD与UAVid-RIS的7项指标中6项达SOTA（含mIoU与P@0.5–P@0.9），RIS-LAD验证集全部7项指标最优。RIS-LAD上mIoU由49.55升至52.34，P@0.5由46.80升至58.05；UAVid-RIS上独立适配版达73.26 mIoU与86.86 P@0.5。受控裁剪策略对比验证了分歧引导裁剪选择的有效性，目标占比小于1%的航拍目标增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 指称分歧可作为分配局部推理的有效准则。

Abstract: Referring segmentation in low-altitude drone imagery requires distinguishing small, visually similar instances under complex linguistic and spatial constraints. Global inference may yield multiple plausible masks or leave the intended referent insufficiently represented, motivating selective local re-inference. We propose DRA-SAM, a disagreement-driven framework that allocates an additional inference pass according to competing referential hypotheses and an unresolved state. A lightweight EvidenceScorer estimates beliefs over existing candidates and the unresolved state. A training-free Referential Disagreement LOOK policy balances belief-weighted candidate competition and unresolved-region coverage to select a crop for local re-inference. The same benchmark-adapted SAM3-I backbone processes the selected region, and the resulting evidence updates existing hypotheses and introduces new candidates. Among the compared methods, DRA-SAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on six of seven test metrics on both RIS-LAD and UAVid-RIS, including mIoU and P@0.5-P@0.9, and on all seven RIS-LAD validation metrics. On RIS-LAD, DRA-SAM improves the matched global SAM3-I baseline from 49.55 to 52.34 mIoU and from 46.80 to 58.05 P@0.5. On UAVid-RIS, the independently adapted DRA-SAM achieves 73.26 mIoU and 86.86 P@0.5. Controlled crop-policy comparisons demonstrate the effectiveness of disagreement-guided crop selection, while target-size analysis reveals particularly pronounced gains for aerial targets occupying less than 1% of an image. These results establish referential disagreement as an effective criterion for allocating local inference. Code is available at https://github.com/phy709/DRA-SAM.

</details>


### [10] [AV-FABLE: Audio-Visual Manipulation Forensics in Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33413)
*Yuesheng Huang,Hoi Fan Au,Jiaqi Hou,Ruicheng Zhang,Jiarui He,Haocheng Yang,Bin Hu,Kewei Zhang,Weijian Deng,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AV-FABLE双时间线基准（16,650对、998小时、27种编辑配置）用于开放场景长视频音视频稀疏篡改定位，并揭示AV-LLM零样本近随机但冻结特征可读出强取证信号，进而提出NFSR双模态分离读出方法取得域内及三类OOD最优定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频中的局部AI篡改只影响短暂事件，视频级分类不足以支撑取证；现有音视频deepfake基准多为短时说话人头部片段，长视频时序取证主要局限于视觉模态，缺乏音视频双时间线、模态可分的时间定位评测。

Method: 构建AV-FABLE：开放场景长视频中稀疏篡改的原始-篡改孪生对，含独立音频与视觉时间线，支持模态特定时间定位并暴露联合时间线评测所掩盖的错误。分析AV-LLM零样本提示与冻结感知特征的监督读出表现。提出NFSR，用分离的音频与视觉时序解码器读取冻结AV-LLM特征。

Result: AV-FABLE含16,650对孪生样本、998小时、27种编辑配置。AV-LLM零样本提示检测接近随机、时间定位差；冻结特征监督读出可恢复强取证信号。NFSR在域内及全部三种OOD设置（含迁移至外部长视频取证基准的留出视频）取得最佳音视频定位性能。

Conclusion: 篡改证据已编码于AV-LLM表示中但未被语言输出有效暴露；分离模态时序读出是长视频音视频稀疏篡改定位的有效范式。AV-FABLE、代码与模型权重将公开。

Abstract: Localized AI manipulations in long-form videos often affect brief events within otherwise authentic content, making video-level classification insufficient for forensic analysis. Existing audio-visual deepfake benchmarks largely focus on short talking-head clips, while long-form temporal forensics remains predominantly visual. We introduce AV-FABLE, a dual-timeline benchmark for sparse manipulations in open-scene long-form audio-visual videos. AV-FABLE contains 16,650 pristine--manipulated twin pairs (998 hours) created with 27 editing configurations, with separate audio and visual timelines that enable modality-specific temporal localization and expose errors hidden by joint-timeline evaluation. On AV-FABLE, zero-shot prompting of audio-visual large language models (AV-LLMs) yields near-chance detection and poor temporal localization. In contrast, supervised readouts of their frozen perception features recover strong forensic signals, suggesting manipulation evidence is encoded in these representations but not effectively exposed through language outputs. Motivated by this observation, we propose NFSR(Native Forensics via Separated Readouts), which reads frozen AV-LLM features with separate audio and visual temporal decoders. NFSR achieves the best audio-visual localization performance in-domain and across all three out-of-distribution settings, including transfer to held-out videos from an external long-video forensic benchmark. AV-FABLE, code, and model weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [11] [KDHNet: A Knowledge-Driven Hybrid Network for Limited-Calibration SSVEP Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33058)
*Zhentao He,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KDHNet，将知识驱动的解析空间滤波（TDCA/TRCA + 可学习子带加权）与数据驱动的SSVEPformer解码器结合，并用以目标引导最小二乘变换实现跨被试对齐，在低校准SSVEP解码中优于纯解析与纯深度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSVEP-BCI虽信息传输率高，但可靠解码依赖充足的被试专属校准数据：解析空间滤波（如TDCA/TRCA）在小样本下有效，却受线性形式限制、表征能力有限；深度模型表征灵活但少样本易过拟合。需兼顾二者优势。

Method: 滤波组预处理后，级联TDCA与TRCA提取判别性、试次可重复成分；引入可学习子带权重融合频谱信息；由SSVEPformer解码器学习时–频表征并分类。跨被试场景下，用目标引导最小二乘变换，仅凭每类一个带标签目标试次对齐源域试次。消融中对TDCA投影采用固定、解析初始化和随机初始化优化三种方式比较。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上覆盖被试内/被试间协议、5种分析窗与多档校准比例：被试内15种窗口设置中14种、全部10种校准比设置下平均准确率与ITR最高；被试间取得最佳或次佳，且校准数据越少增益越大。消融显示固定TDCA投影优于从解析或随机初始化再优化。

Conclusion: 将固定知识先验与可训练神经模块相结合，对低校准SSVEP解码有效，可支撑更实用、鲁棒且即插即用的BCI系统。

Abstract: Steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP)-based brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) achieve high information transfer rates but often require sufficient subject-specific calibration data for reliable decoding. Analytical spatial filters are effective with limited data, but their linear formulations constrain representation capacity. Deep models learn more flexible representations but are prone to overfitting when calibration data are scarce. We propose KDHNet, which combines knowledge-driven analytical processing with data-driven representation learning. After filter-bank preprocessing, cascaded task-discriminant component analysis (TDCA) and task-related component analysis (TRCA) extract discriminative and trial-reproducible components. Learnable sub-band weighting then fuses spectral information, and an SSVEPformer decoder learns temporal and spectral representations for classification. For inter-subject decoding, a target-guided least-squares transformation aligns source trials using one labeled target trial per class. Extensive experiments were performed on three public datasets, covering intra- and inter-subject protocols, five analysis windows, and multiple calibration ratios. In intra-subject evaluation, KDHNet achieved the highest mean accuracy and information transfer rate in 14 of 15 window settings and all ten calibration-ratio settings. In inter-subject evaluation, KDHNet obtained the best or second-best performance, and the gains were generally larger with less calibration data. Ablation studies indicated that the estimated TDCA projection performed better when fixed than when optimized from analytical or random initialization. Combining fixed knowledge priors with trainable neural modules is effective for low-calibration SSVEP decoding. This framework supports more practical, robust, and plug-and-play BCIs. Code is available at https://github.com/2665023991/KDHNet.

</details>


### [12] [Freeze What Is Settled, Cache What Is Smooth: Spatial Freezing Keeps Step Caching in Its Low-Error Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34177)
*Baiyan Li,Ganxuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreezePlan 在扩散 Transformer 指令式图像编辑中重新分配加速预算：先冻结未编辑区域以降低每步成本，再把剩余预算交给 TaylorSeer 步缓存，实现 5.05 倍端到端加速、29.8 dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练加速分为步缓存（复用跨步特征）与区域冻结（跳过未编辑区域计算）两类，且已有工作将二者组合，但缺乏对"高速加速目标应如何在两者间分配"的系统分析；作者希望厘清预算拆分的收益来源并提升组合效率。

Method: 提出每步成本模型（在开发集上拟合，用于预测延迟与加速上限）；分析冻结对每步成本的降低效应，使步缓存可用更保守（误差更小）的阈值达成同等总加速；将冻结 token 驱动到模型的一步 x₀ 估计而非回填参考图；最终方法 FreezePlan 保留 5 个完整步，其余预算分配给 TaylorSeer。

Result: 成本模型对留出样本的延迟预测中位误差 4.0%，复现 RegionE 加速上限 2.73×（预测）vs 2.70×（实测）；x₀ 填充在每个开发样本上优于参考图回填；90% 的实测案例中组合误差低于两项误差之和；预注册测试划分上 5.05× 加速、29.8 dB PSNR，VLM 评分无显著下降（TaylorSeer 单用在 5.02× 时仅 23.5 dB），曲线可延展至 5.99×@28.0 dB；替换为先前方法的区域选择器仅使 PSNR 中位数下降 0.5–0.6 dB。

Conclusion: 加速增益主要来自调度与预算拆分，而非区域选择器本身；冻结降低每步成本、为缓存换取更紧的误差预算，是组合两类加速方法的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editors built on diffusion transformers run tens of denoising steps. Training-free acceleration either reuses features across steps (step caching) or stops computing the regions an edit leaves untouched (region freezing), and the two have been combined before. We ask how a high speed-up target should be split between them. On Qwen-Image-2.1, freezing does more than remove tokens: it lowers the cost of every step, so the step cache can reach the same overall speed-up with a conservative threshold that keeps its error small. A per-step cost model fitted on development runs predicts held-out latency with a median error of 4.0% and reproduces the speed ceilings of the region methods we re-implemented (2.73$\times$ predicted vs. 2.70$\times$ measured for RegionE). Driving frozen tokens to the model's one-step $x_0$ estimate beats returning them to the reference image on every development sample, and in 90% of measured cases the composed error stays below the sum of the two individual errors. The resulting method, FreezePlan, keeps five full steps and gives the rest of the budget to TaylorSeer. On a pre-registered test split it runs 5.05$\times$ faster end to end at 29.8 dB PSNR to the original output, with no significant drop in a VLM judge score; TaylorSeer at 5.02$\times$ reaches 23.5 dB. The curve extends to 5.99$\times$ at 28.0 dB. Swapping in the region selectors of prior methods under our schedule lowers median PSNR by only 0.5--0.6 dB, which places the gain in the schedule and budget split rather than in the selector.

</details>


### [13] [MOSAIC: Towards a Universal Latent Space for Computational Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33059)
*Dibaloke Chanda,Praveen Madiraju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MOSAIC，系统研究 12 个 patch 级与 6 个 slide 级病理基础模型（PFM）的潜在空间相似性、对齐与互操作性，考察异构 PFM 是否学习的是同一潜在表示的"不同坐标系"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型在架构、预训练目标、训练数据和表示维度上差异巨大，但尚不清楚它们学习的是本质不同的特征空间，还是同一共享潜在表示的不同坐标系统；缺乏统一的比较与对齐框架。

Method: （1）用互补的相似度度量量化表示一致性，并分析架构、预训练目标、网络深度与放大倍率对潜在几何的影响；（2）评估多种对齐策略，将独立学习的表示映射到公共潜在空间；（3）通过跨模型一致性、检索、几何保持以及面向分子与形态预测任务的下游多示例学习（MIL）进行评估。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅给出评估维度：跨模型一致性、检索性能、几何保持性与下游 MIL 表现；并提供代码、基准与附加结果的开源链接。

Conclusion: MOSAIC 提供了统一框架，使研究从"表示相似性"推进到构建共享且可功能迁移的病理表示空间，为异构 PFM 的互操作性研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) differ substantially in architecture, pretraining objective, training data and representation dimensionality, yet it remains unclear whether they learn fundamentally different feature spaces or alternative coordinate systems of a shared underlying representation. We present MOSAIC, a systematic study of latent-space similarity, alignment and interoperability across $12$ patch-level and $6$ slide-level PFMs. We first quantify representational agreement using complementary similarity measures and analyze how architecture, pretraining objective, network depth and magnification influence latent geometry. We then evaluate multiple alignment strategies for mapping independently learned representations into a common latent space. The resulting representations are evaluated through cross-model agreement, retrieval, geometry preservation, and downstream multiple-instance learning across molecular and morphological prediction tasks. MOSAIC provides a unified framework for studying whether heterogeneous PFMs can move beyond representational similarity toward a shared and functionally transferable pathology representation space. Our code, benchmarks and additional results are available at https://researchsubmissions66.github.io/MOSAIC/.

</details>


### [14] [MARC: Mechanism-Aware Reflective Correction for Articulated Object Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33060)
*Xiong Yang,Junkun Hao,Zijian Xiao,Zipeng Ye,Yuchen Xie,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARC用共享的可执行运动学图统一程序构建、运动求解与纠错，从图像和文本生成能真正执行预期运动的铰接机构，在20个对象测试集上达到67.8%有效运动覆盖率与60.0%总体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从图像和文本生成铰接物体，不仅需要合理的部件和可动关节，更需要装配后的机构能执行预期运动；对闭链与耦合机构尤其困难，单个连接错误就可能导致运动卡死或部件分离。

Method: 提出Mechanism-Aware Reflective Correction（MARC）：在程序构建、运动求解和纠错之间共享一个可执行运动学图，将物理连接绑定到局部附着坐标系，并表达闭环与关节坐标耦合。给定驱动输入后，数值求解器计算从动运动，并把误差归因到具体连接与构型；智能体据此诊断可能原因、修正连接或几何，并沿原运动路径重新测试。

Result: 在20对象测试集上，MARC配合Kimi-K3取得67.8%有效运动覆盖率和60.0%总体成功率，优于六个基线；从相同初始候选出发，纠错使两项指标分别提升13.7和20.0个百分点；消融实验显示基于图的机构支持与图引导修复均有效。

Conclusion: 以可执行运动学图为中枢、结合误差归因与迭代修复的机制感知方案，能显著提升铰接机构生成的可执行性与成功率；图结构支持与图引导修复是性能增益的关键来源。

Abstract: Generating an articulated object from images and text requires more than plausible parts and movable joints: the assembled mechanism must execute the intended motion. This is particularly challenging for closed-chain and coupled mechanisms, where an error in one connection can block motion or separate connected parts. We introduce MARC, Mechanism-Aware Reflective Correction, which shares an executable kinematic graph across program construction, motion solving, and correction. The graph binds physical connections to local attachment frames and represents loop closures and joint-coordinate couplings. Given prescribed driver inputs, a numerical solver computes follower motion and associates errors with specific connections and configurations. The agent uses these diagnostics to probe possible causes, revise connections or geometry, and retest the mechanism along the original motion paths. On a 20-object test set, MARC with Kimi-K3 achieves 67.8% valid motion coverage and 60.0% overall success, outperforming six baselines. Starting from the same initial candidates, correction improves these scores by 13.7 and 20.0 percentage points, respectively. Ablations further show the benefits of graph-based mechanism support and graph-guided repair. Project homepage: https://demianmusk.github.io/MARC-Home/.

</details>


### [15] [Oddin: Latent Visual Reasoning in Unified Multimodal Models via Self-Distillation from Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33062)
*Jaeyong Ko,Hankyeol Kim,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Oddin框架，使统一多模态大模型无需生成中间图像即可进行潜空间视觉推理，在七个视觉推理任务上超越交错生成基线与闭源VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型通过交错文本-图像生成实现视觉推理，但显式生成中间图像带来显著计算开销；现有潜空间视觉推理方法多依赖不具备图像生成能力的VLM。

Method: 用紧凑的潜空间token替代中间图像，全部由理解分支处理，生成分支仅负责最终输出图像；采用离线自蒸馏训练，含三个目标：最终图像生成损失、文本蒸馏损失、特殊token的交叉熵损失。

Result: 在VBVR-Pro的七项视觉推理任务上平均性能超越交错生成基线与闭源VLM；定性分析显示潜空间token能关注输入图像中的相关区域。

Conclusion: 统一多模态大模型可在不生成中间图像的情况下完成复杂视觉推理。

Abstract: Unified large multimodal models (ULMMs) are able to perform visual reasoning through interleaved text-image generation, but generating explicit intermediate images incurs significant computational overhead. While visual latent reasoning addresses this, most existing methods rely on vision-language models (VLMs) incapable of visual generation. We propose Oddin, a novel framework enabling latent visual reasoning in ULMMs. Oddin replaces intermediate images with compact latent tokens processed entirely by the understanding branch, reserving the generation branch for the final output image. We train the model via offline self-distillation with three objectives, final image generation losses, text distillation losses, and cross-entropy losses for special tokens. Oddin surpasses interleaved generation baselines and closed-source VLMs on average across seven VBVR-Pro visual reasoning tasks. Qualitative analysis reveals that latent tokens attend to relevant regions in an input image, demonstrating that ULMMs can perform complex visual reasoning without generating intermediate images.

</details>


### [16] [A Canary in the Neural Mine: Catching an Image Classifier's Silent, Confident Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33063)
*Wonseok Shin,Songkuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CANARY：一种免训练检测器，用倒数第二层特征PCA残差上的类条件kNN距离（分位数校准）与MSP单权重融合，弥补MSP对正交特征变化的盲区，在385个shift单元中382个匹配或超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的视觉模型自动接受高置信预测，导致高置信错误静默传播。标准失败预测器MSP是分类头读出的标量，对与其置信梯度正交的特征变化一阶盲。已有的特征空间检测器未能有效补充MSP，需要解释其失效原因并寻找真正互补的信号。

Method: 对特征空间检测器做因子分析，提出"条件冗余原理"：所测量的类可分性几何已被MSP编码，融合时坍缩。残存信号是离流形残差上的类条件典型性，且其分布随骨干网络从近高斯到重尾变化，需非参数估计。据此实现CANARY：在倒数第二层特征的PCA残差上计算逐类kNN距离，用验证数据分位数校准，再以单个验证调优权重与MSP融合；所有参数仅用分布内数据选择。

Result: 在五个骨干网络上，385个shift单元中382个（e-AURC）、379个（AUROC）匹配或提升，最差回归在种子噪声内（−0.0002 AUROC）。增益遵循"余量定律"：基线MSP质量越低增益越大，可用单一干净验证数值预测改进；在ImageNet-A/R、ObjectNet等自然分布偏移上增益最大。

Conclusion: 可解释MSP的结构性盲区与特征检测器的条件冗余失效，并表明非参数化的类条件典型性残差信号是有效互补源；CANARY以数据高效、免训练、低风险的方式显著提升OOD/偏移检测鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deployed vision models auto-accept confident predictions, so high-confidence errors propagate silently. Maximum Softmax Probability (MSP), the standard failure predictor, has a structural blind spot: as a scalar read from the classifier head, it is first-order blind to feature variation orthogonal to its confidence gradient. Through a factorial analysis of feature-space detectors, we identify a conditional-redundancy principle explaining why they fail to complement MSP: the class-separability geometry they measure is already encoded in MSP and collapses under fusion. The signal that survives is class-conditional typicality on the off-manifold residual---and because this residual's distribution varies by backbone, from near-Gaussian to heavy-tailed, it must be estimated non-parametrically. We instantiate these findings as CANARY, a training-free detector that computes a per-class kNN distance on the PCA residual of the penultimate feature, percentile-calibrates it on validation data, and fuses it with MSP through a single validation-tuned weight. CANARY's benefit follows a headroom law: gains grow as baseline MSP quality falls, so a single clean-validation number forecasts the improvement. With every parameter selected on in-distribution data alone, CANARY matches or improves 382 of 385 shift cells (e-AURC; 379 on AUROC) across five backbones, with worst-case regression within seed noise ($-0.0002$ AUROC) and the largest gains on natural distribution shifts (ImageNet-A/R, ObjectNet)---where deployed models are most exposed.

</details>


### [17] [ProtoEEG: Structured Prototype Alignment for EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33065)
*Kieren Yu,Chang Huang,Ziyang Liu,Kaishun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoEEG将EEG-图像对齐从稠密图像嵌入匹配改为共享可学习视觉码本上的软组合，用原型对比等约束实现结构化跨模态对齐，在THINGS-EEG2 200类检索上达64.0% Top-1，并显著优于基于稠密嵌入目标的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG视觉解码面临头皮信号噪声大、空间分辨率粗、跨试次与跨被试变异大的问题；现有系统多以稠密图像嵌入作为对齐目标，但该嵌入混合了语义内容与实例特有的纹理、姿态和背景，要求EEG提供本不具备的细节，且无法显式说明支撑预测的视觉因素。

Method: 提出原型中介框架ProtoEEG：可训练EEG编码器与冻结视觉教师对同一K=64的原型码本输出分配分布，并以加权重建构成结构化表示用于检索与图像生成。训练信号包括原型对比（主信号）、跨模态分布对齐、实例锚点（保留细粒度判别）与多样性正则（防止原型冗余）。共享分配剖面使瓶颈可直接检验（原型保留、随机选择、身份打乱）。

Result: 五种子平均下THINGS-EEG2 200类检索达64.0% Top-1、91.5% Top-5；在EEG-ImageNet重训练后80类检索达44.8% Top-1、73.8% Top-5；七项重建指标均排名第一。消融显示移除原型对比使Top-1下降56.9个百分点，打乱原型身份后降至0.5%。原型分析显示分配呈分布式、码本使用广泛，而非单一原型路由。

Conclusion: 结构化对齐目标可作为单纯增大编码器或教师容量的实用替代方案；原型中介的瓶颈提供了可解释、可检验的跨模态对齐机制。

Abstract: EEG visual decoding remains difficult because scalp recordings are noisy, spatially coarse, and variable across trials and subjects. Most systems align each trial with a dense image embedding, although that embedding mixes semantic content with instance-specific texture, pose, and background. This target can demand details absent from EEG and provides no explicit account of the visual factors supporting a prediction. We propose \textbf{ProtoEEG}, a prototype-mediated framework that reformulates EEG--image alignment as soft composition over a shared learnable visual codebook. A trainable EEG encoder and a frozen visual teacher produce assignment distributions over the same $K=64$ prototypes. Their weighted reconstructions form a structured representation for retrieval and image generation. Prototype contrast supplies the main cross-modal training signal, distribution alignment coordinates codebook use across modalities, an instance anchor preserves fine-grained discrimination, and diversity regularization prevents redundant prototypes. The shared assignment profile also makes the learned bottleneck directly testable through prototype retention, random selection, and identity shuffling. Across five seeds, ProtoEEG reaches 64.0\% Top-1 and 91.5\% Top-5 on THINGS-EEG2 200-way retrieval. After retraining on EEG-ImageNet, it obtains 44.8\% Top-1 and 73.8\% Top-5 in 80-way retrieval. ProtoEEG also ranks first on all seven reconstruction metrics. Removing prototype contrast lowers Top-1 by 56.9 percentage points, while shuffling prototype identities reduces it to 0.5\%. Prototype analyses further show distributed assignments and broad codebook use rather than one-prototype routing. These results support structured alignment targets as a practical alternative to increasing encoder or teacher capacity alone.

</details>


### [18] [OvisOCR3 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33066)
*Yu Xia,Yinglun Li,Yuhui Chen,Jinlong Huang,Yuyang Wanyan,Shengze Shi,Yifei Song,Yue Duan,Shubo Lin,Jianshan Zhao,En Lin,Shiyin Lu,Qing-Guo Chen,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OvisOCR3：一个0.8B统一模型，覆盖文档解析、通用OCR、关键信息抽取、文档图表描述与跨页表格重建五类任务，通过继续预训练、域平衡中训练、多教师在线策略蒸馏与混合任务强化学习训练，在多项公开与内部基准上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR相关任务通常由通用视觉语言模型或专用OCR模型分别处理，缺乏单一紧凑模型对多任务族的统一覆盖；作者希望验证小参数规模模型能否在多样化OCR任务上同时具备强性能。

Method: 构建0.8B统一模型，联合建模五类OCR相关任务；采用真实与合成数据进行继续预训练，并以域平衡的中训练平衡不同数据量与响应长度带来的监督不均；随后通过多教师在线策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation）将五个领域专用4B教师模型的能力迁移至共享模型，最后进行带领域特定奖励的混合任务强化学习。

Result: OmniDocBench v1.6总体得分96.74；PureDocBench、Real5-OmniDocBench、MDPBench总体得分最高；OCRBench v2英文/中文分项65.9与61.3，排名第一；OCRBench及四个KIE基准均优于基线；DocFigCapBench定位F1最高，描述得分接近Qwen3.8-Max；跨页表格在CrossTabBench的Relation F1与MPDocBench-Parse的截断表格TEDS上领先。

Conclusion: 紧凑的0.8B模型可在多样化OCR相关任务上取得强性能，表明“多任务统一+蒸馏+强化学习”的训练范式可有效替代大模型或任务专用模型方案；模型已开源。

Abstract: We introduce OvisOCR3, a 0.8B model for a broad range of OCR-related tasks. OvisOCR3 unifies document parsing, general OCR, key information extraction (KIE), document figure captioning, and cross-page table reconstruction in a single model. We train the model on real and synthetic data through continued pretraining and use domain-balanced mid-training to balance supervision across tasks with different data volumes and response lengths. Multi-teacher on-policy distillation transfers expertise from five domain-specific 4B teachers into the shared model, followed by mixed-task reinforcement learning with domain-specific rewards. We compare OvisOCR3 with general-purpose vision-language models and specialized OCR models on public and in-house benchmarks covering all five task families. In these comparisons, it achieves an overall score of 96.74 on OmniDocBench v1.6 and the highest overall scores on PureDocBench, Real5-OmniDocBench, and MDPBench. On OCRBench v2, it ranks first, scoring 65.9 and 61.3 on the English and Chinese splits, respectively. It also outperforms the baselines on OCRBench and all four KIE benchmarks. On DocFigCapBench, it achieves the highest localization F1 and a caption score close to that of Qwen3.8-Max. For cross-page tables, it leads in Relation F1 on CrossTabBench and truncated-table TEDS on MPDocBench-Parse. These results demonstrate that a compact 0.8B model can achieve strong performance across diverse OCR-related tasks. OvisOCR3 is available at https://huggingface.co/ATH-MaaS/OvisOCR3.

</details>


### [19] [Decentralized Multi-View, Multi-Zone Tracking for Scalable Camera Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33069)
*David Voihanski,Mor Sinai,Ben Zion Bobrovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向可扩展相机网络的去中心化多视角跟踪系统：单视角检测投影到BEV坐标后由各自管理动态空间分区的独立多视角跟踪器融合，并引入去中心化轨迹统一层（锁定协议＋周期性global collate合并），配合延迟输出缓冲回溯修正历史，实现跨分区全局ID一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单实例集中式多视角跟踪难以扩展到空间上分区的大规模相机网络；需在保持全局身份（ID）一致性的同时实现去中心化、可扩展的跟踪。

Method: 1) 单视角检测投影至鸟瞰图（BEV）坐标；2) 由独立多视角跟踪器融合，每个跟踪器负责一个动态空间分区（动态区域多边形）；3) 去中心化轨迹统一层，采用锁定协议与周期性轨迹合并（global collate）；4) 延迟输出缓冲机制，回溯施加历史修正，延迟窗口吸收异步协调时延；5) 基于共识的协调机制与验证框架。

Result: 在所评估配置下，系统维持全局ID一致性，相较单实例基线仅有极小精度损失。

Conclusion: 去中心化分区式多视角跟踪结合轨迹统一层与延迟缓冲，可在保证可扩展性的同时以接近集中式基线的精度实现跨分区全局ID一致。

Abstract: We present a decentralized multi-view tracking system for scalable camera networks. Single-view detections are projected to bird's-eye-view (BEV) coordinates and fused by independent multi-view trackers, each managing a dynamic spatial zone. To support scalability across spatially partitioned camera networks, we introduce a decentralized layer for trajectory unification, utilizing a locking protocol and periodic track-merging operations termed global collate. This process is supported by a delayed-output buffering mechanism that retroactively applies history corrections. The configurable delay window absorbs asynchronous coordination latency and helps stabilize cross-zone identity consistency. We detail the design of dynamic zone polygons, consensus-based coordination, and the validation framework, which together maintain global ID consistency with minimal accuracy loss compared to a single-instance baseline in the evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [20] [PhaseBreak: Proactive Temporal Protection against Unauthorized Image-to-Video Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33070)
*Jiashu Zhang,Xuanyou Li,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhaseBreak，通过光谱"重定时"（temporal rearrangement）而非单纯衰减时间特征来主动保护图像，阻止其被I2V扩散模型用于生成稳定运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I2V扩散模型可将单张人像/照片动画化，导致未经授权的图像被复用于运动合成。现有频谱扰动方法全局衰减时间表示，对细微或空间局部化运动的控制能力有限。

Method: 核心思路：生成动态不仅取决于时间特征强度，还取决于特征事件发生的顺序。PhaseBreak为白盒光谱重定时保护方法，沿真实时间轴将中间去噪特征匹配到其干净对应特征的受约束时间重排；并使用主体感知掩码将扰动限制在被描绘主体上。

Result: 在Stable Video Diffusion与DynamiCrafter上评估显示，受保护图像产生更大的短期运动变化与更低的时间规律性，阻碍从发布图像中进行稳定的运动定制。

Conclusion: 以时间重排替代全局衰减的相位/频谱破坏策略，可在保留分享流程的有界扰动下更精准地破坏I2V运动生成，实现主体局部化的主动视觉保护。

Abstract: Image-to-Video (I2V) diffusion models can animate a single portrait or photograph, enabling shared images to be repurposed for motion synthesis without the subject's authorization. We study proactive visual protection against such use: before an image is released, its owner applies a bounded perturbation that preserves the sharing workflow but disrupts downstream motion generation. Existing spectral disruption objectives globally attenuate temporal representations and therefore provide limited control over subtle or spatially localized motion. Our key idea is that generated dynamics depend not only on temporal feature strength, but also on the order in which feature events occur. We propose PhaseBreak, a white-box spectral retiming protection method that matches intermediate denoiser features to a constrained temporal rearrangement of their clean counterparts. PhaseBreak operates along the true temporal axis and uses a subject-aware mask to confine the protective perturbation to the depicted subject. Evaluations on Stable Video Diffusion and DynamiCrafter show that protected images induce greater short-term motion variation and lower temporal regularity, impeding stable motion customization from the released image.

</details>


### [21] [DuoPIC: Uncertainty-Aware Voxel-Level Digital Twins of Cerebral Perfusion with Transport PDEs in Acute Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33071)
*Anh Duong Tran,Quang-Anh N. D.,Toan Sy Pham,Trang H. Hoang,Minh N. Do,Duy Ton Mai,Huan X. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DuoPIC——无需参考图的脑灌注数字孪生，用Windkessel血流模型驱动示踪剂输运PDE，配合群体级神经闭合（快/慢通路，守恒示踪剂质量与MTT）和tempered后验不确定性量化。在ISLES'17 MR（AUC 0.745）与ISLES'24 CT（0.743）病变检测上取得SOTA，神经PDE使重建误差降19%、95% MTT区间覆盖率由88%升至94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑灌注数字孪生需将示踪剂动力学与CBF、CBV、MTT关联，但必须满足数据驱动、物理一致与不确定性感知；现有方法通常依赖参考图（reference maps），缺乏构造性守恒与可靠的不确定性刻画。

Method: DuoPIC：以Windkessel血流模型驱动示踪剂输运偏微分方程（PDE），无需参考图；引入群体级神经闭合将每个区域分为快、慢两条通路，并在构造上守恒示踪剂质量与MTT；采用tempered后验对每个估计量进行不确定性量化。

Result: 体素级图谱在ISLES'17 MR上病变检测AUC 0.745、ISLES'24 CT上0.743，达到SOTA；在留出患者上，神经PDE使该分室的再重建误差降低19%，并将95% MTT区间覆盖率从88%提升至94%。

Conclusion: DuoPIC表明物理约束（Windkessel+PDE）、构造性守恒的神经闭合与后验不确定性量化可协同实现无参考图、物理一致且可量化的脑灌注数字孪生，兼顾灌注参数估计精度、下游病变检测性能与区间校准。

Abstract: Digital twins of cerebral perfusion relate tracer dynamics to cerebral blood flow (CBF), blood volume (CBV) and mean transit time (MTT), but must be data-driven, physically consistent and uncertainty-aware. We introduce DuoPIC, a digital twin that needs no reference maps, in which a Windkessel flow model drives a tracer transport partial differential equation (PDE). A population-level neural closure divides each region into fast and slow pathways with tracer mass and MTT conserved by construction, and a tempered posterior quantifies the uncertainty of every estimate. Its voxel maps set the state of the art for lesion detection on ISLES'17 MR (AUC 0.745) and ISLES'24 CT (0.743). On held-out patients, the neural PDE reduced the compartment's reconstruction error by 19% and lifted 95% MTT interval coverage from 88% to 94%. Code: https://github.com/detinatran/duopic

</details>


### [22] [VLMProbe: An Open-source Library for VLM Probing and Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33072)
*Gexin Huang,Haisheng Fu,Yanting Yang,Chen Zhao,Ruinan Jin,Jiaqi Liu,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开源库 vlmprobe，统一集成 lens 读出、注意力归因、因果干预与 SAE 分析，用于系统比较视觉-语言模型的可解释性方法，并揭示不同证据间的一致性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 VLM 如何利用视觉信息，需要把内部表征与模型行为联系起来；但现有可解释性方法分散、缺乏统一接口与可比性，难以跨架构系统比较。

Method: 构建 vlmprobe：统一模型接口内整合 lens 读出、注意力归因、因果干预与稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析；提供共享 adapter、校准与 checkpoint 工具、评估流水线与可视化。据此在视觉-语言、语言、视觉模型上做匹配的案例研究与预训练稀疏字典的受控评估。

Result: 表征读出与空间解释依赖分析目标与观测位点；局部归因估计可能与实测干预效应不一致；SAE 评估显示重建保真度、概念读出与空间定位之间存在明显权衡。

Conclusion: 单一解释方法不足，应组合多类证据来评估解释；vlmprobe 为方法选择、结论比较与行为假设检验提供可复用基础（代码已开源）。

Abstract: Understanding how vision-language models use visual information requires connecting internal representations to model behavior. We present vlmprobe, an open-source library that integrates lens readouts, attention attribution, causal interventions, and sparse autoencoder (SAE) analysis within a common model interface. Shared adapters, calibration and checkpoint utilities, evaluation pipelines, and visualizations support systematic analysis across architectures. Using this framework, we compare methods across vision-language, language, and vision models through matched case studies and controlled evaluations of pretrained sparse dictionaries. The comparisons show that representation readouts and spatial explanations depend on the analysis objective and observation site, and that local attribution estimates can disagree with measured intervention effects. SAE evaluations further expose distinct trade-offs in reconstruction fidelity, concept readout, and spatial localization. Together, these results support combining multiple forms of evidence to evaluate an interpretation. vlmprobe provides a reusable basis for selecting methods, comparing their conclusions, and testing hypotheses about model behavior. The code is available on github.com/ubc-tea/vlm-probe

</details>


### [23] [AGQ: Action-Guided Ultra-Low-Bit Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33074)
*Xuhong Qiu,Zhuoyu Wu,Qiawei Zheng,Wenhui Ou,Wenqi Fang,Raphaël C. -W. Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动作引导量化（AGQ），在存储预算下按动作敏感性与权重复原代价测度分配各矩阵位宽，结合逐通道校准、逐矩阵统一位宽、按位分组无损编码与分块恢复，在LIBERO上实现W3下92.7%成功率，W1.5*配置配合无损编码达6.10×压缩并保留25.5%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型权重规模大，难以部署在显存受限的机载平台；超低位量化虽可压缩权重，但保持任务性能与控制权重复原开销仍具挑战。

Method: AGQ以动作敏感性与实测的权重复原代价代理指标为依据，在存储预算约束下为各矩阵分配位宽；逐通道校准补偿量化误差，矩阵内保持统一位宽以维持规整布局；采用按位依赖的分组无损编码降低常驻权重存储，并用分块恢复限制临时缓冲区。

Result: LIBERO上W3达92.7%任务成功率，分别超AWQ与GPTQ 1.2与3.7个百分点；W2.5仍保留70.5%成功率。非对称配置W1.5*（视觉权重W4、语言权重参数加权平均1.5 bit）结合无损编码实现相对BF16的6.10×整模型压缩，并保留25.5%成功率。

Conclusion: 将动作敏感性与权重复原代价纳入位宽分配，可在存储预算下兼顾超低位量化的任务性能与部署开销；配合无损编码与分块恢复，可实现显著压缩，非对称极低位配置仍保留一定可用性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable robots to perform tasks specified through natural-language instructions, but their large weight footprint challenges deployment on memory-constrained onboard platforms. Ultra-low-bit quantization reduces this footprint, yet preserving task performance and controlling weight recovery costs remain challenging. We propose Action-Guided Quantization, which combines action sensitivity with a measured proxy for weight recovery cost to allocate matrix bitwidths under storage budgets. Per-channel calibration compensates for quantization error, while a uniform bitwidth within each matrix preserves regular layouts. Bit-dependent grouped lossless coding reduces resident weight storage, and block-wise recovery limits temporary buffers. On LIBERO, AGQ achieves 92.7% task success at W3, exceeding AWQ and GPTQ by 1.2 and 3.7 percentage points, respectively, and retains 70.5% success at W2.5. An asymmetric configuration, denoted W1.5$^{*}$, uses W4 vision weights and a parameter-weighted average of 1.5 bits for language weights. With lossless coding, it achieves $6.10\times$ complete-model compression relative to BF16 while retaining 25.5% task success. Code and experimental results are available at https://github.com/Qiuxu00/AGQ

</details>


### [24] [Diagnosing and Mitigating Evidence Misgrounding in Multimodal Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33075)
*Zhiwu Wang,Yichen Wu,Jinqian Yang,Yizhou Yang,Renzhen Wang,Qian Zhao,Deyu Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaterBird-Edit分析集揭示多模态知识编辑中的“证据误锚定”问题，并提出检索式框架VICE，以视觉上下文示范显式暴露任务相关证据，从而在保持终身编辑性能的同时显著降低对受控背景变化的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态知识编辑仅以答案级指标评估，会掩盖证据误锚定：编辑虽在原图上输出目标答案，却依赖偶然的上下文线索而非目标视觉证据，答案正确不等于知识被正确绑定到视觉证据上。

Method: 构建WaterBird-Edit分析集，将鸟类栖息地标签与背景上下文解耦，在保留目标证据、只改变背景的条件下检验编辑后预测的稳定性；提出VICE（Visual In-Context Editing）框架，将编辑存入外部记忆，通过检索并以视觉上下文示范方式施加编辑。

Result: 在三个标准基准上取得强终身编辑性能；在WaterBird-Edit上显著降低对受控背景变化的敏感性。

Conclusion: 答案级指标不足以评估多模态知识编辑，需引入证据级（背景扰动）诊断；基于检索与视觉上下文示范的VICE在维持终身编辑效果的同时提升了编辑对目标视觉证据的依赖。

Abstract: Multimodal knowledge editing aims to update visually grounded facts in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) while preserving unrelated text and visual behaviors. However, standard answer-level metrics can hide evidence misgrounding: an edit may produce the target answer on the original image yet remain sensitive to incidental context rather than the intended visual evidence. To examine this issue in a controlled setting, we construct WaterBird-Edit, an analysis set that decouples bird habitat labels from background context and measures whether edited predictions remain stable when the target evidence is preserved but the background changes. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose VICE (Visual In-Context Editing), a retrieval-based framework that stores edits in external memory and applies them through visual in-context demonstrations designed to expose task-relevant evidence. Experiments on three standard benchmarks show that VICE achieves strong lifelong editing performance, while results on WaterBird-Edit show that it substantially reduces sensitivity to the controlled background shifts. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/Wang-Z-W/VICE-main.

</details>


### [25] [Evaluating Visual Language Models on Visual Grounding for Visually Impaired People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33078)
*Fairooz Azim,Lydia Achour,Claudia Borg,Lina Rojas-Barahona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究面向视障人士（VIP）的视觉问答中开源视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉定位（VG）能力，在 VizWiz-VQA-Grounding 数据集上评估并提出可解释框架 VG-SHAP，发现模型虽能生成正确答案却难以定位到正确图像区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位是VLM多模态理解能力的体现，在辅助视障人士的场景中，它不仅是技术特性，更是信任与安全需求，因此有必要系统评估VLM在此类真实场景下的定位表现。

Method: 采用含视障人士拍摄的真实图像与VizWiz-VQA-Grounding数据集及挑战赛，对开源VLM进行视觉定位评估；提出VG-SHAP，将已有可解释性框架扩展为可给出视觉定位解释的方法，从而支持对黑盒VLM的评估。

Result: 在VizWiz-VQA-Grounding挑战赛上的结果显示，VLM尽管能生成正确答案，却难以在图像中定位到正确区域；这些结论受限于可解释框架中所用分割模型的质量。

Conclusion: VLM在面向视障人士的辅助场景中存在"答对但定位不准"的显著缺陷，视觉定位能力仍需改进；未来需进一步提升分割模型质量并开展更多研究以验证结论。

Abstract: Visual grounding (VG) in visual question answering (VQA) is a fundamental capability of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), enabling the detection of the most relevant region in an image to answer the question. This reflects the model's capability of multimodal comprehension, which is crucial when interacting with visually impaired people (VIP). Indeed, visual grounding, when applied to assistive scenarios, is not merely a technical feature, but rather a trust and safety requirement. In this work, we study visual grounding in VLMs for visually impaired people. To this end, we assess open-source VLMs on visual grounding using the VizWiz-VQA-Grounding dataset and challenge, which contains real-life images taken by VIP. We propose VG-SHAP, which extends an existing explainability framework to provide visual grounding explanations, allowing the evaluation of black-box VLMs. Our results on the VizWiz-VQA-Grounding challenge show that despite generating correct answers, VLMs struggle to ground in the proper region in the image. However, these results are constrained by the quality of the segmentation model of our explainability framework and further research is necessary.

</details>


### [26] [Rethinking Video Storage for AI Training: Breaking Cross-Frame Dependencies for Efficient Random Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33079)
*Zetian Song,Qize Yu,Yue Chen,Bohan Zhou,Bowen Ping,Yuqi Ye,Haocheng Tang,Haotian Zhang,Qizhe Wang,Shiyu Huang,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma,Wen Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对大规模AI训练中视频数据随机时序采样导致的解码效率瓶颈，提出"依赖感知稀疏解码"与可选的"依赖解耦随机访问（DDRA）编码"协同设计，将每个目标帧的重建限制在至多3帧内，从而在保持重建质量的前提下显著提升加载吞吐并降低存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI训练依赖海量视频数据集并使用随机时序采样，而现有视频存储范式为顺序播放优化、采用复杂的帧间参考结构以最大化压缩效率。将其用于随机采样工作负载时会出现严重解码效率瓶颈：独立JPEG帧和短GOP等变通方案虽减少解码量，却带来更大的存储和传输体积。因此需在保留帧间预测收益的同时消除随机访问的解码放大。

Method: 论文指出解码放大有两个来源：对不相关帧的顺序重建，以及为采样目标重建其参考链。方案通过联合设计解码调度与参考结构来同时解决两者：(1) 依赖感知稀疏解码——仅重建被请求帧及其所需参考帧，跳过无关重建，且不转码源视频；(2) 可选的离线DDRA编码——将每个内部帧限制为两个界定性的帧内编码锚点，进一步减少参考重建。二者结合后，每个请求目标的重建上界为3帧，且与帧内周期无关。

Result: 在大型机器人语料库上的实验表明：在可比重建质量下，该方法相较LeRobot v3实现1.45×的加载吞吐，同时媒体负载减少58.7%。

Conclusion: 通过协同设计解码调度与视频参考结构，可在不牺牲重建质量的前提下大幅缓解随机采样工作负载的解码放大问题，兼顾压缩效率与随机访问性能，为AI训练数据管道中的视频存储提供了更优范式。

Abstract: Large-scale AI training increasingly relies on massive video datasets with random temporal sampling patterns. However, existing video storage paradigms are optimized for sequential playback and employ sophisticated inter-frame reference structures to maximize compression efficiency. When applied to the random-sampling workloads of AI training, these paradigms face substantial decoding-efficiency bottlenecks. Workarounds such as independent JPEG frames and short groups of pictures (GOPs) reduce decoding work at the cost of larger storage and transfer volumes. In this paper, we identify two sources of decode amplification: sequential reconstruction of unrelated frames and reconstruction of the reference chains required by sampled targets. We address both sources by co-designing the decoding schedule and video reference structure. Dependency-aware sparse decoding reconstructs only requested frames and their required references, skipping unrelated reconstruction without transcoding the source videos. Optional offline Dependency-Decoupled Random Access (DDRA) encoding further reduces reference reconstruction by restricting each interior frame to two bounding intra-coded anchors. Together, sparse decoding and DDRA retain inter-frame prediction while bounding reconstruction to at most three frames per requested target, independent of the intra period. Experiments on a large-scale robotics corpus show that, at comparable reconstruction quality, our method achieves $1.45\times$ the loading throughput of LeRobot v3 with 58.7% less media payload.

</details>


### [27] [SWIFT-Cell: Segmentation Without Iterative Flow Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33081)
*Devansh Lalwani,Swapnil Bhat,Muralikrishna H H,Anand Reddy,Maulik Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将通用分割器Cellpose-SAM（CPSAM）蒸馏为0.63M参数的U-Net模型SWIFT-Cell，在保持接近CPSAM精度的同时实现秒级甚至亚秒级推理速度，速度超过传统分水岭检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPSAM精度高但速度慢（8192×8192区域需58-303秒），无法满足交互式秒级检索需求；QuPath分水岭检测器虽快但精度不足。需要在速度与精度间取得平衡，服务于时间关键的交互式细胞检索场景。

Method: 以CPSAM生成的掩码为唯一监督信号，仅在1,202张H&E和IHC图块上训练一个0.63M参数的U-Net进行知识蒸馏；在三个8192×8192开发区域和八个H&E/IHC/ISH数据集上评测，涵盖PQ、召回率、F1及病理学家盲法接受率等多维度指标。

Result: CPU（4核，int8）推理2.0-5.2秒，比QuPath检测器快2.5-5.1倍，GPU上0.36-0.49秒；PanNuke上保留95%的CPSAM panoptic quality（0.593 vs 0.627），NuCLS上持平（0.546 vs 0.540）；Ki-67上比QuPath优0.205 PQ，比CPSAM低0.079；病理学家盲法接受率96%（CPSAM 83%，QuPath 53%）；ER/PR检测F1为0.882，PD-L1为0.83。

Conclusion: SWIFT-Cell在精度上接近CPSAM，速度显著快于分水岭检测器，可满足交互式秒级检索需求，是一个兼顾精度与效率的实用细胞分割方案。

Abstract: Generalist segmenters such as Cellpose-SAM (CPSAM) outline cells accurately across stains but slowly; watershed detectors such as QuPath's are fast but less accurate. In time-critical use, such as interactive retrieval of cells from a user-drawn region, CPSAM at the reference settings takes 58-303 s per measured 8192 x 8192 region on an RTX 4500 Ada GPU, beyond a seconds-scale interactive target. We distil CPSAM into SWIFT-Cell (SWIFT), a 0.63M-parameter U-Net trained only on CPSAM masks of 1,202 H&E and IHC tiles. On three 8192 x 8192 development regions SWIFT takes 2.0-5.2 s on 4 CPU cores in int8, 2.5-5.1x faster than QuPath's detector on the same cores, and 0.36-0.49 s on a GPU. Against human annotations on eight H&E, IHC and ISH sets it keeps 95% of CPSAM's panoptic quality (PQ) on PanNuke (0.593 vs. 0.627) and is level on NuCLS (0.546 vs. 0.540). On Ki-67 IHC masks scored to the outline centre it is 0.205 PQ above QuPath's better mode, 0.079 below CPSAM and 0.027 below InstanSeg (CI includes 0; at the outer edge 0.008 below CPSAM); on 212 pathologist-outlined nuclei in Ki-67 fields of 12 untouched patients its recall is 0.925 (CPSAM 0.920, InstanSeg 0.901; paired difference CIs include 0); one method-blinded pathologist accepted its outlines in 96% of 100 fields (60 from two development tissue patients, 40 hematology; CPSAM 83%, QuPath 53%; tissue 100% for SWIFT and CPSAM); on ER/PR points from 12 patients its detection F1 is 0.882 (CPSAM 0.896, InstanSeg 0.857), and on 4-reader PD-L1 points 0.83 (CPSAM 0.85, readers 0.87). SWIFT is close to CPSAM in accuracy and faster than watershed detection.

</details>


### [28] [Ampe-DB: A Dataset for Human Activity and Interaction Analysis Using the Ghanaian Game Ampe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33130)
*Ruth Senior Biney,Isaac Kojo Yedonu Aboah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于加纳传统Ampe游戏的多模态数据集Ampe-DB（15人、637片段，含RGB、双人姿态、元数据与运动标签），并在固定、配对留出、被试留出三种协议下评测四类运动识别与二值运动关系分类，发现固定基准高分掩盖了跨人与跨配对泛化失败，姿态模态更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作识别基准缺乏文化情境下的运动与交互形式，对跨个体、跨配对的身份相关泛化评估不足。

Method: 构建Ampe-DB数据集；采用三种评测协议：去重安全的固定基准、四折配对留出、对被试做被试留出诊断；基线包括冻结的Kinetics-400预训练R(2+1)D-18 RGB编码器、双层双向GRU姿态序列模型，以及经验证集选择的RGB-姿态后期融合。

Result: 四类识别RGB宏F1从固定基准0.955降至配对留出0.418、被试留出0.384；姿态分别为0.957/0.811/0.839；融合为0.976与配对留出0.824，但在被试偏移下未稳定优于姿态；二值运动关系识别在留出评测下明显变难，异腿配置尤甚。

Conclusion: 固定基准的高分可能掩盖跨人与跨配对的泛化失败；Ampe-DB可支撑细粒度运动识别、人际交互、多模态学习与身份感知泛化研究。

Abstract: Human activity recognition benchmarks underrepresent culturally situated forms of movement and interaction. We introduce Ampe-DB, a multimodal dataset based on Ampe, a traditional Ghanaian game involving coordinated jumping, leg movements, and two-person interaction. It contains 637 canonical clips from 15 participants, with RGB video, two-person pose representations, participant and pair metadata, four-class movement labels, and derived binary movement-relation labels.
  We evaluate four-class movement recognition and binary movement-relation classification using three protocols: a duplicate-safe fixed benchmark, four-fold pair-held-out evaluation, and participant-held-out diagnostics for participants with complete class coverage. Baselines comprise a frozen Kinetics-400-pretrained R(2+1)D-18 RGB encoder, a two-layer bidirectional GRU for pose sequences, and validation-selected RGB-pose late fusion.
  For four-class recognition, RGB macro-F1 falls from 0.955 on the fixed benchmark to 0.418 under pair-held-out evaluation and 0.384 under participant-held-out evaluation. Pose is more robust, achieving 0.957, 0.811, and 0.839 across the same protocols. Fusion reaches 0.976 on the fixed benchmark and 0.824 under pair-held-out evaluation, but does not consistently improve on pose under participant shift. Binary movement-relation recognition also becomes substantially harder under held-out evaluation, especially for different-leg configurations.
  These findings show that strong fixed-benchmark scores can mask failures to generalize across people and pairings. Ampe-DB supports research on fine-grained movement recognition, human-human interaction, multimodal learning, and identity-aware generalization.

</details>


### [29] [WorldCast: World-Consistent Character Video Generation with Large-Angle Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33135)
*Lei Cao,Yu Liu,Wenhua Liao,Peng Zhang,Fei Wang,Hao Zhang,Jiajun Le,Daiguo Zhou,Jian Luan,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对大角度相机轨迹下角色视频生成难题，提出CLACT数据集/基准与WorldCast框架，通过点云反投影几何引导和几何对齐记忆注意力（GAMA）注入多视角角色信息，实现精确轨迹跟随与角色几何、外观、身份保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在任意初始方位下跟随包含旋转、平移与半径变化的大角度轨迹；且大视角变化会暴露初始帧未见区域，导致几何畸变、外观退化与身份漂移。作者希望系统研究这些耦合挑战。

Method: 构建CLACT大规模角色中心数据集与基准（多样大角度相机轨迹、精确相机几何、多视角角色观测）；提出WorldCast框架：用估计深度将初始帧反投影为3D点云，对齐点云尺度与目标轨迹并沿线渲染以获得显式几何引导与覆盖掩码；引入GAMA，利用初始帧与多视角角色锚点联合估计的相对位姿，将锚点与目标视角几何统一到公共坐标系，指导视图相关角色特征的检索与注入。

Result: 在多样大角度相机轨迹上的评测显示，方法能准确跟随轨迹，同时保持角色几何、外观与身份。

Conclusion: 显式几何引导结合几何对齐的多视角记忆注入，可有效解决大角度相机运动下角色视频生成中的覆盖缺失与一致性退化问题。

Abstract: Precise camera control is essential for cinematic character video generation, yet existing methods struggle to follow large-angle trajectories with combined rotation, translation, and radius variation from arbitrary initial azimuths. Such viewpoint changes also reveal character regions unseen in the initial frame, causing geometric distortions, appearance degradation, and identity drift. To systematically study these coupled challenges, we introduce CLACT, a large-scale Character-centric dataset and benchmark covering diverse Large-Angle Camera Trajectories, with accurate camera geometry and multi-view character observations for training and evaluation. We further propose WorldCast, a framework for world-consistent character video generation under diverse large-angle camera motions. Specifically, we back-project the initial frame into a 3D point cloud using estimated depth, align the point-cloud scale with the target camera trajectory, and render it along the trajectory to obtain explicit geometric guidance and coverage masks. To complement the incomplete character information, we introduce Geometry-Aligned Memory Attention (GAMA). Using relative camera poses estimated jointly from the initial frame and multi-view character anchors, we express anchor and target-view geometry in a common coordinate frame. GAMA uses this geometric alignment to guide the retrieval and injection of view-relevant character features during generation. Evaluations across diverse large-angle camera trajectories demonstrate accurate trajectory following while preserving character geometry, appearance, and identity.

</details>


### [30] [Learning Pathology-Sensitive Representations for Unsupervised Retinal OCT Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33143)
*Tania Haghighi,Sina Gholami,Behafarin Emam,Jennifer I Lim,Theodore Leng,Atalie C Thompson,Hamed Tabkhi,Minhaj Nur Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督视网膜OCT异常检测与定位框架，通过解剖感知重建与基于合成结构扰动的对比三元组学习，将预训练视觉表征适配到正常视网膜解剖结构，无需疾病标注；系统分析预训练策略、表征适配与异常打分机制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCT深度学习依赖预定义疾病类别，难以发现罕见或未见异常；视觉基础模型虽具可迁移表征，但在识别偏离正常视网膜解剖的病理变化方面效果尚不明确。

Method: 无监督框架：以解剖感知重建和对比三元组学习（由合成结构扰动引导）将预训练视觉表征适配至正常解剖；无需疾病特异标注；系统性消融预训练策略、解剖感知表征适配、异常打分机制（像素空间重建误差与特征空间偏差）。

Result: 将预训练表征适配到正常视网膜结构可提升异常敏感性；像素级重建误差与特征空间偏差对病理变化识别提供互补信号。

Conclusion: 解剖感知表征学习对超越预定义疾病类别的可泛化视网膜异常检测框架具有重要意义。

Abstract: Deep learning approaches for retinal optical coherence tomography (OCT) predominantly rely on predefined disease categories, limiting their ability to detect rare or previously unseen abnormalities. While visual foundation models provide transferable representations, their effectiveness in identifying pathological deviations from normal retinal anatomy remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce an unsupervised framework for retinal OCT anomaly detection and localization that adapts pretrained visual representations to normal retinal anatomy through anatomy-aware reconstruction and contrastive triplet learning guided by synthetic structural perturbations, without requiring disease-specific annotations. We systematically investigate the influence of pretraining strategies, anatomy-aware representation adaptation, and anomaly scoring mechanisms on the detection and localization of unseen retinal abnormalities. Our findings demonstrate that adapting pretrained representations to normal retinal structure improves anomaly sensitivity, while pixel-space reconstruction errors and feature-space deviations provide complementary signals for identifying pathological changes. These findings underscore the importance of anatomy-aware representation learning and offer insights into developing generalizable retinal anomaly detection frameworks beyond predefined disease categories.

</details>


### [31] [Gaussian Episodic Memory for Long-Term Spatiotemporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33158)
*Ling-Chia Chen,Abdelrhman Werby,Kaan Aytekin,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEMS将可持久更新的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）地图与智能体推理流程结合，并发布面向半静态环境的时空问答基准GEMS-Bench，在3D物体定位与时空问答上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境持续发生半静态变化，机器人需要同时刻画空间与时间演化的记忆表征。现有方案要么依赖结构化表示（局限于构建时捕获的实体与属性），要么依赖图像记忆（受限于已观测视角），难以兼顾细节与演化。

Method: 1) 持久化时空3DGS地图：一次性重建，后续仅在场景变化区域更新，仅存储修改部分并跨时间保留视觉细节；2) 智能体推理流程：基于该地图按需渲染新视角，迭代累积证据以回答时空问题并依据自然语言查询进行物体定位；3) 构建GEMS-Bench基准，含3个真实环境与12个合成环境、共473对问答。

Result: 在场景更新后的新视角合成、3D物体定位、时空问答与存储效率四方面评估：GEMS在3D物体定位与时空问答上优于近期基线，渲染质量与专用场景更新方法相当。

Conclusion: GEMS通过“增量更新的3DGS地图+智能体式证据累积推理”的统一框架，为半静态环境下的机器人时空记忆提供了兼顾视觉细节、存储效率与推理能力的可行方案，且配套基准可推动后续研究。

Abstract: Real-world environments undergo continual semi-static changes, requiring a memory representation that captures both the spatial and temporal evolution of a scene. Existing spatio-temporal memories for robots typically rely either on structured representations, which are limited to the entities and attributes captured during construction, or on image-based memories, which are constrained to previously observed viewpoints. We introduce GEMS, a framework that combines a persistent spatio-temporal 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) map with an agentic reasoning pipeline. The 3DGS map is reconstructed once and subsequently updated only in regions where the scene changes, storing only these modifications while preserving visual detail across time. The agentic pipeline leverages this map to render novel views on demand, iteratively accumulating evidence to answer spatio-temporal questions and ground objects from natural-language queries. To advance future research, we introduce GEMS-Bench, a spatiotemporal question-answering benchmark focused on semi-static environments. It comprises 3 real-world and 12 synthetic environments, spanning 473 question-answer pairs. We evaluate GEMS on novel-view synthesis quality across scene updates, 3D object grounding, spatiotemporal question answering, and storage efficiency. GEMS outperforms recent baselines in 3D object grounding and spatiotemporal question answering, while achieving rendering quality on par with dedicated scene-update methods. Code and data: https://abwerby.github.io/GEMS/

</details>


### [32] [World-in-Context: Interactive Video World Modeling with In-Context Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33169)
*Harold Haodong Chen,Rui Peng,Rongjin Guo,Lingteng Qiu,Kejie Qiu,Chao Xu,Zilong Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World-in-Context 框架，用 In-Context Conditioning (ICC) 将视角、动作、相机三类控制统一为显式 token 置于共享自注意力空间，并引入 Interaction-Transport RoPE 保持跨执行区间时序连续性；5B 模型导航性能追平 22B 模型，视角切换成功率达 93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型存在三点缺陷：视角模式隐含在文本描述中、动作与相机运动相互混淆、控制信号经由碎片化的架构通路注入导致只能间接建模相互依赖关系。作者希望以统一、显式的条件化方式协调视角、动作与相机三类控制。

Method: 提出 World-in-Context 框架，核心为 In-Context Conditioning (ICC)：不使用专用条件通路，而将视角、动作、相机控制编码为显式 token，直接在共享自注意力空间中上下文化。分析表明显式视角 token 可解耦冲突动态，并从单视角数据中涌现视角切换能力；动作与相机输入分别主导局部响应性与全局稳定性。另提出 Interaction-Transport RoPE (IT-RoPE)，以累积控制转移参数化时间注意力，维持执行区间之间的时序连续。

Result: 在 WBench 上，World-in-Context-5B 导航性能 $81.5$ 与 22B EchoWM 的 $81.6$ 相当，视角切换成功率 $93.8\%$，显著优于现有 5B 基线（$18.8\%$）。受控分析显示在更丰富的条件组合下具有复合优势、良好的数据效率，并自然适用于因果 rollout。

Conclusion: 将多模态控制显式 token 化并统一于上下文条件化，是一种简洁高效的世界模型控制范式，可在小参数规模下达到大模型级性能，并具备良好的扩展性与数据效率。

Abstract: Interactive video world models require coordinated control over viewing perspectives, character actions, and camera motion. Existing methods typically leave view modes implicit in text captions, conflate action with camera motion, and inject controls through fragmented architectural pathways that capture interdependencies only indirectly. In this work, we present World-in-Context, an interactive world modeling framework powered by In-Context Conditioning (ICC). Rather than relying on specialized conditioning pathways, World-in-Context unifies view, action, and camera controls into explicit tokens contextualized directly within a shared self-attention space. Our formulation reveals that (i) explicit view tokens disentangle conflicting dynamics and unlock emergent view switching from single-view data, while (ii) action and camera inputs play complementary roles in governing local responsiveness and global stability. To preserve temporal continuity across execution intervals, we further introduce Interaction-Transport RoPE (IT-RoPE), parameterizing temporal attention with accumulated control transitions. On WBench, World-in-Context-5B achieves navigation performance on par with 22B EchoWM ($81.5$ vs. $81.6$) and attains a $93.8\%$ perspective-switching success rate, substantially outperforming existing 5B baselines ($18.8\%$). Controlled analyses further demonstrate compounding advantages of World-in-Context under richer conditioning mixtures, favorable data efficiency, and natural applicability to causal rollouts.

</details>


### [33] [Support-guided OOD Removal with Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33194)
*Alireza Kheirandish,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SORD（Support-guided OOD Removal with Diffusion），仅依赖损坏支撑的二值掩码与一个在干净信号上预训练的扩散模型，迭代恢复被局部化分布外（OOD）污染的信号，无需配对数据或损坏的统计模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实信号常被局部化、结构化且分布未知的扰动污染（间歇性背景噪声、水印、投影阴影等），这类污染属于分布外（OOD）损坏，缺乏可用的统计先验，传统方法难以处理。

Method: SORD 是迭代算法，输入为损坏支撑的二值图（binary support map）与一个在干净信号上预训练的单一扩散模型，通过引导采样在被污染区域外保持一致性、在支撑区域内利用扩散先验重建，从而剥离 OOD 内容；不使用配对训练数据，也不对损坏建模。

Result: 在自然图像与人脸（手写数字、其他图像、水印、阴影等空间局部损坏）及不同乐器音频（其他乐器、人声等时间局部损坏）上评测：性能与基于同一扩散先验和同一掩码的后验采样修复相当，且常优于后者；优于需为每个源单独训练扩散模型的源分离基线；在部分基准上超越使用配对干净/损坏数据训练的监督网络。

Conclusion: SORD 表明"支撑掩码 + 单一干净数据扩散先验"足以有效去除局部化 OOD 损坏，无需配对数据与损坏统计模型，具有良好的通用性与竞争力；代码已开源。

Abstract: Many real-world signals are corrupted by localized, structured perturbations whose distribution is unknown: an intermittent background noise in a music recording, a watermark stamped on a photograph, a cast shadow. We study the task of recovering a clean signal $x$ from an observation $y$ containing such a localized out-of-distribution (OOD) corruption. We propose Support-guided OOD Removal with Diffusion, SORD, an iterative algorithm to recover $x$ using only two ingredients: a binary map of the corruption support and a single diffusion model pretrained on clean signals. SORD does not require any paired data or any statistical model of the corruption. We evaluate SORD on natural images and human faces with spatially localized corruptions including handwritten digits, other images, watermarks, and shadows, as well as audio recordings of different musical instruments with temporally localized corruptions including other instruments and human speech. SORD is competitive with, and often outperforms, posterior-sampling inpainting given the same diffusion prior and the same mask, source-separation baselines that require a separate diffusion model for each source, and, on several benchmarks, supervised networks trained on paired clean and corrupted signals. The code is released here: \href{https://github.com/voilalab/SORD}{https://github.com/voilalab/SORD}.

</details>


### [34] [Of Chains and Gains: What Reinforcement Learning changes on Reasoning-based Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33203)
*Yixuan Li,Yuxuan Jiang,Hanwei Zhu,Hancheng Zhu,Fan Zhang,Shiqi Wang,David Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统检验了RLVR在图像质量评估(IQA)中对VLM的增益是否超出采样效应、是否依赖显式推理，发现RLVR确实提升评分并产生更有用的思维链，但评分对链的依赖反而下降，且奖励可能诱发对MOS相关文本捷径的利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作常将RLVR在IQA上的性能提升归因于评分前推理的改善（体现为更可信的CoT），但尚不清楚这些增益是否只是采样效应、以及是否真正依赖显式推理，论文旨在严格厘清这一问题。

Method: 采用三类实验：(1) 采样感知评估（大预算max_g@k，区分平均与超越平均的增益）；(2) 受控推理干预（思维链移植、固定思维链后评分、推理时对思维token中质量方向进行steering）；(3) 捷径实验（检验模型是否利用与MOS相关的文本线索）。在7B与32B规模模型上对比RL训练模型与基座模型。

Result: RL模型在大预算max_g@k下优于基座模型，但超越平均的增益主要集中于分布内；RLVR产生的思维链移植到基座模型后可提升其表现，且在固定思维链时评分得到改善；然而推理过程中评分对思维链的依赖减弱（7B比32B更明显）；对思维token中探测到的质量方向进行steering对评分影响甚微；RL还可通过与MOS相关的文本线索提升评分准确率，可能虚高基准成绩。

Conclusion: RLVR能提升质量评分并生成更有用的思维链，但更好的评分并不意味着对显式推理的更强依赖；基于分数的奖励还可能促使模型依赖与质量无关的捷径。作者希望该初步研究能启发IQA中更具体、更扎实的推理方案设计。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has improved the performance of large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on image quality assessment (IQA). Existing approaches often attribute these gains to improved reasoning before scoring, supported by increasingly plausible Chain-of-Thought (CoT). However, whether these gains exceed sampling effects and how they depend on explicit reasoning remain unclear. We rigorously investigate these questions through sampling-aware evaluation, controlled reasoning interventions, and shortcut experiments. We find that RL-trained models outperform base models under large-budget $\operatorname{max}_g@k$ evaluation, while improvements beyond averaging are concentrated in-distribution. RLVR produces chains that raise base-model performance when transplanted and improves scoring with the chain held fixed.
