Yet scoring becomes less dependent on the chain during inference, more clearly at 7B than at 32B. Steering the probed quality direction in thinking tokens also has little effect on scoring. Finally, RL can improve scoring accuracy by exploiting textual cues correlated with mean opinion scores (MOSs), which could inflate benchmark performance. Together, these findings show that RLVR raises quality scoring and produces more useful chains, but that better scoring does not imply a stronger reliance on explicit reasoning, while score-based rewards can also encourage reliance on quality-irrelevant shortcuts. We hope this preliminary study can enlighten more and more concrete reasoning schemes in IQA.

</details>


### [35] [The Locality Trade-off in Masked Patch Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33223)
*Xi Weng,Zhaoyu Liu,Lianyu Hu,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示掩码图像块自蒸馏（如iBOT）在带来语义收益的同时存在尺度相关的位置性代价：小模型中加入iBOT显著削弱patch对应性但基本保留外观可解码性，大模型中对应性改善但像素/纹理证据在线性层面更难获取；Gram anchoring无法保证两种属性同时提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码patch自蒸馏是DINOv2/DINOv3等视觉基础模型的关键组件，但其对局部视觉信息的影响尚不明确，需要厘清语义抽象与局部细节（外观、空间对应）之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出两种无需标注的诊断指标，将可恢复的局部外观与空间对应性区分开；并在不同规模模型上分析加入iBOT的影响，同时考察Gram anchoring对patch关系保持的作用。

Result: 小模型中iBOT显著削弱patch对应性而基本保留外观可解码性；大模型中对应性改善，但像素与纹理证据在线性探针下更不可及；Gram anchoring虽保持patch关系，却不能保证两类属性同时提升。即规模化改变了位置性代价的形式而非消除它。

Conclusion: 视觉表征的评估与设计应围绕语义抽象、外观保持与空间对应这三类互补需求展开，该工作提供了无需标注的诊断工具与代码。

Abstract: Masked patch self-distillation is a key component of modern vision foundation models, including DINOv2 and DINOv3, yet its implications for local visual information remain unclear. We show that its semantic benefits can coexist with a scale-dependent locality cost. Using two annotation-free diagnostics, we distinguish recoverable local appearance from spatial correspondence. In smaller models, adding iBOT substantially weakens patch correspondence while largely preserving appearance decodability. In larger models, correspondence improves, but pixel and texture evidence becomes less linearly accessible in deep features. Thus, scaling changes the form of the locality cost rather than uniformly eliminating it. Our analysis of Gram anchoring further shows that preserving patch relations does not guarantee simultaneous improvements in both properties. These findings motivate evaluating and designing visual representations around the complementary demands of semantic abstraction, appearance preservation, and spatial correspondence. Our code is available at https://github.com/winci-ai/jea.

</details>


### [36] [Automated identification of ivory carvings using machine learning: A proof-of-concept approach to detecting the illegal wildlife trade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33243)
*Andrius Tamosiunas,Simone Mozzon,David Bacon,Caroline Cox,Alan Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于U-Net(ResNet34骨干)+迁移学习的语义分割方案，从图像中区分象牙与骨骼，作为在线象牙非法交易自动筛查的概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际象牙贸易虽被禁止，但大象仍遭盗猎；象牙制品主要在在线市场交易。由于广告量巨大、品类繁多，且象牙与合法材料外观相似，执法部门难以识别非法交易。现有识别方法（FTIR光谱、显微术）虽可靠，但需接触实物，无法处理照片，因而无法筛查海量在线图像，缺乏基于图像的自动化识别工具。

Method: 采用语义分割框架，U-Net架构配合ResNet34骨干网络，在541张原始图像经增强后的数据集上进行迁移学习；构建含象牙及类似雕刻象牙物品的图像数据集，试验多个模型，并在独立测试集上评估分类性能。

Result: 在独立测试集上，分类器AUC达0.81，在置信样本上总体分类准确率为81%。

Conclusion: 该最佳模型可作为概念验证，用于构建在图像含大象象牙指示性特征时有效识别象牙及其制品的系统，为辅助执法部门检测非法象牙交易提供机器学习框架的初步步骤。

Abstract: Despite a ban on the international trading of recently poached ivory, elephants are still killed for their tusks. Ivory and its products are mainly sold in online marketplaces. However, due to the huge volume and variety of adverts and the similarity of ivory with other materials that can be legally sold, it is hard for enforcement officials to identify illicit trading and prosecute sellers. Existing techniques for identifying elephant ivory, such as Fourier-transform infrared spectroscopy and microscopy, can reliably distinguish ivory from other materials, but require physical access to the item and cannot be applied to photographs alone. As a result, these methods are unsuitable for screening the large volume of images posted in online marketplaces, leaving a gap for automated, image-based identification tools. In this proof of concept work, we investigate the potential of machine learning techniques for image analysis to automatically distinguish ivory objects from bone. We employ a semantic segmentation framework using a U-Net architecture with a ResNet34 backbone, leveraging transfer learning on an augmented dataset of 541 original images. Using a dataset of images of ivory and items resembling carved ivory, several models were trialled. On a distinct test set, the classifier achieved an Area Under the Curve (AUC) of 0.81 and an overall classification accuracy of 81% on confident samples. The paper presents the best-performing model as a proof of concept for developing a system that can effectively identify ivory and its products when indicative features of elephant ivory are present in an image. This work provides initial steps towards a machine learning-based framework designed to advise law-enforcement in detecting illegal elephant ivory trade.

</details>


### [37] [Explaining Basketball Shot Classifications with Grad-CAM: An Investigation of Shortcut Reliance with Spatiotemporal Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33263)
*Hongyi Tian,Robin Cohen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文用Grad-CAM与遮挡式模糊实验检验Basketball-51上训练的R(2+1)D-18模型是否依赖虚假时空相关，发现中央区域（射手、篮网）对预测贡献显著，模型主要依赖任务相关区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 篮球动作识别被用于高风险场景（如专项训练指导），但其深度学习模型所依据的证据缺乏理解；作者关注模型是否依赖虚假的时空相关（spurious spatiotemporal correlations）进行投篮结果分类。

Method: 在Basketball-51数据集上训练R(2+1)D-18；用Grad-CAM生成时空显著性图，并以逐步模糊最显著像素的方式评估其faithfulness；再对20张显著图进行人工视觉审查以识别重复出现的显著区域；并通过中央区域模糊与随机模糊的对照实验量化依赖程度。

Result: Grad-CAM显著集中于片段的空间中央区域，包括射手、篮网，偶尔包括场边观众。中央区域模糊后准确率为43.7%，原始测试准确率为69.3%，随机模糊为50.9%；中央模糊相较随机模糊额外降低7.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 模型主要依赖任务相关的视觉区域而非虚假线索，但7.2个百分点的差异提示仍需持续审计（continual audits）以保障可信与公平的体育分析。

Abstract: Basketball action recognition is used in high-stakes scenarios to inform such tasks as specialized coaching, yet the evidence used by its deep-learning models is poorly understood. This paper investigates whether there is evidence that an R(2+1)D-18 model trained on the Basketball-51 dataset relies on spurious spatiotemporal correlations when classifying basketball shot outcomes. Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) was used to generate spatiotemporal saliency maps, whose faithfulness was evaluated by progressively blurring the most salient pixels. Twenty saliency maps were then visually reviewed for recurring salient regions. Grad-CAM highlighted spatially central regions of clips including the shooter, net, and occasionally the courtside crowd. Blurring interventions were conducted to support these patterns. Blurring the central region resulted in an accuracy of 43.7%, compared to the original test accuracy of 69.3% and random-blurred accuracy of 50.9%. The additional 7.2 percentage-point reduction from central blurring compared to random blurring suggests that the model relies on task-relevant visual regions; this highlights the need for continual audits to ensure trustworthy, equitable sports analytics.

</details>


### [38] [Image Clustering with Vision-Language Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33265)
*João P. Matos-Carvalho,Nuno Fachada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统比较了9条视觉-语言表示流水线在7个图像数据集、4种聚类配置下的252种组合，并单独分析L2归一化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言模型给出的图像表示是否适合聚类，会随特征提取方式、图像域与划分算法而变化，需要系统评测完整流水线而非单一环节。

Method: 固定Ward连接层次聚类，比较9条表示流水线（8条视觉特征提取，含2条纯视觉参照；1条先生成描述再嵌入文本）在7个基准上的表现，共252种基线组合；另对63个数据集-表示对做原始维度下原始嵌入与L2归一化嵌入的敏感性分析。

Result: Qwen3-VL在5个数据集上ARI最高（MNIST达0.925），BLIP-2在CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100上领先（ARI分别为0.925和0.591）。归一化在每个数据集上都保留了最高ARI表示，35对ARI上升、26对下降、2对几乎不变；SVHN最高ARI仍偏低，归一化后由0.097升至0.098。基于描述文本的流水线在孤立数字上ARI较高，但未稳定超过直接视觉特征。

Conclusion: 应针对目标域选择并评估完整的"表示-聚类"流水线，并显式考虑特征提取与归一化方式；不存在普适最优的表示方案。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models provide image representations whose suitability for clustering may vary with feature extraction, image domain, and partitioning algorithm. We compare nine representation pipelines across seven image benchmarks and four clustering configurations, yielding 252 baseline combinations. Eight pipelines extract visual features, including two vision-only references; the ninth generates descriptions and embeds the resulting text. With agglomerative clustering (Ward linkage) fixed, Qwen3-VL achieves the highest adjusted Rand index (ARI) on five datasets, including 0.925 on MNIST, while BLIP-2 leads on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ARIs of 0.925 and 0.591, respectively. A separate sensitivity analysis compares raw and L2-normalized embeddings at their original dimensionality across all 63 dataset-representation pairs. Normalization preserves the highest-ARI representation in every dataset, increases ARI in 35 pairs, decreases it in 26, and leaves two numerically unchanged. On the SVHN dataset, the highest ARI remains low, rising from 0.097 to 0.098 after normalization. The evaluated caption-mediated pipeline obtains relatively high ARI on isolated digits but does not consistently exceed direct visual features. These findings support selecting and evaluating complete representation-clustering pipelines for the intended domain, with explicit attention to extraction and normalization choices.

### [39] [KINESYS: Test-Time Adaptation for Reference-Based Effect Editing in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33292)
*Noah Frahm,Luchao Qi,Haoze Li,Dan B Goldman,Sambit Bhattacharya,Roni Sengupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KINESYS：一种测试时自适应框架，结合参考感知与源感知反馈，实现"效果参考编辑"，在保持原图构图内容的同时迁移参考图中的视觉特效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉特效难以用语言描述，用参考图更易表达，但需语言指明该迁移哪些属性。现有单次编辑方法难以做细粒度编辑，易导致源内容被意外改动或偏离参考效果。

Method: 提出 KINESYS 测试时自适应框架，融合 reference-aware 与 source-aware 两类反馈以迁移目标效果并保留原始镜头构图与内容；同时设计鲁棒优化策略，在多个噪声样本与多次优化运行上微调生成模型，以获得多样且保源的编辑结果。

Conclusion: 基于参考图+语言的测试时自适应编辑可有效兼顾特效迁移与源内容保持，为细粒度效果参考编辑提供可行方案。

Abstract: Visual effects are often easier to demonstrate via reference than to describe in words. We study effect-reference editing, where a reference image shows a desired visual effect and language identifies which properties should guide the edit. Existing single-pass editing techniques often fail to make fine-grained edits, resulting in unintended changes to source content or deviations from the reference effect. We introduce KINESYS, a test-time adaptation framework for effect-reference editing that combines reference-aware and source-aware feedback to transfer the desired effect while preserving the composition and content of the original shot. To ensure KINESYS can produce a diverse set of edits that preserve the source, we develop a robust optimization strategy in which the generative model is fine-tuned across multiple noise samples and optimization runs. Evaluation with VLM judges, source preservation metrics (LPIPS, TPIPS), and a user study shows that KINESYS outperforms state-of-the-art image editors in source preservation; on effect transfer, it remains competitive with Nano Banana 2 and outperforms open-weight editors.

</details>


### [40] [Compression of Vision Foundation Models for Retinal OCT via Growing-Neural-Gas Codebooks and Graph-Guided Masked Image Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33340)
*Ali Yavari,Tilman Schmoll,Rainer A. Leitgeb,Wolfgang Drexler,Thomas Schlegl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出拓扑感知离散化框架，将大型视网膜基础模型蒸馏为紧凑学生网络，用离散码本作为掩码图像建模的重建目标，实现22M参数学生接近甚至超过86M教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜影像的视觉基础模型规模大、计算开销高，难以在床旁部署。核心问题是：一个基础模型的下游效用有多少能在压缩进小网络后保留下来。

Result: 将86M参数教师蒸馏为22M参数学生，FLOPs至少减少3.6倍；在三个分类和两个分割基准上学生接近甚至在部分设置下超过教师，差异幅度与方向依赖于教师、任务、指标与学生容量；外部OLIVES基准上每个指标均超过MIRAGE教师（平衡准确率98.06% vs 95.22%）；对DINOv3域外教师，学生在视网膜层分割上超过教师。

Conclusion: 紧凑视网膜模型具备潜在部署价值，可跨OCT数据集、设备与临床任务迁移，包括未参与学生预训练的三个数据集；视网膜专用预训练带来的增益具有任务依赖性——分割最大，而分类上通用域教师已具竞争力。

Abstract: Vision foundation models for retinal imaging are large and computationally demanding, limiting point-of-care deployment. We ask how much of such a model's downstream utility survives compression into a smaller network. We propose a topology-aware discretization framework that distills a continuous foundation model into a discrete codebook, then uses it as the reconstruction target for masked image modeling (MIM). It combines gradient-free codebook learning via Growing Neural Gas, which yields an adjacency graph over the codes, with a graph-distance soft cross-entropy loss that replaces one-hot MIM targets with topologically graded supervision. With student pretraining on 383 public Duke iAMD optical coherence tomography (OCT) volumes, the pipeline distills an 86M-parameter teacher into a 22M-parameter student with at least 3.6x fewer FLOPs. The student performs close to, and in some settings above, its teacher across three classification and two segmentation benchmarks, with the magnitude and direction of the difference depending on the teacher, task, metric, and student capacity. On the external OLIVES benchmark, it surpasses its MIRAGE teacher on every metric (98.06% vs. 95.22% balanced accuracy). It works with two Vision Transformer (ViT-Base) teachers, retinal (MIRAGE) and natural-image (DINOv3); with the out-of-domain teacher, the student outperforms it on retinal-layer segmentation, indicating that the added value of retina-specific pretraining is task-dependent: largest for segmentation, while a general-domain teacher is competitive for classification. These results yield compact retinal models potentially suitable for deployment, transferring across OCT datasets, devices, and clinical tasks, including three datasets not used in the student's pretraining.

</details>


### [41] [Real Time Motion Detection in Video Inspired by Photonic Time Stretch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33351)
*Callen MacPhee,Yiming Zhou,Bahram Jalali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoViD是一种受光子时间拉伸物理启发的运动估计算法，将视频视为像素级光学时间序列波形，直接在宽带信号上实现高分辨率、实时的运动表征与运动放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动感知方法（背景减除、帧差、光流）在复杂动态场景中表现不佳，且往往以牺牲空间分辨率换取计算可行性。

Method: 基于光子时间拉伸原理，将视频建模为像素关联的光学时间序列波形：物体运动穿过像素会展宽时间序列频谱，经群延迟色散后产生仅在有运动时存在的时域相位；用时域-空域群延迟色散模板（对应介电函数）对特定运动轨迹进行分类，直接在宽带视频信号上运算，无需窄带空间信道化与金字塔重建。

Result: 在运动表征与运动放大上取得前所未有的性能，可在4K分辨率下以近200帧/秒、8K下70帧/秒实时输出全分辨率运动图，比经典光流或学习方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该算法使超高分辨率实时运动分析成为可能，可支撑机器人及自主设备中的运动检测与补偿等应用。

Abstract: Motion perception is fundamental to computer vision and physical AI, and plays a central role in robotics, autonomous vehicles, human-computer interaction, and video compression. Traditional approaches such as background subtraction, frame-differencing and optical flow algorithms often struggle in complex or dynamic scenes, typically sacrificing spatial resolution for computational tractability. Here we introduce MoViD (Motion estimation via Virtual dispersion and phase Detection), a physics-inspired algorithm grounded in the principles that govern photonic time stretch - a highly successful ultrafast realtime data acquisition platform. Drawing inspiration from the optical physics therein, MoViD treats video as a collection of optical time series waveforms associated with pixels. Motion of an object through a pixel broadens the time series spectrum and, after group delay dispersion, induces a temporal phase that exists only if there is motion. Spatiotemporal group delay dispersion templates, corresponding to dielectric functions, classify specific motion trajectories. Unlike conventional empirical phase-based motion-analysis pipelines that require narrow-band spatial channelization and pyramid reconstruction, the physics-based MoViD operates directly on the broadband video signal, vastly increasing its computational speed. The resulting algorithm achieves unprecedented performance in both motion characterization and motion magnification, providing full-resolution motion mapping in real time at nearly 200 frames per second at 4K resolution and 70 frames per second for 8K video - orders of magnitude faster than classical optical flow approaches or learning-based methods. Notably, such extreme performance enables real time motion analysis at ultrahigh resolution for motion detection and compensation in robotics and other autonomous devices.

</details>


### [42] [Detecting Temporal Concepts in Video Transformers via a No-False-Positives Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33353)
*Jianghuai Li,Andrew Ren,Greg Shakhnarovich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出No-False-Positives（NFP）检验，通过具统计特异性保证的统计方法识别视频模型中真正编码时间概念（如物体运动）的特征，并揭示"因果关系式干预"未必优于非时间特征基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型架构上支持编码时间信息，但运动与外观变化相互纠缠：看似编码"运动"的特征，可能只是编码"不同位置的空间存在"等非时间概念，其激活仅与运动相关。现有可解释性方法缺乏对"假阳性时间特征"的严格统计控制。

Method: 提出NFP检验——一种具备统计特异性形式化保证的统计检验，用于判定特征是否编码时间概念；设计专门刺激（控制运动与外观变化）。将其应用于VideoMAE、V-JEPA2、TimeSformer的表示，并以DINOv2（图像编码器）作阴性对照验证；再用合成表示空间考察检测率与"方向在时间子空间投影"的关系，并比较SAE特征在时间子空间上的范数占比；最后对时间SAE特征做干预实验。

Result: 三个视频模型均存在时间特征；DINOv2（纯图像编码器）未检出时间特征，验证检验有效；合成空间中检测率随方向在时间子空间的投影增大而提升，且被判定为时间性的SAE特征其范数在时间子空间的投影占比显著高于未判定特征；对时间SAE特征的干预在跨模型中结果不一。

Conclusion: NFP检验可在严格特异性保证下识别视频模型中的时间特征，避免运动—外观纠缠导致的假阳性；但"干预时间特征即改变时间行为"的因果假设并不稳健，干预时间特征未必优于非时间特征基线，提示需对可解释性特征的时间语义与因果作用保持审慎。

Abstract: Video models have the architecture to support encoding temporal aspects of the input, i.e. concepts that require more than one frame to define, like object movement. However, because motion and appearance change are entangled, a feature that appears to encode temporal concepts like object movement may instead encode non-temporal concepts like spatial presence at different positions whose activations merely correlate with motion. To resolve this, we introduce the No-False-Positives (NFP) test, a statistical test with a formal guarantee of statistical specificity for identifying features encoding temporal concepts. Using specially designed stimuli, we apply this test to representations produced by VideoMAE, V-JEPA2, and TimeSformer, and observe that temporal features exist in each model. We validate the test first by showing it yields no temporal features for the DINOv2 image-encoder. Then, using a synthetic representation space, we show that detection rate increases with a direction's projection onto the temporal subspace, and that Sparse Autoencoder features test-flagged as temporal project much more of their norm onto the temporal subspace than unflagged features. Interventions on temporal SAE features yield mixed results across models, suggesting that intervening on temporal features may not outperform a non-temporal feature baseline.

</details>


### [43] [Tight Learned Event-Inertial Odometry (TLEIO): a Transformer-Based Architecture for End-to-End Motion from Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33356)
*Matteo Cascone,David Jensen,Alessandro Pirini,Angelo Stefanini,Pietro Venè,Marta Tintoré Gazulla,Javier Hidalgo Carriò*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TLEIO：一种紧凑的滤波式事件-惯性里程计，从体素化事件流学习短窗口相对运动并与IMU紧耦合，无需特征跟踪与位姿优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备微秒级时间分辨率、高动态范围和稀疏异步测量，适合快速运动与复杂光照下的里程计；但现有系统多将事件作为帧的补充，或依赖特征跟踪、对应搜索与优化型后端，难以发挥事件感知的效率优势。

Method: 前端EventsFormer单次前向从事件体素预测局部位移及各轴不确定性；后端采用随机克隆EKF，将重叠的短窗口预测与IMU传播紧融合，推理时无需显式特征轨迹与位姿优化；网络直接由位姿位移监督训练，无需事件光流标签。

Result: 在TartanAir上，平均ATE由IMU-only基线TLIO的2.26 m降至1.10 m；相较基于优化的事件里程计方法，运行时间最高降低48.9%；并在真实DAVIS240c序列上完成验证。

Conclusion: 学习式短窗口相对运动约束与滤波后端紧耦合，可在无特征跟踪、无位姿优化的情况下实现高精度且高效的事件-惯性里程计。

Abstract: Event cameras provide microsecond temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and sparse asynchronous measurements, making them compelling for odometry under fast motion and challenging illumination. Yet, accurate event-based state estimation remains difficult: existing systems often use events as a complement to frames or rely on feature tracking, correspondence search, and optimization-heavy backends, limiting the efficiency promised by event sensing. We introduce Tight Learned Event-Inertial Odometry (TLEIO), a compact filter-based pipeline that learns short-window relative motion directly from voxelized event streams and tightly fuses the resulting constraints with IMU propagation. Its learned front-end, EventsFormer, predicts local displacements and per-axis uncertainty from event voxels in a single forward pass, while a stochastic-cloning EKF fuses these overlapping predictions with IMU propagation, producing metric trajectories without explicit feature tracks and pose optimization at inference time. The network is trained directly from pose displacements, without requiring event-flow labels. We evaluate TLEIO on the TartanAir/TartanEvent benchmark and on real DAVIS240c sequences. On TartanAir, TLEIO reduces average ATE from 2.26 m to 1.10 m over the IMU-only TLIO baseline, while achieving up to 48.9% lower runtime than optimization-based event odometry methods.

</details>


### [44] [Fast Instinct, Slow Reason: A Dual-Speed Agentic Framework for Self-Configuring Multi-Object Tracking in Satellite Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33396)
*Fan He,Baining Wu,Yan Li,Xiaoyang Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出双速智能体框架 AUSPEX（算法）与 CORVUS（系统），在卫星视频多目标跟踪中无需目标标签即可在线自适应配置跟踪器，并在边缘设备上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星视频多目标跟踪需持续监测，但离线调优的跟踪器缺乏在数十秒观测内自适应的机载智能与效率。根源在于现有自适应仍需重标注与重训检测器，无法在轨及时完成；而仅调跟踪器配置虽能强控精度，却依赖有标签或代理评估，语言智能体每决策又耗时耗token。

Method: 提出双速智能体框架：快直觉（离线编译）与慢推理（服务任务）。算法 AUSPEX（Arbitration of Unlabelled Shortlists from Prior Experience）从有标签源任务学习覆盖性候选短名单与无标签排序器，每条序列提交一套配置。系统 CORVUS（Cascaded Onboard Runtime with Verified Uplinked Skills）级联反射层、技能层与智能体层，经验证的技能在每次观测第2.5秒完成提交，语言智能体负责自然语言任务。

Result: 在 SatMTB 与 SatVideoDT 上配合三种近期检测器，无目标标签条件下 AUSPEX 平均提升 IDF1 2.84–2.94 点、MOTA 3.21–3.29 点；在16种方法中精度-标签权衡最优；1.25次跟踪遍历下 IDF1 损失不超过0.28点。CORVUS 完成四项任务，执行准确率达0.99，目标召回处于感知上限，常规决策零token消耗，可在 Jetson Xavier NX 运行，并容纳六个族系共15个语言模型而不改变任何提交。

Conclusion: 通过"离线快直觉+在线慢推理"的双速设计与级联机载运行时，实现了无标签、低开销、可扩展的卫星视频多目标跟踪自适应，兼具精度、标签效率、边缘部署可行性与模型可替换性。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking in satellite video underpins persistent monitoring, yet offline-tuned trackers lack the onboard intelligence and efficiency to adapt within an observation of tens of seconds. The root is that adaptation still means relabelling and retraining the detector, which cannot run on board in time. Tuning the tracker configuration strongly controls accuracy without touching the network, yet search needs labelled or proxy evaluations, and a language agent costs seconds and tokens per decision. We propose a dual-speed agentic framework pairing a fast instinct compiled offline with a slow reason serving the mission. Its algorithm, Arbitration of Unlabelled Shortlists from Prior Experience, or AUSPEX, learns a covering shortlist and a label-free ranker from labelled source tasks and commits one configuration per sequence. Its system, a Cascaded Onboard Runtime with Verified Uplinked Skills, or CORVUS, cascades reflex, skill, and agent layers, so that a verified skill commits 2.5 s into each observation while a language agent serves natural- language missions. On SatMTB and SatVideoDT with three recent detectors, AUSPEX raises IDF1 by 2.84 to 2.94 points and MOTA by 3.21 to 3.29 points on average without target labels, balances accuracy and labels best among sixteen methods, and loses at most 0.28 IDF1 points at 1.25 tracking passes. CORVUS executes four missions with execution accuracy up to 0.99 and target recall at the perception ceiling, spends no token on the routine decision, runs on Jetson Xavier NX, and hosts fifteen language models from six families without altering any commitment, demonstrating its extensibility and generalization.

</details>


### [45] [Rethinking Microscopy Generation: Co-Designed Diffusion for Biologically Interpretable Single-Cell Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33398)
*Shumpei Takezaki,Duway Nicolas Lesmes-Leon,Ryoma Bise,Gillian Lovell,Bianca Migliori,Andreas Dengel,Sheraz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将显微图像生成问题重构到单细胞层面，提出将输入条件与模型潜在表示共同对齐到同一组语义细胞测量轴（BioVAE），在LIVECell上验证了语义轴条件显著优于高维条件，生成细胞经生物学家盲评确认真实可信，逐轴遍历可产生稳定可解释的表型变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显微图像生成模型虽能生成逼真的整幅视野图像，但缺乏对单个细胞外观进行有原则控制的方式，即无法按可解释、可测量的表型维度调节细胞形态。

Method: 将生成任务下沉到单细胞级别，并联合设计输入控制与模型内部表示：（1）输入端把大量标准细胞测量聚合为少量命名、可测量、可遍历的语义轴（大小、伸长率、亮度、纹理等）；（2）模型端提出BioVAE，其编码器-解码器潜在空间显式对齐到同一组测量。关键在于仅当条件也迁移到语义轴时，结构化潜空间才带来收益。

Result: 在LIVECell数据集上，语义轴条件相比高维条件显著提升生成保真度；生物学家盲评研究确认生成细胞的真实感；沿单个语义轴的遍历产生稳定且可解释的表型变化。

Conclusion: 单细胞级、语义轴对齐的联合设计是可控显微图像生成的有效范式，输入条件与潜表示须协同结构化才能发挥结构化潜空间的作用。

Abstract: Generative models for microscopy can already produce realistic whole-image fields of view, but offer no principled way to control how each individual cell looks. We address this gap by recasting microscopy generation at the single-cell level and designing the input controls and the model's internal representation jointly, so that both are organized along the same biologically meaningful directions. On the input side, we aggregate a long list of standard cell measurements into a small set of semantic axes (size, elongation, brightness, texture, etc.) that are each named, measurable, and traversable. On the model side, we introduce BioVAE, an encoder-decoder whose latent space is explicitly aligned with the same set of measurements. A key finding is that the benefit of biologically structuring the latent emerges only when conditioning is also moved to the semantic axes, showing that the two ends must be designed jointly. Experiments on LIVECell show that semantic-axis conditioning substantially improves generative fidelity over high-dimensional conditioning, a blind study with biologists confirms the realism of generated cells, and per-axis traversals produce stable and interpretable phenotypic changes.

</details>


### [46] [Bidirectional Cross-frequency Domain Interaction Network for UHD Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33402)
*Bo Fu,Hai Chi,Liyan Wang,Shilin Fu,Wei Wang,Kin-Man Lam,Jinshan Pan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiFreqNet，通过双向跨频域交互（DFI）在VAE多尺度表征中实现低频与高频特征互促增强，用于UHD低光照图像增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UHD LLIE方法多依赖单一空间域或频域建模，或独立处理不同频率分量，未能联合利用空间-频率互补信息及光照与结构信息跨频域的内在关联，导致光照不一致、残余噪声与细节伪影。

Method: 将双域特征交互模块（DFI）嵌入预训练VAE的多尺度表征中；DFI含空间-频谱感知低频增强块（S²LFE）与跨频增强块（CFE）。S²LFE以高频结构线索引导低频恢复，联合建模空间与频谱信息以恢复全局曝光和空间变化光照并抑制传感器噪声；CFE利用增强后的低频上下文引导高频自适应细化。

Result: 在四个通用及UHD LLIE基准上，性能与SOTA方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 双向跨频域交互可有效耦合光照与结构信息，兼顾一致性光照、噪声抑制与细节保真，适用于UHD低光照增强。

Abstract: Existing Ultra-High-Definition (UHD) low-light image enhancement (LLIE) methods typically rely on either spatial- or frequency-domain modeling, or independently process different frequency components for efficient restoration. However, they often fail to jointly exploit complementary spatial--frequency information and the inherent relationship between illumination and structural information across frequencies, leading to inconsistent illumination, residual noise, and detail artifacts. In this paper, we propose a novel Bidirectional Cross-frequency Domain Interaction Network (BiFreqNet) for UHD LLIE. Specifically, BiFreqNet incorporates a Dual-domain Feature Interaction (DFI) module into the multi-scale representations of a pretrained variational autoencoder (VAE) to perform reciprocal enhancement between low- and high-frequency features. DFI consists of a Spatial and Spectral-Aware Low-Frequency Enhancement (S$^2$LFE) block and a Cross-Frequency Enhancement (CFE) block. S$^2$LFE exploits high-frequency structural cues to guide low-frequency restoration and jointly models spatial and spectral information to recover global exposure and spatially varying illumination while suppressing sensor noise. CFE further leverages the enhanced low-frequency contextual information to guide adaptive high-frequency refinement, enabling faithful reconstruction of structures and fine details. Extensive experiments on four general and UHD LLIE benchmarks demonstrate that BiFreqNet achieves competitive or superior performance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [47] [How Robust Are CNNs and Vision Transformers for Alzheimer's MRI Classification Across Datasets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33416)
*Hafija Aktar,Molly Croes,Frank S. Menniti,Abdeltawab Hendawi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建多数据集基准框架，在统一预处理与评估下比较五种架构在三个MRI数据集上的阿尔茨海默病分期表现，ViT-Large在三者上均最优，最高达99.84%准确率、99.86%宏F1，且早期阶段类别间混淆明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MRI的阿尔茨海默病分期准确评估对理解疾病进展与开发计算机辅助工具很重要，深度学习已显示潜力；但单数据集评估难以区分性能差异源于架构特性还是数据集特定效应，缺乏可比性。

Method: 提出多数据集基准框架，采用标准化预处理与统一评估协议；比较五种架构：ResNet-50、ReXNet-150、Swin Transformer、ViT-Base、ViT-Large；在三个分别代表严重类别不平衡、训练-测试分布失配与大规模数据增强的MRI数据集上进行评估。

Result: 各数据集上性能模式总体一致；ViT-Large在三个数据集上均最佳，最高99.84%准确率与99.86%宏F1；架构间差异在分布失配下最显著，在增强数据设定下明显缩小；类别层面性能较低主要出现在相邻早期阶段类别，尤其Very Mild与Mild。

Conclusion: 应跨多数据条件评估深度学习架构，而非依赖单数据集性能；相邻早期阶段的细粒度区分仍是主要瓶颈，需针对性改进。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of Alzheimer's disease stages from MRI is important for understanding disease progression and supporting the development of computer-aided analysis tools. Deep learning models have shown promising results for Alzheimer's disease classification from MRI. However, when evaluated on single datasets, it is difficult to determine whether the observed performance reflects consistent architectural behavior or dataset-specific effects. In this work, we present a multi-dataset benchmarking framework with standardized preprocessing and evaluation. We compare five architectures: ResNet-50, ReXNet-150, Swin Transformer, ViT-Base, and ViT-Large. The models are evaluated across three MRI datasets representing severe class imbalance, train-test distribution mismatch, and large-scale augmentation. The results show broadly consistent performance patterns across datasets, with ViT-Large achieving the best performance on all three datasets and reaching up to 99.84% accuracy and 99.86% macro F1. Performance differences between architectures are most pronounced under distribution mismatch and considerably smaller in the augmented-data setting. Across datasets, lower class-level performance is observed primarily among adjacent early-stage categories, particularly Very Mild and Mild impairment. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating deep learning architectures across multiple data conditions rather than relying on single-dataset performance.

</details>


### [48] [Dynamic Query as Budgeting for Tiny Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33430)
*Yi Zhang,Di Xiong,Ge Gao,Xiangyue Zhang,Yihang Qiu,Yuxuan Zhou,Shuo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将基于DETR的小目标检测器中动态查询机制解耦为“数量”与“选择”两种效应，论证其本质是依据目标密度进行查询预算分配，进而提出采用轻量非学习预算器的BUTR框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DETR的小目标检测器使用动态查询机制应对严重的密度不平衡，但将“解码多少查询”与“解码哪些查询”两种效应纠缠在一起，导致难以判断增益来源、动态查询设计复杂化，且额外的学习预测器与启发式过滤带来开销和选择错误。

Method: 解耦动态查询的两种效应，证明其主要作用是基于目标密度的预算分配而非复杂的查询选择；据此提出单调且保守的预算原则，并设计BUTR框架：使用轻量、非学习的预算器从编码器置信度估计输入相关的查询预算K(x)，保留标准Top-K(x)查询初始化，不引入额外学习模块或启发式过滤。

Result: 在800×800分辨率下，BUTR-L在AI-TOD-v2上达到35.7 AP、47.8 FPS，在VisDrone上达到29.8 AP、42.1 FPS（NVIDIA T4 GPU）；在COCO上的推理阶段集成进一步验证其对通用场景检测的适用性。

Conclusion: 动态查询的有效性主要来自查询预算分配而非选择；简约的非学习预算器即可在保持效率的同时取得有竞争力的小目标检测性能，并可推广至通用检测场景。

Abstract: Recent DETR-based tiny object detectors adopt dynamic query mechanisms to handle severe density imbalance. However, existing designs entangle two effects: how many and which queries are decoded. This entanglement obscures what drives the gains and complicates dynamic-query design; additional learned predictors and heuristic filtering also introduce overhead and potential selection errors. We disentangle these effects and show that dynamic query primarily acts as \emph{budgeting}---allocating decoder query capacity according to object density---rather than intricate query selection.Motivated by this view, we formulate a monotonic and conservative budgeting principle and propose a budgeting-based DETR (BUTR), a streamlined framework that uses a lightweight, non-learned budgeter to estimate an input-dependent query budget $K(x)$ from encoder confidences. BUTR retains standard Top-$K(x)$ query initialization without additional learned modules or heuristic filtering in the budgeter.At $800\times800$ resolution, BUTR-L achieves 35.7 AP at 47.8 FPS on AI-TOD-v2 and 29.8 AP at 42.1 FPS on VisDrone on an NVIDIA T4 GPU. Inference-time integration on COCO further demonstrates its applicability to general scenario detection. Code is available at https://github.com/zhangyi-magic/BUTR.

</details>


### [49] [Semantic-First MixGRPO: Reliable Emotional Image Generation via Temporal Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33460)
*Zhenxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以"随机窗口位置"（概率流时间）作为流式图像生成中的情感属性控制变量，用语义优先调度（前20步ODE建立内容、后4步SDE做情感探索）在FLUX.1-dev上取得质量-情感-成本的最佳折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控图像合成需要在修改视觉情感的同时不改变提示词指定的物体、布局与关系。现有方法依赖条件架构或辅助损失来解决这一矛盾，而混合ODE/SDE采样主要只被用于加速GRPO训练，未被当作属性控制维度研究。

Method: 基于FLUX.1-dev与EmotiCrafter式效价-唤醒条件，在匹配设置下比较固定早期/中期/晚期4步SDE窗口、渐进式MixGRPO与全路径Flow-GRPO。鉴于第0步噪声最大，提出"语义优先"调度：第0–19步用确定性ODE采样建立内容，仅在20–23步施加随机探索与策略更新。

Result: 三个随机种子下CLIPScore 29.662±0.276，跨情感DINO一致性80.1±0.9，语义漂移2.9%，训练成本较全路径Flow-GRPO降低33.5%。全路径随机训练在效价-唤醒目标上更准确，体现真实权衡。语义优先调度在情感与语义指标上均优于渐进式调度，在5个极端/中性目标下95.98%图像保留主体，并在30人研究中获得最高语义保持与整体偏好评分。

Conclusion: 概率流时间可作为一种实用的图形学控制维度，用于实现可靠的情感外观操控；随机窗口位置是平衡语义保真、情感准确性与训练开销的有效控制量。

Abstract: Controllable image synthesis should modify visual affect without replacing the objects, layout, or relations specified by a prompt. Existing methods address this tension through conditioning architectures or auxiliary losses, while mixed ordinary differential equation (ODE) and stochastic differential equation (SDE) sampling has mainly been used to accelerate group relative policy optimization (GRPO). We instead study stochastic-window position as an attribute-control variable for flow-based image generation. Using FLUX.1-dev with EmotiCrafter-style valence-arousal conditioning, we compare fixed early, middle, and late four-transition SDE windows, progressive MixGRPO, and full-path Flow-GRPO under matched settings. Because step 0 has the highest noise, our semantic-first schedule uses deterministic ODE sampling at steps 0-19 to establish content, then confines stochastic exploration and policy updates to steps 20-23. Across three seeds, it achieves a CLIPScore of $29.662\pm0.276$, cross-affect DINO consistency of $80.1\pm0.9$, and semantic drift of 2.9%, while reducing training cost by 33.5% relative to full-path Flow-GRPO. Full-path stochastic training remains more accurate on valence-arousal targets, revealing a genuine trade-off. Semantic-first nevertheless improves both affect and semantic metrics over progressive scheduling, preserves the principal subject in 95.98% of images at five extreme or neutral targets, and receives the highest semantic-preservation and overall-preference ratings in a 30-participant study. These results identify probability-flow time as a practical graphics control dimension for reliable affective appearance manipulation.

</details>


### [50] [Qwen-Image-PE: Learning Visual Design for Text-to-Image Prompt Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33472)
*Zekai Zhang,Yanran Zhang,Shengming Yin,Yan Shu,Zhening Liu,Bingxuan Zhao,Chenyang Zhu,Donghao Zhou,Jiahao Li,Jiahao Wang,Jie Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Kun Yan,Lihan Jiang,Ningyuan Tang,Qihang Ai,Rui Song,Shuo Cao,Xiao Xu,Xiaoyue Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Zhendong Wang,Zhouyang Li,Zihao Liu,Zikai Zhou,Bo Zheng,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Qwen-Image-PE，通过"基于字幕的逆向蒸馏（CRD）"监督微调 + "组内设计奖励（GDR）"强化学习两阶段训练，让提示增强学习细粒度视觉设计维度；在 Qwen-Image-Bench 上提升 7.81 分，9B 版本超越所有基线并泛化到 4 个 T2I 骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示增强方法侧重保留用户意图与整体视觉吸引力，对光照、构图、布局、细节等细粒度设计维度缺乏显式建模，难以将欠明确请求转为连贯的视觉规格。

Method: 两阶段训练：阶段一用精心设计图像的稠密字幕作设计监督，进行 Caption-Based Reverse Distillation（CRD）监督微调；阶段二用 Groupwise Design Reward（GDR）强化学习，对同一请求的所有候选图像进行组内联合比较，以细化光照、构图、布局与细节。

Result: 在 Qwen-Image-Bench + Qwen-Image-2.1 上，397B 版较原始请求提升 7.81 分，9B 版优于全部已评估 PE 基线；视觉设计、美学、图像质量与人类偏好均有增益。PE 沿训练数据、模型规模、信息密度三轴可扩展，且信息密度充分是有效增强的前提；可泛化到 4 个额外 T2I 骨干，在 HunyuanImage-2.1 与 FLUX 3 Image Klein 上甚至超过原生提示增强器。

Conclusion: 以"设计学习"为核心的提示增强可显著提升生成质量并具备跨骨干可迁移性；模型规模与数据之外，信息密度是扩展的关键前提。已开源 Qwen-Image-PE-9B。

Abstract: Learning visual design is essential for prompt enhancement (PE) to turn underspecified user requests into coherent visual specifications for text-to-image (T2I) generation. Existing methods preserve user intent and improve overall visual appeal, with less explicit emphasis on fine-grained design dimensions. To address this gap, we introduce Qwen-Image-PE, which learns visual design through two-stage training. Supervised fine-tuning with Caption-Based Reverse Distillation (CRD) uses dense captions of well-designed images as design supervision. Reinforcement learning with Groupwise Design Reward (GDR) jointly compares all candidate images for the same request to refine lighting, composition, layout, and detail. On Qwen-Image-Bench with Qwen-Image-2.1, Qwen-Image-PE-397B improves over original requests by 7.81 points, while Qwen-Image-PE-9B outperforms all evaluated PE baselines. Further evaluations show gains in visual design, aesthetics, image quality, and human preference. We find that PE scales along three axes: training data, model size, and information density, with sufficient information density as a prerequisite for effective enhancement. Moreover, we demonstrate that Qwen-Image-PE generalizes to four additional T2I backbones, even surpassing native prompt enhancers on HunyuanImage-2.1 and FLUX 3 Image Klein. We release Qwen-Image-PE-9B at https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen-Image-2.1-PE-T2I.

</details>


### [51] [MarineLife-16K: An Attribute-Rich Video-Text Dataset for Motion-Aware Marine Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33478)
*Quang-Trung Truong,Thanh-Nghia Nguyen,Phu-Phong Vo,Jaeyeon Kim,Cheng Chen,Jie Zhang,Binh-Son Hua,Duc Thanh Nguyen,Ivor Tsang,Sai-Kit Yeung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出大规模海洋视频-文本数据集，含13类细粒度属性标注（2000个视频-文本对），并衍生16,080个视频问答对，系统评测闭源与开源视频大模型在VQA与视频描述任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海洋视频数据集存在样本数量少、文本描述不够细粒度、缺乏详细视觉属性等问题，难以支撑复杂海洋环境下的视频理解研究。

Method: 构建野外（in-the-wild）大规模视频-文本数据集：2,000个视频-文本对，每场景最多4个目标，标注13类属性（目标视觉/几何属性、行为、环境上下文、相机运动），覆盖多光照条件、不同时段与7个国家；据此生成16,080个视频-问题对（12,080多项选择、4,000开放式）；并在视频问答与视频描述任务上对闭源与开源视频-LLM进行基准评测。

Result: (i) 闭源模型通常性能更优；(ii) VLM能较好处理目标运动，但对复杂相机运动处理不佳；(iii) 闭源模型难以感知相机运动；(iv) VLM在静态相机场景下推理能力不足。

Conclusion: 该数据集为海洋视频理解提供了细粒度、多属性的评测基准，揭示了现有视频-LLM在相机运动感知与静态相机推理方面的明显短板，为后续模型改进指明方向。

Abstract: Marine video understanding is crucial for a wide range of real-world applications. However, existing marine video datasets suffer from limitations, including the limited number of data samples, fine-grained text descriptions, and detailed visual attributes. To address these limitations, we introduce a large-scale, video-text dataset featuring rich, in-the-wild visual attributes tailored for complex marine environments. Our dataset comprises 2,000 video-text pairs annotated with 13 attributes covering up to four objects per scene, object visual/geometric properties, behaviors, environmental context, and camera motion, spanning diverse lighting conditions, times of day, and 7 countries. From these annotations, we generate 16,080 video-question pairs (12,080 multiple-choice, 4,000 open-ended). Furthermore, we benchmark both proprietary and open-source video-LLM models on video question answering and video captioning tasks. We find that (i) proprietary models often achieves superior performances; (ii) VLMs handle object motion well but struggle with complex camera motion; (iii) closed-source models struggle to perceive camera motion; and (iv) VLMs struggle to reason in static cameras.

</details>


### [52] [Energy Efficient Lung Tumor Segmentation with a modified CBAM ENet Skip Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33479)
*Sharan Thummagunti,Aayushi Padia,Abhiram Koppuravuri,Prabhu Prasad B M,G N Girish*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ENet的轻量级肺肿瘤分割模型，融合UNet跳跃连接与CBAM注意力，在430,052参数、0.549 GMAC下于LIDC-IDRI上取得DSC 0.90812，兼顾精度与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进深度学习分割模型虽DSC高，但单次前向需大量浮点运算、依赖GPU加速、推理慢，难以在计算资源受限的临床环境部署。

Method: 以ENet为主干获取实时推理与低计算量；在编解码阶段间引入UNet式跳跃连接实现特征复用；嵌入轻量CBAM提升特征表达。模型在TCIA/MSD公开数据上训练，在LIDC-IDRI上评估，并用Grad-CAM提供可视化解释。

Result: DSC达0.90812（阈值0.5），仅430,052参数、0.549 GMAC；相较UNet计算量减少约79倍、速度快至10倍，参数量比SegNet少约22倍。

Conclusion: 该紧凑模型在保持高分割精度的同时大幅降低计算与存储开销，适合资源受限场景的临床部署并降低成本；Grad-CAM可定位需改进的分割区域。

Abstract: Lung tumor segmentation plays a vital role in early detection and treatment planning of lung cancer. Although state-of-the-art deep learning models achieve high dice score coefficients (DSC), each forward pass requires computing large amounts of floating point operations, making them computationally expensive, requiring GPU acceleration, and having slow inference speed, limiting real-world clinical deployment. We propose a lightweight, energy-efficient model that leverages the ENet architecture for its real-time inference capability and low computational cost, enhanced with UNet-style skip connections between encoder and decoder stages for feature reuse and the lightweight Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) for improved feature representation. The model is trained on publicly available data from The Cancer Imaging Archive (TCIA) via the Medical Segmentation Decathlon Challenge (MSD) and evaluated on the Lung Image Database Consortium and Image Database Resource Initiative (LIDC-IDRI) dataset. Our model achieves a DSC of 0.90812 (threshold of 0.5) with its compact architecture of 430,052 parameters and 0.549 Giga Multiply-Accumulate operations (GMAC), making it up to 10 times faster, requires 79 times less GMAC than UNet, and has 22 times fewer parameters than SegNet. These characteristics make it well-suited for deployment in clinical settings where computational resources are limited and decrease the cost of utilizing such a system. Additionally, the explainable AI technique of Grad-CAM is used to provide visual interpretability of the model's predictions and to identify regions where segmentation performance requires improvement.

</details>


### [53] [Can One Hear Geometry? Gauge-Invariant Spectral Descriptors for Text-to-B-rep Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33484)
*Hongzhu Yi,Hao Liu,Tao Yu,Tiankun Yang,Yujia Yang,Zhenghao Zhang,Tianyu Zong,Zhenyu Guan,Bingkang Shi,Haopeng Jin,Yuanxiang Wang,Zhongtian Luo,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于群作用与谱几何不变量的文本到B-rep实体检索框架，显式构造280维不变描述子，在Text-to-3D实体检索上达到88.6% Recall@5，较SOTA提升13.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到3D实体检索需在姿态、尺度与记录约定变化下对齐语言与精确几何。现有方法将B-rep离散为点云/网格，或从面、边、拓扑学习特征，存在信息损失或缺乏显式不变性保证的问题。

Method: 以群作用刻画平移、旋转、反射、均匀缩放及轮廓记录约定引起的等价变换；针对草图-拉伸实体，融合截面区域、轮廓边界、拉伸结构三个互补层次的几何信息，构造固定280维谱几何描述子；并采用描述子一致的多正样本集合与几何属性监督促进文本-实体结构对齐。

Result: 在Text-to-3D实体检索任务上取得88.6% Recall@5，较此前最优方法提升13.6个百分点，验证显式几何不变量对精确3D实体检索的有效性。

Conclusion: 将不变性作为描述子构造的显式约束，可有效避免离散化信息损失，为文本到B-rep实体的精确检索提供可靠方案。

Abstract: Text-to-3D solid retrieval requires aligning natural language with precise geometry despite variations in pose, scale, and recording conventions. Existing methods discretize Boundary Representation (B-rep) models into point clouds or meshes, or learn features from faces, edges, and topology, risking information loss or lacking explicit invariance guarantees. We propose a text-to-B-rep retrieval framework based on group actions and spectral geometric invariants, making invariance an explicit constraint on descriptor construction. Specifically, we use group actions to characterize equivalence transformations induced by translation, rotation, reflection, uniform scaling, and contour recording conventions. For sketch-extrusion solids, we construct a fixed 280-dimensional spectral geometric descriptor by integrating geometric information from three complementary levels, including cross-sectional regions, contour boundaries, and extrusion structures. Furthermore, we use descriptor-consistent multi-positive sets and geometric attribute supervision to promote structural alignment between text and solids. On Text-to-3D solid retrieval, our method achieves 88.6% Recall@5, exceeding the previous state of the art by 13.6 percentage points and demonstrating the effectiveness of explicit geometric invariants for precise 3D solid retrieval.

</details>


### [54] [Explicit Frictional Contact Modeling in Neural Garment Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33492)
*Thanh-Nguyen Truong,Vu-Hoang Tran,Ching-Chun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模块化接触算子（Modular Contact Operator），在不重训练、不迭代求解的前提下，于预测后显式解析摩擦：估计法向载荷、按Coulomb条件做闭式修正，并以线性化膜波传播运动，实现可按 μ 单调调控的粘连/滑移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理自监督的GNN衣物仿真将布料-人体摩擦隐含在训练能量中，推理时无可设置的摩擦系数 μ；即便为特定 μ 重训练，模型对 μ 也几乎无响应。而优化类求解器虽能依据反作用力逐接触判定粘滑状态，却需每步重新优化衣物状态、代价高昂。

Method: 在冻结预测器之后附加模块化接触算子：估计法向载荷；沿跨人体三角面的路径施加闭式Coulomb修正；将诱导运动作为线性化膜波在布料中传播。无需重训练、无需迭代求解。

Result: 在 HOOD 与 ContourCraft 上，粘连随 μ 单调上升、滑移单调下降，与参考求解器的响应趋势一致，而系数特定重训练表现平坦；同时保持低穿透、与参考一致的拉伸，运行时间仅为求解器的 1/8。

Conclusion: 通过推理后显式解析摩擦，可在保持低成本的同时赋予GNN衣物仿真可控、物理一致的摩擦行为，弥补隐式摩擦与逐接触Coulomb条件缺失的不足。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) simulate garments efficiently through physics-based self-supervision, but leave body-garment friction implicit or inside training energies, with no friction coefficient $μ$ to set at inference; retraining the tested GNNs with a coefficient-specific friction term barely responds to $μ$. Optimization-based solvers decide each contact's stick-slip state from the reaction that governs it, but only by re-optimizing the garment state at every step; a friction term in a one-pass training objective acts as a fixed-weight penalty on sliding, and the trained weights apply no per-contact Coulomb condition at inference. We therefore resolve friction explicitly after prediction: a Modular Contact Operator, attached to a frozen predictor without retraining or an iterative solve, estimates the normal load, applies a closed-form Coulomb correction along a path across body triangles, and propagates the induced motion through the cloth as a linearized membrane wave. On HOOD and ContourCraft, it raises sticking and lowers slip monotonically in $μ$, tracking a reference solver's response while coefficient-specific retraining stays flat, with low penetration and reference-consistent stretching at an eighth of the solver's runtime.

</details>


### [55] [FreqPKC: Frequency-Aware PeakConv for Radar Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33504)
*Yangyong Deng,Pengyu Zhang,Shaoqiu Song,Xiaotao Huang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从频域视角重新审视PeakConv算子，提出频率感知的FreqPKC及分割框架FreqPKC-Net，在多个雷达语义分割数据集上超越现有SoTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载毫米波雷达语义分割中，现有PeakConv将保护带宽设为可学习参数，却未显式刻画该算子的频率响应，导致高频细节与低频噪声的平衡缺乏理论依据。

Method: 分析PKC的频率响应，提出显式建模频谱特性的FreqPKC；设计Frequency-Aware模块自适应推断保护带宽以调节有效带宽，在目标区增强高频、背景区低通抑噪；构建FreqPKC-Net，融合频率引导自适应调制与多视角跨视角融合，并兼容单视角输入。

Result: 在CARRADA、CARRADA-RAC及自建RS-SAR数据集上，FreqPKC-Net在距离-多普勒与距离-角度视图上均稳定优于现有SoTA方法。

Conclusion: 将CFAR启发的算子纳入频域分析框架可有效提升雷达语义分割性能，频率感知的带宽自适应与跨视角融合具有良好的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Automotive millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar has attracted increasing attention for semantic segmentation in autonomous driving, owing to its robustness under adverse weather and lighting conditions. Recent works have introduced radar-specific neural operators such as PeakConv (PKC), inspired by constant false alarm rate (CFAR) detection, to better model the peak-dominated characteristics of radar images. However, existing methods typically treat the guard bandwidth as learnable parameters, without explicitly characterizing the frequency response of the PKC operator. In this work, we revisit PKC from a frequency-domain perspective and propose Frequency-Aware PeakConv (FreqPKC), which explicitly models and exploits its underlying spectral characteristics. By analyzing the frequency response of PKC, FreqPKC enables the network to capture a broader range of frequency components and adaptively adjust its effective bandwidth. With the proposed Frequency-Aware (FA) module, the network can infer appropriate guard bandwidths to balance high- and low-frequency information: it enhances high-frequency components in target regions for more precise segmentation while suppressing noise through low-frequency filtering in background areas. Building on FreqPKC, we further develop FreqPKC-Net, a radar semantic segmentation (RSS) framework that integrates frequency-guided adaptive modulation with cross-view fusion for multi-view inputs, and can also be adapted to single-view data. Experiments on the public datasets CARRADA, CARRADA-RAC, and a self-built dataset named RS-SAR demonstrate that FreqPKC-Net consistently outperforms existing SoTA methods on both range-Doppler and range-angle views.

</details>


### [56] [Component Pooling Trained Across Orientations for Few-Shot Remote Sensing Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33518)
*Bingchen Yan,Wenheng Guo,Yuye Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免参数的分量池化网络 RoCoP，通过非负分解与分量打分替代全局平均池化，并配合随机旋转视图训练，提升小样本遥感场景分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小样本遥感场景分类方法多以全局平均池化汇总特征图，将界定场景的少量目标与其周围大面积背景同等对待；同时忽略了航拍图像不具备规范朝向这一特性。

Method: RoCoP 由特征提取器、非负分解模块（NDM）与分量打分模块（CSM）构成。NDM 以确定性初始化和单次乘性更新将每幅图像的局部描述子分解为少量加性分量，使同一目标的位置聚为同组；CSM 依据激活强度为各分量加权，并将权重转为软掩码用于池化。两模块均对特征位置置换不变，故仅骨干网络对旋转敏感，训练时对每幅图像的随机旋转视图进行分类。

Result: 在 NWPU-RESISC45、AID、UC Merced 上，分量池化与旋转视图训练互补：在 NWPU-RESISC45 与 AID 上，5-way 1-shot 精度较强迁移学习基线提升约 2.5 个百分点，且推理阶段不增加参数。相同协议下优于复现的度量类与任务自适应方法，NDM 与 CSM 插入现有方法亦可带来提升。

Conclusion: 分量池化与旋转视图训练有效且互补，可免参数、即插即用地增强现有小样本遥感分类方法；方法不适用于基类集极小的情形。

Abstract: Few-shot remote sensing scene classification (FSRSSC) aims to recognize novel scene categories from only a few labeled images. Most FSRSSC methods summarize the feature map of an image by global average pooling, which treats the few objects that define an aerial scene and the large background around them alike, and they rarely consider that aerial images have no canonical orientation. To address these issues, we propose RoCoP, a parameter-free component pooling network trained across orientations. RoCoP consists of a feature extractor, a non-negative decomposition module (NDM) and a component scoring module (CSM). NDM factorizes the local descriptors of each image into a few additive components with a deterministic initialization and a single multiplicative update, so that the positions of one object are grouped together. CSM weights each component by its activation magnitude and converts the weights into a soft mask for pooling. Because both modules are invariant to permutations of feature positions, the only rotation-sensitive part of the pipeline is the backbone, which we train to classify randomly rotated views of every image. Experiments on NWPU-RESISC45, AID and UC Merced show that component pooling and rotated-view training are complementary: together they improve a strong transfer-learning baseline by about 2.5 points in 5-way 1-shot accuracy on NWPU-RESISC45 and AID, without adding parameters at inference. Under an identical protocol, RoCoP outperforms re-implemented metric-based and task-adaptive methods, and NDM and CSM also improve existing methods when plugged into them. We further identify the regime in which the method does not apply, namely very small base sets.

</details>


### [57] [Promise and Limits of Cross-Architecture Knowledge Distillation for Lightweight Medical Vision Transformers (LiteMed-ViT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33520)
*Shuaibu Shehu,Elijah M. Flomo,Mohammad Safiya,Anurodh Kumar,Subodh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiteMed-ViT：一种卷积stem+双token蒸馏训练的轻量ViT（0.234M参数），在MedMNIST子集上精度与鲁棒性可比紧凑CNN，但在DermaMNIST等场景存在明确性能瓶颈并给出条件分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗影像的边缘端部署存在两难：CNN过重不适合边缘设备，ViT缺少归纳偏置而依赖大规模训练数据。作者希望探究"面向数据高效ViT的双token蒸馏"在跨架构（CNN教师→轻量ViT学生）场景下能被推到何种极限，并明确其失效条件。

Method: 构建LiteMed-ViT：带卷积stem的双token ViT，采用类别平衡的蒸馏目标；以ResNet18为教师进行跨架构双token蒸馏。模型仅0.234M参数（比ResNet18少47.8倍）、内存占用0.89MB。在三个模态与类别均衡度不同的MedMNIST子集上，与三个紧凑CNN基线对比，并在MedMNIST-C腐蚀基准下评估鲁棒性，同时分析分辨率、教师强度与教师校准的影响。

Result: BloodMNIST上达95.05%（优于全部三个基线），PathMNIST上达89.15%（优于其中两个）；在MedMNIST-C腐蚀下对各子集至少与所有紧凑CNN同等鲁棒，且在BloodMNIST、PathMNIST上优于它们，随腐蚀严重度增加相对排名不下降。局限：DermaMNIST上平衡准确率最多落后最强紧凑CNN 1.9个百分点，且提升输入分辨率至224像素无收益（同条件下ResNet18提升12.6点）；更强教师并不能带来更准的学生，过度自信的教师会降低学生DermaMNIST平衡准确率，除非进行校准。

Conclusion: 跨架构双token蒸馏可在极小参数预算下使轻量ViT在部分医学数据集上达到甚至超过紧凑CNN的精度与鲁棒性，但其收益依赖于数据集特性（模态、类别均衡度）；在细粒度/类别不均衡数据上存在平台期，且教师强度与置信度校准是关键约束条件。

Abstract: Running deep models for medical image analysis at the point of care is challenging with standard architectures: convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are often too heavy for edge devices, while Vision Transformers (ViTs) require massive training datasets to compensate for missing inductive biases. This study investigates how far the dual-token distillation used to train data-efficient ViTs can be pushed when applied across architectures, from a CNN-based teacher into a lightweight ViT student. LiteMed-ViT is introduced as a dual-token ViT featuring a convolutional stem and a class-balanced distillation objective; at 0.234M parameters, LiteMed-ViT carries 47.8x fewer parameters than its ResNet18 teacher at a memory footprint of just 0.89 MB. Evaluated against three compact CNN baselines on three MedMNIST subsets of varying modality and class balance, LiteMed-ViT attains higher accuracy than all three baselines on BloodMNIST (95.05%) and two on PathMNIST (89.15%). Under MedMNIST-C corruptions, it is at least as robust as every compact CNN across all subsets, outperforming them on BloodMNIST and PathMNIST without declining in relative standing as severity increases. Particular attention is devoted to identifying the exact conditions where performance plateaus. On DermaMNIST, LiteMed-ViT's balanced accuracy remains up to 1.9 points below the strongest compact CNNs and does not benefit from higher input resolutions up to 224 pixels, where ResNet18 gains 12.6 points. Stronger teachers do not yield a more accurate student, and overconfident ones lower its DermaMNIST balanced accuracy unless calibrated.

</details>


### [58] [Patch-Derived or Register Tokens? Comparing Variable-Length Image Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33540)
*Philipp Schenkel,Tobias Schürmann,Omer Sahin Tas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 比较两种变长图像tokenizer策略（patch衍生token vs register token）：极端压缩下patch token重建更好，中等预算下register略优，高预算下相当；下游生成任务中register更优，但加入表征对齐后patch token可追平或反超；两者都学习到空间局部化表征，register token自发形成隐式二维空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流图像tokenizer将latent排布在下采样二维网格上，导致序列长度与图像分辨率强绑定，无法灵活控制token预算。作者希望比较两种"变长"token策略在重建与生成任务中的表现差异。

Method: 对比两类变长token方案：(1) patch衍生token，具有显式空间组织且计算高效；(2) register token，无预定义空间布局但计算开销更大。在极端压缩、中等和高token预算下评估重建质量；在下游生成任务中比较生成性能；并引入与外部预训练视觉编码器的表征对齐；最后分析两种方案学到的空间表征结构。

Result: 极端压缩下patch衍生token重建显著更优；中等预算下register token重建质量略高；高预算下两者相当。下游生成任务中register token优于patch衍生token；加入表征对齐后patch衍生token生成性能大幅提升，可依token预算追平或超越register token。两者均学到空间局部化表征：patch token基本保留显式空间组织，register token在无预定义布局下发展出隐式二维空间结构；表征对齐削弱这种空间组织约束，使单个token具有更强的非局部影响。

Conclusion: 变长tokenizer中token类型的选择需权衡token预算与任务类型；表征对齐是缩小patch衍生token在生成任务上差距的有效手段，并会改变token的空间组织性与非局部影响力。

Abstract: Image tokenizers convert pixels into compact latent sequences for efficient generative modeling. Most approaches arrange latents on a downsampled two-dimensional grid, tying sequence length to image resolution. We compare two variable-length strategies: computationally efficient patch-derived tokens, which inherit explicit spatial organization, and more demanding register tokens, which have no predefined spatial layout. Our reconstruction results reveal that patch-derived tokens perform substantially better under extreme compression, whereas register tokens achieve slightly better reconstruction quality at intermediate budgets. At high token budgets, both approaches achieve similar reconstruction quality. For downstream generative tasks, register tokens outperform patch-derived tokens. Adding representation alignment with external pretrained visual encoders substantially improves the generative performance of patch-derived tokens, allowing them to match or outperform register tokens depending on the token budget. We further find that both approaches learn spatially localized representations. Patch-derived tokens largely retain their explicitly imposed spatial organization, while register tokens develop an implicit two-dimensional spatial structure despite having no predefined layout. Representation alignment reduces this spatial organizational constraint, enabling individual tokens to exert a stronger non-local influence.

</details>


### [59] [Cross-Scale Sparse Attention for Referring Segmentation in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33551)
*Yuxi Li,Lu Si,Xia Liao,Yidong Shen,Zheng Wei,Jun Zhang,Xin Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨尺度稀疏注意力模型CSSAM与M³RRSIS-D数据集，用于指代遥感图像分割，兼顾跨尺度跨模态对齐与稀疏注意力，并覆盖多目标、多跳关系与多粒度三类推理维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRSIS方法多采用顺序式对齐—融合流水线，对跨尺度视觉层级与语言依赖的联合建模不足；稠密注意力难以适应遥感目标的稀疏性与空间分散性；现有数据集缺乏对多目标指代、多跳关系推理和多粒度描述的结构化评测。

Method: 提出CSSAM，包含两个模块：跨尺度模态对齐融合模块（CMFM）实现统一的跨尺度跨模态融合；动态多模态稀疏混合注意力（DMSHA）选择性关注有信息量的视觉—文本token，减少稠密视觉交互。同时构建M³RRSIS-D数据集，围绕多目标（M¹）、依赖指代的多跳（M²）、多粒度（M³）三个推理维度设计。

Result: 在M³RRSIS-D及两个公开RRSIS基准上的大量实验表明，CSSAM在准确率、细粒度推理和高IoU定位指标上均取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: CSSAM验证了跨尺度稀疏注意力在真实遥感场景复杂语义推理中的有效性；数据集为RRSIS的模型开发与评估提供了结构化支撑。

Abstract: High-resolution remote sensing (RS) imagery provides fine spatial detail over large areas, making it essential for fine-grained scene understanding. Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation (RRSIS) aims to segment regions described by natural language, yet many existing methods rely on sequential alignment--fusion pipelines with limited joint modeling of cross-scale visual hierarchies and linguistic dependencies. Moreover, dense attention mechanisms struggle with the sparsity and wide spatial dispersion of RS targets, while current datasets offer limited structured evaluation of multi-target references, multi-hop relational reasoning, and multi-granularity descriptions, limiting both model development and evaluation. To address these challenges, we propose a Cross-Scale Sparse Attention Model (CSSAM), which aligns multiscale visual features with complex referring expressions through two key modules. The Cross-scale Modality-aligned Fusion Module (CMFM) performs unified cross-scale cross-modal fusion, while the Dynamic Multimodal Sparse Hybrid Attention (DMSHA) selectively attends to informative visual--textual tokens, reducing dense visual interactions. Furthermore, we introduce M$^{3}$RRSIS-D, a RRSIS dataset explicitly designed around three core reasoning dimensions: multi-target (M$^{1}$), reference-dependent multi-hop (M$^{2}$), and multi-granularity (M$^{3}$) expressions. Extensive experiments on M$^{3}$RRSIS-D and two public RRSIS benchmarks demonstrate that CSSAM achieves competitive performance across accuracy, fine-grained reasoning, and high-IoU localization metrics, validating its effectiveness for complex semantic reasoning in realistic RS scenarios. Code, dataset, and model weights will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [60] [End-to-end prostate lesion segmentation and detection from undersampled diffusion-weighted MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33554)
*Lukas T. Rotkopf,Marco Schlimbach,Kevin Kröninger,Stefan O. Schoenberg,Heinz-Peter Schlemmer,Jens Kleesiek,Moritz Rempe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端网络VarNet-Seg，直接利用多线圈复数DWI数据与线圈敏感度信息进行前列腺病灶分割，在欠采样条件下优于基于重建图像的常规方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于DWI的前列腺病灶自动检测依赖重建后的图像，未能充分利用全部采集信息（多线圈复数k空间数据与线圈敏感度）。

Method: 纳入269例fastMRI前列腺检查及体素级病灶标注（64例含PIRADS 4/5病灶）。VarNet-Seg由6级变分级联的k空间变分网络（VarNet）与图像域3D分割U-Net组成。对照模型采用相同分割结构，分别输入全线圈合并图像（CC-Seg）、固定RSS重建（RSS-Seg）或固定SENSE重建（SENSE-Seg）；并进行端到端训练与数据一致性（DC）开关的消融实验。五折交叉验证，评估全采样、模拟欠采样、加噪及两者组合下的Dice与患者级AUROC。

Result: VarNet-Seg在各欠采样水平下Dice最高；八倍加速时Dice为0.22，SENSE-Seg为0.14，无DC的VarNet-Seg为0.16，与SENSE-Seg配对差0.08（95% CI 0.04–0.11）。全采样时VarNet-Seg与RSS-Seg相当（0.40 vs 0.39）。患者级AUROC由全采样0.75降至八倍加速0.63，RSS-Seg对应为0.76与0.64。

Conclusion: 多线圈网络在模拟欠采样下较常规模型改善了前列腺病灶分割，匹配消融表明端到端训练与数据一致性均有贡献；尚需前瞻性评估以确定其在加速前列腺DWI中的临床可用性。

Abstract: Automated prostate lesion detection from diffusion-weighted MR imaging (DWI) conventionally relies on reconstructed images and does not fully use all acquisition information. We present an end-to-end network that uses complex multicoil DWI data and coil sensitivity information for prostate lesion segmentation.
  We included 269 fastMRI prostate examinations with additional voxel-level lesion annotations. Of these, 64 examinations contained PIRADS 4 or 5 lesions. The proposed network, VarNet-Seg, consists of a k-space variational network (VarNet) with six variational cascades and an image-space 3D segmentation U-Net. Comparator models used the same segmentation architecture with fully coil-combined images (CC-Seg), fixed root-sum-of-squares reconstruction (RSS-Seg), or fixed SENSE reconstruction (SENSE-Seg). We performed ablations with end-to-end training and data consistency (DC) enabled or disabled. We evaluated lesion segmentation using Dice and patient-level detection using AUROC in five-fold cross-validation with full sampling, simulated undersampling, added noise, or both.
  VarNet-Seg achieved the highest Dice at every undersampling level. At eight-fold acceleration, Dice was 0.22 for VarNet-Seg, 0.14 for SENSE-Seg and 0.16 for VarNet-Seg (no DC). The paired difference from SENSE-Seg was 0.08 (95% CI, 0.04-0.11). With full sampling, VarNet-Seg and RSS-Seg performed similarly (0.40 vs 0.39). Patient-level AUROC decreased from 0.75 with full sampling to 0.63 at eight-fold acceleration; corresponding RSS-Seg AUROCs were 0.76 and 0.64.
  The proposed multicoil network improved prostate lesion segmentation under simulated undersampling compared to conventional models. Matched ablations supported contributions from both end-to-end training and data consistency. Prospective evaluation is needed to establish whether this approach can be used clinically in accelerated prostate DWI.

</details>


### [61] [GeoLift: Grounding Video Spatial Reasoning in Explicit 3D Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33558)
*Xinglin Li,TingTing Long,Jingzhi Zhou,Jiajing Chen,Jian Yang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 GeoLift，用前馈3D重建把单帧目标检测结果映射到共享度量3D场景，以superpoint跨视角关联同一目标，从而显式计算空间关系与多步路径，在冻结权重下显著提升MLLM的视频空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM能识别单帧物体，但难以跨视角整合观察以完成复杂空间任务。以路径规划为代表性任务可观察到：随所需转弯数增加，模型准确率急剧下降，需要三个及以上转弯时部分模型接近随机猜测。这说明仅识别地标不足，模型还必须跨视角连接观测并在共享空间中追踪地标布局。

Method: GeoLift为免训练框架：冻结的MLLM负责2D grounding，预训练几何模型执行前馈3D重建，将各帧检测到的物体映射到共享度量3D场景；利用称为superpoint的共享几何区域，在无需学习匹配器的情况下跨视角关联同一物体的观测；重建的3D图支持显式计算空间关系与多步路径，并为距离和尺寸估计提供几何证据；所有模型权重冻结。

Result: 在VSI-Bench上，Qwen3-VL-4B平均分由55.4%提升至64.1%，路径规划准确率由30.9%提升至53.6%；在MindCube上达到59.8%准确率。

Conclusion: 通过共享3D图连接帧级目标grounding，可在无需任务特定训练的情况下改善复杂空间推理，表明跨视角几何整合是提升MLLM视频空间理解的有效途径。

Abstract: Video spatial reasoning requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to understand how objects are arranged and related across viewpoints. While these models can recognize objects in individual frames, integrating these observations to solve complex spatial tasks remains challenging. We examine route planning as a representative task and observe that existing models become substantially less accurate as the number of required turns increases, with several models achieving accuracy close to random guessing on routes requiring three or more turns. This finding suggests that recognizing landmarks alone is insufficient: models must also connect observations across viewpoints and track how landmarks are arranged in a shared space. Motivated by this observation, we introduce GeoLift, a training-free framework that uses feed-forward 3D reconstruction to map objects detected in individual frames into a shared metric 3D scene. GeoLift combines a frozen MLLM for 2D grounding with pretrained geometry models for scene reconstruction. It then uses shared geometric regions, called superpoints, to associate observations of the same object across views without a learned matcher. The reconstructed 3D map enables explicit computation of spatial relations and multistep routes, and provides geometric evidence for estimating distances and sizes. With all model weights frozen, GeoLift improves Qwen3-VL-4B's average score on VSI-Bench from 55.4% to 64.1%, including an increase in route-planning accuracy from 30.9% to 53.6%, and achieves 59.8% accuracy on MindCube. These results show that connecting frame-level object grounding through a shared 3D map can improve complex spatial reasoning without task-specific training.

</details>


### [62] [What Remains OOD? OODConceptBench for Open-World Concept Recognition in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33564)
*Zhiran Luo,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Sicong Li,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 OODConceptBench，一个基于图文对的分层开放世界概念识别基准（L1 共 30,752 张图像，L2 困难子集 14,285 张，覆盖 7 类共 560 个概念短语），评测 37 个视觉语言模型，发现 L2 上所有模型识别率均低于 L1、发布更晚的模型未必更强、整体提升不代表各类别一致改进，说明大规模视觉语言预训练时代 OOD 仍是挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练模型使零样本泛化模糊了已知与未知概念的边界，现有评测无法直接揭示不同视觉语言模型究竟能识别哪些开放世界概念，因此需要一个面向图像-概念对、可分层量化开放世界概念识别能力的基准。

Method: 构建分层基准 OODConceptBench：全量集 L1（30,752 张图像）与更难的子集 L2（14,285 张图像），涵盖 7 个开放世界类别、560 个概念短语；按发布年份、模型家族与变体三个维度对 37 个视觉语言模型进行系统评测。

Result: 三点主要发现：(1) 全部 37 个模型在 L2 上的目标概念识别率均低于 L1；(2) 后发布的模型不一定具备更强的开放世界概念识别能力；(3) 整体识别率的提升并不意味着各基准类别上一致改善。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使在大规模视觉语言预训练时代，OOD（分布外）概念识别仍是未解决的挑战；模型能力需按概念类别与难度分层评估，而非仅依赖聚合指标。

Abstract: In the era of large-scale pretrained models, zero-shot generalization has increasingly blurred the boundary between known and unknown concepts. However, current evaluations do not directly reveal which open-world concepts different vision-language models can recognize. To study this problem, we introduce OODConceptBench, a hierarchical benchmark for open-world concept recognition on image-concept pairs. The full benchmark set (L1) contains 30,752 images, and the harder subset (L2) contains 14,285 images. The benchmark covers 560 concept phrases across seven open-world categories. We evaluate 37 vision-language models across release year, model family, and variant. We obtain three main findings. First, target-concept recognition is lower on L2 than on L1 across all 37 evaluated models. Second, later-released models do not necessarily achieve stronger open-world concept recognition. Third, an increase in overall recognition does not imply consistent improvement across all benchmark categories. Together, these results show that OOD remains a challenge even in the era of large-scale vision-language pretraining.

</details>


### [63] [Distilling Dense Trajectory Teachers For Large-Motion Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33566)
*Shuofeng Sun,Linqing Zhao,Haibin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DTTD后训练框架，用模型自身在稠密轨迹上的可靠生成作为教师，结合几何感知奖励与累积课程，将视频世界模型的生成能力从稠密小步轨迹迁移到大步稀疏相机运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型主要用帧间相机运动小的密集采样视频训练，推理时面对稀疏、大步长轨迹会出现冻结帧、相机运动不准和严重几何漂移；作者将其归因于训练时的渐进视角变化与推理所需的大视角变化之间的失配。

Method: 给定稀疏轨迹，插值额外相机位姿构造覆盖同一路径的稠密轨迹，由冻结基座模型在该稠密轨迹上生成可靠教师视频；用位姿对齐的教师观测评估原始稀疏轨迹下生成的候选视频，使模型成为自身的生成教师，无需额外视频数据或更强的外部生成器。进一步将稠密教师监督与评估跨帧一致性和轨迹跟随的几何感知奖励结合，形成偏好用于离线偏好优化；并引入累积课程，逐步纳入更大运动步长同时保留较简单轨迹以减少能力遗忘。

Result: 摘要未提供定量实验结果；方法上的预期效果是缓解稀疏大步轨迹下的冻结帧、相机运动不准确与几何漂移，并在稳定扩展运动范围的同时减轻能力遗忘。

Conclusion: 通过自蒸馏+几何感知偏好优化+累积课程的后训练方案，可把视频世界模型从熟悉的稠密轨迹推广到大幅相机运动，且不依赖额外数据或外部更强生成模型。

Abstract: Video world models are expected to generate videos that accurately follow specified camera trajectories while maintaining consistent 3D structures. However, existing models are predominantly trained on densely sampled videos with small inter-frame camera motions. When applied to sparse, large step trajectories, they often produce frozen frames, inaccurate camera motion, and severe geometric drift. We attribute this degradation to a mismatch between the gradual viewpoint transitions seen during training and the large viewpoint changes required at inference time. To address this problem, we propose Dense Trajectory Teacher Distillation (DTTD), a post-training framework that transfers a model's generation capability from dense trajectories to large camera motions. Given a sparse trajectory, DTTD interpolates additional camera poses to construct a dense trajectory covering the same path. The frozen base model then generates reliable teacher videos along this familiar dense trajectory, and pose aligned teacher observations are used to evaluate candidate videos generated under the original sparse trajectory. In this way, the model serves as its own generation teacher, requiring neither additional video data nor a stronger external video generator. We further combine dense teacher supervision with geometry aware rewards that assess cross frame consistency and camera trajectory following, and use the resulting preferences for offline preference optimization. To stably expand the model's motion range, we introduce a cumulative curriculum that progressively incorporates larger motion steps while retaining simpler trajectories to reduce capability forgetting.

</details>


### [64] [CADonatello: Towards Real-World Multi-Part 3D CAD Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33575)
*Ahmet Serdar Karadeniz,Dimitrios Mallis,Parsa Akbari Chianeh,Kseniya Cherenkova,Anis Kacem,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CADonatello，一个面向真实三维扫描的部件感知参数化 CAD 重建框架：先借助基础级 3D 部件分割模型将输入几何分解为结构上有意义的组件，再对每个组件通过“截面驱动的草图参数化 + 拉伸”流水线独立重建；同时构建 CC3D Cross-Sections 数据集以实现从干净 CAD 草图图像到扫描截面图像的域适应，从而提升对含噪多部件对象的泛化与重建可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 3D 扫描的 CAD 重建方法在标准基准上表现良好，但在真实场景（多部件对象、含噪声等扫描伪影）中效果显著下降。根本原因在于现有 CAD 重建数据集以单部件模型和干净几何输入为主，限制了模型向真实场景的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 使用基础级（foundation-scale）3D 部件分割模型将输入几何分解为结构上有意义的组件；2) 对每个组件独立执行截面驱动（cross-section-driven）流水线：先进行草图参数化，再执行拉伸（extrusion）以得到参数化 CAD 模型；3) 构建 CC3D Cross-Sections 数据集，包含真实扫描的栅格化截面图像与来自对齐 CAD 模型的对应参数化草图的成对样本，用于域适应。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法提升了对含噪扫描的泛化能力，并能在多个真实世界 CAD 逆向工程基准上可靠地重建复杂多部件对象。

Conclusion: 通过部件分解与截面驱动重建相结合，并辅以面向扫描截面的域适应数据集，CADonatello 有效缓解了合成—真实域差异带来的性能退化，为真实扫描条件下的多部件参数化 CAD 逆向重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing methods for CAD reconstruction from 3D scans achieve strong performance on standard benchmarks, yet their effectiveness often degrades in real-world scenarios where objects consist of multiple parts and scan artifacts such as noise. A key reason is that current CAD reconstruction datasets predominantly contain single-part models and clean geometric inputs, limiting the ability of learned models to generalize to realistic settings. In this work, we introduce CADonatello, a part-aware framework for reconstructing parametric CAD models from real-world 3D scans. Our approach first decomposes the input geometry into structurally meaningful components using a foundation-scale 3D part segmentation model, and then reconstructs each component independently through a cross-section-driven pipeline that performs sketch parameterization followed by an extrusion. To improve the robustness of sketch parameterization to scan-derived cross-sections, we further introduce CC3D Cross-Sections, a dataset of paired rasterized cross-section images from realistic scans and their corresponding parametric sketches derived from aligned CAD models. This dataset enables effective domain adaptation from clean CAD sketch images to scan-derived cross-section images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach improves generalization to noisy scans and enables reliable reconstruction of complex multi-part objects across multiple real-world CAD reverse engineering benchmarks.

</details>


### [65] [ZoomSEM: accelerating large-scale brain connectomics with intelligent SEM super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33580)
*Jiateng Shou,Zhiwei Xiong,Ruikang Xu,Zhicheng Wang,Peiyao Shi,Te Shi,Haiqun Jin,Chunying Yin,Jun Guo,Manfred Auer,Hanchuan Peng,Ruobing Zhang,Feng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于深度学习的SEM超分辨方法ZoomSEM，用真实低/高分辨配对训练，实现4×、8×、16×放大并保持连接组学所需的精细神经结构，最高约30倍有效加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 串行电镜是大体积生物成像与连接组学的关键手段，但通量是主要瓶颈，立方毫米级脑成像需数月乃至数年；现有深度学习超分方法受限于小放大倍数或依赖合成数据，加速有限、实用价值不足。

Method: ZoomSEM在真实SEM低分辨率与高分辨率图像对上训练，从快速低分辨扫描中恢复4×、8×乃至16×高分辨图像，并在多类生物样本数据集上验证其对真实低分辨采集条件的泛化能力。

Result: 可可靠恢复高分辨图像且保留连接组分析关键的精细神经元结构，实现最高约30倍的有效加速；跨多样生物样本数据集表现出稳健泛化。

Conclusion: 该方法使高通量、大体积、高分辨率串行电镜成像成为可能，有望推动连接组学及相关领域发展。

Abstract: Serial electron microscopy (EM) is essential for large-volume biological imaging and connectomics, yet its throughput remains a major bottleneck, with cubic-millimeter-scale brain imaging requiring months or years. Existing deep learning-based EM super-resolution methods are limited to small upscaling factors or rely on synthetic data, offering modest acceleration and limited practical impact. We introduce ZoomSEM, a deep learning-based super-resolution method to dramatically accelerate SEM imaging. Trained on real low- and high-resolution scanning electron microscopy (SEM) pairs, ZoomSEM enables reliable recovery of 4$\times$, 8$\times$, and up to 16$\times$ higher-resolution images from fast low-resolution scans while preserving fine neuronal structures critical for connectomic analysis, achieving up to 30-fold effective acceleration. Extensive experiments across datasets of diverse biological samples demonstrate robust generalization under realistic low-resolution acquisition. This advance enables high-resolution, large-volume serial EM imaging at unprecedented speed, poised to transform connectomics and related fields.

</details>


### [66] [UltraFlash: Accelerating Megapixel Visual Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33581)
*Phuc Lai,Anh Nguyen,Phong Nguyen,Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraFlash 通过优化级联扩散中的像素空间转移（VAE 反复解码/编码）来加速百万像素级图像生成：Latent Hyperloop 用学习到的潜空间捷径替代中间 RGB 往返，Sparse Patch-based Decoding 用统计量缓存实现稀疏分块解码，在 4K 生成上约提速 2 倍且质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型在远超训练分辨率的百万像素合成中依赖级联精化，每阶段需像素空间分辨率转换加潜空间去噪。已有加速工作集中于降低去噪成本，而作者指出一个互补且被忽视的瓶颈：表示反复经由 VAE 移动（像素空间转移）——中间潜变量需解码为 RGB、缩放再重编码，最终潜变量还需高重叠分块解码以抑制边界伪影，这些 VAE 操作开销大。

Method: 提出 UltraFlash，包含两个组件：(1) Latent Hyperloop：用学习到的潜空间捷径近似 decode-resize-encode 变换，避免中间过程的重复 VAE 解码与编码；(2) Sparse Patch-based Decoding：针对 GroupNorm 统计量不一致导致的分块接缝问题，跨块复用缓存统计量，从而以更小重叠实现稀疏 VAE 解码，加速最终重建。

Result: 集成到先进级联基线后，UltraFlash 将 4K 生成延迟降低约 2 倍，同时图像质量有所提升。

Conclusion: 优化像素空间转移可同时提升效率与保真度，是通向快速、高质量百万像素视觉合成的有效路径；表明除去噪成本外，VAE 相关转移开销是值得关注的加速方向。

Abstract: Megapixel visual synthesis with latent diffusion models requires operating far beyond the resolutions at which these models are typically trained. Cascade refinement is a leading strategy for this setting: it starts from a base image at a native resolution and progressively upsamples it to the target resolution through multiple refinement stages. Each stage typically performs a pixel-space resolution transition followed by latent-space denoising. While prior acceleration efforts mainly focus on reducing denoising cost, we identify a complementary and underexplored bottleneck: the repeated movement of representations through the VAE. We call these operations pixel-space transitions. In cascade pipelines, intermediate latents are often decoded to RGB, resized, and re-encoded before refinement, while final latents are decoded patch by patch with dense overlap to suppress boundary artifacts. We propose UltraFlash, a transition-efficient approach that accelerates these VAE-heavy operations. UltraFlash has two components. Latent Hyperloop replaces intermediate RGB round trips with a learned latent-space shortcut that approximates the decode-resize-encode transformation without repeated VAE decoding and encoding. Sparse Patch-based Decoding accelerates final reconstruction by addressing patch seams caused by inconsistent GroupNorm statistics and reusing cached statistics across patches, enabling sparse VAE decoding with much smaller overlap. Integrated into a state-of-the-art cascade baseline, UltraFlash reduces 4K generation latency by approximately 2x while improving image quality. These results show that optimizing pixel-space transitions can improve both efficiency and fidelity, offering an effective route toward fast, high-quality megapixel visual synthesis.

</details>


### [67] [Mix Forcing: Causal Pre-Training via Mixture Attention for Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33589)
*Zelin Gao,Hao Ouyang,Jiapeng Zhu,Qiuyu Wang,Jingye Chen,Zichen Liu,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Mix Forcing 因果预训练范式与 MoBA 注意力，将双向视频扩散模型转化为高质量自回归交互式视频世界模型，支持一小时以上长时序生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型需要自回归生成，但直接把预训练双向视频扩散模型用教师强制改造成自回归模型会破坏其视觉先验，导致自回归生成质量下降。

Method: 提出 Mix Forcing：在自注意力中同时进行教师强制训练与双向训练，前者学习因果的 next-chunk 预测以支持自回归推演，后者保留预训练视觉先验；并设计 chunk-wise 交叉注意力，每个视频块同时条件于共享环境提示与自身局部提示，避免未来指令泄漏。基于 14B 图像到视频模型进行因果预训练后，用一致性蒸馏与分布匹配蒸馏得到四步自回归生成器。

Result: 在内部基准及 WBench、SANA-WM-Bench 等公开基准上取得最优视觉质量，事件跟随及时且相机控制精确；在一小时以上的 rollout 中保持高保真长时序生成且无明显质量退化。

Conclusion: 混合教师强制与双向注意力可在不牺牲预训练视觉先验的前提下获得强因果初始化，配合蒸馏实现少步自回归生成，为长时交互式视频世界模型提供了可行范式。

Abstract: While interactive video world models require autoregressive generation, directly adapting pretrained bidirectional video diffusion models into autoregressive ones with teacher forcing can disrupt their visual priors and degrade autoregressive generation quality. In this paper, we introduce Mix Forcing, a causal pre-training paradigm for interactive video world models, implemented with the Mixture-of-Bidirectional-and-Autoregressive (MoBA) attention. Mix Forcing jointly trains a video diffusion model with both teacher forcing and bidirectional training, yielding a strong causal initialization for few-step post-training. In self-attention, Mix Forcing leverages teacher forcing to learn causal next-chunk prediction for autoregressive rollout, while using bidirectional attention to preserve pretrained visual priors. To support online text interaction, Mix Forcing further adopts a chunk-wise cross-attention design that conditions each video chunk on a shared environment prompt and its own local prompt, without exposing future instructions to the current chunk. Following causal pre-training on a 14B image-to-video model, we apply consistency and distribution matching distillation to obtain a four-step autoregressive generator. Experiments on our internal benchmark and public benchmarks, including WBench and SANA-WM-Bench, show that our model achieves state-of-the-art visual quality and timely event following with precise camera control. Notably, our model supports high-fidelity long-horizon generation without quality degradation even over one-hour rollouts. Project page is here: https://zelingao98.github.io/mixforcing

</details>


### [68] [Allocation Before the Clock: Graph, Set and Game Theory of Professional League of Legends Objective Setups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33626)
*Zheng Gao,Zhenyu Liu,Yifan Yang,Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将《英雄联盟》中立目标前的团队布防建模为"有墙地图上的分配问题"，提出 HASSE-QRE 模型（集合划分 + 测地 Voronoi 控制区 + 量子响应均衡的投入博弈），在 MSI/Worlds 2025 的 164 场比赛中验证：落后队伍更常放空坑位，且队伍定价的是战斗、行进与投入人数而非目标本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主导性分析工具（如 CS2 的 TAGNet）依赖图网络嵌入聚类并以簇胜率衡量战术质量，缺乏对"谁去坑位、谁守线、何时投入"这一分配决策的结构化刻画；本文希望用可解释的分配与博弈框架替代纯聚类式评估，并检验职业队是否真的对中立目标本身赋予策略价值。
