Method: 将以坑位为核心的布防形式化为三重结构：(1) 阵型为格 Π₅ 上的集合划分；(2) 控制区为导航图上的测地 Voronoi 划分；(3) 投入决策为以胜率为计价的分配博弈，求解为量子响应均衡（QRE）。数据为 MSI 与 Worlds 2025 全部 164 场、2,994 个团队布防，小地图轨迹与 Riot 官方坐标校验；采用全模型序列 bootstrap 重拟合检验模式稳定性，并以对数似然代价做变量消融。

Result: 落后队伍放空坑位的频率显著高于领先队伍：首个男爵处 +34.8 个百分点，Atakhan 处 +30.4 个百分点。HASSE-QRE 识别出六种可由 bootstrap 重拟合复现的布防模式（ARI 0.819，与 TAGNet 仅 K-Means 步骤的 0.813 相当）。模型表明队伍定价战斗、行进与已投入人数，而非目标本身（权重 0.018；将其固定为 1 损失 59.9 个对数似然点）。均衡仅复现约四分之一的投入差距，且几乎全部经由状态变量而非策略渠道；对团队层面对比按暴露度加权可使结论跨过 p=0.05（0.046 对 0.052）。

Conclusion: 布防行为主要由状态变量（如比分差距）驱动，而非由对中立目标的策略性估值驱动；均衡机制的解释力有限，且显著性结论对暴露度加权敏感。作者公开轨迹、布防数据与代码，为后续可复现的 MOBA 战术分析提供基准。

Abstract: Before every neutral objective in professional League of Legends, each team decides which of its five players walk to the pit, which hold a lane, and when to commit. The leading analysis of such setups, TAGNet for Counter-Strike 2, clusters graph-network embeddings of player positions and reads cluster win rates as tactical quality. We treat a setup as an allocation on a walled map: formations are set partitions on the lattice $Π_5$, control is a geodesic Voronoi split of a navigation graph, and commitment is an allocation game solved as a quantal response equilibrium priced in win probability. On all 164 games of MSI and Worlds 2025 (2,994 team-setups, minimap tracks validated against Riot positions), trailing teams leave the pit empty far more often than leading teams, by +34.8 percentage points at the first baron and +30.4 percentage points at Atakhan. Our model, HASSE-QRE, finds six setup patterns that whole-model series-bootstrap refits reproduce (ARI 0.819, comparable to 0.813 for TAGNet's $K$-Means step alone) and indicates that teams price fights, travel and committed players, not the objective itself (weight 0.018; fixing it to one costs 59.9 log-likelihood points). The equilibrium reproduces a quarter of the commitment gap, almost all of it through state variables rather than its strategic channels. Weighting a team-level contrast by exposure moves it across $p=0.05$ (0.046 against 0.052). We release the tracks, setups and code.

</details>


### [69] [ONOT/TONO-Print: A Paired Benchmark for Identity-Document Portrait Quality under Document Production Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33627)
*Alexander Vinogradov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ONOT/TONO-Print配对基准，用于评估证件制作工艺偏移下的人脸图像质量模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证件人像在制作中被打印、雕刻及模板整合等处理改造，会改变纹理、对比度、色彩等视觉属性，但不一定改变生物特征或呈现信息，现有质量评估模型在此类域偏移下的稳健性缺乏系统评测。

Method: 基于ONOT与TONO人像构建小型配对基准，合成五种制作工艺变体（碳粉、喷墨、染料升华、激光雕刻、热转印），并包含一个由喷墨与碳粉设备打印扫描的实拍子集；据此对比生物特征类与ICAO相关人脸质量组件在制作前后的行为。

Result: 部分质量估计器在制作后出现显著域偏移，另一些模型表现相对稳定。

Conclusion: 证件制作工艺会显著影响部分人脸质量评估模型，需在质量评估中考虑制作域偏移，并提供了可复用的配对评测基准。

Abstract: Identity-document portraits are modified during document production by printing, engraving, and integration into the document template. These transformations may change image texture, contrast, colour, and other visual properties without necessarily changing the underlying biometric or presentation information. In this work, we introduce ONOT/TONO-Print, a small paired benchmark for evaluating face-image quality models under identity-document production shift. The benchmark is based on ONOT and TONO portraits and contains synthetic variants representing five common production techniques: toner, inkjet, dye sublimation, laser engraving, and thermal transfer. A smaller physical subset contains portraits printed and scanned using inkjet and toner devices. The benchmark is used to compare the behaviour of biometric and ICAO-related face-quality components before and after production. Results show that production can introduce substantial domain shift for some quality estimators, while other models remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [70] [DROA-CLIPSeg: Prompt-Guided Thin Crack Segmentation in Low-Light Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33635)
*Vinita Shukla,Partibha Jangra,Vinayak Pathak,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DROA-CLIPSeg：将低光照路面裂缝分割建模为退化感知、提示接地的结构化缺陷分割问题，在冻结 CLIPSeg 上仅训练约1.93M参数（全模型1.3%），即可超越9个零样本与4个微调视觉语言基线，并零样本泛化到 MVTec AD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照路面裂缝分割受对比度下降、阴影、车灯眩光、接缝、污渍和湿缝干扰而退化；现有裂缝分割方法多假设光照良好，低光照增强方法虽提升视觉质量却不显式保持裂缝拓扑结构。

Method: 1) QKV 低秩适配（LoRA）注入视觉与文本编码器的 Q/K/V 投影，提升裂缝特定的图文对齐且不微调骨干；2) 域残差正交适配器（DROA）将中间特征分解为主裂缝域分支（捕捉重复性低光照裂缝模式）与正交残差分支（保留被噪声、阴影或过度平滑抑制的微弱、细薄、断裂证据）；3) 构建 CrackLingo-9K 与 CrackLingo-12K，即 Crack9k/Crack12k 的裂缝感知提示文本增强版本。

Result: 仅训练约1.93M参数（占全模型1.3%）条件下，DROA-CLIPSeg 在两个数据划分上均优于9个零样本与4个微调视觉语言基线；可零样本泛化至 MVTec AD，跨纹理与缺陷形态有效；并有大量消融实验支撑。

Conclusion: 退化感知 + 提示接地的参数高效适配范式可在不微调骨干的前提下显著提升低光照裂缝分割性能与跨域泛化能力；代码已开源。

Abstract: Pavement crack segmentation under low light is degraded by reduced contrast, shadows, headlight glare, joints, stains, and wet seams. Existing crack segmentation methods mostly assume good illumination. Low-light enhancement methods improve visual quality, but they do not explicitly preserve crack topology. To address these limitations, we formulate low-light crack segmentation as a degradation-aware, prompt-grounded structural defect segmentation problem and propose DROA-CLIPSeg, a parameter-efficient framework that adapts a frozen CLIPSeg vision language backbone using two lightweight modules. QKV Low-Rank Adaptation is injected into the Q/K/V projections of the vision and text encoders to improve crack-specific image text alignment without backbone fine-tuning. The proposed Domain Residual Orthogonal Adapter (DROA) decomposes intermediate features into a dominant crack-domain branch, which captures recurring low-light crack patterns, and an orthogonal residual branch, which preserves faint, thin, and fragmented evidence usually suppressed by noise, shadow, or over-smoothing. We also introduce CrackLingo-9K and CrackLingo-12K, text augmented versions of Crack9k and Crack12k with crack-aware prompts. Training only approximately 1.93 M parameters, corresponding to 1.3% of the full model, DROA-CLIPSeg outperforms 9 zero-shot and 4 fine-tuned vision language baselines on both splits, generalises zero-shot to MVTec AD across textures and defect morphologies, and is supported by extensive ablations. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/VShuklacode/DROA-CLIPSeg

</details>


### [71] [UniQuery: Unifying Vision Models with a Joint Query Head for Cross-Domain Pedestrian Attribute Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33639)
*Nguyen Dang Duy Tan,Linh T. T. Tran,Huynh Nguyen Phat,Vo Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniQuery，联合微调 DINOv3 与 CLIP 实现属性特定融合的行人属性识别方法，挑战赛隐藏测试集 Challenge_Avg 达 0.8001。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域细粒度属性识别受遮挡、外观变化与图像分布偏移影响；现有多基础编码器集成以固定融合权重拼接 logits，所有属性共享同一混合方式且编码器从未联合训练，缺乏属性自适应性。

Method: 联合微调 DINOv3 与 CLIP，设计两个各对应一个编码器的 query head，外加一个 joint query head，其属性 query 同时关注两个编码器的 token；最终预测为三个 query head 的 logits 平均。在 UPAR Challenge 2027（WACV 2027 Real-World Surveillance workshop）基准上，用 PA100k、PETA、Market-1501 的共享属性集训练，采用 leave-one-dataset-out 评测。

Result: 在两个留出数据集上，UniQuery 的 Challenge_Avg 均优于独立集成；消融显示对独立训练编码器仅学习融合权重相对 logit 平均无提升，冻结编码器会降低 Challenge_Avg；隐藏测试集 Challenge_Avg 为 0.8001。

Conclusion: 属性特定融合需与编码器联合微调才有效，单纯学习融合权重或冻结编码器收益有限；UniQuery 在挑战赛基准上取得 0.8001 的 Challenge_Avg。

Abstract: Pedestrian Attribute Recognition (PAR) predicts semantic attributes of individuals, such as gender, age, clothing and accessories, and its descriptions support attribute-based person retrieval and person re-identification. However, accurately recognizing fine-grained attributes across domains remains challenging due to occlusion, appearance variations, and shifts in image distributions. Ensembles of independently trained foundation encoders combine their logits with fixed fusion weights, so every attribute uses the same mix and the encoders are never trained together. We propose UniQuery, which instead jointly fine-tunes DINOv3 and CLIP for attribute-specific fusion. It combines two query heads, one for each encoder, with a joint query head whose attribute queries attend to tokens from both encoders, and the prediction is the average of the logits of the three query heads. We evaluate UniQuery on the benchmark of the UPAR Challenge 2027 at the WACV 2027 Real-World Surveillance workshop, where models are trained on PA100k, PETA and Market-1501 with a shared set of attributes. In leave-one-dataset-out experiments, UniQuery outperforms the independent ensemble in Challenge_Avg on both held-out datasets. Ablations show that learning fusion over independently trained encoders does not improve upon logit averaging in the mean over the two held-out datasets, while freezing the encoders lowers the Challenge_Avg of UniQuery. On the hidden test set of the benchmark, UniQuery achieves a Challenge_Avg of 0.8001.

</details>


### [72] [LENS: Likelihood Evaluation and Neighbor Smoothing for Attribute-Based Person Retrieval on Unseen Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33641)
*Nguyen Dang Duy Tan,Vo Thanh Nguyen,Linh T. T. Tran,Huynh Nguyen Phat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LENS 通过精确查询的似然打分、属性组互斥正则与免训练 kNN 平滑，提升属性行人检索在未见相机上的 mADM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性行人检索按语义属性二值查询对监控图像排序，但在未见相机上预测概率发生偏移，导致 mADM 下降。常见流程用 L1 距离排序，无法区分"单个属性高置信度不匹配"与"多属性小不确定度"；而基于源域验证数据的后处理重校准因逐属性映射与新相机不匹配而无法迁移。

Method: 提出 LENS（Likelihood Evaluation and Neighbor Smoothing）：微调 DINOv3 与 CLIP 模型并蒸馏学生模型；训练中用组交叉熵对互斥属性组正则化；推理时按精确查询的对数似然对候选打分；并用同一次前向得到的 DINOv3 外观特征做近邻上的 logit 平滑（免训练 kNN）。

Result: 在留出数据集上三项改动均提升 mADM：似然打分使主集成模型全部六项检索指标提升，并使全部 48 个开发模型 mADM 提升；免训练 kNN 平滑在 46 个测试的开发模型上均有效。UPAR 2027 隐藏测试集上 LENS 达到 68.17 mADM。

Conclusion: 以似然替代 L1 距离并结合属性组约束与邻居平滑，可在无需目标域重校准的情况下稳健迁移到未见相机，验证了各设计的独立增益。

Abstract: Attribute-based person retrieval ranks surveillance images from a binary query of semantic attributes, but mean Average Attribute Degree of Match (mADM) drops on unseen cameras as predicted probabilities shift. Common pipelines rank gallery images by their L1 distance to the query, which penalizes a confident single-attribute mismatch no more than small uncertainties across many attributes, while post-hoc recalibration fitted on source validation data fails to transfer because the per-attribute maps fitted on source data do not match those of new cameras. We propose LENS (Likelihood Evaluation and Neighbor Smoothing), combining fine-tuned DINOv3 and CLIP models with a distilled student. LENS regularizes mutually exclusive attribute groups with group cross-entropy in training, scores gallery images by the log-likelihood of the exact query, and smooths gallery logits over nearest neighbors using DINOv3 appearance features from the same forward pass. On held-out datasets, all three changes raise mADM: the likelihood score improves all six retrieval metrics for the main ensemble and mADM for all 48 development models, and training-free kNN smoothing improves all 46 tested development models. On the hidden test set of the UPAR 2027 benchmark, LENS reaches 68.17 mADM.

</details>


### [73] [PreView: Enhancing GUI Grounding with Preprocessed Views and Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33645)
*Wenjun Hou,Kaishuai Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PreView：一种无需再训练的无参数微调式辅助预处理器，通过生成任务相关视图与候选特定线索改进已有 GUI grounding 模型的输入，在五个基准上提升多个模型的平均定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounding 需在杂乱截图中定位小控件并判断自然语言指令指向哪个控件；裁剪与放大虽能暴露局部细节，但仍可能存在目标歧义，且现有 grounding 模型受限于输入质量。

Method: 提出 PreView 辅助预处理器，不训练原 grounding 模型。它准备两类互补输入特征：（1）用于定位的任务相关视图——由训练好的轻量辅助模型预测并精化中心点，裁剪出局部视图并放大，用于缩放式 grounding；（2）用于目标理解的候选特定线索——视觉线索（带位置提示的红圈标记）与描述候选身份或功能的文本线索。冻结的 grounding 模型基于这些准备好的输入预测最终点击位置。

Result: 在五个基准上，PreView 在不再训练 grounding 模型的前提下，提升了 Qwen3-VL-2B、OS-Atlas-4B 和 Qwen3-VL-4B 的平均定位准确率；并通过大量分析考察了视图与线索的贡献、中心精化以及视觉输入预算的影响。

Conclusion: PreView 作为一种即插即用的输入侧预处理方案，可在不微调 grounding 模型的情况下有效提升 GUI grounding 精度，验证了任务相关视图与候选特定线索的互补价值。

Abstract: GUI grounding requires locating small controls in cluttered screenshots and identifying which control a natural-language instruction refers to. Cropping and enlargement can expose local detail, but the resulting image may still leave ambiguity about the intended target. We propose PreView, an auxiliary preprocessor that improves the inputs to an existing grounding model without further training that model. PreView prepares two complementary types of input features: task-relevant views for localization and candidate-specific cues for target understanding. A trained lightweight auxiliary model predicts and refines a center, crops the screenshot into a local view, and enlarges that view for zoomed-in grounding. It also prepares a visual cue (i.e., a red-circle marker with a location hint) and a textual cue describing the candidate's identity or function. The frozen grounding model uses the prepared inputs to predict the final click. Across five benchmarks, PreView improves the average grounding accuracy of Qwen3-VL-2B, OS-Atlas-4B, and Qwen3-VL-4B without further training these grounding models. Extensive analyses examine the contributions of views and cues, center refinement, and visual input budgets.

</details>


### [74] [FreeView: Robust 3D Reconstruction from Uncurated Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33656)
*Junho Kim,Hyunyoung Choi,Seongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeView：免训练框架，从无筛选图像集合中联合选择过滤参考视图并剔除干扰视图，结合视觉与几何证据实现稳健3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无筛选图像集合中存在无关或不重叠视图；现有前馈重建模型虽有内部视图一致性信号可支持干扰剔除，但参考条件过滤依赖人工精选的干净参考视图，实际场景难以保证。

Method: 利用集合级共识识别代表视图作为可靠过滤参考；针对每个输入集合，融合互补的视觉与几何证据自适应地进行视图剔除；可即插即用于VGGT与pi^3，无需人工参考或手动校准剔除阈值。

Result: 多数据集实验显示高干净视图保留率与干扰剔除率，在显著外观与视角变化下仍保留有用视图，实现可靠的3D重建。

Conclusion: FreeView以免训练、自动化的方式消除了对精选参考与人工校准的依赖，为无筛选视图集合的稳健三维重建提供了通用方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing a coherent 3D scene from uncurated image collections remains challenging due to the presence of irrelevant or non-overlapping views. Recent work has shown that feed-forward reconstruction models contain internal view-consistency signals that support distractor rejection. However, reference-conditioned filtering relies on a curated clean reference, which is not guaranteed in practical image collections. To address this limitation, we introduce FreeView, a training-free framework that jointly selects a filtering reference and rejects distractor views from an uncurated collection. FreeView leverages collection-level consensus to identify a representative view that serves as a reliable reference for filtering. It then adapts view rejection to each input collection by combining complementary visual and geometric evidence. This approach preserves useful views under substantial appearance and viewpoint variations while rejecting distractors. FreeView integrates with both VGGT and pi^3, removing the need for a manually curated reference or manual calibration of view rejection. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate high clean-view retention and distractor rejection rates, leading to reliable 3D reconstruction from uncurated views.

</details>


### [75] [Tracking Is Not Sensing: Artifact-Aware Thermal Video Respiration Estimation in Head-Free Marmosets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33661)
*Penghao Wang,Sen Yang,Fenghua Xie,He Wang,Xindong Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出用热成像视频从头部自由活动的普通狨猴中非接触估计呼吸率，指出"视觉可靠的跟踪≠生理有效的测量"，并提出选择性时间衰减正则化（STAR）以抑制高运动伪影，达到2.23 bpm的MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通狨猴是社会神经科学的重要非人灵长类模型，呼吸率可反映唤醒、应激与生理调节状态。接触式传感器虽准确但准备负担大且干扰头部自由的行为范式；热视频提供非接触替代方案，但狨猴头部自由运动下呼吸相关温度信号微弱、局部化，且易受头动、姿态变化与毛发干扰。

Method: 使用同步热成像视频与接触式呼吸记录作为对照；分析显示狨猴呼吸可从热视频恢复但高度依赖ROI选择；随后改为从热成像片段中提取学习到的时序信号来估计呼吸率，并提出STAR（选择性时间衰减正则化）：训练时通过局部时间插补选择性衰减高运动的温度不连续段，使模型正则化到与呼吸一致的动态，而推理阶段对原始视频不做改动，并在多种深度学习骨干网络上验证。

Result: 在头部自由场景下，视觉上看似合理的鼻部跟踪仍会产出与接触呼吸不一致的曲线，证明"跟踪并非传感"；STAR在多个骨干网络上均改善估计性能，MAE达2.23 bpm，而基于跟踪ROI的分析为20.76 bpm。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了视频生理传感中跟踪可信度与测量有效性之间的本质差距，并表明在头部自由条件下，基于学习时序信号结合STAR正则化可显著优于传统ROI跟踪流程，实现可靠的狨猴呼吸率非接触监测。

Abstract: Continuous physiological monitoring is essential for linking behavior, internal state, and neural activity in neuroscience and translational research. In common marmosets, a key nonhuman primate model for social neuroscience, respiratory rate provides a basic readout of arousal, stress, and physiological regulation. Contact sensors can provide accurate measurements, but they add preparation burden and may interfere with head-free behavioral protocols. Thermal video offers a noncontact alternative by capturing breathing-related temperature changes around the nose and mouth. However, in head-free marmosets, these cues are weak, localized, and easily corrupted by head motion, pose changes, and fur. Here we show that this setting exposes a broader challenge in video-based physiological sensing: visually reliable tracking does not necessarily imply physiologically valid measurement. Using synchronized thermal video and contact respiration recordings, we show that marmoset respiration is recoverable from thermal video but strongly ROI-sensitive. Under head-free motion, visually plausible nasal tracking can still produce traces inconsistent with contact respiration, demonstrating that tracking is not sensing. We therefore estimate respiratory rate from learned temporal signals extracted from thermal clips and introduce Selective Temporal Attenuation Regularization (STAR). STAR selectively attenuates high-motion thermal discontinuities through local temporal imputation during training, regularizing models toward respiration-consistent dynamics while leaving inference unchanged on raw video. Across multiple deep-learning backbones, STAR improves head-free estimation, reaching 2.23 bpm MAE compared with 20.76 bpm for tracked ROI analysis.

</details>


### [76] [Rescuing Your Pruning: Rethinking Visual Token Pruning and Post-Training Recovery in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33688)
*Peiran Yin,Hao Wu,Zihao Yang,Haozhe Hu,Xudong Wang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统研究视觉token剪枝后的后训练恢复能力与联合压缩策略，发现后训练可挽回大部分性能损失，且编码器侧剪枝对端到端加速更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法多为training-free、即插即用，将性能下降视为压缩的必然代价，并一味设计更复杂的剪枝规则。作者质疑该观点，提出两个问题：损失中有多少可通过后训练挽回；在恢复基础上如何通过联合压缩将剪枝推到极限。

Method: 构建282K样本的后训练混合数据集，覆盖通用VQA、推理、OCR与grounding；在统一协议下对36种drop/merge token配置进行后训练；并分析编码器与LLM在原生分辨率MLLM预填充阶段的耗时占比，比较不同剪枝位置与保留比例的联合压缩方案。

Result: 后训练可挽回大部分损失，包括OCR与grounding任务，且预算越紧时其缩小剪枝模型与稠密模型差距的适应增益越大。原生分辨率MLLM中预填充时间由视觉编码器而非LLM主导，因此剪枝位置比剪枝数量更重要：编码器保留40% token比投影层保留10%更快。最佳配置为编码器轻剪、投影层激进剪、LLM不动，通用理解与推理保持稠密水平；LLM阶段加速3.49x，但端到端首token时间仅加速1.54x。

Conclusion: 后训练应作为token剪枝的组成部分而非事后补救；联合压缩需优先考虑编码器侧的token削减；LLM阶段加速比会高估端到端收益，为后续联合视觉token压缩研究提供参考，数据、协议与代码将公开。

Abstract: Visual token pruning substantially reduces the inference cost of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet existing methods are mostly training-free and plug-and-play. They treat the resulting performance drop as an inevitable cost of compression and pursue increasingly complex pruning rules. We rethink this view through two questions: how much of the loss can post-training rescue, and, building on this recovery, how can joint compression push pruning to its limit? We curate a 282K-sample post-training mixture covering general VQA, reasoning, OCR, and grounding, and post-train 36 configurations that drop or merge tokens under a unified protocol. Post-training rescues most of the loss, even for OCR and grounding, and its adaptation gain in closing the gap between the pruned and the dense model grows as the budget tightens. For joint compression, we find that the vision encoder, not the LLM, dominates prefill time in native-resolution MLLMs, so where tokens are removed matters more than how many: keeping 40% of tokens at the encoder runs faster than keeping 10% at the projector. Our best configuration prunes the encoder lightly and the projector aggressively, leaves the LLM intact, and keeps general understanding and reasoning at the dense level. It accelerates the LLM stage by 3.49x but the end-to-end time-to-first-token by only 1.54x, showing that LLM-stage speedups overstate end-to-end gains. We hope these findings offer insights for future research on joint visual token compression. Our data, protocol, and code will be released.

</details>


### [77] [PathKGen: Pathology Knowledge Assisted MLLM for Explainable Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33691)
*Nadun Rajapaksha,Sandun Herath,Devnith Wijesinghe,Isiri Amani Withanawasam,Hasindri Watawana,Ranga Rodrigo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PathKGen——一种知识图谱辅助的多模态大语言模型，用于组织病理全切片图像（WSI）报告生成，通过可见、可解释的知识推理增强MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM仅依赖神经参数中的分布进行推理，受模型规模限制，所含知识与推理逻辑有限；且黑箱推理难以被人类解读。知识图谱可部分外化医学知识的承载，但在病理报告生成领域尚缺乏探索。

Method: PathKGen含两条分支：(1) 视觉路径，利用病理基础模型学习输入MLLM的视觉token；(2) 知识路径，通过查询病理专用知识图谱提取WSI关键形态学特征，学习知识token输入。该模块化知识路径与视觉对齐MLLM互补，可通过构建领域知识图谱扩展至其他应用。

Result: 在WSI-Bench数据集上全面评估，PathKGen超越此前SOTA方法，并以视觉直观的图形式揭示诊断推理过程。

Conclusion: 引入知识图谱外化结构化医学知识并显式化推理组件，可有效提升病理报告生成的性能与可解释性，且知识路径具良好模块化与可迁移性。

Abstract: Whole Slide Image (WSI) analysis serves as the fundamental method for the clinical diagnosis of cancer in digital histopathology. Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have enabled state-of-the-art (SOTA) automated histopathology report generation. However, MLLMs reason purely through distributions encoded in their neural parameters, limiting the amount of knowledge and reasoning logic they can contain given the model scale. Their black-box reasoning poses another challenge to the human interpretation of intermediate reasoning. We address these issues by utilizing knowledge graphs to inject external structured knowledge for MLLMs to reason over, while making this reasoning component visible. Knowledge graphs are capable of partially externalizing the containment of medical knowledge, an area that remains underexplored in pathology report generation. We introduce PathKGen, a knowledge graph-assisted MLLM for histopathology report generation. PathKGen has two main branches: (1) a visual path that learns visual token inputs to the MLLM using pathology foundation models, and (2) a knowledge path that learns knowledge token inputs by extracting key morphological features of WSIs through querying a pathology-specific knowledge graph. A comprehensive evaluation on WSI-Bench dataset shows that PathKGen surpasses previous SOTA methods while revealing diagnostic reasoning as visually intuitive graphs. Due to its modular design, the proposed knowledge path is complementary to vision-aligned MLLMs and can be extended to other applications by curating domain-specific knowledge graphs to generate knowledge tokens.

</details>


### [78] [Scalable Unrolled Chains for MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33695)
*Emiljo Mëhillaj,Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种内存高效的展开变分网络训练框架：随机采样单个级联并回传最终重建损失，配合轻量反向传播与时间条件共享网络，使训练内存与链长解耦，在fastMRI膝/脑重建上优于其他局部训练方法，部分设定超过E2E-VarNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 展开变分网络在加速MRI重建上性能领先，但级联数量与宽度增加时，标准端到端训练的内存开销变得难以承受，限制了模型规模扩展。

Method: 1) 每次迭代仅更新随机采样的一个级联，但仍以最终重建损失作为监督；2) 采用"激活轻量"的反向传播，将误差信号从链末端传到被采样级联，不存储整链激活，从而解耦训练内存与链长；3) 使用跨级联共享的时间条件网络，使参数量与链长无关；4) 近似仅来自冻结的归一化统计量，并通过训练精确梯度对照实验验证其影响。

Result: 在标准端到端反传耗尽GPU显存的链长与级联宽度下仍可训练；fastMRI单线圈膝与多线圈脑重建上持续优于其他局部训练方法；高SNR膝切片在8×超过E2E-VarNet、4×持平；完整脑验证集8×超过、4×相当，且参数量更少、峰值内存更低、每轮迭代训练更快。

Conclusion: 通过随机级联采样、激活轻量反传与时间条件参数共享，可在不牺牲重建质量的前提下显著降低展开MRI网络的训练内存并支持更长更宽的级联，是一种可扩展的展开网络训练方案。

Abstract: Unrolled variational networks achieve state-of-the-art performance for accelerated MRI reconstruction, but standard end-to-end training becomes prohibitively memory intensive as the number and width of cascades increase. We propose a memory-efficient training framework that updates a randomly sampled cascade while retaining supervision from the final reconstruction loss. The error signal is propagated from the end of the chain to the sampled cascade using an activation-light backward pass that avoids storing full chain activations, thereby decoupling training memory from chain length. A time-conditioned network shared across cascades further keeps the parameter count independent of chain length. The backward pass is approximate only through frozen normalization statistics, and a trained exact gradient control shows the approximation leaves reconstruction quality unchanged. The framework remains trainable at chain lengths and cascade widths where standard end-to-end backpropagation exhausts GPU memory. On fastMRI single-coil knee and multi-coil brain reconstruction, our method consistently outperforms other local training approaches. On high-SNR knee slices, our method surpasses E2E-VarNet at $8\times$ and matches it at $4\times$, while on the complete brain validation set it surpasses E2E-VarNet at $8\times$ and achieves comparable performance at $4\times$, with fewer parameters, lower peak memory, and faster per-iteration training. Code is available at https://github.com/emehillaj/scalable-unrolled-mri.

</details>


### [79] [E3tereo: Improving Stereo Matching at Depth Boundaries with Learned Geometric Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33704)
*Qi Xiong,Qinghua Zhao,Yongqiang Xi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: E3Stereo 通过从视差监督中学习几何边缘，并将其与主干视差及图像细节结合以预测全分辨率视差修正，从而显著降低深度边界处的视差误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度边界是立体匹配的主要误差来源：在 Scene Flow 上 IGEV 的几何边缘像素仅占评估像素的 6%，却贡献了约一半的总绝对视差误差；而光度边缘可能由纹理变化引起、不对应深度不连续，是边界精化的不可靠线索。

Method: 提出 E3Stereo：从视差监督中学习几何边缘；将该边界线索与主干视差及图像细节结合，预测全分辨率视差修正；同一边缘图在训练中用于加权强调边界误差，并通过联合优化使边缘预测器自适应视差精化。

Result: 在 Scene Flow 上，跨 RAFT-Stereo、IGEV、MonSter 三种主干，深度边界处 EPE 降低 9.5%–14.3%，整体 EPE 亦下降；微调后，基于 IGEV 的模型在 ETH3D、KITTI 2012、KITTI 2015 的全部十一项报告指标上均优于 IGEV。

Conclusion: 以几何边缘为显式边界线索并进行联合优化，可有效且通用地改善立体匹配的边界与整体精度，且方法可插拔于多种主干网络。

Abstract: Depth boundaries remain a major source of error in stereo matching. For IGEV on Scene Flow, geometric edge pixels account for only 6% of evaluated pixels but about half of the total absolute disparity error. Photometric edges can respond to texture changes without corresponding depth discontinuities, making them unreliable cues for boundary refinement. We propose E3Stereo, which learns geometric edges from disparity supervision and combines these boundary cues with the backbone disparity and image details to predict full-resolution disparity corrections. The same edge map also emphasizes boundary errors during training, while joint optimization allows the edge predictor to adapt to disparity refinement. On the Scene Flow dataset, E3Stereo reduces end-point error (EPE) at depth boundaries by 9.5%-14.3% across RAFT-Stereo, IGEV, and MonSter, while also reducing overall EPE. After fine-tuning, our IGEV-based model improves upon IGEV on all eleven reported metrics across ETH3D, KITTI 2012, and KITTI 2015. The source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [80] [Traffic-Context-Aware Driver Emotion Recognition with Expectation-Deviation-Driven Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33713)
*Longhao Yan,Jinhao Liang,Kaidi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出交通情境感知的驾驶员情绪识别框架TADER，将舱内面部信号与舱外交通场景结合，以"期望偏差"（预测与观测车辆轨迹之差）建模情绪，在AIDE和manD数据集上准确率提升5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶员情绪识别方法主要依赖舱内图像，忽视了周围交通交互对驾驶员情绪反应的影响，缺乏对情绪成因的外部情境建模。

Method: TADER包含两部分：(1) 基于视觉的基础识别器，从舱内面部图像提取时空表征推断情绪；(2) 交通情境感知融合模块，从舱外交通场景量化期望偏差，并通过注意力嵌入融合将其注入基础识别器。为缓解舱内外配对数据稀缺，采用迁移学习，将大规模自然驾驶数据集上训练的轨迹预测模型迁移至小型配对数据集。

Result: 在自然驾驶与模拟驾驶环境中，TADER均持续优于现有最先进方法；在AIDE和manD数据集上准确率提升5%。

Conclusion: 引入舱外交通情境信息可有效提升驾驶员情绪识别性能，验证了以期望偏差刻画情绪成因的合理性。

Abstract: Driver emotion provides an important indication of how drivers perceive and evaluate ongoing traffic situations, making reliable emotion recognition an important capability for effective human-vehicle interaction. Yet, most existing driver emotion recognition approaches rely primarily on in-cabin images and overlook how surrounding traffic interactions shape driver emotional responses. To address this limitation, we propose a traffic-context-aware driver emotion recognition (TADER) framework that integrates in-cabin signals with out-of-cabin traffic scenarios, modeling driver emotions as arising from expectation deviation represented by the discrepancy between predicted and observed vehicle trajectories. The proposed framework comprises two key components: (1) a vision-based base recognizer that extracts spatiotemporal representations from in-cabin facial images to infer driver emotions, and (2) a traffic-context-aware fusion module that quantifies expectation deviation from external traffic scenarios and integrates it into the base recognizer via attention-based embedding fusion. To overcome the scarcity of paired in-cabin and out-of-cabin data, we introduce a transfer learning strategy that transfers a trajectory prediction model trained on large-scale naturalistic datasets to smaller paired datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TADER consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across both naturalistic and simulated driving environments by incorporating traffic context information into emotion recognition, resulting in improvements of 5% in accuracy on the AIDE and manD datasets.

</details>


### [81] [A SegFormer-based model for spatial Daylight Factor prediction with comprehensive obstruction encoding and regulatory validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33737)
*Stanislava Fedorova,Alejandro Pacheco Diéguez,Christoph Berkmiller,Alberto Floris,Libny Pacheco,Marie-Claude Dubois*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种在BIM环境内直接预测采光系数(DF)空间分布的机器学习方法，秒级输出、无需模型导出或人工整理，并在真实建筑上达到可替代初步采光模拟的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅采光合规评估依赖耗时的光线追踪模拟与专业模型准备，难以融入迭代式建筑设计流程。现有模型未能同时覆盖五项关键因素：外部遮挡(含阳台)编码、自身建筑高度、可变材料反射率、非正交房间几何、以及法规验证。

Method: 构建两个模型：固定反射率的默认材料模型(用于初步设计校核)与项目特定可变反射率的自定义材料模型。二者均以约20,000个Radiance合成模拟房间训练，并在留出合成数据集及18个真实建筑模型的102个房间上验证；目标为DF空间分布图预测，直接嵌入BIM。

Result: 默认材料模型合成数据δ₁=83.4%、MAE=0.11% DF；真实建筑上瑞典BBR合规面积MAE 4.85 pp(偏差+2.31 pp)，挪威TEK17房间平均DF MAE 0.304% DF(偏差+0.287% DF)。自定义材料模型误差高1.7–2.4倍但明显集中于BBR合规临界区(DF<1%)，该区MAE 0.105% DF、合规IoU 0.883；所有高估合规案例均为临界情形(距BBR阈值≤10.7 pp，中位3.2 pp；距TEK17阈值≤0.36% DF，中位0.10% DF)。

Conclusion: 默认材料模型可推荐替代初步采光模拟；自定义材料模型尚需改进，暂不宜用于最终合规核验。

Abstract: Daylight compliance assessment in residential buildings requires time-consuming raytracing simulations and specialist model preparation, limiting its integration into iterative architectural design. We present a machine learning-based methodology for predicting the spatial distribution of the Daylight Factor (DF) directly within Building Information Modelling (BIM) environments, delivering results in seconds without model export or manual curation. The approach addresses five gaps not jointly covered by any existing model: external obstruction encoding including balconies, own building height, variable material reflectances, non-orthogonal room geometries, and regulatory validation. Two models are developed: a default-materials model with fixed reflectances for preliminary design checks, and a custom-materials model with variable project-specific reflectances. Both are trained on about 20,000 synthetic Radiance-simulated rooms and validated on a held-out synthetic dataset and 102 rooms from 18 real building models. The default-materials model achieves $δ_1$ = 83.4% and MAE = 0.11% DF on synthetic data, and on real buildings predicts BBR (Swedish) compliance area within 4.85 pp MAE (bias +2.31 pp) and TEK17 (Norwegian) mean room DF within 0.304% DF MAE (bias +0.287% DF). The custom-materials variant shows 1.7-2.4$\times$ higher error across all metrics. Both models concentrate accuracy in the BBR compliance-critical zone (DF < 1%), achieving a sub-1% DF MAE of 0.105% DF and Compliance IoU of 0.883 on real buildings. All cases where compliance is overestimated are borderline, within 10.7 pp of the BBR threshold (median 3.2 pp) and 0.36% DF of the TEK17 threshold (median 0.10% DF). The default-materials model is recommended as a replacement for preliminary daylight simulations; the custom-materials variant requires further development before use in final compliance verification.

</details>


### [82] [Computer Vision Navigation for an Autonomous Vehicle Model Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33755)
*Dinis Fernandes,Tiago Ribeiro,António Ribeiro,Carolina Lopes,Gil Lopes,A. Fernando Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估近端策略优化（PPO）在仿真道路环境中控制车辆速度与前轮转向的能力，输入为车载摄像头图像，经特征提取后由策略输出后轮转速与转向角指令，策略迭代更新后可在简化环境中完成车道导航，并给出提升鲁棒性的改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建适用于测试先进强化学习算法的仿真环境本身复杂困难，需要一个可供其他开发者复用的框架，同时验证PPO这类算法在端到端视觉车辆控制任务上的可行性与相对传统算法的实现优势。

Method: 在仿真环境中训练与测试PPO算法：以车辆摄像头采集的图像帧为输入，经特征提取模块处理后送入策略网络，输出后轮旋转速度（控制车速）与前轮转向角（控制方向）；策略按帧迭代更新以优化性能。PPO采用近端裁剪（proximity/clipping）区间约束策略更新幅度，超参数调优负担小于前代算法。

Result: 强化学习算法成功生成了能够在简化仿真环境中完成道路线内导航的策略；在某些场景下PPO表现优于实现更复杂、需调更多超参数的前代算法。

Conclusion: PPO可有效用于基于视觉的车辆速度与转向控制。作者指出系统在更复杂场景下的鲁棒性仍需改进，所搭建的仿真环境与流程可作为通用框架，供后续研究者测试不同方法与算法。

Abstract: This work aims to assess the capabilities of the reinforcement learning algorithm, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), in developing a policy capable of controlling the speed and steering angle of a vehicle model to navigate within simulated road lines.
  The control system's input is a camera, and its outputs are the rotational speed of the rear wheels and the steering angle of the front wheels, controlling the vehicle's speed and direction, respectively. The algorithm is trained and tested in a simulated environment, where it receives image frames from the vehicle's camera, processes them through a feature extraction algorithm, and feeds them into the policy. For each frame, the policy outputs speed and direction commands for the vehicle. The policy is iteratively updated to enhance its performance.
  The reinforcement learning algorithm successfully generates a policy capable of navigating the vehicle in a simplified environment. Potential improvements are suggested to enhance the system's robustness in more complex scenarios.
  Creating a simulation environment suitable for testing an advanced reinforcement learning algorithm is a complex task. This project offers a framework for other developers to test different approaches and algorithms. The tested algorithm, PPO, uses proximity intervals - an innovative approach in reinforcement learning. In some scenarios, the algorithm achieved better performance than predecessor algorithms, which are typically harder to implement and require tuning more hyperparameters.

</details>


### [83] [Road Infrastructure Perception for Autonomous Driving: Taxonomy, Representations, Tasks, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33758)
*Guoyang Zhao,Weiqing Qi,Kai Zhang,Zhihai Bi,Tianshuai Hu,Zeying Gong,Dezong Zhao,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述以“功能导向”视角系统整合道路基础设施感知研究，提出几何-结构、功能-空间、法规-语义三类基础设施分类，并梳理感知模态、场景表征、方法与评测，指出当前基准在关系推理、时序一致性、鲁棒性和规划导向评估方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路基础设施感知是自动驾驶的基础，但车道检测、可行驶区域估计、交通标志识别、在线地图构建等任务在任务定义、传感假设、输出表征、数据集与评测协议上高度碎片化，缺乏统一的功能性框架来整合各任务、表征与评测。

Method: 综述性方法：首先界定道路基础设施感知的范围，将现有研究归纳为几何-结构、功能-空间、法规-语义三类互补基础设施；据此系统回顾传感模态、场景表征、代表性方法、基准数据集与评测协议，并分析表征选择对评测与下游可用性的影响。

Result: 梳理出领域从图像空间识别向度量级、矢量化、拓扑感知、占据栅格化及规则约束的场景建模演进；揭示现有基准在关系推理、时序一致性、鲁棒性与规划导向评估上的局限；提出统一基础设施表征、开放环境泛化、关系与规则感知推理、下游导向评测等未来方向。

Conclusion: 通过在共同功能框架下连接任务、表征与评测，该综述将道路基础设施感知确立为面向地图兼容、行为相关的自动驾驶场景理解的连贯基础。

Abstract: Road infrastructure perception is a fundamental component of autonomous driving, as it recovers the structured road elements that define roadway geometry, delimit operational space, and encode traffic regulations. Although individual tasks such as lane detection, drivable-space estimation, traffic-sign recognition, and online map construction have been extensively studied, the field remains fragmented across task definitions, sensing assumptions, output representations, datasets, and evaluation protocols. This survey provides a function-oriented synthesis of road infrastructure perception. We first clarify its scope and organize existing studies into three complementary categories: geometric-structural, functional-spatial, and regulatory-semantic infrastructure. Based on this taxonomy, we review sensing modalities, scene representations, representative methods, benchmarks, and evaluation protocols, emphasizing the field's evolution from image-space recognition toward metric, vectorized, topology-aware, occupancy-based, and rule-grounded scene modeling. We further examine how representation choices affect evaluation and downstream usability, revealing limitations of current benchmarks in relation reasoning, temporal consistency, robustness, and planning-oriented assessment. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions toward unified infrastructure representation, open-environment generalization, relation- and rule-aware reasoning, and downstream-oriented evaluation. By connecting tasks, representations, and evaluation under a common functional framework, this survey aims to establish road infrastructure perception as a coherent foundation for map-compatible and behavior-relevant autonomous-driving scene understanding.

</details>


### [84] [C-DFF: Concept-Level Diagnostics for Mechanism Parity in Simulation-Based Validation of Autonomous Driving Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33760)
*Danial Safaei,Siddartha Khastgir,Matthew Higgins,Jeroen Ploeg,Mohsen Alirezaei,Son Tong,Ludo Gielen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出概念级机制一致性诊断方法 C-DFF，用于成对真实-合成图像评估，揭示输出一致并不等于系统依赖相同视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真验证中，真实与合成图像的任务输出匹配并不能保证系统依赖相同的视觉证据，尤其在安全关键的感知功能上存在迁移验证缺口。

Method: 从冻结的被测系统（SUT）中提取决定性证据，借助现成分割模型将其投影到共享语义概念空间，比较真实与合成的概念重要性向量，输出标量机制一致性得分及概念漂移诊断，标出哪些场景概念在仿真中变得更重要或不重要。

Result: 在 KITTI ↔ Virtual KITTI 2 上以 2126 对匹配样本评估三种冻结 SUT（转向回归、YOLOP 可行驶区域、YOLOP 车道线）。C-DFF 具备 SUT 判别性，能给出可解释的概念级摘要，支撑验证分诊与仿真器调试；与任务输出数值指标并列报告时表明输出一致本身不能确立机制一致性。

Conclusion: C-DFF 可作为基于仿真的自动驾驶测试验证流程中的实用语义诊断层。

Abstract: Simulation is widely used in autonomous-driving validation, but matching task outputs between real and synthetic images does not guarantee that a system relies on the same visual evidence. This creates a validation gap for transfer, especially in safety-critical perception functions. We present Concept-Decisive Feature Fidelity (C-DFF), a concept-level mechanism-parity diagnostic for paired real-synthetic evaluation. Starting from decisive evidence extracted from a frozen system-under-test (SUT), C-DFF projects that evidence into a shared semantic concept space using off-the-shelf segmentation and compares real and synthetic concept-importance vectors. The result is not only a scalar mechanism-parity score, but also a concept-shift diagnostic that identifies which scene concepts become more or less decisive in simulation. We evaluate C-DFF on KITTI $\leftrightarrow$ Virtual KITTI 2 with 2126 matched pairs across three frozen SUTs (steering regression, YOLOP drivable-area, YOLOP lane-line). Across these tasks, C-DFF is SUT-discriminative and yields interpretable concept-level summaries that support validation triage and simulator debugging. When reported alongside task-specific output-value context metrics, C-DFF shows that output agreement does not by itself establish mechanism parity. These results position C-DFF as a practical semantic diagnostic layer for validation and verification workflows in simulation-based AV testing.

</details>


### [85] [Segmentation of Moving Objects During Camera Motion Using the OWL Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33789)
*Daniel Raviv,Juan D. Yepes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出基于OWL感知函数的解析框架，从单目运动中的逐点平移方向聚类实现多个独立运动刚体的分割，无需训练、深度或语义先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动分割多依赖学习、深度信息或语义先验；作者希望利用OWL中looming与rotation两个运动线索的闭式结构，得到一个纯解析、无需训练的逐点多刚体分割判据。

Method: OWL将感知looming L与感知旋转ω编码为相对3D结构的闭式表示，由此逐图像点可解析恢复相对平移单位方向t̂；同一非旋转刚体上各点共享t̂，不同刚体取值不同，故对逐点t̂估计聚类即完成分割。基于OWL的2D复数形式，用四元数将框架推广到3D单相机运动情形。

Result: 给出可行性与推导：推导出t̂的逐点解析恢复公式及基于t̂一致性的分割准则，并在3D下以四元数完成单运动相机的情境建模；框架具备并行性、相机旋转不变性，且无需训练数据、深度信息与语义先验。

Conclusion: OWL的闭式结构足以支撑独立运动刚体的解析分割，为免训练、旋转不变的单目多刚体运动分割提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present a principled, analytical framework for segmenting multiple independently moving rigid objects during single camera motion, built directly upon the OWL perception function. OWL encodes two fundamental visual motion cues, perceived looming $L$ and perceived rotation $ω$, into a single closed-form representation of relative 3D structure. A key consequence of this structure is that the unit direction of relative translation $\hat{t}$ is analytically recoverable per image point from $L$ and $ω$ alone. All points belonging to a non-rotating rigid body share the same $\hat{t}$, whereas points on different rigid bodies generally yield distinct values. This furnishes a natural segmentation criterion: clustering per-point $\hat{t}$ estimates partitions the visible scene into independently moving rigid bodies. Building on the 2D complex form of OWL, we derive the framework in 3D using quaternions, for a single moving camera. The approach is parallel, camera rotation-invariant, and requires no training data, no depth information, and no semantic priors.

</details>


### [86] [Whispers from the Neighbors: Rethinking Caption Consensus as Inference-Time Evidence for Vision-Language Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33798)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Belal Shoer,Abhirama Subramanyam Penamakuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结双编码器在推理时利用同一图像的多条邻近描述（multiple captions）的收益，发现融合图像相似度与对图像自身描述相似度可显著提升文本到图像检索R@1（同源标注者3.4–16.8点），收益依赖描述来源；并揭示"未解释残差"评分的表面增益主要源于支持集构建方式差异而非真正的新信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索基准与很多数据集为每张图附有多条描述，但双编码器仍孤立地判断每个图文对，忽略了邻近描述的潜在信息。作者想探究：在编码器冻结的情况下，推理期引入这些邻近描述究竟能带来多少收益、收益从何而来。

Method: 提出在推理时融合图像相似度与"对图像自身描述集合"的相似度（captions consensus）；在九个冻结编码器上评测不同描述来源（同源标注者、现成captioner、不同标注源、复述训练文本编码器）的影响；进一步分析"对描述未解释的图像残差部分打分"这一细化方案，在留一法（leave-one-out）下分解增益来源（支持集大小、查询移除），并在固定支持索引下考察其等价性；最后在组合性基准上分析机制。

Result: 同源标注描述使T2I R@1提升3.4–16.8点；现成captioner仅1.9–7.2点；不同标注源至多1点；复述训练文本编码器强化文本侧。残差评分在LOO下看似增益更大，但该优势源于正确候选与负候选的支持集构建方式不同——支持集大小与查询移除各贡献约一半；在固定支持索引下，残差退化为对已有描述信息的重加权。组合性基准上，描述帮助在于将候选与人类描述匹配，而非提升图文grounding。

Conclusion: 邻近描述是廉价且有效的推理期信号，但其收益高度依赖描述与查询的来源一致性；看似新颖的残差打分实为评估设置造成的假象（重加权）。研究强调需谨慎区分真实增益与评估伪迹，为利用多描述检索提供了诊断性结论。

Abstract: An image is rarely described only once. Retrieval benchmarks and many collections attach several captions to each picture, yet dual encoders still judge every image-caption pair in isolation. We ask what these neighboring captions contribute when a frozen encoder consults them at inference time. Fusing image similarity with similarity to an image's own captions improves text-to-image R@1 by 3.4 to 16.8 points across nine frozen encoders when the captions come from the same annotators as the queries. The gain falls to 1.9 to 7.2 points when an off-the-shelf captioner writes them, and to at most one point when they come from a different annotation source; a paraphrase-trained text encoder strengthens the text side further. A natural refinement, scoring the part of the image that the captions leave unexplained, appears to add much more under a leave-one-out evaluation. We show that this advantage arises because the correct candidate's supports are built differently from those of the negative candidates, with set size and query removal each contributing about half, and that under a fixed support index the residual reduces to a reweighting of what the captions already provide. On compositional benchmarks, the captions help by matching candidates to human descriptions of the image, without improving image-text grounding. The code is available at: https://github.com/nhatkhangcs/clip_consensus

</details>


### [87] [ProMo3D: Probing Motion Cues in Frozen 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33802)
*Xiaoang Zhang,Mert Kiray,Yordanka Velikova,Manoj Biswanath,Benjamin Busam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于探针的运动蒸馏（PMD），利用冻结的3D基础模型token中的低维运动线索，以轻量读出实现数据高效的动态4D运动分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D基础模型已革新多视图重建，但动态4D理解仍依赖大规模运动监督或针对特定架构的手工启发式规则。作者质疑是否需要高容量解码器重新学习运动特征，探索冻结3D基础模型token是否已蕴含可被轻量读出函数揭示的运动相关信息。

Method: 对三个代表性骨干做探针实验，验证静态-动态可分性可从冻结token表示中解码；对π³X进行秩约束与谱分析，发现运动线索集中于低维子空间。据此提出PMD：将针对固定锚点token的成对运动状态兼容性得分转换为稠密运动掩码，仅在160个YouTube-VOS视频、单张16GB GPU上训练。

Result: PMD无需目标基准微调即可零样本迁移至DAVIS、SegTrackv2和FBMS-59；搭配多视图预训练几何骨干时，优于免训练启发式方法，并接近更重的有监督与自监督方法。

Conclusion: 冻结3D基础模型中的运动解码可以是轻量且数据高效的，运动相关信息以低维形式内嵌于token表示中，无需大规模运动监督即可实现有效动态分割。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models have recently reshaped multi-view reconstruction, yet dynamic 4D understanding still often relies on large-scale motion supervision or hand-crafted, architecture-specific heuristics. We ask whether frozen 3D foundation model tokens already encode motion-relevant information that can be exposed by lightweight readout functions, rather than re-learning motion-specific features with high-capacity decoders. Through probing on three representative backbones, we find that static--dynamic separation is decodable from frozen token representations. Our rank-restricted and spectral analyses on $π^3$X reveal that the recovered motion cues are concentrated in a low-dimensional subspace. Building on this, we propose Probing-based Motion Distillation (PMD), which converts pairwise motion-state compatibility scores against a fixed anchor token into dense motion masks. Trained on only 160 YouTube-VOS videos using a single 16 GB GPU, PMD transfers zero-shot to DAVIS, SegTrackv2, and FBMS-59 without target-benchmark fine-tuning. With multi-view pretrained geometric backbones, PMD outperforms training-free heuristics and approaches substantially heavier supervised and self-supervised methods, suggesting that motion decoding from frozen 3D foundation models can be lightweight and data-efficient.

</details>


### [88] [Grounded Exploration for Open-Ended Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33810)
*Jieyu Zhou,Mehdi Ben Ayed,Shubham Gupta,Zhenyu Tan,Jiajing Xu,Dmitry Kislyuk,Eric Tzeng,Christopher J. MacLellan,Charles Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架：先识别多样且可行的编辑范围，再将其实现为可执行的编辑指令，从而在开放式图像编辑中兼顾歧义保留与指令落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式图像编辑请求（如"装饰这个房间"）存在多种合理解读。直接采样会带来缺乏依据的随机变化，而显式多样性提示往往只产生表面差异。作者希望在不提前消除合理歧义的前提下，对这类请求进行落地（grounding）。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段识别多样化、可行的编辑范围（edit scopes）；第二阶段将这些范围实现为可执行的编辑指令（executable edit instructions）。

Result: 在300个开放式图像编辑任务上，相比直接多样性提示，平均语义分散度提升24.6%，平均落地性（groundedness）提升17.1%，并产生更多有效且互异的实现结果。

Conclusion: 在实现之前先结构化可行编辑方向，能够支持更有用、更有依据的探索性生成。

Abstract: Open-ended image-editing requests, such as ``decorate this room,'' admit multiple valid realizations. Direct sampling may produce ungrounded variation, whereas explicit diversity prompting often produces superficial differences. We study how to ground these requests without prematurely eliminating their legitimate ambiguity. We introduce a two-stage framework that first identifies diverse, feasible edit scopes and then realizes them as executable edit instructions. Across 300 open-ended image-editing tasks, our approach achieves 24.6% higher mean semantic dispersion and 17.1% higher mean groundedness than direct diversity prompting, while producing more valid and distinct realizations. These results show that structuring feasible edit directions before realization supports more useful grounded exploration.

</details>


### [89] [Grounding on Request: Learning Modality Control in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33841)
*Maxime Dassen,Alexander Martin,Andrew Yates,Reno Kriz,William Walden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出以冲突保留的反事实干预（Flip/Stay）评估VLM的模态接地控制，并通过学习模态新词嵌入与反事实孪生边际（CTM）监督来训练这种控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅看准确率无法判断模型究竟遵循了所请求的模态证据，还是凭参数知识答对；需要区分"答对"与"按指定模态作答"。

Method: 构建冲突保留的反事实干预指标：Flip衡量初始正确答案是否随被请求模态的编辑而翻转，Stay衡量竞争模态被编辑时答案是否稳定。沿用新词学习思路，为视觉与文本接地各引入新词，仅训练其嵌入而冻结预训练模型；并提出反事实孪生边际（CTM）监督，用成对干预教导答案概率应如何随所请求证据变化。在3个VLM、3个现有基准及自建多属性Ground-CF基准上评测，并测试真实新闻视频。

Result: 学习到的模态词相比自然语言指令提升了控制力；每种模态仅需单个学习嵌入即可提供强且轻量的视觉控制，在留出值上平均退化很小；LoRA总体最强，但其视觉优势在留出评估下收窄；反事实监督在标准监督较弱的若干设置中提升了控制；在新闻视频中，当相关证据更易获取时视觉控制提升。

Conclusion: 可靠的模态控制不仅需检验答案是否正确，还需检验其是否持续跟随所选模态的编辑；冲突保留的反事实评估与轻量嵌入方法为模态接地控制提供了有效诊断与改进路径。

Abstract: When an image and its caption disagree, can a user reliably control which one a vision-language model (VLM) follows? Accuracy alone cannot answer this question, since a model might give the correct answer from parametric knowledge without following the requested evidence. We therefore evaluate modality grounding control through conflict-preserving counterfactual interventions. Specifically, Flip measures whether an initially correct answer follows an edit to the requested modality, while Stay measures whether it remains stable when the competing modality is edited. To learn this control, we build on neologism learning by introducing new words for visual and textual grounding, training only their embeddings while keeping the pretrained model fixed. We also introduce Counterfactual Twin-Margin (CTM) supervision, which uses paired interventions to teach how answer probabilities should change when the requested evidence changes. Across three VLMs on three existing benchmarks and our multi-attribute Ground-CF benchmark, we find that learned modality words improve control over natural-language instructions. Surprisingly, a single learned embedding per modality provides strong lightweight visual control and shows little average degradation on held-out values. LoRA remains strongest overall, although its visual advantage narrows under held-out evaluation. Moreover, counterfactual supervision improves control in several settings where standard supervision is weaker. Finally, we test our method on real-world news video and find that visual control improves when the relevant evidence is made easier to access. Overall, our results show that reliable modality control requires testing not only whether an answer is correct, but whether it continues to follow edits to the modality of choice.

</details>


### [90] [TrackTok: Object-Centric Video Tokenization with Semantically Persistent Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33857)
*Nhi Pham,Christopher Wewer,Bernt Schiele,Jonas Fischer,Jan Eric Lenssen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackTok是一种面向对象、跨时间一致的视频tokenization框架，将同一语义对象在多帧中绑定为稳定token，从而以更少token实现高效视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于patch或整体（holistic）的tokenizer产生的token身份通常绑定固定图像位置，即使对象移动、形变或被遮挡，token也无法对应同一语义对象，导致潜在表示冗余、token数量庞大，制约下游生成建模效率。

Method: 提出TrackTok框架，学习时间持续的对象中心表征，将跨帧视觉特征绑定为稳定语义单元，生成对象对齐、跨时间一致的token序列，形成更紧凑的连续潜在视频表示；并将其与先进流模型结合用于下游生成建模。

Result: 在UCF-101类别条件生成上，以8倍更少潜在token达到可比的FVD且延迟更优；在Kinetics-600帧预测任务上，于低token预算条件下优于现有1D tokenization基线。

Conclusion: 对象对齐且时间持续的视频tokenization在质量-效率权衡上具有优势，是面向可扩展视频生成的有前景方向，减少潜在token数量对高效建模至关重要。

Abstract: We introduce TrackTok, an efficient video tokenization framework that produces object-centric tokens, i.e., tokens that consistently encode the same semantic objects across time, even as it moves, deforms, or undergoes partial occlusion. Conventional patch-based or existing holistic tokenizers produce token identities often tied to fixed image locations. In contrast, our approach learns temporally persistent object-centric representations that bind visual features across frames into stable semantic units. This enables substantially more compact, continuous latent video representations, reducing the number of tokens required for downstream generative modeling with state-of-the-art flow models, while preserving the semantic structure needed for high-quality synthesis. Our tokenizer offers a favorable quality-efficiency trade-off. TrackTok achieves comparable class-conditional generative FVD on UCF-101 dataset at 8 times fewer latent tokens and better latency, and outperforms existing 1D tokenization baselines on frame prediction task on Kinetics-600 across low token budgets. These results suggest that object-aligned and temporally persistent tokenization is a promising direction for scalable video generation, where reducing latent token counts is critical for efficient modeling. Our code for this work is available at https://github.com/phamleyennhi/tracktok.

</details>


### [91] [Late Breaking Results: Audio-Language-Action Models with Direct Speech Conditioning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33866)
*Enfu Nan,Pu Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Lin Zhao,Juyi Lin,Chen Wang,Weiwei Chen,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Speech-$\pi_{0.5}$ 直接将原始语音映射为电机指令，跳过显式文本转写，在保持 LIBERO 任务成功率的同时把响应延迟降至 ASR 流水线的 1/3，并对未见口音具备鲁棒泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型只能接受键入文本指令，限制了在自然语音场景下的部署；而在 VLA 前串联语音识别（ASR）的传统方案会引入额外延迟并造成误差传播。

Method: 基于 $\pi_{0.5}$ 构建：采用跨模态投影将语音帧直接转换为音频 token；采用拆分式 LoRA 适配（独立的音频适配器与任务适配器），并配合两阶段训练。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上响应延迟比 ASR 流水线低 $3\times$，任务成功率保持可比；对未见口音泛化稳健，而单一适配器在相同条件下性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 语音到动作的端到端直接映射可有效规避 ASR 级联的延迟与误差传播问题；拆分 LoRA 设计是提升语音泛化能力的关键。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable robots to follow instructions in typed text, limiting broader deployment in natural speech. The traditional solution by prepending a speech recognition before VLA to convert speech into texts would incur additional latency and propagate errors. To address this, we present Speech-$π_{0.5}$, which maps raw speech directly to motor commands without explicit textual transcriptions. Specifically, built on $π_{0.5}$, we use a cross-modal projection to convert voice frames directly into audio tokens with significant response latency reduction. To further improve the accuracy, we adopt split LoRA adaptation with dedicated audio and task adapters, under a two-stage training. Speech-$π_{0.5}$ achieves $3\times$ lower response latency than ASR pipeline while maintaining competitive task success on LIBERO, and generalizes robustly to unseen accents where a single adapter degrades significantly.

</details>


### [92] [RelateGS: Reliable Semantic and Relational Evidence for 3D Scene Understanding with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33868)
*Seongjun Choi,Sangyoun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RelateGS是一种结构化语言感知高斯表示，在语义抬升阶段抑制不可靠观测，并将多视角物体观测整合为持久3D图，识别欠观测关系并引导对已有信息帧的定向重查询，以补足关系证据，从而提升3D视觉定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言感知3DGS方法虽引入语义与关系线索，但关系感知定位不仅依赖信息表示，还取决于场景侧证据是否可靠充分；多视角语义聚合易引入弱观测，且物体关系在给定相机轨迹下可能欠观测，导致目标消歧证据模糊。

Method: 提出RelateGS结构化语言感知高斯表示：1）在语义特征抬升时选择性抑制不可靠观测；2）将多视角物体观测整合为持久3D图；3）由图识别欠观测的物体关系，并引导对已有信息帧进行定向重查询以补充证据。

Result: 在Sr3D+与Nr3D数据集上取得有竞争力的3D定位性能，Acc@0.25分别较此前最好结果提升7.9和1.7个百分点。

Conclusion: RelateGS通过选择性过滤不可靠语义观测并结合3D关系图引导的定向重查询，有效缓解关系证据不足问题，验证了场景侧证据可靠性与充分性对语言驱动3D视觉定位的重要性。

Abstract: Language-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has advanced language-driven 3D scene understanding by incorporating semantic and relational cues into reconstructed scenes. However, relation-aware grounding depends not only on representing such information, but also on whether the underlying scene-side evidence is reliable and sufficient. Multi-view semantic aggregation can incorporate weak observations, while object relations can remain under-observed along the available camera trajectory, leading to ambiguous evidence for target disambiguation. We present RelateGS, a structured language-aware Gaussian representation that selectively refines semantic and relational evidence for 3D visual grounding. RelateGS suppresses unreliable observations during semantic feature uplifting and consolidates multi-view object observations into a persistent 3D graph. The graph further identifies under-observed object relations and guides targeted re-querying of informative existing frames to complement their evidence. Experiments on Sr3D+ and Nr3D demonstrate competitive 3D grounding performance, improving the previous best by 7.9 and 1.7 percentage points at Acc@0.25, respectively.

</details>


### [93] [Semantic Concept Steering Breaks the Explanation Drift Loop in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33877)
*Yehonatan Elisha,Oren Barkan,Noam Koenigstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出解释感知持续学习中的"自指漂移循环"结构缺陷，提出C³L：用离线发现的类别一致语义概念作为正则锚点，切断漂移循环，并借助LRP相关性守恒产生"挤出效应"抑制虚假背景关联，在五个基准、20+基线上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中的灾难性遗忘不仅表现为精度下降，还表现为"解释漂移"：模型保持预测准确率的同时，注意力从诊断性特征悄然转向虚假相关。已有的像素级显著性正则以模型自身冻结的显著性图为监督目标，构成自指漂移循环，反而跨任务强化虚假注意力。

Method: 提出C³L（Concept-Consistent Continual Learning）。正则锚定到离线发现、独立于模型状态的类别一致语义概念（如"弯曲喙""条纹图案"），从而（i）目标不受模型漂移影响；（ii）同类所有实例间具备跨实例语义一致性。无需人工标注概念集，采用全自动的概念发现与空间定位。结合近似LRP相关性守恒，概念区域与背景区域竞争同一预测的相关性，在概念区域内强制高相关性即隐式压制虚假背景关联（挤出效应），无需显式惩罚。

Result: 在五个基准、20余个基线上的广泛评测显示，在准确率、遗忘程度、解释质量、虚假相关缓解及分布外鲁棒性方面均达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 解释感知持续学习的关键在于正则监督目标的独立性：以离线、类别一致的语义概念替代模型自产显著性图，可同时提升精度保持与解释忠实度，并通过挤出效应无显式惩罚地抑制虚假相关。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting in continual learning (CL) manifests not only as accuracy degradation but also as \emph{explanation drift}: models maintain predictive accuracy while silently shifting attention from diagnostic features to spurious correlations. This work identifies and resolves a previously overlooked structural flaw in explanation-aware CL: pixel-level saliency regularization derives supervision targets from the model's own frozen saliency maps, creating a \emph{self-referential drift loop} that actively reinforces spurious attention across tasks. We introduce \textbf{C$^3$L} (Concept-Consistent Continual Learning), which breaks this loop by anchoring regularization to class-consistent semantic concepts (e.g., ``curved beak'', ``striped pattern'') discovered offline, independent of model state, providing (i) target independence from model drift, and (ii) cross-instance semantic consistency across every instance of a class. We identify that our concept-based objective, combined with approximate LRP relevance conservation, induces a \emph{crowding-out effect}: since concept and background regions compete for the relevance of the same prediction, enforcing high relevance within concept regions implicitly suppresses spurious background correlations without explicit penalization. \method does not require human-annotated concept sets, as it employs a fully automated concept discovery and spatial grounding. Extensive evaluation across five benchmarks and over 20 baselines demonstrates state-of-the-art performance in accuracy, forgetting, explanation quality, spurious correlation mitigation, and out-of-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [94] [WMEval: Evaluating Interactive World Models Grounded on Physical Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33894)
*Lanxiang Hu,Yixin Huang,Abhilash Shankarampeta,Zilin Dai,Yuyuan Wu,Kun Qian,Junze Liu,Arvind Srinivasan,Mingjia Huo,Will Lin,Kai Zhong,Weiqi Zhang,Tian Wang,Qingjun Cui,Tajana Rosing,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WMEval基准，以Unity物理仿真作为高保真真值，从感知层与物理因果层双重评估交互式世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型基准主要关注感知相似度，缺乏对机器人、游戏引擎、数字孪生等高风险应用至关重要的物理因果一致性评估。

Method: 以Unity构建9个多样化场景作为高保真真值预言机；在仿真器与世界模型中执行匹配的动作序列，基于仿真器导出的状态作为参考，从动作可控性、场景与角色一致性、提示遵循、运动学、流体力学、物理因果及光照等维度进行感知级与物理因果级双重评估。

Result: 对当前最先进模型的实验表明，在标准感知指标下无法显现、但对真实部署至关重要的维度上仍存在显著改进空间。

Conclusion: WMEval将交互式世界模型评估从感知视觉层面推进到基于状态的客观验证，为下游应用提供更贴近实用的评测范式；代码已开源。

Abstract: Interactive world models hold immense potential for high-stakes applications in robotics, game engines, and immersive digital twins, yet existing benchmarks primarily focus on perceptual similarity rather than the physical-causal coherence essential for real-world utility. We introduce WMEval, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates interactive world models against Unity-based physical simulations. By leveraging Unity as a high-fidelity ground-truth oracle, we shift evaluation beyond perceptual visual assessment toward the objective state-based verification required for downstream applications. Spanning 9 diverse scenes, WMEval assesses action controllability, scene and character consistency, prompt adherence, kinematics, fluid mechanics, physical causality, and lighting. By executing matched action sequences in both the simulator and the world model, WMEval enables evaluation at both the perceptual level and the physical-causal level, using simulator-derived states as reference. Experiments on state-of-the-art models reveal significant room for improvement in dimensions that remain hidden by standard perceptual metrics but are vital for real-world deployments. Our code is available at: https://github.com/hao-ai-lab/WMEval.

</details>


### [95] [Rank-1 Weight-Corrected Trainable Weight Averaging for Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33909)
*Akito Shinohara,Hiroaki Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将SVD提取的秩一权重主导成分作为权重平均的修正方向，由ID分类目标学习其贡献，从主导成分视角改进OOD检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RankFeat/RankWeight等秩分析表明OOD过度自信与特征空间和权重空间中的低秩主导成分密切相关；权重平均直接在参数空间操作且能提升泛化，因此尝试从主导成分角度将其用于OOD检测；但TWA对每个checkpoint仅分配单一系数，无法独立控制各主导权重成分。

Method: 在TWA基础上，用奇异值分解提取权重矩阵的前若干秩一成分作为修正方向，并通过in-distribution分类目标学习这些方向的贡献系数，实现主导权重成分的独立调节。

Result: 在ResNet50上，使用MSP与RankWeight评分、有无Outlier Exposure设置下，方法在所评估数据集上均优于fine-tuning和标准TWA；Mahalanobis评分结果参差，说明收益依赖OOD评分协议；基于MSP与Energy的目标层分析显示，修正靠近分类器层更有利。

Conclusion: 以SVD主导成分为可学习修正方向的权重平均是改善OOD检测的有效途径，但其效果受评分协议与修正层位置影响，需结合具体OOD评分方式选择配置。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection identifies inputs outside the training distribution. Rank-based analyses such as RankFeat and RankWeight suggest that OOD overconfidence is closely related to dominant low-rank components in feature and weight spaces. Since weight averaging operates directly in parameter space and improves model generalization, we consider it for OOD detection from a dominant-component perspective. Trainable Weight Averaging (TWA) assigns a single coefficient to each checkpoint, limiting independent control of dominant weight components. We extend TWA with leading rank-one weight components extracted by Singular Value Decomposition as correction directions, whose contributions are learned through the in-distribution classification objective. On ResNet50, our method improves over fine-tuning and standard TWA across the evaluated datasets with MSP and RankWeight, both with and without Outlier Exposure. Mahalanobis-based evaluation yields mixed results, indicating that the benefit depends on the OOD scoring protocol. Target-layer analyses with MSP and Energy favor correction near the classifier.

</details>


### [96] [Texture-Aware Strategies for Subregion Style Transfer Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33910)
*Cheng Ji,Yu Zhao,Xiaochong Jiang,Wenting Yang,Shiqi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出纹理感知的子区域风格增强框架，优先替换纹理响应强的区域进行风格迁移，并扩展为“纹理区域+辅助随机区域”的混合区域策略，在分类精度与鲁棒性上优于现有基于风格的增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将风格增强与随机擦除结合的方法，依赖均匀随机选区域或固定几何约束，难以覆盖信息量高的纹理模式，且限制了增强的多样性。

Method: 基于纹理响应强度选择子区域并用风格迁移块替换（纹理感知子区域风格增强）；进一步引入混合区域策略，将纹理驱动区域与一个辅助随机区域组合，隐式带来更多样的风格扰动。

Result: 实验显示该方法取得更高的分类准确率，并相较现有基于风格的增强方法提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以纹理响应引导子区域选择可有效弥补随机/几何约束选区的不足，混合区域策略进一步增强增强多样性与模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: A common strategy for data augmentation is to apply style transfer on training images to enrich appearance variations. Recent approaches have combined style augmentation with random erasing by selectively replacing image subregions with style-transferred patches. However, existing methods rely on uniformly random region selection or fixed geometric constraints, which may fail to cover informative texture patterns and limit augmentation diversity. To address these limitations, we propose a texture-aware subregion style augmentation framework that prioritizes regions with strong texture responses for style replacement. Furthermore, we extend this framework with a hybrid region strategy that combines a texture-driven region with an auxiliary random region, implicitly enabling more diverse style perturbations. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves higher classification accuracy and improves robustness compared with existing style-based augmentation methods.

</details>


### [97] [TurboWAM: Learning What Matters for Fast World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33941)
*Guile Wu,Shuangzhi Li,Xingxin Chen,Chengjie Huang,Sam Hosseini,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurboWAM：一种快速世界动作模型，通过结构化世界表示聚合与结构化动作监督，并结合减少执行层数与跨去噪步表示缓存，实现低推理延迟的机器人策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型在机器人策略学习中表现优异，但推理延迟高，制约实际部署。现有加速方法（减少测试时未来帧想象、加速动作预测）未显式考虑不同视频世界模型层与动作预测的相关性差异，导致冗余计算和表示利用次优。

Method: 提出TurboWAM，包含：1）结构化世界表示聚合模块，依据各层表示与演化中的动作去噪状态的相关性自适应融合不同世界模型层的表示；2）结构化动作监督机制，在反向传播时对不同世界模型层的动作监督梯度进行自适应调制，促进世界表示学习；3）推理阶段减少执行的世界模型层数并跨动作去噪步缓存相关表示以降低计算开销。

Result: 在多个机器人操作基准上，TurboWAM在保持与最先进方法相当的任务成功率的同时，实现了低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过按相关性聚合世界表示并结合推理期层级裁剪与表示缓存，可有效缓解世界动作模型的推理延迟瓶颈，兼顾效率与任务性能。

Abstract: World action models have demonstrated promising performance in robot policy learning, yet their high inference latency remains a critical bottleneck for real-world deployment. Recent approaches have sought to improve inference efficiency by reducing test-time future-frame imagination or accelerating action prediction. However, these methods often leverage representations from all video world model layers without explicitly considering their varying relevance to action prediction, leading to redundant computation and suboptimal representation utilization. In this work, we propose TurboWAM, a fast world action model that efficiently aggregates structured world representations conditioned on action predictions. Specifically, TurboWAM introduces a structured world representation aggregation module that adaptively fuses representations from different world model layers according to their relevance to the evolving action denoising state. Furthermore, TurboWAM employs a structured action supervision mechanism that adaptively modulates gradients from action supervision across different world model layers during backward propagation, facilitating world representation learning. During inference, by reducing the number of world model layers executed and caching relevant representations across action denoising steps, TurboWAM substantially reduces computational overhead. Extensive experiments on multiple robot manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that TurboWAM achieves low inference latency while maintaining competitive task success rates compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [98] [Accuracy Is Not Enough: An Efficiency- and Statistics-Aware Benchmark of ResNet50 and Vision Transformer for Explainable Multi-Class Gastrointestinal Diseases Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33965)
*Akwasi Asare*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在Kvasir八类GI图像分类上，ResNet50与ViT-B/16精度等效（92.12% vs 91.70%，TOST等效检验成立），但ResNet50计算与延迟成本低3–4倍；真正制约部署的是捷径学习与域偏移，而非架构精度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内镜图像自动分类有望支持胃肠道病变的早期诊断，但现有研究往往仅依据准确率选择架构，忽略了效率、校准、捷径学习和跨域泛化等部署关键因素，缺乏受控且可复现的对比基准。

Method: 在公开Kvasir数据集（4,000张图像、八分类）上对ResNet50与ViT-B/16进行受控对比：采用分层划分（测试集400张）及覆盖全部图像的分层分组5折交叉验证；用双单侧检验（TOST）在±3个百分点内检验等效性；比较参数量、运算量、CPU单图延迟与GPU吞吐、交叉验证校准（ECE）；引入边缘导向MobileViT-S进行比较，并做遮挡实验（遮挡ScopeGuide伪影嵌入区）检验捷径学习；在500张带专家掩膜未见息肉图像上用Grad-CAM评估定位（约3.6倍于随机）；在去重后的GastroVision外部子集（1,998张）上评估域偏移下的性能。

Result: 交叉验证中ResNet50与ViT-B/16准确率为92.12%与91.70%，差异+0.43个百分点（90% CI −0.33至+1.18，p<0.001），等效性成立。效率方面ResNet50参数量少3.6倍、运算量少4.3倍、CPU单图延迟低2.5倍（69 vs 177 ms）、GPU吞吐高3.8倍；校准相近（ECE 0.021 vs 0.023）。MobileViT-S在测试划分上最高（95.5%），但遮挡ScopeGuide后下降9.4个百分点，ResNet50无显著影响。两模型Grad-CAM均聚焦病灶（3.6倍于随机）。外部去重GastroVision子集上三模型精度均降至78–80%，相互差异仍很小。

Conclusion: 架构间的精度差异相对于捷径学习和域偏移的影响很小，而效率差异显著；资源受限的筛查场景下高效CNN是合理的默认选择，但任何模型在缺乏本地验证前都不具备部署就绪性。

Abstract: Automated classification of gastrointestinal (GI) findings in endoscopic images could support early diagnosis, but architectures are often chosen on accuracy alone. We present a controlled, reproducible benchmark of ResNet50 and ViT-B/16 for eight-class GI classification on the public Kvasir dataset (4,000 images), using a stratified split (400 test images) and stratified group 5-fold cross-validation over all images. In cross-validation, ResNet50 and ViT-B/16 reached 92.12% and 91.70% accuracy, and a two one-sided tests (TOST) procedure showed equivalence within a +/-3 percentage-point margin (difference +0.43 points, 90% CI -0.33 to +1.18; p < 0.001). The architectures differed mainly in cost: ResNet50 had 3.6 times fewer parameters and 4.3 times fewer operations, 2.5-fold lower single-image CPU latency (69 vs. 177 ms) and 3.8-fold higher GPU throughput. Cross-validated calibration was similar (expected calibration error 0.021 vs. 0.023). An edge-oriented transformer, MobileViT-S, was most accurate on the test split (95.5%) but lost 9.4 points when an acquisition artefact present in some classes (the ScopeGuide inset) was occluded, whereas ResNet50 was not significantly affected. On 500 unseen polyp images with expert masks, both models' Grad-CAM maps focused on the lesion (3.6 times chance). On a de-duplicated external subset of GastroVision (1,998 images), accuracy fell to 78-80% for all three models and differences between them remained small. Accuracy differences between architectures were thus small relative to the effects of shortcut learning and domain shift, whereas efficiency differences were large. An efficient CNN is a defensible default for resource-constrained screening, but no model is deployment-ready without local validation.

</details>


### [99] [Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Network for Single-Shot 3D Reconstruction from Fringe Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33988)
*Xinjie Zhou,XiaoLei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合Mamba-Transformer网络用于单帧条纹图到高度的回归，在单张变形条纹图下取得RMSE 0.548 mm、SSIM 0.9985。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧高频条纹图的FPP三维重建需同时保持长程空间一致性与精细几何细节；卷积网络难以建模长程依赖，而基于窗口的Transformer只能随深度逐步建立全局交互，二者均受限。

Method: 构建基于Swin的U形架构，在中分辨率编码器阶段引入视觉状态空间建模；设计轴向膨胀视觉状态空间（ADVSS）模块，通过局部、膨胀、轴向深度可分离卷积增强预扫描特征编码，同时保留四方向选择性扫描，从而以线性复杂度实现长程建模并保持方向感知的局部条纹结构。

Result: 在公开FPP数据集上，MAE、RMSE、绝对相对误差与SSIM四项指标均优于hNet、SwinConvUNet、UHRNet与HiDNet；仅用一张变形条纹图即达RMSE 0.548 mm、SSIM 0.9985，相较UHRNet与HiDNet分别将RMSE降低65.2%与59.6%。

Conclusion: 将选择性状态空间建模与分层窗口注意力相结合，可有效支撑高精度单帧FPP重建。

Abstract: Deep learning facilitates rapid single shot three dimensional (3D) reconstruction using fringe projection profilometry (FPP), but recovering accurate surface height from a single high frequency fringe image remains challenging because both long-range spatial consistency and fine geometric details must be preserved. Convolutional networks are limited in modeling long range dependencies, whereas window-based Transformers establish global interactions only progressively with increasing depth. To address these limitations, a hybrid Mamba Transformer network is proposed for fringe to height regression. The network adopts a Swin based U shaped architecture and introduces visual state space modeling at the intermediate resolution encoder stage. An axial and dilated vision state space (ADVSS) block is further developed to enhance prescan feature encoding through local, dilated, and axial depthwise convolutions while retaining four directional selective scanning. This design combines linear complexity long range modeling with direction aware preservation of local fringe structures. Experiments on a publicly available FPP dataset show that the proposed method outperforms hNet, SwinConvUNet, UHRNet, and HiDNet in mean absolute error(MAE), root mean square error (RMSE), absolute relative error, and structural similarity index measure (SSIM). Using only one deformed fringe image, the proposed method achieves an RMSE of 0.548 mm and an SSIM of 0.9985, reducing RMSE by 65.2% and 59.6% relative to UHRNet and HiDNet, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining selective state space modeling with hierarchical window based attention for accurate single-shot FPP reconstruction.

</details>


### [100] [Explaining Navigation Decisions: Stage-Aware Visual Attribution for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34028)
*Yiyuan Yao,Xinpeng Ding,Minglei Chen,Fei Gao,Lihuo He,Jie Yuan,Aijun Li,Nannan Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向视觉语言导航(VLN)的阶段感知归因框架，解决现有可解释性方法对指令阶段不敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM驱动机器人执行语言指令导航时，其导航决策的可解释性对可靠部署至关重要。现有可解释性方法忽视了VLN的关键挑战：当前动作的证据可能分散在历史与当前观测中，且对应不同指令阶段；由此导致"指令阶段不敏感"现象——不同导航阶段仍产生相似解释。

Method: (1) 构建以动作为条件、跨观测的时间维度指令—视觉归因；(2) 将归因投影到指令空间以估计阶段关联，并据此引导聚合相关历史证据以精化当前解释；(3) 引入指令引导的空间滤波器，抑制孤立响应同时保留紧凑的指令相关区域。

Result: 在多种VLN策略与基准上，归因质量优于现有方法；可作为失败案例分析的诊断工具；在Unitree G1机器人上验证了真实导航场景的适用性。

Conclusion: 阶段感知归因能有效提升VLN决策解释的质量，兼具诊断分析与真实机器人部署价值。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly enable robots to follow language instructions, understanding their navigation decisions becomes important for reliable deployment. Existing explainability methods overlook a key challenge in Vision-Language Navigation (VLN): evidence for the current action may be distributed across historical and current observations and associated with different instruction stages. We identify instruction-stage insensitivity, where existing methods produce similar explanations despite changes across different navigation stages. To address this issue, we propose a stage-aware attribution framework for VLN. We first construct action-conditioned temporal instruction--visual attributions across observations. These attributions are projected into instruction space to estimate stage associations, which guide the aggregation of relevant historical evidence for refining the current explanation. An instruction-guided spatial filter further suppresses isolated responses while preserving compact, instruction-relevant regions. Experiments across multiple VLN policies and benchmarks show improved attribution quality over existing methods. We further demonstrate that our method acts as a diagnostic tool for analyzing failure cases, and validate its applicability to real-world navigation on a Unitree G1 robot.

</details>


### [101] [Fovea-JEPA: Overcoming Geometric Blind Spots in V-JEPA for Camera Motion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34033)
*Xiangkun Shi,Hanhui Li,Shiyue Zhang,Chang Liu,Junwen Pan,Zhenbang Sun,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fovea-JEPA，通过识别V-JEPA的"几何盲区"并用几何专家VGGT进行选择性特征融合，提升相机运动识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机运动识别需将视角变化与物体运动、场景动态分离，依赖几何证据。视频-语言模型偏重语义线索；V-JEPA等联合嵌入预测架构虽具强时空表征，但部分区域几何线索表征不足，在时间反转监督下整体提升却在若干运动类别上退化，而几何导向的VGGT在各运动类别上一致提升，说明存在几何盲区。

Method: 以V-JEPA为基座、VGGT为几何专家，利用二者预测误差构建干预伪标签，弱监督训练一个盲区预测器；该预测器输出token级响应图并聚合为片段级干预分数，据此引导视觉特征与几何特征的自适应融合，实现选择性干预，在引入互补几何信息的同时保留可靠的视觉预测。

Result: 在CameraMotionDataset、CameraBench与ShotBench上验证有效，相较此前报道的SOTA基线分别提升9.67、8.67与24.38个百分点。

Conclusion: 选择性注入互补几何信息可有效弥补V-JEPA的几何盲区，Fovea-JEPA在相机运动识别任务上显著优于现有基线，代码与模型将于录用后开源。

Abstract: Camera motion recognition requires separating viewpoint changes caused by camera translation, rotation, or zoom from object motion and scene dynamics. Video-language models often rely on semantic cues and struggle with the geometric evidence needed for this task. Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures such as V-JEPA offer strong spatio-temporal representations, but our experiments suggest that they underrepresent geometric cues in some regions. Under temporal-reversal supervision, V-JEPA improves overall but degrades on several motion families, whereas a weaker geometry-oriented VGGT predictor improves across all families. We refer to these geometrically underrepresented regions as geometric blind spots and propose Fovea-JEPA to identify and address them. Using V-JEPA as the base model and VGGT as a geometry expert, we construct intervention pseudo-labels from their prediction errors to weakly supervise a blind-spot predictor. The predictor produces token-wise response maps, which are aggregated into a clip-level intervention score to guide adaptive fusion of visual and geometric features. This selective intervention introduces complementary geometric information while preserving reliable visual predictions. Experiments on CameraMotionDataset, CameraBench, and ShotBench demonstrate the effectiveness of Fovea-JEPA, with gains of 9.67, 8.67, and 24.38 percentage points over previously reported state-of-the-art baselines, respectively. Code and models will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [102] [What Sketches Tell Us about LVLMs: Conventions, Grounding, and Localisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34037)
*Chaitat Utintu,Ahmed Bourouis,Arkaprabha Basu,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的简笔画探针，通过部件构造顺序、空间注意力定位、定位的层/头机制位置三种读出，揭示LVLM保留的视觉惯例及其所在位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM基准只判断答案对错，无法揭示模型保留了哪些视觉惯例、这些惯例位于何处；简笔画剥离纹理光照等信息、仅保留人类认为足以识别的笔画，可暴露这种隐藏结构。

Method: 免训练简笔画探针，含三种读出：时间维度的部件构造顺序、空间维度的注意力定位、机制维度的定位层/头位置。在9个LVLM上评估部件顺序、5个模型上评估注意力读出，覆盖两个带部件标注的简笔画数据集；并使用置空、扣留、错配等控制实验。

Result: (1) 模型部件顺序与人类惯例一致：人类趋同时模型再现规范顺序，人类差异处模型也变异性对应；控制实验将该惯例定位在语言模型中。(2) 部件级定位在冻结模型上、无定位监督即恢复部件与物体区域。(3) 定位信号集中于狭窄的中层网络带，其位置随基座语言模型而非参数量，且在物体与场景简笔画间稳定。

Conclusion: 提供面向简笔画LVLM的实用诊断工具：无需微调、无需辅助分割器，层带可兼作头选择先验，在所有测试模型与数据集上定位mAcc@0.1持平或提升。

Abstract: LVLM benchmarks tell us whether a model answers correctly, but not which visual conventions it has retained, nor where those conventions live. Sketches expose this hidden structure. A sketch removes texture, lighting, and photographic context while preserving the strokes humans judged sufficient for recognition. If an LVLM can operate on that abstraction, the surviving signal is structural rather than merely photometric. We introduce a training-free sketch probe with three readouts: temporal part-construction order, spatial attention-based grounding, and the mechanistic layer/head locus of grounding. We evaluate nine LVLMs on part order and five on the attention readouts, over two part-annotated sketch datasets. Three findings emerge. First, LVLM part order tracks human convention: models reproduce canonical orderings where human drawings converge, and become correspondingly variable where human orderings vary. Controls that blank, withhold, and mismatch the sketch locate this convention in the language model. Second, part-level grounding survives the abstraction: attention heads selected from the frozen model with an existing recipe recover part and object regions on object and scene sketches, with no grounding supervision. Third, the grounding signal concentrates in a narrow mid-network band whose location follows the base language model rather than parameter count in the two families we compare, and remains stable across object and scene sketches. The result is a practical diagnostic for sketch-driven LVLMs: no fine-tuning, no auxiliary segmenter, and a layer band that doubles as a head-selection prior, matching or improving grounding mAcc@0.1 in every model and dataset we tested.

</details>


### [103] [Learning the Autoregressive: Direct Score-Difference Estimation with Amortized Future Credit for Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34092)
*Zixuan Duan,Yabo Chen,Xunzhi Xiang,Xin Zhang,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Chi Zhang,Qi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Leta 是一种少步蒸馏框架，用 ScoreDelta 直接估计师生修正、用 FutureCredit 学习跨 chunk 信用信号，在一次前向中构建联合教师引导更新场，优于 DMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AR 视频生成的少步蒸馏先初始化 AR 学生再用 DMD 精炼，但存在两问题：(1) DMD 需先估计完整学生 score 再构成师生修正，方向获取是间接的，尽管初始化后的学生已足够可用；(2) 分离历史训练（detached-history）使生成器无法将当前 chunk 对后续 chunk 的影响计入信用分配。

Method: 提出 ScoreDelta：从去噪残差中直接估计教师-学生修正，并证明其与 score-difference 估计的总体等价性，以及在固定特征线性模型有限容量下的条件近似优势；提出 FutureCredit：利用下一 chunk 教师残差的向量-雅可比积（VJP）学习辅助的跨 chunk 更新信号。二者在生成器更新时通过单次前向合并为联合教师引导更新场；教师评估与信用 VJP 仅用于估计器训练。

Result: 受控实验中，Leta 相对 DMD 在 VBench 上提升 0.9 分、VideoAlign 上提升 0.66；在更高生成器更新频率（G:E=1:1）下优势保持，VBench 提升 1.6、VideoAlign 提升 0.35。

Conclusion: 通过将师生修正直接化与跨 chunk 信用分配显式化，Leta 在少步 AR 视频蒸馏中取得稳定且对更新频率更鲁棒的增益。
