Abstract: Few-step distillation for autoregressive (AR) video generation commonly first initializes an AR student and then refines it with distribution matching distillation (DMD). This refinement process faces two challenges. First, although initialization already yields a reasonably capable student, DMD obtains the refinement direction indirectly by estimating the full student score before forming the teacher-student correction. Second, detached-history training prevents the generator from assigning credit to a current chunk for its influence on subsequent chunks. We introduce Leta, a few-step distillation framework that addresses these challenges with ScoreDelta and FutureCredit. ScoreDelta directly estimates the teacher-student correction from denoising residuals, while FutureCredit learns an auxiliary cross-chunk update signal from vector-Jacobian products (VJPs) of next-chunk teacher residuals. Leta combines these signals into a joint teacher-guided update field in a single forward pass during generator updates. Teacher evaluations and credit VJPs are used only for estimator training. For ScoreDelta, we establish population equivalence to score-difference estimation and a conditional approximation advantage under limited capacity in a fixed-feature linear model. In our controlled experiments, Leta improves over DMD by 0.9 points on VBench and 0.66 on VideoAlign. This advantage persists at a higher generator update frequency (a generator-to-estimator update ratio of G:E=1:1), where Leta improves over DMD by 1.6 on VBench and 0.35 on VideoAlign. Project page: https://scdzx.github.io/Leta/.

</details>


### [104] [LegoOcc++: Towards Consistent Open-Vocabulary 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34093)
*Changqing Zhou,Yueru Luo,Han Zhang,Zeyu Jiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LegoOcc框架，在仅二值占据监督下，用语言嵌入式高斯统一几何与语义表示，实现室内外开放词汇3D占据预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇3D占据预测对复杂环境中的具身智能体很重要，但室内场景存在密集遮挡、复杂几何和细粒度长尾语义的挑战；同时体素级语义标注昂贵，因此研究仅需二值占据标签的几何监督设定。

Method: 以Language-Embedded Gaussians为统一表示：几何端提出opacity-aware、基于Poisson的Gaussian-to-Occupancy公式，在二值监督下实现稳定的体素聚合；语义端将高斯特征渲染为图像，与冻结的开放词汇模型对齐，并引入Progressive Temperature Decay缓解splatting中的特征混合；进一步提出Instance-Level Semantic Consistency减少碎片化预测，以及Covisibility-Aware Support-View Selection提供信息量大、冗余少的多视角语义监督。

Result: 在室内与室外基准上的大量实验表明，该方法取得较强的开放词汇占据性能，验证了框架的有效性与泛化性；并扩展至户外驾驶场景以检验跨环境通用性。

Conclusion: 仅几何监督的开放词汇占据可行且有效，高斯-语言统一表示配合视角选择与实例一致性机制可显著提升室内外开放词汇占据质量；代码将开源。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D occupancy prediction is important for embodied agents operating in complex environments whose semantic categories extend beyond fixed taxonomies. While recent studies have explored open-vocabulary occupancy in outdoor driving scenarios, indoor environments remain challenging due to dense occlusions, complex geometry, and fine-grained long-tailed semantics. To reduce the need for expensive voxel-level semantic annotations, we study a geometry-only supervision setting requiring only binary occupancy labels. We build upon Language-Embedded Gaussians as a unified representation coupling 3D geometry with language-aligned semantics. For geometry learning, we introduce an opacity-aware Poisson-based Gaussian-to-Occupancy formulation that enables stable volumetric aggregation under binary occupancy supervision. For semantic learning, we render Gaussian features into images and align them with a frozen open-vocabulary model, together with Progressive Temperature Decay to reduce feature mixing during Gaussian splatting. Building on this framework, we further introduce Instance-Level Semantic Consistency to alleviate fragmented object predictions, and Covisibility-Aware Support-View Selection to provide informative and less redundant multi-view semantic supervision. We also extend our framework to outdoor driving scenarios to evaluate its generality across substantially different environments. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves strong open-vocabulary occupancy performance, validating the effectiveness and generality of our framework. Code will be available at https://github.com/JuIvyy/LegoOcc.

</details>


### [105] [StickFigure: Enabling Pose-Only IoT Cameras for Privacy-Preserving Edge Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34130)
*Aveline Xiaoyuan Han,Chenshu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StickFigure 是一种仅输出姿态的 IoT 摄像头系统，在实时监测中保持原始 RGB 帧本地短暂存储，仅导出紧凑骨架流用于下游人体活动识别（HAR），并通过轻量级网络设计在资源受限 IoT 摄像头上实现高精度设备端姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内 IoT 摄像头可用于辅助生活与活动感知空间，但导出原始 RGB 帧会泄露面部、衣着与家居细节等隐私，而许多体动任务并不需要这些信息；同时，在资源受限的 IoT 摄像头上提取准确姿态流仍很困难。

Method: 提出两个核心设计：(1) 激活感知姿态骨干网络（activation-aware pose backbone），在空间下采样后分配通道容量；(2) 轻量级分层 MLP 精修器（hierarchical MLP refiner），利用中间视觉特征与运动学上下文，选择性修正肘部与手腕坐标。系统在设备端完整实现。

Result: 在 NTU RGB+D 与 Penn Action 上，相比最先进的 MCU 姿态模型取得最高姿态精度与最佳下游 HAR 效用；相比基于 RTMPose-S 的基线，参数量减少 96.5%、每帧 MACs 减少 99.3%；在典型 STM32H753 MCU 上实现 0.107 s 的 p95 延迟。

Conclusion: StickFigure 为面向海量 IoT 摄像头的隐私感知边缘视觉建立了新的工作点（operating point），在精度、效率与隐私保护之间取得了新的平衡。

Abstract: Indoor IoT cameras can support assisted living and activity-aware spaces, but exporting raw RGB frames raises privacy concerns by exposing facial, clothing, and household details that body-motion tasks often do not require. We present StickFigure, a pose-only IoT camera system that, during real-time monitoring, keeps raw frames local and ephemeral while exporting only compact skeleton streams sufficient for downstream human activity recognition (HAR). While pose estimation has matured on GPUs, extracting accurate pose streams on resource-constrained IoT cameras remains difficult. To overcome these challenges and enable on-device pose streams on (almost) any IoT camera, we present two core designs in StickFigure: an activation-aware pose backbone that allocates channel capacity after spatial downsampling and a lightweight hierarchical MLP refiner that selectively corrects elbow and wrist coordinates using intermediate visual features and kinematic context. We fully implement StickFigure and evaluate it on public benchmarks and real-world deployment. Compared with state-of-the-art MCU pose models, StickFigure achieves the highest pose accuracy and downstream HAR utility on both NTU RGB+D and Penn Action, while using 96.5% fewer parameters and 99.3% fewer MACs per frame than RTMPose-S-based baselines, achieving 0.107 s p95 latency on a representative STM32H753 MCU. We believe StickFigure establishes a new operating point for privacy-aware edge vision on massive IoT cameras.

</details>


### [106] [Reversion, Retention, and Semantic Intervention: Measuring Functional Content in CLIP Fine-Tuning Deltas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34144)
*Chih-Ting Liao,Kezhen Bao,Xin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过“回退”与“保留”两种操作，量化微调权重变化（fine-tuning deltas）对任务性能的实际因果贡献，揭示CLIP微调中的“操作冗余”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间可解释性可给微调增量赋予语义描述，但无法说明这些被识别的权重更新对任务性能究竟有多重要，尤其当其他更新也能支撑相同预测时。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将reversion（把选定参数恢复为预训练值）与retention（仅保留选定微调更新）配对使用；在合成金融图表、Flowers102、EuroSAT、FGVCAircraft上用ViT-L/14，在Flowers102上用ViT-B/16评测CLIP，采用统一配方微调最后六个视觉block；并设置等大小、层对齐的随机子集基线，以及语义选中方向的移除实验。

Result: 1）仅注意力VO更新即可恢复43-87%的准确率提升，但回退VO对图表准确率无影响；交叉熵损失在Charts与EuroSAT上保持该对比，正确类分数margin揭示额外贡献。2）VO权重在每种设置下恢复的损失改善均超过十个等大小随机子集；VO与MLP保留联合修正25-81%的预训练错误，体现“操作冗余”。3）移除语义选中方向比匹配随机移除使VO保留恢复下降更多（全部五个设置）。

Conclusion: 语义对齐、移除代价与保留恢复是判断微调更新功能角色的互补证据；二者结合可更可靠地刻画微调增量在给定CLIP配方中的功能作用。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts pretrained models to new tasks, but identifying which weight changes support the resulting behavior remains difficult. Weight-space interpretability assigns semantic descriptions to these changes, called fine-tuning deltas. Such descriptions leave open how much task performance depends on the identified updates, particularly when other updates can support the same predictions. We address this gap by pairing reversion, which restores selected parameters to their pretrained values, with retention, which keeps only selected fine-tuning updates. We evaluate Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models on synthetic financial charts, Flowers102, EuroSAT, and FGVCAircraft with ViT-L/14, and on Flowers102 with ViT-B/16, using a shared recipe that tunes the final six visual blocks. Our contributions are threefold. First, attention value-output (VO) updates alone recover 43-87% of the accuracy improvement, although reverting them leaves chart accuracy unchanged. Cross-entropy loss preserves this contrast on Charts and EuroSAT, while correct-class score margins reveal additional contributions. Second, VO weights recover more loss improvement than all ten equally sized, layer-matched random subsets in each setting. VO and multilayer perceptron (MLP) retentions jointly correct 25-81% of pretrained errors, demonstrating overlapping recovered behavior, which we call operational redundancy. Third, removing semantically selected directions reduces VO-retained recovery more than matched-random removal in all five settings. Together, these results distinguish semantic alignment, removal cost, and retained recovery as complementary evidence about the functional role of fine-tuning updates within the evaluated CLIP recipe.

</details>


### [107] [MuSTEL: A Large-Scale Multilingual Synthetic Text-Line Image Dataset for OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34148)
*Majid Adibian,Hossein Saberi,Hossein Zeinali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MuSTEL，一个超1450万标注文本行图像的大规模合成多语言OCR数据集，用于波斯语、阿拉伯语与英语的行级识别训练与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR系统性能强依赖训练数据的规模与多样性；而波斯语、阿拉伯语、英语混排场景下，同一文本行内可能共存不同书写方向、字体与样式，现有数据难以覆盖此类复杂情况。

Method: 基于自然语言语料（含源自源文本的多语言内容）合成文本行图像；生成框架支持字体能力感知选择、行内字体与样式切换、混合方向渲染、高亮与下划线；使用经校验的711个字族、2009个字体文件构建排版覆盖；并施加多样背景、模糊、几何畸变、倾斜、笔画宽度与保存质量扰动以模拟文档外观；数据集划分为训练集及预设的小/大测试集。

Result: 构建了包含超过1450万标注文本行图像的MuSTEL数据集；并进行统计分析，刻画语言构成、转写长度、词汇与字符序列多样性、图像尺寸、排版及渲染条件。

Conclusion: MuSTEL为多语言行级OCR系统的训练与评测提供资源，可支撑跨语言、跨书写方向与跨视觉条件的识别鲁棒性研究。

Abstract: Optical character recognition (OCR) systems depend strongly on the scale and diversity of their training data. One approach to document OCR first analyzes the page layout and extracts text-line images, which are then transcribed by a recognition model. Multilingual line-level recognition involving Persian, Arabic, and English presents additional challenges because different writing directions, fonts, and styles may coexist within a single line. This paper introduces MuSTEL, a large-scale synthetic multilingual OCR dataset containing more than 14.5 million annotated text-line images, organized into a training partition and predefined small and large test sets. Samples are generated from natural-language corpora, including multilingual content already present in the source texts. The generation framework incorporates capability-aware font selection, within-line font and style changes, mixed-direction text rendering, highlighting, and underlining. A validated repository of 2,009 font files from 711 families supports broad typographic coverage, while varied backgrounds, blur, geometric distortion, skew, stroke width, and image save quality provide diverse document-like appearances. Statistical analysis characterizes the dataset's language composition, transcription lengths, lexical and character-sequence diversity, image dimensions, typography, and rendering conditions. MuSTEL provides a resource for training and evaluating multilingual line-level OCR systems and investigating recognition robustness across languages, writing directions, and visual conditions.

</details>


### [108] [Humans in Space: Can MLLMs Construct Human-Centric Spatial Understanding via Active Exocentric Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34149)
*Tianchen Guo,Tengyue Zhang,Chen Liu,Ling Chen,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SATE-Bench，一个包含 316 个真实场景、2,525 条多轮 QA 的交互式基准，用于评估 MLLM 的视觉自我意识与主动外视角探索能力，并揭示当前模型"过早停止探索却高置信度报告"及主动探索训练导致精度下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 空间能力评估多为静态、被动观察范式。在复杂人类中心场景中，遮挡会隐藏关键几何证据，被动基准迫使模型"猜测"而非识别自身视觉不确定性，缺乏对模型主动获取证据能力的考察。

Method: 构建 SATE-Bench：316 个真实场景、2,525 条多轮 QA，包含被动多视角控制设置与主动探索设置。主动设置中模型从严重遮挡视角出发，需迭代查询相邻视角直至证据充分"sate"问题，采用带步数惩罚的效率奖励评分；并对现有多模态大模型进行评测与匹配视角对照分析，另用 GRPO 微调检验奖励视角探索的效果。

Result: SOTA MLLM 过早终止探索却报告高置信度，且在细粒度 3D 几何任务上表现不佳；匹配视角对照表明主动—被动差距主要源于证据获取不足。GRPO 微调显示奖励视角探索虽提升主动探索行为，但整体准确率下降，模型过度探索且无法跨轮次融合不断扩展的多视角上下文。

Conclusion: 扩展被动多视角感知并不能自发产生主动空间推理能力，未来模型需具备"知道往哪看、如何整合所见、何时停止"三项能力。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at zero-shot reasoning, their spatial capabilities are primarily evaluated through static, passive observation. In complex, human-centric scenes, occlusion often hides crucial geometric evidence, and passive benchmarks force models to guess rather than recognize their own visual uncertainty. To bridge this gap, we introduce SATE-Bench, a novel interactive benchmark comprising 316 real-world scenes and 2,525 multi-turn QA pairs to evaluate visual self-awareness and active exocentric view-exploration. It combines a Passive multi-view control with an Active exploration setting, where models start from a severely occluded view and must iteratively query adjacent viewpoints until they gather enough evidence to fully ``sate'' the question, scored by a step-penalized efficiency reward. Evaluating state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals that they terminate exploration prematurely while reporting high confidence, and struggle with fine-grained 3D geometric tasks; matched-view controls further attribute the Active--Passive gap primarily to insufficient evidence acquisition. Moreover, fine-tuning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) shows that rewarding view-seeking increases active exploration but lowers overall accuracy, as models over-explore and fail to fuse expanding multi-view contexts across turns. These findings indicate that scaling passive multi-view perception does not intrinsically yield active spatial reasoning, highlighting the need for models that know where to look, how to integrate what they see, and when to stop. Code and data are available at https://gtc-gh.github.io/SATE-Bench/

</details>


### [109] [Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Crowd Flow Control Using Dynamic Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34163)
*Alhanof Alolyan,He Wang,Amani Alonayzan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仿真驱动的强化学习框架，无需标注数据即可学习人群屏障布局，在未见人流密度下减少行人停顿并维持流量调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型公共活动（如朝圣）中人群屏障被广泛用于调控行人流动，但固定布局即使能控制整体人流，也常造成不必要的停顿与局部拥堵；而缺乏"给定场景的最优屏障布置"数据集，使优化布局困难。

Method: 从分割后的人群场景出发构建真实感仿真环境以复现观测到的行人分布，再在该环境中训练Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）智能体持续调整屏障位置；相较监督方法，该框架可探索真实数据中未出现过的屏障配置。

Result: 在未见过的行人数量下评估学习策略，其持续优于现有人工设计的屏障布局，减少行人停顿的同时保持有效的流量调控。

Conclusion: 结合计算机视觉人群仿真与强化学习，可为优化人群控制基础设施提供有效框架。

Abstract: Crowd barriers are widely used to regulate pedestrian movement during large public events such as pilgrimages. However, fixed barrier layouts often create unnecessary stopping and local congestion even when they successfully control the overall crowd flow. Finding better barrier configurations is difficult because there is no dataset that provides the best barrier placement for a given crowd scene. We address this challenge by introducing a simulation-driven reinforcement learning framework that learns barrier configurations without requiring labelled training data. Starting from a segmented crowd scene, we construct a realistic simulation environment that reproduces the observed pedestrian distribution. This environment is then used to train a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent that continuously adjusts barrier positions to improve pedestrian movement. Unlike supervised approaches the proposed framework allows the agent to explore barrier configurations that have never been observed in real data. We evaluate the learned policy under unseen numbers of pedestrians. The learned policy consistently outperforms the existing manually designed barrier configuration by reducing pedestrian stops while maintaining effective flow regulation. These results demonstrate that combining computer vision crowd simulation and reinforcement learning provides an effective framework for optimizing crowd control infrastructure.

</details>


### [110] [GeoPrompt: Geometry-Conditioned Prompt Learning for Underwater Open-Vocabulary Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34190)
*Shanshan Han,Yuxi Wang,Can Qin,Yujie Chen,Cong Pan,Haitao Li,Guoqiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何条件提示学习框架GeoPrompt，利用深度几何结构先验增强水下开放词汇实例分割的视觉-文本对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇实例分割依赖视觉区域特征与文本类别嵌入的对齐，但水下环境的颜色衰减、散射、浑浊与低对比度使视觉外观失真，区域特征偏离对应语义概念，导致相似度不可靠，尤其削弱无法通过类别特定监督校正的未见类识别。

Method: 核心洞察是几何结构在水下退化场景中比视觉外观更稳定。具体包括：几何引导视觉适应模块（GVAM），提取深度导出的结构先验并注入多尺度视觉特征；几何条件提示学习（GCPL），由结构表示生成几何感知文本分类器；并将几何感知分类器与固定及图像条件文本分类器集成以改善开放词汇语义对齐。

Result: 在MARIS-OpenIS基准上取得最先进性能，并在水下开放词汇实例分割上带来一致提升。

Conclusion: 以几何结构作为稳定条件替代脆弱的纯外观对齐，可有效缓解水下视觉退化对开放词汇识别的影响。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary instance segmentation (OVIS) provides a promising solution for marine perception by recognizing and segmenting categories beyond predefined label spaces. Existing OVIS methods usually rely on vision-language alignment, where visual region features are matched with textual category embeddings to recognize both seen and unseen classes. However, this alignment assumption becomes fragile in underwater environments. Color attenuation, scattering, turbidity, and low contrast distort visual appearance, causing region features to deviate from their corresponding semantic concepts. As a result, the similarity between degraded visual features and textual classifiers becomes unreliable, which particularly weakens the recognition of unseen underwater categories that cannot be corrected by category-specific supervision. To address these issues, we propose GeoPrompt, a geometry-conditioned prompt learning framework for underwater open-vocabulary instance segmentation. Our key insight is that geometric structure is more stable than visual appearance in degraded underwater scenes. Specifically, GeoPrompt introduces a geometry-guided visual adaptation module (GVAM) to extract depth-derived structural priors and inject them into multi-scale visual features. It further develops a geometry-conditioned prompt learning (GCPL) that generates geometry-aware textual classifiers from structural representations. These geometry-aware classifiers are integrated with fixed and image-conditioned textual classifiers to improve open-vocabulary semantic alignment. Experiments on the MARIS-OpenIS benchmark show that GeoPrompt achieves state-of-the-art performance and brings consistent improvements on underwater open-vocabulary instance segmentation.

</details>


### [111] [Timing Matters Twice: When to Query and When to Skip in Flow-Matching On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34191)
*Renye Yan,Junyi Zhan,Jikang Cheng,Wei Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhaseOPD：一种相位感知的查询—跳过框架，用学生速度预测在线识别生成阶段，为每个教师选择合适监督状态并跳过冗余 rollout，仅执行 5.56/16 个位置，训练时间降 41.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy 蒸馏中不同评价维度（美学、OCR 精度）与不同任务（生成、编辑）关注去噪过程的不同阶段，有效监督发生在不同阶段；而现有方法从预定义时间步分布采样监督状态，忽略阶段差异并造成计算浪费。由此引出两个时序决策：何时自适应查询教师，何时跳过剩余 rollout。

Method: PhaseOPD 基于预测干净潜变量的变化量在线识别生成相位，仅复用学生已有的速度预测、不额外评估模型；对每个任务指定的教师依据其相位偏好选择监督状态；在所有查询决策确定后跳过未使用的 rollout 后缀，保持单次查询的速度蒸馏目标。

Result: 在 SD3.5-M 上，PhaseOPD 每个本地 batch 仅执行 16 个 rollout 位置中的 5.56 个，相较 DanceOPD 训练时间减少 41.3%，同时提升 Aesthetic、GenEval 与 OCR 分数。

Conclusion: 按生成相位自适应安排教师查询与提前跳过，可在不改变蒸馏目标的前提下显著降低 OPD 训练开销并提升多维度性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) enables flow-matching models to acquire complementary capabilities from specialized teachers through supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, different evaluation dimensions, such as aesthetics and OCR accuracy, and different tasks, such as image generation and editing, emphasize different aspects of the denoising process, suggesting that their effective supervision may occur at different stages. Existing methods typically select supervision states from predefined timestep distributions, which may overlook these phase-dependent differences and incur unnecessary computational overhead. This motivates two critical timing decisions: when to adaptively query teachers for effective supervision based on the evolving generative process, and when to skip the remaining rollout to avoid redundant computation.
  In this paper, we introduce PhaseOPD, a phase-aware query-and-skip framework that identifies generative phases online from revisions of predicted-clean latents, using existing student velocity predictions without additional model evaluations. For each task-assigned teacher, PhaseOPD selects a supervision state based on its phase preference and skips the unused rollout suffix once all query decisions are finalized, preserving the single-query velocity-distillation objective. On SD3.5-M, PhaseOPD executes only 5.56 of 16 rollout positions per local batch, reducing training time by 41.3% relative to DanceOPD while improving Aesthetic, GenEval, and OCR scores.

</details>


### [112] [MIS-VQA: An Annotation-Driven Surgical Vision-Language Dataset for Complex Surgical Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34210)
*Mohamed Hamdy,Iheb Zouari,Fatmaelzahraa Ahmed,Muraam Abdel-Ghani,Muhammed Arsalan,Ponnuthurai Suganthan,Khalid Al-Jalham,Abdulaziz Al-Ali,Shidin Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIS-VQA手术视觉语言数据集，将EndoVis18与CholecT50的异质标注转化为结构化文本场景表示并由LLM生成临床连贯QA，涵盖简洁/详细描述、感知级定位与多步推理四类任务，并配套LLaVA-SurgXpert模型族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术AI数据集多为单任务监督（识别或模板化QA），医学VQA资源依赖合成模板、缺乏自然描述监督、标注稀疏，难以支撑空间关系与流程上下文的推理；亟需多模态、带空间监督、可分层评测的统一基准。

Method: 构建标注驱动的数据生成流水线：利用EndoVis18与CholecT50，将phase、instrument、interaction、triplet和segmentation标注转换为统一上下文，引导大语言模型生成临床连贯的问答对；通过置信度过滤的分割与跨标注一致性校验缓解标注稀疏，实现大规模空间定位监督；同时提出LLaVA-SurgXpert系列模型，比较不同视觉编码器对多模态推理的影响。

Result: MIS-VQA支持从原子视觉定位到组合式推理的分层评测；LLaVA-SurgXpert在现有手术VQA基线上取得一致提升，同时揭示感知定位与高层推理之间存在的能力差距，而这一差距在此前基准中被掩盖。

Conclusion: MIS-VQA为带空间监督与多层级推理评估的手术视觉语言理解提供了统一基准，表明视觉表示显著影响多模态推理表现，并暴露了感知与推理能力不匹配的问题。

Abstract: Modern surgical AI increasingly requires multimodal understanding rather than isolated perception, yet existing datasets remain largely limited to single-task supervision such as recognition or templated question answering. Current medical VQA resources often rely on synthetic templates, lack natural descriptive supervision, or provide sparse annotations that hinder reasoning about spatial relations and procedural context. We introduce MIS-VQA, a surgical vision-language dataset generated through an annotation-driven pipeline that converts heterogeneous surgical annotations into structured textual scene representations. Using EndoVis18 and CholecT50, we transform phase, instrument, interaction, triplet, and segmentation labels into unified contexts that guide large language models to generate clinically coherent question-answer pairs. MIS-VQA supports four response types-concise and detailed descriptions, perception-level grounding, and multi-step reasoning-enabling hierarchical evaluation from atomic visual grounding to compositional reasoning. To address annotation sparsity, we incorporate confidence-filtered segmentation and cross-annotation consistency checks, enabling spatially grounded supervision at scale. MIS-VQA establishes a unified benchmark for surgical vision-language understanding with spatial supervision and multi-tier reasoning evaluation. To demonstrate its utility, we introduce LLaVA-SurgXpert, a family of surgical vision-language models with visual encoders to analyze how visual representations influence multimodal reasoning. Experiments show consistent improvements over existing surgical VQA baselines while revealing capability gaps between perceptual grounding and higher-level reasoning hidden in prior benchmarks.

</details>


### [113] [StaX-Net: Stackelberg Interleaved Asymmetric Network for Cross-Modality Medical Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34214)
*Amin Golnari,Saeid Sanei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StaX-Net，一种基于Stackelberg博弈的交错式非对称网络，通过部分耦合的编码器-判别器结构、FAST层及非对称优化，实现解剖一致性的跨模态医学图像转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态医学图像转换存在解剖不一致的合成问题，可能危及临床诊断，需在保持解剖结构一致性方面加以约束。

Method: 构建部分耦合的编码器-判别器结构，共享模块增强生成器与判别器的架构特征对齐；引入2D特征聚合与空间变换（FAST）层，在学习使用特权信息（LUPI）范式下蒸馏目标监督先验，训练时蒸馏目标域先验、推理时仅用源域变换；采用Stackelberg启发的非对称优化，包括显式共享参数冻结与非对称学习率以稳定领导者-跟随者训练。

Result: 共享模块提升架构特征对齐；FAST层使合成过程受目标域先验约束，相对于标准生成架构可获得目标域先验引导的特征表示，提升解剖一致性（摘要未给出具体定量指标）。

Conclusion: StaX-Net通过非对称结构设计与特权信息蒸馏有效约束跨模态合成，代码已开源，但摘要未提供量化结果与消融数据。

Abstract: Cross-modality medical image translation is challenged by anatomically inconsistent synthesis that can compromise clinical diagnosis. We propose StaX-Net, a Stackelberg interleaved asymmetric network that incorporates a partially coupled encoder-discriminator structure, and a 2D feature aggregation and spatial transformation (FAST) layer that distills target-supervised priors under the learning using privileged information paradigm. The shared blocks between the generator and discriminator enhance architectural feature alignment. To stabilize the leader-follower training, a Stackelberg-inspired asymmetric optimization scheme employs explicit shared-parameter freezing and asymmetric learning rates. The FAST layer distills target-domain priors during training and applies a source-only transform at inference. This constrains the synthesis process through target-informed feature representations relative to standard generative architectures. The code is available on GitHub for public use.

</details>


### [114] [From Presence Labels to Bounding Boxes: Can Multimodal Large Language Models Scale Surgical Instrument Localization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34218)
*Mohamed Hamdy,Muraam Abdel-Ghani,Fatmaelzahraa Ahmed,Sifna Nasar,Mariam Ahmed,Khalid Al-Jalham,Abdulaziz Al-Ali,Shidin Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的MLLM标注框架，将手术帧与器械存在性标签转换为精确边界框，在Cholec80胆囊切除数据上系统评估其可靠性、成本与设计敏感性，并公开释放285,977个边界框的空间扩展标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术器械的空间定位是手术场景理解的基础，但现有标注仅覆盖部分帧、子视频或数据集变体，未覆盖完整手术流程；人工扩展标注耗时且依赖专家；弱监督与检测器伪标签需任务特定训练且可能不精确。因此需要可扩展、低成本且可靠的标注方案。

Method: 设计基于多模态大模型（MLLM）的标注框架，利用手术帧与已有的器械存在性标签生成边界框；采用六种MLLM及多次调用配置实例化；在Cholec80腹腔镜胆囊切除数据上，通过分层抽样由两名标注者独立标注1,000帧，并由专家外科医生复核其中一套建立参考标准；同时对比结构化多阶段流水线与单次调用基线。

Result: 器械集合F1为88.2–92.7，但mIoU仅0.180–0.735，表明识别性能相近却可能掩盖巨大定位差异；结构化多阶段流水线达到0.784 mIoU，优于最佳单次调用基线的0.735，接近人工一致性0.814；基于最优流水线构建并公开释放Cholec80空间扩展，覆盖80个视频全部184,498个已标注帧，共285,977个边界框，标注与人工参考已发布于GitHub。

Conclusion: MLLM可支撑可扩展的手术器械边界框标注，但集合级识别指标会掩盖定位质量差异，需以mIoU等定位指标评估；结构化多阶段设计显著提升定位精度并接近人工一致性；所释放的完整流程级空间标注可支持检测、跟踪与视觉-语言定位等下游应用。

Abstract: Spatial localization of surgical instruments through bounding-box annotations is a foundational component of surgical scene understanding, supporting detection, tracking, interaction analysis, and grounded vision-language applications. Yet existing annotations often cover selected frames, video subsets, or dataset variants rather than complete procedures. Manual extension is time-consuming and expert-dependent. Additionally, weakly supervised and detector-based pseudo-labels require task-specific training and may be imprecise. In this study, we design a scalable MLLM-based annotation framework that converts surgical frames and existing instrument-presence labels into accurate bounding boxes, and systematically analyze its reliability, cost, and design sensitivity. We instantiate the framework using six MLLMs and a multi-call configuration on laparoscopic cholecystectomy in Cholec80. Using a carefully designed stratification scheme, two annotators independently label 1,000 frames, and an expert surgeon reviews one annotation set to establish the reference. Results show that instrument-set F1 ranges from 88.2 to 92.7,whereas mIoU ranges from 0.180 to 0.735, showing that comparable recognition can conceal large localization differences. A structured multi-stage annotation pipeline achieves 0.784 mIoU, compared with 0.735 for the best single-call baseline and 0.814 human-human agreement. Using the best accuracy pipeline, we create and publicly release a spatial extension of Cholec80 comprising 285,977 bounding boxes across all 184,498 annotated frames from the 80 videos. The annotations and human references are made available on GitHub

</details>


### [115] [CropReward: A Human-Preference Benchmark and Reward Model for Aesthetic Image Cropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34221)
*Xiangfei Sheng,Xiaofeng Pan,Leida Li,Qian Zhang,Biao Wang,Johan Wagemans,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文构建CropBench基准揭示现有AIC评价指标与人类偏好的对齐有限，并提出MLLM奖励模型CropReward及偏好对齐裁剪模型CropAlign。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美学图像裁剪（AIC）评价长期依赖IoU等几何指标与图像美学评估（IAA）模型，其与人类偏好不一致的问题虽被观察却缺乏系统验证，亟需可靠反映人类偏好的评价体系。

Method: 构建CropBench（1,019张图像、106,995对人类偏好对，每候选集含原图与14种AIC方法产出的候选裁剪）；基于该基准提出MLLM奖励模型CropReward，通过候选裁剪与原图对比输出美学提升/退化分数；用其构建300k规模的CropReward-300k数据集，并训练无需人工标注裁剪框的偏好对齐模型CropAlign。

Result: CropBench系统研究表明，从IoU到现成IAA模型的现有评价标准与人类偏好仅有限对齐；CropReward与人类偏好的对齐显著优于现有指标；CropAlign超越当前最先进AIC方法。

Conclusion: 现有AIC评价标准可靠性不足，基于MLLM的奖励模型可有效替代并指导AIC模型优化，实现更符合人类偏好的裁剪。

Abstract: Aesthetic image cropping (AIC) aims to improve image composition and aesthetic quality by removing extraneous or distracting content. Over the past decade, AIC methods have evolved from specialized vision models to multimodal large language models(MLLMs). Reliable evaluation that reflects human preferences is essential for benchmarking AIC methods and guiding model optimization. However, the current AIC evaluation relies largely on geometry-based metrics such as intersection over union (IoU), as well as image aesthetic assessment(IAA) models. Although inconsistencies with human preferences have long been observed, the robustness and reliability of these evaluation criteria remain underexplored. To bridge this gap, we first construct CropBench, a large-scale benchmark comprising 106,995 human preference pairs for 1,019 images, with each candidate set containing the original image and candidate crops produced by 14 AIC methods. A systematic study on CropBench reveals that the existing criteria, ranging from IoU to off-the-shelf IAA assessment models, only exhibit limited alignment with human preferences. Building upon CropBench, we further propose CropReward, an MLLM-based reward model that evaluates a candidate crop by comparing with the original uncropped image and outputs a score indicating explicit aesthetic improvement or degradation relative to the original. In addition, we employ CropReward to construct CropReward-300k, the largest AIC dataset to date, and further develop CropAlign, a preference-aligned AIC model trained solely with CropReward supervision, without human-annotated crop boxes. Experimental results demonstrate that CropReward aligns with human preferences significantly better than existing criteria. CropAlign also surpasses the state-of-the-art AIC methods, demonstrating the advantage of CropReward in guiding AIC model optimization.

</details>


### [116] [BoundedGS: Memory-Efficient High-Resolution Training for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34233)
*Xiaobin Deng,Changyu Diao,Min Li,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BoundedGS 通过分离光栅化与投影反向传播、按实测负载限制活跃局部光栅化工作集，把三维高斯泼溅的高分辨率训练显存从 9.97 GiB 降至 1.76 GiB（降 82.3%），并保持重建质量、缩短训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 3D Gaussian Splatting 训练中，像素与高斯瓦片相交数激增，使光栅化缓存与投影、梯度状态在峰值内存时共存，显存占用过高成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出 BoundedGS：利用完整视图更新可聚合各区域梯度、区域前向/反向缓存可在贡献收集后释放的性质，将光栅化与投影反向传播分离，并按实测负载界定活跃局部光栅化工作集；辅以候选索引投影、紧凑梯度存储以缓解视图级峰值，以及 CPU 辅助结构重建与有界图像供给管理渲染之外的资源。

Result: 固定约 6M 高斯的分辨率扫描中，1K–16K 渲染工作集稳定在 1.073–1.077 GiB；11K 下热启动耗时 19.79 s，全帧执行为 21.76 s。5 个原生 5K 室外场景上，完整训练将平均渲染工作集由 9.97 降至 1.76 GiB（82.3%），重建质量相近，训练时间由 36.7 分钟缩短至 33.9 分钟。

Conclusion: 通过有界化局部光栅化工作集与视图级峰值优化，BoundedGS 实现了与分辨率近似无关的显存占用，使高分辨率高斯泼溅训练在质量不受损的前提下更省内存、更快。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D Gaussian Splatting training increases pixel and Gaussian-tile intersection counts, expanding rasterization caches that coexist with projection and gradient state at peak memory. A complete-view update aggregates gradients across the view; each region's forward and backward caches can be released after its contribution is collected. BoundedGS separates rasterization and projection backward passes and bounds the active local rasterization working set according to its measured load. Candidate-indexed projection and compact gradient storage address the view-level peaks exposed by this change, while CPU-assisted structure rebuilding and bounded image supply manage resources outside rendering. In a resolution sweep at a fixed count of about 6M Gaussians, the measured rendering working set stays within 1.073-1.077 GiB from 1K to 16K; at 11K, a warm pass takes 19.79 seconds versus 21.76 for full-frame execution. Across five native 5K outdoor scenes, full training reduces the mean rendering working set from 9.97 to 1.76 GiB (82.3%), retains similar reconstruction quality, and shortens training time from 36.7 to 33.9 minutes.
  Project page: https://xiaobin2001.github.io/BoundedGS-Web/

</details>


### [117] [ParetoGS: High-Quality Continuous-Budget 3D Gaussian Splatting with Sparse Adapted Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34235)
*Xiaobin Deng,Changyu Diao,Min Li,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ParetoGS 用少量预算锚点+残差图谱描述3DGS成员变化与参数演化，实现无需逐预算优化的连续预算解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展3DGS中，从高预算模型剪枝得到的低预算表示质量落后于独立优化的模型：减少高斯数量会同时改变成员集合与存活者承担的渲染任务，直接继承的参数难以适配低预算。

Method: 交替进行剪枝与固定成员自适应，构建具有持久身份标识的嵌套锚点；将各锚点相对完整模型的参数变化编码为紧凑残差图谱；解码时对共享成员做状态插值，并渐进引入新成员的渲染贡献，把稀疏锚点连成连续预算表示。

Result: 13个场景上，在各自完整模型10%规模下，平均PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS优于前缀方法且高斯更少；共享完整模型下在1%/5%/10%预算超过FlexGS-imp，在1%/0.1%超过NanoGS-imp；Garden上非锚点质量在高保留率接近独立优化GNS的经验参考，部分低预算点超过；编码将平均额外状态从390.0MB降至85.7MB，平均PSNR变化0.01dB。

Conclusion: 紧凑锚点+残差图谱可高效实现质量—预算曲线上的连续可解码表示，在精度与存储间取得良好折衷。

Abstract: Scalable 3D Gaussian Splatting allows one scene representation to serve different Gaussian-count budgets, yet its quality-budget curve may lag behind models optimized separately for each budget. Reducing the number of Gaussians changes both the selected members and the reconstruction tasks carried by the survivors; parameters inherited from a high-budget model may fit a low-budget representation poorly. We propose ParetoGS, which describes member changes and parameter evolution with a small set of adapted budget anchors and decodes intermediate budgets without separate optimization at every requested count. ParetoGS alternates pruning and fixed-member adaptation to build nested anchors with persistent identities, and encodes their parameter changes relative to the full model in a compact residual atlas. At decoding, it interpolates the states of shared members and gradually introduces the rendering contributions of new members, connecting sparse anchors into a continuous-budget representation. Across 13 scenes, ParetoGS attains better average PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS with fewer Gaussians than the compared prefix methods at 10% of each method's own full model. From a shared full model, it also outperforms FlexGS-imp at its trained 1%, 5%, and 10% budgets and NanoGS-imp at the 1% and 0.1% budgets. On Garden, its non-anchor quality approaches an empirical reference from independently optimized GNS models at higher retention ratios and exceeds it at some lower-budget points. Across 13 scenes, encoding reduces mean additional state from 390.0 to 85.7 MB with an average PSNR change of 0.01 dB.
  Project page: https://xiaobin2001.github.io/ParetoGS-Web/

</details>


### [118] [RacerGS: Residual-Autoregressive Context for 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34263)
*Wenqing Wang,Huimin Zeng,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RacerGS是一种紧凑3DGS压缩框架，通过哈希投影、FiLM调制自回归GRU上下文建模和自适应熵编码实现高压缩率且保持渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能快速高质量渲染新视角，但参数量大，成为存储与传输的瓶颈，亟需在保持保真度前提下大幅降低存储体积的压缩方案。

Method: 1) 哈希投影模型：将锚点哈希编码投影为紧凑且空间连贯的上下文特征；2) FiLM调制的自回归GRU上下文模型：利用锚点特征组间因果依赖，以循环隐状态精化熵模型参数；3) 自适应熵编码：对量化后的锚点-属性残差相对上下文预测均值进行编码，利用空间一致性降低符号熵。

Result: 相较原始3DGS实现150倍以上压缩，相较Scaffold-GS实现28倍压缩，同时提供可比渲染质量；在五个数据集上，存储缩减与率失真性能均优于已有3DGS压缩方法。

Conclusion: RacerGS通过空间连贯的哈希特征表达与自回归上下文熵建模，有效解决了3DGS存储瓶颈，在压缩率与率失真性能上达到领先水平。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a promising approach for fast, high-quality novel-view rendering. However, its large parameter size remains a bottleneck for storage and transmission. We propose RacerGS, a compact 3DGS compression framework that achieves scene reconstruction at significantly reduced storage sizes with preserved fidelity. To obtain compact and spatially coherent features, we design a hash projection model that projects the anchor hash encodings into compact context features. Additionally, we introduce a FiLM-modulated autoregressive GRU context model that utilizes causal dependencies among anchor feature groups and uses recurrent hidden states to refine entropy-model parameters, improving bitrate efficiency. Furthermore, we adaptively entropy-code quantized anchor-attribute residuals relative to their context-predicted means, leveraging spatial consistency to reduce symbol entropy. Overall, RacerGS achieves more than 150x compression over vanilla 3DGS and 28x over Scaffold-GS, while providing comparable rendering quality. Extensive experiments across five datasets demonstrate that RacerGS outperforms prior 3DGS compression methods in both storage reduction and rate-distortion performance.

</details>


### [119] [Robust Single-Parameter Point Cloud Registration using Truncated Lp Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34271)
*Seong Hun Lee,Patrick Vandewalle,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TLP方法，用不同p值的截断Lp函数为对应关系赋优先级分数、选择假设并精化模型，实现仅一个可调参数的鲁棒点云配准，且具GPU并行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实3D特征匹配常伴随高外点率；多参数方法需针对数据集调参、实用性差，因此希望以单参数方案兼顾鲁棒性与实用性。

Method: 以截断Lp函数（不同p值）计算对应关系的优先级分数，用于假设选择与最终模型精化；并利用GPU并行化高效评估假设。

Result: 可在平均不到一秒内从数千条对应关系中完成配准；在公开基准上精度高于当前最优的单参数方法，并在四个数据集中的三个上优于两种常用多参数方法，运行时间具竞争力。

Conclusion: TLP以仅一个可调参数在高外点率下实现高精度、快速的点云配准，实用性强于需数据集特定调参的多参数方法。

Abstract: We propose a novel method for robust point cloud registration with only one tunable parameter. Our method, dubbed TLP, uses truncated $L_p$ functions with different values of $p$ to assign priority scores to correspondences, select the hypothesis, and refine the final model. By leveraging the robustness of truncated functions, TLP effectively handles the high outlier rates commonly encountered in real-world 3D feature matching. We also exploit GPU parallelization to evaluate hypotheses efficiently, enabling registration from thousands of correspondences in under a second on average. With only one tunable parameter, our method offers greater practicality than multi-parameter methods that require dataset-specific tuning of several parameters. Evaluations on public benchmark datasets demonstrate that TLP achieves higher accuracy than state-of-the-art single-parameter methods. Furthermore, it outperforms two widely used multi-parameter methods on three of the four evaluated datasets while maintaining competitive runtime.

</details>


### [120] [ChronoReplay: One Answer Interface Is Not Enough for Streaming Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34283)
*Yihao Hu,Yitong Lin,Pan Wang,Zhihao Wen,Hang Wang,Yiwen Zhao,Deyuan Liu,Yutong Li,Mingda Wan,Yuchen Mao,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"接口锁定"(interface lock-in)现象并给出ChronoReplay方法，将异构空间监督组织为按时间排序的episode回放训练，单检查点在OVO-S-Bench、OST-Bench、UCS-Bench上同时提升（OST-Bench +13.41），并配合推理时的自适应帧选择，以更低视觉成本获得额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练空间VLM回答"物体在哪一侧"并不要求模型保留决定该答案的度量位移；而监督距离估计又可能几乎不约束类别关系。当后训练侧重某一读出接口时，提升集中于该接口，向其他接口的迁移弱甚至退化，即"interface lock-in"（接口锁定）问题。

Method: ChronoReplay：将异构空间监督以时间顺序组织为episode进行回放，保留各数据源原生标签(targets)，并在各监督家族(family)间进行平衡监督。论文设置同数据对照实验以分离各成分：答案序列化(answer serialization)、家族平衡学习(family-balanced learning)、时间顺序回放(对比打乱顺序的shifted-order控制组)；推理阶段在固定模型下用自适应帧选择改变证据获取。

Result: 在相同观测访问(observation access)条件下，单一ChronoReplay检查点在OVO-S-Bench、OST-Bench、UCS-Bench上同时提升，其中OST-Bench提升13.41点，并在所比较的监督设置中取得三项基准最高均值。同数据对照显示：答案序列化使增益在OST与UCS间重新分配；家族平衡学习优于普通联合SFT与自然轮次加权；时间顺序回放在两个配对随机种子上比打乱顺序控制组高约2.41个OST点。自适应帧选择在固定模型下带来额外推理增益且视觉成本更低。

Conclusion: 流式空间能力取决于三点：监督所保留的区分信息、学习中异构历史数据的组织方式、以及测试时推理可用的因果证据。解决接口锁定需在监督设计与证据获取两个层面协同考虑。

Abstract: Training a spatial VLM to answer which side an object lies on does not require it to preserve the metric displacement that determines that answer, while supervision for a distance estimate may leave categorical relations largely unconstrained. When post-training emphasizes one readout, improvements can therefore concentrate on that interface while transfer to others remains weak or even degrades; we call this observed specialization interface lock-in. ChronoReplay addresses it by replaying heterogeneous spatial supervision as chronologically ordered episodes, retaining source-native targets, and balancing supervision across families. Under the same observation access, a single ChronoReplay checkpoint improves OVO-S-Bench, OST-Bench, and UCS-Bench together, including a 13.41-point gain on OST-Bench, and achieves the highest three-benchmark mean among the supervision settings compared here. Same-data controls further separate the ingredients of this transfer: answer serialization redistributes gains across OST and UCS, family-balanced learning improves over ordinary joint SFT and natural turn weighting, and chronological replay exceeds a shifted-order control by about 2.41 OST points across two paired seeds. Adaptive frame selection then changes evidence access while keeping the trained model fixed, providing an additional inference-time gain at lower visual cost. Streaming spatial performance depends on what distinctions supervision preserves, how heterogeneous histories are organized during learning, and what causal evidence inference makes available at test time.

</details>


### [121] [EditDistill: Is It Possible to Guide Video Editing with Image Editing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34303)
*Guojun Lei,Hong Li,Hongbing Yang,Lixue Gong,Chi Wang,Zheng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditDistill 将图像编辑模型产生的视觉变换蒸馏为紧凑的 edit descriptor，注入视频扩散模型，从而实现可控、精确且时序一致的视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑已具备细粒度、高可控的视觉修改能力，但视频编辑在精确局部编辑、动态时序效果与跨帧光照一致性方面仍缺乏同等可控性。作者希望把图像级编辑的可控性迁移到视频编辑中。

Method: 给定源视频与编辑提示，先用现成图像编辑模型编辑首帧，得到原始-编辑图像对；将该图像对的视觉变换蒸馏为紧凑表示 edit descriptor；通过轻量融合模块把 descriptor 注入视频扩散模型，引导去噪过程朝目标编辑方向；训练中使用 edit-descriptor alignment 损失与 DINO 结构一致性损失，以对齐编辑轨迹并保留运动与布局；在 flow matching 下推导出等价的推理时引导公式。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在标准基准上表现强劲，并在多种场景下生成高质量视频编辑结果。

Conclusion: 将图像编辑的视觉变换蒸馏为可注入的 descriptor 并结合结构一致性约束与流匹配引导，是提升视频编辑可控性与一致性的一条有效路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in image editing have enabled fine-grained and highly controllable visual modifications. However, achieving similar levels of controllability in video editing remains an open challenge, particularly for precise local edits, dynamic temporal effects, and consistent lighting across frames.In this paper, we propose EditDistill, a framework that distills image-level visual transformations from edits to guide video editing.Specifically, given a source video and an edit prompt, we first generate an edited version of the initial video frame using an off-the-shelf image editing model. We then distill the visual transformation of this original-edited image pair into a compact representation termed an edit descriptor.This descriptor is injected into a video diffusion model through our lightweight fusion module, which steers the denoising process toward the desired edit. To align the video trajectory with the demonstrated image edit while preserving motion and layout, we train with edit-descriptor alignment and DINO structural consistency losses, and derive an equivalent inference-time guidance formulation under flow matching.Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves strong performance on standard benchmarks and produces high-quality video editing results across diverse scenarios. For more details and videos, please refer to the anonymous webpage: https://yu-shaonian.github.io/EditDistill-Web/.

</details>


### [122] [Real-Time Conveyor Belt Defect Detection via Edge TPU-Accelerated YOLOv11 on Raspberry Pi 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34304)
*Muhammad Nur Bijak Bestari,Mulki Indana Zulfa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出并验证了一套基于Raspberry Pi 5 + Google Coral Edge TPU的低功耗边缘AI视觉系统，采用INT8全整数量化后的YOLOv11n对输送带四类缺陷（纵向撕裂、孔洞、老化补丁、冲击穿刺）进行实时检测，mAP@0.5达0.764，在320×320分辨率下实现30–40 FPS、70–90 ms端到端延迟，并在水泥厂现场严苛粉尘环境下稳定运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输送带是水泥等重工业连续物料输送的关键环节，未检测到的结构异常会引发断带、停机和安全事故；深度学习方法虽精度高，但依赖高功耗工作站GPU，难以在偏远工业边缘点部署，因此需要低功耗、可现场落地的实时检测方案。

Method: 构建超过3000个增强样本的多源工业数据集（西爪哇某运行中水泥厂、开源数据集与自动采集）；采用轻量化YOLOv11n架构训练；进行训练后全整数量化（INT8）并编译为面向脉动张量执行（systolic tensor）的Edge TPU模型；部署于Raspberry Pi 5 + Coral Edge TPU，配IP防护外壳与RTSP PoE工业相机进行现场实测。

Result: 四类缺陷检测精度precision 0.742、recall 0.730、mAP@0.5 0.764；320×320分辨率下吞吐30–40 FPS，端到端延迟70–90 ms；熟料粉尘环境下CPU约55±5 ℃、Edge TPU约65 ℃达到热平衡，零丢帧。

Conclusion: 边缘AI可在连续制造场景中弥合高精度计算机视觉与低成本预测性维护之间的差距，为工业边缘端的实时输送带缺陷检测提供可行、经济且可靠的部署范式。

Abstract: Conveyor belts are vital arteries in continuous bulk material handling, particularly in heavy process industries such as cement manufacturing. Undetected structural anomalies (longitudinal tears, holes, degraded patch repairs, and impact punctures) cause catastrophic belt snapping, costly downtime, and severe safety hazards. While deep learning offers high accuracy, existing models rely on power-hungry workstation GPUs, hindering practical deployment at remote industrial edge points. In this paper, we propose, implement, and validate an end-to-end, low-power edge AI vision system for real-time conveyor belt defect detection deployed on a Raspberry Pi 5 accelerated by a Google Coral Edge TPU coprocessor. The system leverages an optimized YOLOv11 nano (YOLOv11n) architecture trained on a multi-source industrial corpus exceeding 3,000 augmented samples from an operational cement plant in West Java, open-source datasets, and automated captures. The model undergoes post-training full integer quantization (INT8) compiled for systolic tensor execution. Experimental results demonstrate robust detection capability, achieving precision of 0.742, recall of 0.730, and mAP@0.5 of 0.764 across four defect classes. Deployed on-site in an IP-rated enclosure interfaced with an RTSP PoE industrial camera, the system achieves stable throughput of 30-40 FPS at 320x320 resolution with an end-to-end latency of 70-90 ms. Operational profiling under harsh clinker dust confirms thermal equilibrium at 55 +/- 5 deg C on the host CPU and ~65 deg C on the Edge TPU with zero frame loss. This demonstrates that edge AI bridges the gap between high-accuracy computer vision and cost-effective predictive maintenance in continuous manufacturing.

</details>


### [123] [Learning Discriminative Prototypes via Boundary Repulsion MixUp for Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34305)
*Athena Psalta,Vasileios Tsironis,Konstantinos Karantzalos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PBR-ReID原型框架，将类间边界几何显式作为学习目标，配合Boundary Repulsion MixUp正则与两阶段训练，在行人/车辆ReID基准上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReID嵌入需对视角、光照、遮挡等类内变化鲁棒，同时保持视觉相似身份间的清晰分离；最难误差恰出现在易混淆身份交界处，而pairwise损失仅隐式塑造该区域。

Method: 每个身份用可学习原型表示，与DiNOv3骨干联合优化，原型相似度矩阵在每步训练中揭示最易混淆的身份对；据此构建Boundary Repulsion MixUp（BRM），在易混淆对之间构造显式边界代理并将特征推离该边界，而非跨身份插值样本；采用原型预热+EMA稳定联合微调的两阶段训练。

Result: 在行人ReID与车辆ReID基准上实验表现强劲，充分消融验证各组件贡献。

Conclusion: 将易混淆对之间的边界几何作为显式优化目标，并以几何感知正则替代传统MixUp插值，可有效把边界模糊转化为判别信号，提升ReID性能。

Abstract: Object re-identification requires embeddings that are robust to intra-class variation in viewpoint, illumination, and occlusion, yet preserve sharp separation between visually similar identities. The hardest errors arise precisely where confusable identities meet, a region that pairwise losses shape only implicitly. We present PBR-ReID, a prototype-based framework that makes this inter-class geometry an explicit learning target. Each identity is represented by a learnable prototype jointly optimized with a DiNOv3 backbone and the resulting prototype similarity matrix reveals, at every training step, which identities are most confusable. On this signal we build Boundary Repulsion MixUp (BRM), a geometry-aware regularizer that converts standard MixUp's weakness into a discriminative signal. Rather than interpolating samples across confusable identities, which blurs the boundaries that matter most, BRM constructs an explicit boundary proxy between each confusable pair and repels their features away from it, converting boundary ambiguity into a discriminative signal. We adopt a two-stage training protocol consisting of a prototype warmup phase followed by EMA-stabilized joint fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on person and vehicle ReID benchmarks demonstrate strong performance, with thorough ablations confirming each component's contribution. Code is available at https://github.com/psaltaath/pbr-reid.

</details>


### [124] [TopX: Text-to-Motion for Unseen Skeletons from the Skeleton File Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34321)
*Tengjiao Sun,Pengcheng Fang,Xiaoyu Zhan,Dongjie Fu,Xiaohao Cai,Hansung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopX：73M 参数的流匹配 Transformer，仅凭骨架树、静止姿态与关节名即可为任意未见过骨骼生成文本驱动动作，并配套 SkelShift 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作模型只能对训练所用骨骼生成动作，而新角色仅以一个骨架文件的形式到来；本文研究无需训练片段、模板、重定向或微调的“未见骨架”文本到动作生成。

Method: 提出 TopX 流匹配 Transformer，输出关节旋转与根轨迹。动作按静止姿态与骨架尺度归一化；静止姿态以与各关节朝向约定无关的世界坐标系描述符输入，训练中随机化静止约定。注意力在关节维与帧维因式分解，并采用面向任意拓扑骨架图的时空编码：关节间用骨骼图热核坐标旋转 Q/K（对关节顺序与拉普拉斯特征基不变），帧间用旋转式帧序编码。

Result: 构建 SkelShift：统一编解码下四个语料、143,334 条带字幕片段、4–142 关节骨架；按片段/绑定/树类/物种留出 2,043 个骨架上的 4,232 条片段。相较同数据训练的 UniMate，在无训练骨架的测试绑定上，描述符 FID 由 5.73 降至 4.58（以动力学 FID 调优引导时），动力学 FID 由 1.48 降至 0.55（以描述符 FID 调优引导时），且触地穿地帧更少。

Conclusion: 通过骨架固有几何（静止姿态、骨架图热核坐标）与拓扑无关的时空注意力，TopX 实现了跨骨架、免重定向、免微调的文本到动作泛化，并在多重质量指标上优于基线。

Abstract: Text-to-motion models generate for the skeletons they were trained on, while a new character arrives as a skeleton file and nothing else. We study text-to-motion for skeletons that no training clip has used, with no clips of the character, no template, no retargeting and no fine-tuning. We present TopX, a 73M-parameter flow-matching transformer that generates joint rotations and a root trajectory for any kinematic tree from its tree, rest pose and joint names. Motion is normalised by the rest pose and the size of the skeleton, and the rest pose enters as a world-frame descriptor that does not depend on how the rig's author oriented each joint, with rest conventions randomised in training. Attention is factorised over the joints and the frames of a motion and carries a spatial--temporal encoding designed for skeleton graphs of any topology: queries and keys are rotated across joints by a heat-kernel coordinate of the skeleton graph, which is defined for every tree and does not change with the order of its joints or the eigenbasis of its Laplacian, and across frames by a rotary encoding of frame order. To measure what this buys, we build SkelShift: four motion corpora under one codec, 143,334 captioned clips on skeletons of 4 to 142 joints, from which 4,232 clips on 2,043 skeletons are held out by clip, rig, tree class or species and organised along topology, joint count, body plan, species and rig source, and scored by motion quality: Fréchet distances on public kinetic features and on kinematic descriptors, foot skating and ground contact. Against UniMate trained on the same clips, on test rigs without a training clip, TopX lowers the descriptor FID from 5.73 to 4.58 when UniMate's guidance is tuned for the kinetic FID, and the kinetic FID from 1.48 to 0.55 when it is tuned for the descriptor FID, also on motions no training skeleton performs; fewer of its frames sink below the ground.

</details>


### [125] [CrossPair: Unlocking Generalist Visual Transformation Learning in Image Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34333)
*Yibo Zhao,Lifan Jiang,Senlin cheng,Tianrun Wu,Bolei Xu,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Xiaofei He,Dehai Zhao,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossPair 通过把预训练多模态图像编辑器适配为"示例对迁移"模型，从单个前-后示例中提取可迁移的变换关系并施加到查询图像上，统一了修复、编辑、虚拟试衣与风格化等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉任务仍依赖分离的系统处理修复、编辑与风格化等，而"前-后"示例对只有在可迁移变化能与示例场景解耦时才能充当共享指令；因此需要一个能跨内容分离"变换关系"与"场景外观"的统一迁移框架。

Method: 将示例前图、示例后图与查询图固定分配到原编辑器的 VLM 与 VAE 双通路：VLM 提取操作语义，VAE 保留细节外观与结构；引入仅训练时使用的 change-aware DINOv3 目标，在内容不同的场景间对齐特征变化方向，从而在推理阶段无需额外编码器即可引导输出遵循示例关系；并构建 Transform-360K 数据集，按共享变换对条件表征、修复、编辑、虚拟试衣与风格化的过滤图像对进行分组。

Result: 在 Relation252K 衍生的已见与留出变换上，目标一致性与编辑方向相似度优于已发布系统；消融显示语义与视觉示例通路互补，DINO 关系对齐进一步增强迁移；Transform-360K 训练提升了留出操作的总体得分。

Conclusion: 将示例对迁移问题显式建模为"语义—外观"解耦并对齐变化方向，可有效统一多种图像变换任务，且数据规模化的变换分组训练带来对未见操作的泛化增益。

Abstract: Language models switch tasks from demonstrations, but vision still often relies on separate systems for restoration, editing, and stylization. A before--after example offers a shared instruction only if the transferable change can be separated from the scene that demonstrates it. We introduce CrossPair, which adapts a pretrained multimodal image editor to transfer diverse transformations from one exemplar pair. Exemplar-before, exemplar-after, and query images take fixed roles in its native VLM and VAE pathways: the VLM extracts operation semantics, while the VAE retains fine appearance and structure. A training-only, change-aware DINOv3 objective aligns feature-change directions across content-distinct scenes, guiding the output to follow the demonstrated relation without an inference-time encoder. We construct Transform-360K by grouping filtered pairs from conditional representations, restoration, editing, virtual try-on, and stylization under shared transformations. On Relation252K-derived seen and held-out transformations, \method improves target agreement and edit-direction similarity over released systems. Ablations show that the semantic and visual exemplar routes are complementary and that DINO relation alignment further strengthens transfer; Transform-360K training also improves aggregate held-out-operation scores.

</details>


### [126] [Birth-Death Structural Learning for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34344)
*Quan Tran Hong,Long Nguyen-Chi,Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“first-variation birth-death control”，用重建损失的一阶变分得分替代位置梯度/不透明度等代理信号，指导3DGS的克隆-分裂-剪枝，在多基准上取得最优质量，低高斯预算下增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS的质量-效率权衡高度依赖自适应密度控制，但现有克隆/分裂/剪枝规则使用的是位置梯度、不透明度、图像空间误差等代理信号，无法直接回答密度控制的结构性问题：应在何处新增高斯以降低多视图重建损失、哪些基元可近乎无损地剪除。

Method: 冻结当前高斯集合，为每个基元附加辅助“质量坐标”，对多视图损失关于该坐标求导得到变分得分；证明该得分是Splat Regression Model一阶变分的坐标，从而构成born-death结构移动的严格局部下降代理：高得分高斯作为新生位点，低得分且低不透明度高斯被移除。

Result: 在标准3DGS基准上将该得分集成进3DGS-MCMC，取得state-of-the-art重建质量并优于现有方法，在高斯预算紧张时增益最显著。

Conclusion: 基于梯度的变分度量可作为3DGS中代理启发式规则的有原则替代方案，为密度控制提供理论依据。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a standard representation for real-time novel-view synthesis. Yet, its quality-efficiency trade-off still relies heavily on adaptive density control. Existing cloning, splitting, and pruning rules use proxy signals, such as position gradients, opacity, and image-space error. Although effective in practice, these signals do not directly answer the structural question behind density control: where should new Gaussians be allocated to reduce the multi-view reconstruction loss, and which existing primitives can be pruned with minimal penalty. To address this, we propose first-variation birth-death control for 3DGS, a principled approach that replaces heuristic structural decisions with a variational score derived from the reconstruction loss. At each structural update, we freeze the current Gaussian population, attach auxiliary mass coordinates to each primitive, and differentiate the multiview loss with respect to these coordinates. We demonstrate that this score is a coordinate of the first variation of the Splat Regression Model, giving a rigorous local descent surrogate for birth-death moves: high-score Gaussians are selected as birth sites, while low-score, low-opacity Gaussians are selected for removal. Across standard 3DGS benchmarks, integrating this score into 3DGS-MCMC yields state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and outperforms existing methods, delivering the most significant gains under tight Gaussian budgets. Ultimately, our work provides compelling evidence that gradient-based variational metrics offer a principled alternative to proxy heuristics in 3DGS.

</details>


### [127] [Spatially-Aware Self-Organizing Graph Autoencoders via Localized Hebbian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34346)
*Robin Koç,Hazal Moğultay Özcan,Fatoş T. Yarman Vural*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S-LooM：以空间互相关与可学习空间核替代标量消息传递，并用最近邻上采样和软融合训练课程，使动态自组织图自编码器的潜在拓扑更稳定、抗纠缠与噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态自编码器（基于Hebbian可塑性的拓扑自组织）用全局平均池化和标量连接刻画特征图关系，丢失了输入图像的空间信息；同时损失函数与训练动态的突变会引发表示冲击，导致特征学习不稳定。

Method: 提出空间感知自组织图自编码器S-LooM：（1）用空间互相关与空间核在特征图间建立动态连接，以可学习空间核取代静态标量消息传递；（2）解码端用最近邻上采样消除高频反卷积伪影；（3）采用软融合（soft-blending）训练课程，使潜在拓扑在训练中平滑对齐。

Result: 与最先进的动态架构相比，S-LooM获得更稳定的拓扑结构，涌现的图结构对特征纠缠和噪声更具抵抗力；即使在高连接密度下也能形成对应光照、形状、纹理等视觉属性的功能社区。

Conclusion: 将空间信息显式引入自组织图连接并用平滑训练课程，可有效缓解动态自编码器的结构瓶颈与表示冲击，形成语义可解释、鲁棒的潜在拓扑。

Abstract: Traditional autoencoders rely on static network architectures to learn latent representations. Recently we suggested a dynamic architecture inspired by Hebbian plasticity, enabling topological self-organization among convolutional feature maps (R. Koç et al., Proceedings of the SIU, 2026). Early implementations of this paradigm infer relationships among feature maps using global average pooling and model their interactions through scalar-valued connections. This approach falls short to fully exploit the underlying spatial information, embedded in input images. Furthermore, abrupt transitions between different loss functions and training dynamics can introduce representation shocks, leading to unstable feature learning. In this paper, we introduce a Spatially-Aware Self-Organizing Graph Autoencoder (S-LooM) to address these structural bottlenecks. By using spatial cross-correlation, and spatial kernels, S-LooM establishes dynamic connections among feature maps. We replace static scalar message passing of autoencoders with learnable spatial kernels and eliminate high-frequency deconvolutional artifacts observed in decoders using nearest-neighbor upsampling. Via a soft-blending training curriculum, the latent topology aligns smoothly during training. Our experiments show that S-LooM yields a more stable topology, compared to the state of the art dynamic architectures. Meaning, the graph structure that emerge has more resistance to feature entanglement and noise. It forms functional communities responsible for visual attributes, such as illumination, shape and texture even under high connection densities.

</details>


### [128] [A Computationally Efficient Non-Interpolation Virtual Trellis Approach for Mammogram Gradient-based Patterns Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34352)
*Shahid Rasheed,Muhammad Rashid,Seifedine Kadry,Salman A. AlQahtani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需插值、无需转换为六边形网格的虚拟六边形网格（NVHT）算子，直接在方形图像上施加滤波核，降低计算与存储开销，提升乳腺癌分类的特征提取效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医学图像分析（尤其乳腺癌分类）在沿对角轴的模式捕获、计算效率与特征缓冲存储方面存在不足；经典算子（卷积、梯度分析）计算复杂、对硬件要求高，且六边形网格处理通常需要变换与插值。

Method: 设计非插值虚拟六边形网格（NVHT）算子：在原始方形图像上直接应用滤波核，不做插值、不进行方形到六边形的网格变换，以虚拟六边形像素表示实现多方向高灵敏度特征提取。

Result: 相比传统方法，在保持多方向高灵敏度的同时降低存储需求，提升特征提取效率与疾病分类准确率。

Conclusion: NVHT算子在计算与存储成本上更具优势，可作为医学图像特征分析与乳腺癌分类的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Feature analysis is a crucial step in medical image analysis, particularly in breast cancer classification. Traditional image analysis methods often struggle with optimal patterns along the diagonal axis, computational efficiency and feature buffer storage. Different classical operators require high-efficiency hardware for the complex operations, such as image convolution and gradient analysis, which are used for processing complex machine learning models for training and test operations. Here, in this research, we introduce a non-interpolation virtual trellis-based (NVHT) operator that utilizes a filter kernel over the same square image without transformation to a hexagonal trellis. This proposed method is very efficient in reducing computational and storage costs. The proposed operator is used for improved feature extraction efficiency and accuracy in disease classification. This method outperforms conventional approaches by leveraging a hexagonal pixel representation while maintaining high sensitivity in different directions with less storage.

</details>


### [129] [No Cones, No Duals: A Fast and Scalable Solver for NRSfM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34403)
*Suryansh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种快速、可扩展的非刚性结构从运动（NRSfM）求解器，用可微欧氏校正与展开的谱近端求解器替代昂贵的SDP锥规划和ADMM迭代，在保持竞争力的重建精度下取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于形状空间分解的高性能NRSfM方法依赖两类昂贵求解器：用于欧氏校正的半定规划（SDP）锥求解器，以及用于恢复低秩形状的ADMM求解器。这些步骤在长序列和密集点轨迹上扩展性差、耗时高。

Method: 1) 旋转估计：引入可微欧氏校正，直接对每条序列优化小型因子，再用行极投影将每个相机块映射到最近的航正交矩阵；2) 形状估计：提出展开的谱近端求解器，在重排后的形状域中结合重投影梯度步与加权奇异值收缩，并采用FISTA式重启与延拓策略；谱收缩算子本身为经典方法，但被嵌入固定可微求解器中，同时保持NRSfM几何约束。方法类别无关、无需外部训练数据，且可映射到面向加速器的线性代数原语。

Result: 在稀疏、密集、含噪、缺失数据及长序列基准上均取得显著加速且重建精度具竞争力；在含28,887个跟踪点的密集序列上运行时提速7倍至1000倍。

Conclusion: 通过可微欧氏校正与展开谱近端求解替代SDP与ADMM，可在不依赖训练数据的前提下实现可扩展、加速器友好的NRSfM求解，兼顾效率与精度。

Abstract: Non-rigid structure from motion (NRSfM) aims to recover camera motion and time-varying 3D shape from tracked 2D image measurements. Recent high-performing methods show that shape-space factorization remains highly effective for this task. These methods often use costly solvers for two key steps. First, a semidefinite programming (SDP) cone solver to perform Euclidean correction. Next, an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) solver to recover the low-rank shape. These steps often scale poorly to long sequences and dense point tracks. We propose a fast, scalable NRSfM solver that avoids these costly steps. For rotation estimation, we introduce a differentiable Euclidean correction that directly optimizes small factors for each sequence. A row-polar projection then maps each estimated camera block to the closest row-orthonormal matrix. For shape estimation, we introduce an unrolled spectral-proximal solver. It combines reprojection-gradient steps with weighted singular-value shrinkage in the reshuffled shape domain. This solver further uses FISTA-style restart and continuation. The spectral shrinkage operator itself is classical, but we embed it in a fixed differentiable solver, while preserving NRSfM's geometric constraints. The proposed approach is category-agnostic, requires no external training data, and maps naturally to modern accelerator-friendly linear algebra primitives. Across sparse, dense, noisy, missing-data, and long-sequence benchmarks, our solver achieves substantial speedups with competitive reconstruction accuracy, including 7x-1000x runtime improvements on dense sequences with 28,887 tracked points.

</details>


### [130] [On Detection Reliability for Small Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34432)
*Olivier Rukundo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究检验检测器预测框作为小目标分割区域的可靠性，发现将分割限制在预测框内会遗漏框外的匹配分割，存在假阴性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用两阶段范式先用检测框限定分割范围，但小目标场景下预测框是否可靠、框外是否会遗漏真实目标尚缺乏验证。

Method: 以腺病毒颗粒为小目标，采用YOLO26x检测、YOLO26x-Sem分割，并以Mask R-CNN作端到端检测+分割对照；按质心将预测掩码与真值掩码匹配（优先用真值掩码质心作参考），统计匹配质心位于预测框内/外的数量及无匹配分割的真值数。

Result: YOLO26系列：34个参考目标（47.89%）匹配分割质心位于至少一个预测框内，8个（11.27%）在框外，29个（40.85%）无匹配分割；Mask R-CNN：分别为69（97.18%）、0（0.00%）和2（2.82%）。

Conclusion: 预测框内限定分割会排除框外的匹配分割预测，可能引入假阴性；小目标分割不宜简单依赖检测框约束。

Abstract: This study investigates whether predicted bounding boxes are reliable for determining where small-object segmentation should be performed without risking false negatives outside those boxes. The investigation leverages adenovirus particles as small objects, YOLO26x for detection, YO-LO26x-Sem for segmentation, and Mask R-CNN for both detection and segmentation. Segmenta-tion-model predictions were matched to ground-truth adenovirus masks or reference objects using their centroids, with ground-truth mask centroids preferred over ground-truth bounding-box centroids as references for matching. Matched predicted-mask centroids were considered as inside at least one detector-predicted bounding box or outside all such boxes, while ground-truth masks without matched predictions were recorded as having no matched segmentation. For the YOLO26 models, 34 reference objects (47.89%) had matched segmentation centroids inside at least one pre-dicted box, 8 (11.27%) had centroids outside all predicted boxes, and 29 (40.85%) had no matched predicted segmentation. For the Mask R-CNN model, the corresponding counts were 69 (97.18%), 0 (0.00%), and 2 (2.82%). These preliminary experiments demonstrated that restricting segmentation to detector-predicted bounding boxes risks excluding matched segmentation predictions outside those boxes, potentially introducing false negatives.

</details>


### [131] [Task Relevance-Driven Prompt Expansion for Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34461)
*Jiahua Dong,Wenqi Liang,Yang Cong,Yichen Li,Hongyuan Yu,Henghui Ding,Yulun Zhang,Salman Khan,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Task Relevance-driven Prompt Expansion (TRPE)模型，通过OOD检测与半径自适应DBSCAN摆脱对新任务先验知识的依赖，并利用全局任务相关性选择关键prompt对与高相关旧任务进行prompt拼接训练，配合软正交约束缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦持续学习(FCL)方法多假设全局服务器已知新任务的到达时间与类别数量，现实中不成立；同时忽视新旧任务间正向语义关系、且受旧任务无关关系干扰，加剧灾难性遗忘。

Method: 1) 本地客户端用OOD检测器判别新任务到达，代理服务器用半径自适应DBSCAN推断新任务类别数；2) 计算新任务原始梯度与其在旧任务特征空间正交方向梯度的亲和度，从全局视角度量新旧任务语义相关性，据此确定新任务的关键prompt对并识别高相关旧任务，拼接其prompt进行训练；3) 对本地key与prompt梯度施加软正交约束，平衡新任务学习与旧任务遗忘。

Result: 在多种数据集与骨干网络上的实验验证了TRPE模型的有效性。

Conclusion: TRPE无需新任务先验知识即可在联邦场景下实现持续学习，并能利用正向跨任务语义关系、抑制无关干扰，在缓解灾难性遗忘方面取得良好效果。

Abstract: Federated continual learning (FCL) has attracted considerable interest by enabling local clients to continually acquire knowledge from new tasks. However, most FCL methods assume that the global server has prior knowledge of the arrival times and number of categories for new tasks, which is unrealistic in the real world. Furthermore, they neglect the importance of positive inter-task semantic relationships when learning new tasks and are significantly affected by irrelevant relationships from old tasks, thereby exacerbating catastrophic forgetting. To resolve the above challenges, we develop a novel Task Relevance-driven Prompt Expansion (TRPE) model. Specifically, to eliminate the reliance on prior knowledge of new tasks, we propose an out-of-distribution detector to identify the arrival of new tasks within local clients, along with a radius-adaptive DBSCAN to infer the quantity of categories in new tasks on the proxy server. Moreover, to explore positive inter-task relationships, we calculate the affinity between the original gradient for learning new task and its gradient orthogonal to the feature space of old tasks to evaluate semantic relevance between old and new tasks from a global perspective. Then, we use this global task relevance to determine the key-prompt pair for new tasks and identify highly related old tasks. After concatenating their prompts for training, we can leverage such positive inter-task relations to improve the continual learning of newly arriving tasks. To balance the learning of new tasks and the forgetting of old ones, a soft orthogonality constraint is applied to the local key and prompt gradients. Experiments spanning diverse datasets and backbones confirm the efficacy of our TRPE model.

</details>


### [132] [PhysCreator: Creating Physically-Attributed 3D Assets at Will](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34473)
*Shiyan Zheng,Ming Li,Lei Han,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysCreator：从图像直接生成可仿真、具备空间变化物理属性的3D表示的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到3D生成结果多为静态表示，无法在动态交互下表现出物理合理行为；已有物理属性预测多为事后简单预测或依赖昂贵的逐实例优化。

Method: 将物理属性估计重构进统一3D表示学习框架：把图像观测提升为空间结构化的体素对齐隐空间，前馈式联合建模与推断几何、外观和物理属性；并利用视觉-语言蒸馏，将VLM的材料感知语义先验迁移为空间落地的体素级监督，避免密集物理真值标注。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在视觉质量、物理合理性与泛化能力上优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 统一的体素对齐隐空间加视觉-语言蒸馏，可在无需密集物理标注的情况下前馈生成仿真就绪、物理可信的3D资产。

Abstract: Significant advancements in 3D asset creation have enabled high-fidelity 3D generation from images. However, most existing representations remain inherently static, lacking the ability to model physically plausible behaviors under dynamic interactions. We present \textbf{PhysCreator}, a unified framework that directly generates simulation-ready 3D representations with spatially varying physical properties from images. Unlike prior approaches that treat physical attributes as simple post-hoc predictions or rely on expensive per-instance optimization, we reformulate physical property estimation within a unified 3D representation learning framework. We introduce a voxel-aligned latent space by lifting image observations into a spatially structured 3D representation, in which geometry, appearance, and physical properties are jointly modeled and inferred in a feed-forward manner. To enable scalable learning without densely annotated physical ground truth, we leverage vision-language distillation to transfer material-aware semantic priors from powerful vision-language models into spatially grounded voxel-level supervision. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior visual quality, physical plausibility, and generalization compared to prior approaches.

</details>


### [133] [Conviction Without Evidence: Verdict Stability Is Independent of Visual Grounding in Medical Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34520)
*Federico Felizzi,Francesco Andrea Causio,Bianca Destro Castaniti,Olivia Riccomi,Michele Ferramola,Vittorio De Vita,Alessandro Tosi,Antonio Cristiano,Alessia Longo,Lorenzo De Mori,Chiara Battipaglia,Melissa Sawaya,Luigi De Angelis,Marcello Di Pumpo,Alessandra Piscitelli,Pietro Eric Risuleo,Giulia Vojvodic,Mariapia Vassalli,Nicolo Scarsi,Manuel Del Medico*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文检验视觉语言模型在医学问答中"无图也能诊断"的问题：模型对空图像占位符给出的诊断是否比基于真实图像的诊断更"脆弱"。在60道意大利国考医学题上交叉图像替换与两档对抗压力，未发现无根据判断更易被推翻的证据，且能被用户合理查询的认知信号均无法区分"有根据"与"编造"的诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明视觉语言模型在医学问答中可绕过图像、仅凭临床病例文字得出正确诊断。作者追问这一"无根据推理"是否体现在模型对答案的"坚定程度"上——即模型是否以同等强度捍卫由空白占位符得出的诊断，从而考察"证据接地"是否可作为可检测的行为信号。

Method: 采用图像替换协议×两档对抗压力（L1无内容质疑、L4伪造共识）的交叉设计，在60道意大利国家考试医学题上做配对的项目级分析，统计判决翻转率、多轮对话中的判决变化次数（10轮）及翻转目标，并记录基线对空白图像的标记与弃答行为。

Result: L4伪造共识下，有真图翻转率94.4%、无图98.1%（Δ=+0.037，单侧95%上界+0.105）；L1无内容质疑下分别为62.5%与51.8%（Δ=−0.107，95%CI[−0.209,−0.001]），点估计方向与"接地检测器"所需方向相反。两档压力动力学相反：L4下模型首轮即翻转且总翻至被论证的确切选项（110/110），随后冻结，平均1.9次变化；L1下始终不稳定，平均7.4次变化。L1下的不稳定追踪题目内在模糊性而非证据可得性。翻转几乎全为破坏性（99/100）。基线时模型在35/60题上标记空白图像、4–6题弃答，但弃答在压力下不存活。

Conclusion: 无证据表明无接地的判断更脆弱；模型会一次翻转至被论证选项后冻结（被说服）或持续震荡（被扰乱），二者都不构成接地信号。用户可合理咨询的任何认知信号都无法区分有根据的诊断与编造诊断，这对医学VLM的可靠性与用户信任校准构成重要警示。

Abstract: Vision--language models applied to medical question answering can reach correct diagnoses without using the diagnostic image, inferring instead from the clinical vignette. We ask whether this is visible in how \emph{firmly} a model holds its answer: does a model defend a diagnosis derived from a blank placeholder as tenaciously as one derived from a real medical image? Crossing an image-substitution protocol with two levels of adversarial pressure on 60 Italian State Examination items, we find no evidence that ungrounded verdicts are more fragile. In a paired item-level analysis, fabricated consensus (L4) overturns 94.4\% of verdicts with the real image and 98.1\% without ($Δ= +0.037$; one-sided 95\% upper bound $+0.105$); contentless doubt (L1) overturns 62.5\% and 51.8\% ($Δ= -0.107$, 95\% CI $[-0.209, -0.001]$), the point estimate running opposite to the direction a grounding detector requires. The two levels nonetheless produce opposite \emph{dynamics}: under L4 the model flips once, always to the exact option argued for (110/110 first-turn flips), and then freezes, averaging 1.9 verdict changes over ten turns; under L1 it never settles, averaging 7.4 changes. Instability under contentless doubt tracks intrinsic item ambiguity rather than evidence availability, and so does not serve as a grounding signal. Flips are almost entirely corrupting (99/100 across both levels). The model is not unaware of the missing evidence---it flags the blank image at baseline on 35 of 60 items and abstains on 4--6---yet those abstentions do not survive contact with pressure. None of the epistemic signals a user could plausibly consult distinguishes a grounded diagnosis from a confabulated one.

</details>


### [134] [VTC-AUDIT: An Interventional Reliability Audit for Visual-Token Compression under Paired Image Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34545)
*Isaac,Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VTC-AUDIT 框架，在“全模型两张图都对、压缩模型净图也对”的条件子集上度量压缩引入的增量失败风险，发现按偏移图重选保留位置会使失败率系统性上升，并指出净图不确定性是更有效的排序信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉 token 压缩评估只看净准确率与效率平均值，掩盖了图像偏移下压缩模型出错而原始模型仍正确的失败模式，缺少对压缩所引入的增量风险的条件化度量。

Method: 定义仅在 full model 于净图与偏移图均正确、且压缩模型于净图正确的配对样本上估计增量风险；四臂设计交叉两个二值选择（由哪张图决定保留位置、由哪张图提供视觉特征）。审计 3 个开源 8-9B 视觉语言模型、4 个任务、8 个预设偏移，比较两种免训练压缩器（硬剪枝 DivPrune 与剪枝合并 ApET），统一 25% 保留位置预算，共 708,000 条推理记录；并在独立重采样清单上复现，另做池化监控审计（support Jaccard 不稳定性 vs 净图压缩模型不确定性，AUROC 阈值 0.65）。

Result: 在偏移图上重选保留位置（而非复用净图位置）使全部 6 个模型-压缩器组合的失败率上升：DivPrune 上升 3.04–9.60 个百分点，ApET 上升 2.05–4.20 个百分点，所有 95% 区间均不含 0；第二份重采样清单复现全部 6 项上升。池化监控审计中，support Jaccard 不稳定性在 6 个设置下均未达 AUROC 0.65 的实用阈值，而净图压缩模型不确定性全部达标。

Conclusion: 支持集翻转在所审计基准混合下是弱的排序信号，净图压缩模型不确定性信息量显著更高；建议未来视觉 token 压缩评估在净准确率与效率之外一并报告条件可靠性。

Abstract: Visual-token compression is usually evaluated by clean accuracy and efficiency, but these averages do not reveal failures that compression introduces under an image shift where the uncompressed model stays correct. We introduce VTC-AUDIT, which estimates this incremental risk only on pairs where the full model is correct on both images and the compressed model is correct on the clean image. The four arms then cross two binary choices: which image selects the retained token positions, and which image supplies the visual features. We audit three open 8-9B vision-language models on four tasks and eight pre-specified shifts. We evaluate two training-free compressors---hard-pruning DivPrune and prune-and-merge ApET---at a matched 25% retained-position budget, producing 708,000 inference records. Reselecting positions on the shifted image, rather than reusing the clean-image positions, raises the failure rate in all six model-compressor settings. The increase is 3.04-9.60 percentage points for DivPrune and 2.05-4.20 percentage points for ApET, with every 95% interval above zero. Repeating the audit on a separately resampled second manifest reproduces all six increases. In a pooled monitor audit across the four benchmarks, support Jaccard instability fails the practical AUROC criterion of 0.65 in all six settings, whereas clean compressed-model uncertainty clears it. Thus, support turnover is a weak ranking signal under the audited benchmark mixture, while clean compressed-model uncertainty is substantially more informative. These results suggest that future visual-token compression evaluations should report conditional reliability alongside clean accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [135] [GRASP: Group-Adaptive Hybrid RL-SFT Post-Training for Medical VQA without Reasoning Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34561)
*Wanqi Dong,Wanyue Dong,Jingze Ge,Mehul Motani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRASP 在每次更新中同时结合强化学习与监督微调，仅用短答案即可让通用视觉语言模型在医学 VQA 上兼顾拟合与泛化，仅调 1% 参数即在域内与 OOD 上超越多种训练目标及更强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学 VQA 标注数据稀缺、答案短且缺乏推理标注。SFT 易过拟合标签并放大偏差，损害域外泛化；RL（如 GRPO）泛化更好但以组内对比为信号，全对或全错组无正确性信号，从不以金标准答案为目标；现有 SFT-RL 混合方法或分阶段/逐题切换，或依赖专家或更强模型的推理轨迹，而医学 VQA 数据不具备这些。

Method: 提出 GRASP：在每次参数更新中融合 RL 与 SFT，且仅使用短答案。RL 部分使模型自主探索推理；SFT 部分在该推理之后监督金标准答案，提供 RL 缺失的直接监督；二者权重依据模型对每题的正确程度自适应调节。采用 LoRA 仅微调约 1% 参数。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL-8B、InternVL3-8B、Gemma 3-4B 上，GRASP 在域内与 OOD 开放式问题上均优于所有对比训练目标；在覆盖八种成像模态的 OOD 基准上，其训练的 InternVL3-8B 与 Qwen3-VL-8B 的开放式准确率超过全部 10 个对比的通用与医学 VLM，包括数据量多至 2600 倍或可训练参数多至 250 倍以上的模型。

Conclusion: GRASP 以单阶段、仅短答案的自适应 RL+SFT 融合与 LoRA 高效微调，实现医学 VQA 的强域内拟合与优异 OOD 泛化；并开源 12.9K 短答案数据集 MedTerse 及代码。

Abstract: Adapting general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) to medical visual question answering (VQA) is valuable for helping clinicians read images and make diagnoses, but annotated data are scarce and provide only short answers, without reasoning annotations. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) fits these labels but overfits to them and amplifies their biases, eroding out-of-domain (OOD) generalization. Reinforcement learning (RL) better preserves generalization but never uses the gold answer as a training target: GRPO-style algorithms learn from the contrast between correct and incorrect answers within a group of sampled responses, so all-correct or all-wrong groups give no correctness signal. Existing SFT-RL hybrids either apply SFT and RL in separate stages or switch between them per question, or need reasoning traces from experts or stronger models, which medical VQA data lack. We propose GRASP, which combines RL and SFT in every update using only short answers: RL lets the model explore its own reasoning, SFT teaches the gold answer after that reasoning to supply the direct supervision RL lacks, and their balance adapts to how well the model answers each question. Tuning only about 1% of the parameters with LoRA, GRASP outperforms all compared training objectives both in domain and on OOD open-ended questions across Qwen3-VL-8B, InternVL3-8B and Gemma 3-4B. On an OOD benchmark spanning eight imaging modalities, GRASP-trained InternVL3-8B and Qwen3-VL-8B beat all 10 compared general and medical VLMs in open-ended accuracy, including models trained on up to 2,600 times more data or with over 250 times more trainable parameters. We release MedTerse, a dataset of 12.9K short-answer questions, and our code at https://github.com/sea-comet/GRASP-MedVQA.

</details>


### [136] [Recursive CLIP: Vision-Language Distillation with Looped Transformer Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34563)
*Szymon Mazurek,Alberto Presta,Michał Byra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究将CLIP的transformer块递归复用以保持计算深度、增强参数共享，发现CLIP可承受显著的块复用，其影响取决于编码器与下游任务，并带来参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等对比视觉-语言模型通常在各深度层使用独立参数化的transformer块。作者想探究这些块能否被递归复用，同时保留CLIP的可迁移能力，并借此提升参数效率。

Method: 构建递归transformer骨干：用N个块在前向中重复M次，并固定N·M等于标准ViT-B深度以保持计算深度、逐步增加参数共享。提出两族模型：rCLIP（仅视觉编码器递归）与r²CLIP（文本编码器也递归）。在直接对比训练与从DFN-2B预训练的ViT-B/16教师做知识蒸馏（使用CC3M、CC12M）两种设定下研究。

Result: 在零样本分类与跨模态检索上评估，并考察少样本学习、视觉定位与概念遗忘等适应性。结果表明CLIP能容忍大量递归块复用，但效果依赖编码器类型和下游任务；递归在构造上即带来参数效率，因需存储和适配的不同块更少。

Conclusion: 递归块复用是视觉-语言模型一个实用的设计空间，对知识迁移、下游适配与参数效率均有启示。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models such as CLIP typically rely on independently parameterized transformer blocks across depth. We investigate the extent to which these blocks can instead be reused recurrently while preserving the transferable capabilities of CLIP. We study recursive transformer backbones in which a small set of $N$ transformer blocks is applied $M$ times per forward pass. By holding $N \cdot M$ fixed to the depth of a standard ViT-B, our models preserve computational depth while progressively increasing parameter sharing.
