We consider two recursive CLIP model families: rCLIP, where only the vision encoder is recursive, and r$^2$CLIP, where recurrence is also introduced in the text encoder. We study these models under both direct contrastive training and through knowledge distillation from a strong ViT-B/16 teacher pretrained on DFN-2B, using the substantially smaller CC3M and CC12M datasets. We evaluate the resulting models on zero-shot classification and cross-modal retrieval, and further study their adaptability through few-shot learning, visual grounding, and concept unlearning. Our results show that CLIP can tolerate substantial recurrent block reuse, although its effect depends on the encoder and downstream task. Recurrence also provides parameter efficiency by construction, since fewer distinct transformer blocks need to be stored and adapted. Our results establish recurrent block reuse as a practical design space for vision-language models and highlight its implications for knowledge transfer, downstream adaptation, and parameter efficiency.

</details>


### [137] [FlockWorld: Toward Population-Scale Multi-Agent Video World Models through Artificial Life Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34564)
*Meryem Koksal,Yunzhe Wang,Volkan Ustun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出群体规模多智能体视频世界模型测试床FlockWorld与联合生成模型FlockDiT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频世界模型多局限于单玩家，已有的多人模型仅支持小规模、固定数量的智能体，缺乏可扩展的群体尺度多智能体视频生成评测平台。

Method: 构建基于Reynolds Boids算法的人工生命环境FlockWorld：渲染同步的第一人称（自中心）视图裁剪、为不同相机智能体分配独特颜色以跨视图保持身份一致、将规则导出的加速度作为动作，并使相机智能体数量可独立于周围群体规模配置；另提出FlockDiT，一个动作条件潜在扩散Transformer，采用流匹配在FlockWorld数据上训练，联合生成多智能体的同步自中心视频。

Result: 在密度匹配设置下联合生成有助于智能体检测；扩散强制（diffusion forcing）提升长时rollout保真度。

Conclusion: FlockWorld与FlockDiT共同构成可控的多智能体视频生成测试床，为群体尺度世界模型研究提供基准。

Abstract: Most action-conditioned video world models remain limited to a single player, while existing multiplayer models support only small, fixed groups of agents. We introduce FlockWorld, an artificial-life environment built on Reynolds's Boids algorithm for studying population-scale multi-agent video world models. Specifically, FlockWorld renders synchronized egocentric crops, assigns distinct colors to preserve camera-agent identity across views, exposes rule-derived accelerations as actions, and lets the number of camera agents be configured independently of the size of the surrounding population. We further introduce FlockDiT, an action-conditioned latent diffusion transformer trained with flow matching on FlockWorld data to jointly generate synchronized egocentric videos for multiple agents. Our results suggest that joint generation benefits agent detection at density-matched settings and that diffusion forcing improves rollout fidelity. Together, FlockWorld and FlockDiT provide a controlled testbed for multi-agent video generation.

</details>


### [138] [Finetuning Prompt-Based Image Editor for Geometry-Consistent Room Defurnishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34597)
*Prakhar Kulshreshtha,Brian Pugh,Zachary Nussdorfer,Salma Jiddi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在2500对"有家具—空房"图像上微调FLUX.2的LoRA，实现单张房间图去除家具并保持3D几何一致的空房生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张房间图生成空房对削弱现实（diminished reality）、虚拟布景等XR应用有价值，但需同时满足两点：家具区域填充内容真实，且不破坏房间3D几何结构。提示词驱动的图像编辑器（如FLUX.2）真实感强，但结构忠实度不如显式布局条件修复。

Method: 构建2500对"含家具—空房"配对数据作为训练配方，在FLUX.2上微调LoRA适配器，以提升几何一致性，替代显式布局条件输入。

Result: FLUX.2结合该LoRA显著提升几何一致性，同时保留高纹理保真度。

Conclusion: 该单图修复器可作为深度感知、3D一致XR流水线的有效基础模块。

Abstract: Erasing all furniture from a single room image to generate its empty counterpart is valuable for XR applications such as diminished reality and virtual staging. The challenge is twofold: inpainting the furniture regions with realistic content, and doing so without distorting the room's 3D geometry. Prompt-driven image editors, such as FLUX.2, handle realism impressively, but their structural faithfulness is inferior to explicit layout conditioned inpainting. To improve geometric consistency, we propose a training recipe for finetuning a LoRA on 2,500 furnished-empty room pairs. FLUX.2 with our LoRA adaptor significantly improves geometric consistency while retaining the high texture fidelity. This single-photo inpainter can be an effective building block for depth-aware and 3D-consistent XR pipelines.

</details>


### [139] [An Agentic AI Workflow for Liver Lesion Segmentation and Structured Radiology Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34599)
*Fatma Ahmed Mansour,Rahatara Ferdousi,Manar Fathy Elshenawy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个证据驱动的智能体AI框架，将肝脏/病灶分割、确定性定量与结构化报告生成相连，实现可追溯的临床可解释报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动肝脏病灶分割虽已进步，但图像预测与临床可解释的影像报告之间仍存在差距；弥合该差距需要可重复且可追溯的定量测量。

Method: 基于TotalSegmentator的nnU-Net模型分割肝脏、Couinaud分段与病灶；计算工具提取体积、尺寸、计数与解剖位置；LLM按预定义报告模板组织测量结果，并对源测量做数值一致性校验。

Result: 在51例LiTS病例上，分割阶段肝脏Dice为0.947，病灶敏感度75.6%，阳性预测值68.9%；对25,494份MERLIN报告的探索性筛查中，10,208份（40.0%）存在肝脏相关异常。

Conclusion: 将定量影像证据与智能体报告生成相连接，朝以人为中心、强调可追溯性与临床相关性的医学AI迈进一步。

Abstract: Despite advances in automated liver lesion segmentation, a gap remains between image-derived predictions and clinically interpretable radiology reports. Bridging this gap requires quantitative measurements that are both reproducible and traceable. We present an evidence-grounded agentic AI framework that connects liver and lesion segmentation with deterministic quantification and structured reporting. TotalSegmentator-based nnU-Net models delineate the liver, Couinaud segments, and lesions, while computational tools extract volumes, dimensions, counts, and anatomical locations. A large language model (LLM) organizes these measurements into a predefined reporting schema, with numerical consistency checks against source measurements. On 51 LiTS cases, the segmentation stage achieved a liver Dice score of 0.947, lesion sensitivity of 75.6%, and positive predictive value of 68.9%. Exploratory screening of 25,494 MERLIN reports identified 10,208 (40.0%) with liver-related abnormalities. By connecting quantitative imaging evidence with agentic report generation, this work takes a step toward human-centered medical AI that prioritizes traceability and clinical relevance.

</details>


### [140] [FlexiMIND: Combined Pretrained Vision Features and Local Structure for Training-Free Deformable Medical Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34600)
*Mojtaba Safari,Mingzhe Hu,Shansong Wang,Suyang Xi,Joshua Lorenz,Sean Pitroda,Aytekin Oto,Ralph R. Weichselbaum,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无需训练、无需标签的两阶段可变形配准框架FlexiMIND：以冻结的FlexiCT-2D编码器特征建立上下文对应，再由MIND-SSC在ConvexAdam内细化形变；系统比较五种特征耦合策略后发现，更紧的耦合并未优于顺序式、尺度分离的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形配准中对应解剖结构常具有不同强度关系。MIND-SSC等手工模态鲁棒描述子与预训练视觉编码器特征各自提供不同的对应表示，但二者应如何组合尚不明确，需检验"更紧密集成"是否优于"顺序式、尺度分离"设计。

Method: 提出FlexiMIND两阶段框架（training-free、label-free）：第一阶段用冻结的FlexiCT-2D编码器特征建立上下文级对应；第二阶段以MIND-SSC在ConvexAdam中细化形变。在CT-CT（DIR-Lab 4DCT，10例）、MR-MR（ACDC，50例）、MR-CT（Learn2Reg Abdomen MR-CT，8对）上，按统一协议评估五种替代特征耦合策略，指标为TRE、Dice、SDlogJ与局部折叠。

Result: FlexiMIND在DIR-Lab 4DCT上TRE为1.27 mm，ACDC上DSC为0.807，Abdomen MR-CT器官平均DSC为0.825。15项耦合策略-数据集比较中仅4项优于参考方法，Dice最大绝对提升0.0043，而性能下降可达TRE 20.6%、Dice 37.9%。配准精度变化与形变场正则性变化密切相关，更好保持正则性的策略通常最接近参考性能。标志点级分析显示预训练编码器阶段主要减少大对应误差，而非均匀提升局部精度。

Conclusion: 顺序式、尺度分离的两阶段集成（编码器特征负责上下文粗对应、MIND-SSC负责局部细化）是稳健有效的组合方式；更紧密的特征耦合收益有限且易损害形变场正则性，提示耦合设计应以保持正则性为约束。

Abstract: Objective. Deformable image registration remains challenging because corresponding anatomy may exhibit different intensity relationships. Hand-crafted modality-robust descriptors such as Modality-Independent Neighborhood Descriptor with Self-Similarity Context (MIND-SSC) and features from pretrained vision encoders provide distinct representations for correspondence estimation, but how they should be combined remains unclear. We investigate whether tighter integration improves registration over a sequential, scale-separated design. Approach. We introduce FlexiMIND, a training-free and label-free two-stage framework in which frozen FlexiCT-2D encoder features establish contextual correspondence and MIND-SSC refines the deformation within ConvexAdam. Five alternative feature-coupling strategies were evaluated under a protocol across CT-CT (DIR-Lab 4DCT 4DCT, 10 cases), MR-MR (ACDC, 50 patients), and MR-CT (Learn2Reg Abdomen MR-CT, 8 pairs) registration. Performance was assessed using target registration error (TRE), Dice similarity coefficient (Dice), SDlogJ, and local folding. Main results. FlexiMIND achieved a TRE of 1.27 mm on DIR-Lab 4DCT, a DSC of 0.807 on ACDC, and an organ-averaged DSC of 0.825 on Abdomen MR-CT. Across 15 coupling-strategy dataset comparisons, only four outperformed the reference method, with a maximum absolute improvement of 0.0043 in Dice, whereas degradation reached 20.6 % in TRE and 37.9 % in Dice. Changes in registration accuracy were closely associated with changes in deformation-field regularity, and strategies that better preserved regularity generally remained closest to the reference performance. Landmark-level analysis showed that the pretrained encoder stage primarily reduced large correspondence errors rather than uniformly improving local precision. Code is available upon acceptance at https://github.com/mosaf/FlexiMIND.

</details>


### [141] [Do Words Matter? What Text Contributes to Few and Full-Shot Vision-Language Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34610)
*Arda Baris Basaran,Mesut Ceylan,Elgar Fleisch,Filipe Barata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文论证视觉-语言异常检测器从文本提示中读取的并非语义内容，而是锚点的有效尺度（单锚点对）与联合几何排布（多类别共享适配器），语义方向在训练适配器下不可辨识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作普遍将视觉-语言异常检测器的性能增益归因于文本描述的语义内容。但few-shot与full-shot设置中插入了可训练的视觉适配器，使得"语义"与"几何"两种因素混杂；而修改描述同时改变语义与几何，仅凭精度比较无法分离二者，故需以受控实验单独识别文本嵌入究竟贡献了什么。

Method: 将文本嵌入视为锚点，把其贡献分解为语义内容、有效尺度、联合锚点几何三部分。对单个锚点对，证明打分仅通过两锚点之差读取锚点，适配器可旋转以跟随任意替代方向，故该方向不可辨识；并证明差值向量长度相同的替代锚点可产生完全相同的概率图与可达损失。实验覆盖7个医学数据集、3种视觉骨干、两种监督范式、3类适配器族，使用尺度匹配的随机锚点与正交锚点、破坏尺度的锚点，并在MVFA与MadCLIP（以无文本学习尺度替换学习提示）上复现；多类别情形下进一步测试全体类别轴的联合旋转与近共线下的无意义旋转。

Result: 尺度匹配的随机与正交锚点能够追踪正确提示的表现；一旦破坏尺度匹配便出现性能差距，而在不含文本的情况下恢复尺度匹配即可消除该差距。单适配器服务多类别时，整体旋转所有类别轴无任何影响（符合理论预测）；所测CLIP提示的类别轴近乎共线，将其分散的无意义旋转在两个工业基准上反而提升性能。即使在冻结的zero-shot迁移下，随机锚点坐标系与提示坐标系的迁移效果相当，体现的是坐标一致性而非语义。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言异常检测器从提示中读取的是尺度与排布，而非语义内容；"语义增益"的解释在few-/full-shot设置下不成立，提示工程与提示学习的作用应被重新理解为锚点尺度与几何的调节。

Abstract: Vision-language anomaly detectors describe normal and abnormal appearance with text, and their gains are commonly credited to the semantic content of these descriptions. This attribution is plausible in the zero-shot setting, but few- and full-shot detectors interpose a trained visual adapter, and in that setting it has not been isolated. Changing a description changes both its meaning and its geometry, so accuracy comparisons cannot separate the two. We ask what a fixed pair of text embeddings, or anchors, contributes to such a detector: semantic content, effective scale, or joint anchor geometry. For a single anchor pair, the answer is effective scale alone. The score reads the anchors only through their difference, and a trained adapter can rotate to follow any replacement direction, leaving that direction, and with it the linguistic content, unidentifiable. We prove that replacement anchors with the same difference-vector length permit exactly the same probability maps and attainable losses. Across seven medical datasets, three vision backbones, both supervision regimes, and three adapter families, scale-matched random and orthogonal anchors track correct prompts; breaking the scale match opens a gap that restoring it closes without text. The same pattern holds in MVFA and when MadCLIP's learned prompts are replaced by a text-free learned scale. When one adapter serves many categories, the answer expands to their joint arrangement. Rotating all category axes together changes nothing, as predicted. For the tested CLIP prompts, these axes are nearly collinear, and meaning-free rotations that spread them apart improve performance on both industrial benchmarks. Even under frozen zero-shot transfer, a random anchor frame transfers as well as the prompt frame: coordinate consistency, not meaning. What these detectors read from their prompts is scale and arrangement, not semantic content.

</details>


### [142] [P2P-Former: Bridging the Supervision Gap Between Point and Proposal in Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34618)
*Yunchuan Ma,Laiyun Qing,Sai Xu,Guorong Li,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P2P-Former，一个点监督到提案级学习的Transformer框架，通过点感知提案生成(PPG)与点引导语义增强(PSE)及点-提案约束损失(PPCL)，弥合点监督时序动作定位与全监督之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点监督时序动作定位标注成本低（每个动作实例仅需一帧），但点标注信息量远少于提案标注，现有方法主要依赖多实例学习进行片段级分类，导致与全监督模型存在明显性能差距。核心难点有两点：(1)如何生成可靠的提案作为伪真值；(2)如何将点级线索有效融入全监督范式。

Method: P2P-Former：①点感知提案生成(PPG)——用点感知重要性估计函数修正基础定位器输出的提案置信度，再对点标注与候选提案做最优匹配以筛选可靠伪真值；②点引导语义增强(PSE)——从点标注特征聚合原型，经Transformer交互注入片段特征；③点到提案约束损失(PPCL)——强制每个预测提案包含其标注点，提升定位精度。

Result: 在THUMOS14与ActivityNet-1.3数据集上，方法优于多个现有先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将点级监督与提案级学习有机衔接，P2P-Former有效缩小了点监督与全监督时序动作定位的性能差距，验证了可靠伪真值生成与点语义增强结合的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, point-supervised temporal action localization has attracted increasing attention due to its low annotation cost (only one frame per action instance) and competitive detection performance. However, since point annotations are less complete than proposal annotations, existing methods mainly rely on multiple instance learning for snippet-level classification, which leads to a performance gap compared to fully supervised models. To bridge this gap, we propose P2P-Former, a novel point-to-proposal Transformer-based framework that effectively connects point-level supervision with proposal-level learning for temporal action localization. In this work, we identify two key challenges in transitioning from point-level supervision to full supervision: (1) how to generate reliable proposals as pseudo ground-truth, and (2) how to effectively integrate point-level cues into the fully supervised paradigm. To address the first challenge, we propose a Point-aware Proposal Generation (PPG) strategy that refines the confidence of proposals produced by a base locator using a point-aware importance estimation function, and then performs optimal matching between point annotations and candidate proposals to select reliable proposals as pseudo ground-truth. To tackle the second challenge, we introduce a Point-guided Semantic Enhancement (PSE) module that aggregates prototypes from point-annotated features and injects them into snippet features through Transformer-based interactions. Moreover, we design a Point-to-Proposal Constraint Loss (PPCL) that enforces each predicted proposal to include the annotated point, thereby encouraging more accurate localization. Extensive experiments on the THUMOS14 and ActivityNet-1.3 datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms several existing state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [143] [RIDE-Splat: Reflection Removal for In-the-Wild Driving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34632)
*Kevin David Hayes,Alex Hanson,Allen Tu,Pranav Asthana,Matthias Zwicker,Tom Goldstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RIDE-Splat：无干净图像/掩码/预训练网络，从含挡风玻璃反射的驾驶视频中无反射重建3DGS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挡风玻璃拍摄的驾驶视频违反3DGS的世界空间一致性假设：道路场景具视差，而座舱反射固定于相机，导致标准3DGS将反射内容嵌入重建场景。现有方法依赖干净图像、反射掩码或预训练网络。

Method: 将世界一致的车窗透射分量赋予3DGS场景，将主导的相机附着反射建模为一张共享的带符号RGB图像（同时表达附加辐射与透射衰减）。两阶段训练：先联合学习3D几何与带符号反射图，再用去反射后的训练图像拟合干净3DGS模型；推理时丢弃反射图像，保持标准3DGS渲染路径。并发布RIDE-Splat Dataset——首个驾驶视频去反射3D重建基准，含真实驾驶场景、训练视图中的物理挡风玻璃反射，以及单独采集的无反射评估图像。

Result: 在RIDE-Splat Dataset、XLD、Wayve上，全部九项聚合指标优于3DGS、DC-Gaussian、RobustSplat，PSNR最高提升3.59 dB，LPIPS最多降低0.0876；42项逐场景比较中38项最优；去除基线中仍可见的挡风玻璃反射且保持透射场景。

Conclusion: RIDE-Splat可在无额外监督信号的条件下实现驾驶视频无反射3D重建，并推广至多个数据集，为车挡反射下的新视角合成提供有效方案与基准。

Abstract: Driving video captured through a windshield violates the world-space consistency assumed by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS): the road scene exhibits parallax while cabin reflections remain attached to the camera, causing standard 3DGS to embed reflected content into the reconstructed scene. We introduce RIDE-Splat, a pipeline for reflection-free 3D reconstruction from posed driving video without clean images, reflection masks, or pretrained networks. RIDE-Splat assigns world-consistent transmission through the windshield to a 3DGS scene and the dominant camera-attached reflection to one shared, signed RGB image. It builds the clean reconstruction in two passes. First, it learns the 3D geometry and the signed reflection image, representing both added radiance and transmission attenuation. Then, it uses the reflection-removed training images to fit a clean 3DGS model. At inference, the reflection image is discarded, preserving the standard 3DGS rendering path. We also introduce the RIDE-Splat Dataset, the first 3D reconstruction benchmark for reflection removal from driving video -- with real driving scenes, physical windshield reflections in training views, and separately captured non-reflective evaluation images. Across the RIDE-Splat Dataset, XLD, and Wayve, RIDE-Splat outperforms 3DGS, DC-Gaussian, and RobustSplat on all nine aggregate metrics, improving PSNR by up to 3.59 dB and reducing LPIPS by up to 0.0876. We achieve the highest metrics in 38 out of 42 per-scene comparisons and removes windshield reflections that remain visible in the baselines while preserving the transmitted scene.

</details>


### [144] [Regional prediction of hematoma expansion from non-contrast computed tomography in spontaneous intracerebral hemorrhage patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34645)
*Natasha Ironside,Hongyu Kan,Kristofor Pas,Mohammad Shifat-E-Rabbi,Benjamin Newman,Shinjini Kundu,Kareem El Naamani,Tanvir Rizvi,Andrea Becerril-Gaitan,Harrison Snyder,Aarathi Manchikalapudi,Sarah Ratcliffe,Stacie L. Demel,Carl D. Langefeld,Daniel Woo,Stephan Mayer,E. Sander Connolly,Gustavo Rohde,for the ERICH Investigators,the VISTA-ICH collaboration*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究基于ERICH队列建模、VISTA队列验证，提出按血肿部位（脑叶、基底节、丘脑）划分的转运形态学（TBM）定量影像特征模型，用于从入院NCCT预测脑出血血肿扩大，验证集AUROC达0.787，优于临床医师判断与其他机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血肿部位、扩大程度与最终体积共同影响神经功能结局，但现有血肿扩大预测多缺乏可解释、可量化的区域性影像特征。作者旨在构建并验证一个基于区域、兼具定量性与可解释性的NCCT影像特征模型。

Method: 采用ERICH研究作为推导集、VISTA作为验证集；对入院及24±6小时NCCT进行预处理、分割，并按脑叶、基底节、丘脑分区。血肿扩大按区域定义并结合其对临床结局的影响界定。使用基于转运的形态学（TBM）为每例血肿图像生成独特的转运表示，在转运表示上应用线性分类器推导预测模型，并以AUROC与临床医师判断及其他机器学习方法比较。

Result: 推导集574例（脑叶162、基底节257、丘脑155），验证集196例（脑叶31、基底节119、丘脑46）。联合区域模型在验证集预测血肿扩大AUROC为0.787；随部位变化的主要影像特征为体积、异质性、偏心度及血肿外周密度分布。区域TBM模型优于基于临床医师及替代机器学习的方法。

Conclusion: TBM可从入院NCCT中区分并可视化血肿扩大的区域特征，为血肿扩大的区域性影像学特征提供初步证据，有望改进血肿扩大的预测并深化对其病理生理机制的理解。

Abstract: Background The combination of hematoma location, expansion and final volume influence neurological outcome. The aim of this study was to derive and validate a quantitative and interpretable region-based NCCT image feature model for predicting hematoma expansion. Methods The Ethnic/Racial Variations of Intracerebral Hemorrhage(ERICH) study was used for derivation and the Virtual International Trials in Stroke Archive(VISTA) was used for validation. NCCT images at presentation and 24+/-6hours were pre-processed, segmented and categorized into lobar, basal ganglia or thalamic regions. Hematoma expansion was defined by region and according to its effect on clinical outcome. Transport-based morphometry (TBM) produced a unique transport-based representation from each NCCT hematoma image. Linear classifiers applied to transport representations were used to derive a predictive model. The regional TBM model's performance was compared to alternate methods using AUROC analyses. Results The derivation dataset included 574 patients, comprising 162 lobar, 257 basal ganglia and 155 thalamic hematomas. The validation dataset included 196 patients, comprising 31 lobar, 119 basal ganglia and 46 thalamic hematomas. A combined regional model predicted hematoma expansion in the validation dataset with an AUROC of 0.787. The principal image features which varied by location were size, heterogeneity, eccentricity and density distribution towards the hematoma periphery. The regional TBM model outperformed alternative clinician based and machine-learning methods. Conclusions TBM discriminated and visualized regional features of hematoma expansion from presentation NCCT scans. This provides preliminary evidence for regional radiographic features of hematoma expansion. It has the potential to improve hematoma expansion prediction and understanding of hematoma expansion pathophysiology.

</details>


### [145] [StreamVibe: Proactive Video Agents for Evolving User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34662)
*Chengzhi Liu,Yuxiang Lai,Yuzhe Yang,Yanqing Liu,Jiheng Hou,Yue Cao,Haonan Wang,Ruixi Fan,Lan Wang,Sheng Liu,Songyou Peng,Yale Song,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Xin Eric Wang,Jui-Hsin Lai,Youbao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StreamVibe-Bench基准、StreamVibe-VL流式主动模型与StreamVibe-Harness层次化记忆框架，实现从连续观察中推断隐式用户意图并动态适配其演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型多依赖显式请求，忽略隐式用户意图（如浏览网页时"下一步该点哪"），且无法在用户请求持续变化时同步更新状态，难以适用于真实场景的意图感知交互。

Method: 构建StreamVibe-Bench（4.1K评测样本、85K训练样本，覆盖显式/隐式/动态意图）；提出StreamVibe-VL流式主动模型，从连续观察推断隐式意图并动态调整交互；设计StreamVibe-Harness，通过层次化记忆将用户意图维护为具灵活生命周期的持久状态，跟踪最新意图与环境状态。

Result: (i) StreamVibe-VL保持较强通用视频理解能力，响应时机与答案质量较基线提升10.7%；(ii) 现有主动模型在理解用户意图与决定响应时机上仍有局限，隐式与动态意图下尤甚；(iii) StreamVibe-Harness提升持续交互中的响应一致性与适应性，主动交互增益12.3%。

Conclusion: 将用户意图建模为可演化的持久状态并结合层次化记忆，是提升在线主动交互意图感知能力的有效路径；现有主动模型在隐式与动态意图场景仍存在明显短板。

Abstract: Vision-language models are shifting from offline passive understanding toward online proactive interaction. However, prevailing methods largely rely on explicit requests while miss the implicit user intent (e.g., "where should I click next?" while a user navigate a website), meanwhile fail to update the state simultaneously as user requests might update continuously. These limitations make them less suited to intent-aware interaction in practical. To address these challenges, we introduce StreamVibe-Bench, a new benchmark serving as testbed for user intent interaction, with 4.1K evaluation samples and 85K training samples spanning explicit, implicit, and dynamic intents. Building on this benchmark, we develop StreamVibe-VL, a streaming proactive model that infers implicit user intents from continuous observations and dynamically tailors its interactions as these intents evolve. We further introduce StreamVibe-Harness, which maintains user intents as persistent states with a fleixble lifecycles via a hierarchical memory to track the latest user intent and environmental state. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (i) StreamVibe-VL preserves strong general video understanding while improving response timing and answer quality by 10.7% over other baselines; (ii) existing proactive models remain limited in understanding user intent and deciding when to respond, especially under implicit and dynamic intents; and (iii) StreamVibe-Harness further improves response consistency and adaptation over sustained interaction, resulting in a 12.3% gain in proactive interaction.

</details>


### [146] [Good Rankings, Poor Choices? Rethinking Language-Only Vision-Language Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34685)
*Hyuk Kwon,Jaebyeong Jeon,Hyeonseo Jang,Kibok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文重新审视仅语言条件下的视觉语言模型选择（LOVM）评测协议，主张排序类指标不能直接度量所选模型性能，改用简单遗憾（SR）并提出离群鲁棒归一化遗憾（ORNR）；在此协议下提出免训练的 TC-ESR 方法，并指出并修正了原协议中的两处目标性能泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LOVM 评测依赖 top-5 recall、Kendall 秩相关等排序指标，但这些指标衡量的是排序质量，而非最终被选中模型在目标任务上的实际表现，因此无法直接反映模型选择方法的好坏。

Method: 1) 评测协议：采用简单遗憾 SR（最优模型与所选模型之间的性能差），并引入 ORNR，以在兼顾各任务性能量级差异的同时降低对离群值的敏感性。2) 选择方法：提出免训练的 TC-ESR，在源任务上以相似度为权重计算遗憾的加权均值，选择该加权平均遗憾最低的模型。3) 在修正后的协议下重新评测，并识别先前基于排序的协议中两处目标性能泄露来源，给出可同时消除二者的修正方案。

Result: 在 22 个目标任务与 43 个 VLM 上，TC-ESR 在所有对比方法中取得最低的平均 SR 与 ORNR。修正后的协议消除了两处目标性能泄露。

Conclusion: LOVM 评测应以选择遗憾而非排序相关性为核心指标；简单的相似度加权源任务遗憾即可作为有效的免训练选择策略，且先前结论需在无泄露的协议下重新审视。

Abstract: Language-Only Vision-Language Model Selection (LOVM) aims to identify the Vision-Language Model (VLM) with the highest zero-shot accuracy on a target task using only textual information. We revisit the existing LOVM evaluation protocol and argue that ranking metrics, such as top-5 recall and Kendall's rank correlation, do not directly assess the performance of the selected model. We therefore adopt simple regret (SR), defined as the performance gap between the best and selected models, and introduce Outlier-Robust Normalized Regret (ORNR) to account for the varying performance ranges across tasks while reducing sensitivity to outliers. Building on this revised evaluation protocol, we propose Target-Conditioned Expected Source Regret (TC-ESR), a training-free method that selects the model with the lowest similarity-weighted mean regret across source tasks. Across 22 target tasks and 43 VLMs, TC-ESR achieves the lowest mean SR and ORNR among all compared methods. Separately, we identify two sources of target performance leakage in the prior ranking-based evaluation protocol and provide corrections that remove both.

</details>


### [147] [RSKD: Grounding Semantic Consistency in Driving VLMs via Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34689)
*Chuchu Xie,Yang Zhong,Kangan Qian,Hao Ye,Kun Jiang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RSKD框架，依据答案短语的任务重要性与师生语义一致性动态路由蒸馏监督，在自动驾驶多模态VQA中优于学生基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大型多模态模型蒸馏到自动驾驶紧凑模型时，逐token均匀匹配会稀释与规划相关的语义，并放大教师错误。

Method: 在真实答案中定位任务关键语义短语，计算短语级一致性得分，据此在JSD蒸馏与真实标签监督之间加权：语义一致处强化教师信息，不一致处加强GT监督。

Result: 在DrivingVQA、LingoQA上优于对比的学生基线，在MapLMv2上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 按任务重要性与语义一致性路由蒸馏监督，可缓解均匀token匹配的语义稀释与教师误差传播问题。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation can transfer large multimodal models to compact models for autonomous driving, but uniform token-level matching can dilute planning-relevant semantics and amplify teacher errors. We propose RSKD, a semantic-consistency-guided distillation framework that routes supervision according to both the task importance of an answer phrase and its semantic consistency with the teacher prediction. Task-critical semantic phrases are localized in the ground-truth answer, and a phrase-level consistency score controls the balance between Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) distillation and ground-truth supervision. Teacher information is emphasized only where it is semantically consistent, while semantically inconsistent phrases receive stronger ground-truth supervision. We evaluate RSKD on DrivingVQA, LingoQA, and MapLMv2. RSKD improves over the compared student-based baselines on DrivingVQA and LingoQA and remains competitive on MapLMv2.

</details>


### [148] [FGD2HF: Frequency-guided Dual-domain Hypergraph Fusion for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34734)
*Xiaofan Yang,Wei Pan,Yubin Liu,Junyu Wu,Junming Zhang,Jie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FGD2HF双域融合网络，用频域特征引导空域融合与增强，在ADE20K、Cityscapes、SBD上以更低计算量取得有竞争力的分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频域信息有利于语义分割，但现有频域方法存在频域利用不充分、融合效率低、计算复杂度高的问题。

Method: 1) 混合注意力编码器（NAHA），通过邻域注意力与超图注意力联合建模局部与全局关系；2) 小波变换与增强模块（WTE），从上一阶段特征中提取并细化低频与高频线索；3) 频率引导双域融合模块（FGDF），以频域线索对当前阶段的空域特征分组与特征传播进行条件化调制，将全局与局部频率引导注入空域特征的调制、增强与融合，且计算成本低。

Result: FGD2HF-B在ADE20K达48.7% mIoU（较SegMAN-B +0.3%，GFLOPs少18%）；Cityscapes 83.2% mIoU（与SegMAN-B相当，GFLOPs少21%）；SBD较VWFormer-T高1.1% mIoU（GFLOPs少21%）；边界分割精度显著提升。

Conclusion: 频域引导的双域融合可在低计算成本下有效提升语义分割性能，尤其改善边界精度，与众多SOTA方法相比具有强竞争力。

Abstract: Frequency-domain information is beneficial for semantic segmentation. However, existing frequency-based methods often suffer from insufficient frequency utilization, inefficient fusion, and high computational complexity. To address these issues, we propose FGD2HF, a dual-domain fusion network that leverages frequency features to guide spatial-domain fusion and enhancement. Specifically, we introduce a novel Hybrid-Attention Encoder (NAHA) that jointly models local and global relationships through neighborhood and hypergraph attention, enhancing the network's ability to extract effective features. We further introduce a Wavelet Transform and Enhancement (WTE) module to extract and refine low- and high-frequency cues from preceding-stage features, followed by Frequency-guided Dual-domain Fusion (FGDF) modules that use frequency cues to condition current-stage spatial grouping and feature propagation. This design incorporates both global and local frequency guidance into spatial feature modulation, enhancement and fusion with low computational cost. Extensive comparisons with numerous state-of-the-art semantic segmentation methods demonstrate that our method is highly competitive. Our full FGD2HF-B model achieves 48.7% mIoU on ADE20K (+0.3% over SegMAN-B with 18% fewer GFLOPs), 83.2% mIoU on Cityscapes (comparable to SegMAN-B with 21% fewer GFLOPs), and 1.1% higher mIoU than VWFormer-T on SBD with 21% fewer GFLOPs, while also achieving significant improvements in boundary segmentation accuracy.

</details>


### [149] [From Sentinel-2 to 1-m Imagery: Real-World $\times 10$ Cross-Sensor Super-Resolution via Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34742)
*Tailai Chen,Yuluxin Fu,Jie Deng,Shu Luo,Yanan Wu,Danfeng Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨传感器×10遥感超分基准YREB-SR（11559对Sentinel-2 10m与吉林一号1m影像）与CrossVAR视觉自回归模型，在全部3015个测试场景上取得最优PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS/FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从Sentinel-2重建1m影像存在双重困难：输入与参考来自不同卫星，在空间分辨率、光谱响应、辐射度量、成像时间与局部几何上均不一致，不存在可用的合成退化算子；且每维×10的放大倍率远超遥感超分通常的2–4倍设定。

Method: 构建YREB-SR基准：2022年Sentinel-2 RGB合成与重采样至1m网格的吉林一号RGB参考，地理匹配11559对场景（训练6409/验证2135/测试3015）。提出CrossVAR：以Sentinel-2为条件，用视觉自回归模型在九尺度层级上预测1m潜变量，再用不注入噪声的扩散精修器估计量化残差；整幅1600×1600场景以对齐Sentinel-2像素网格的重叠窗口重建，每窗口按解码器训练尺寸解码后以高斯权重融合。

Result: 在3015个测试场景上达到27.60 dB PSNR、0.7725 SSIM、0.1451 LPIPS、7.15 FID，四项指标均为对比方法中最优。

Conclusion: 结果确立了面向大规模跨传感器重建的强自回归基线，代码与实现已开源以保障可复现性。

Abstract: Reconstructing 1-m imagery from Sentinel-2 combines two difficulties. The input and the reference are observed by different satellites, which differ in spatial resolution, spectral response, radiometry, acquisition time, and local geometry, so no synthetic degradation operator links them. The required magnification of ten in each spatial dimension also far exceeds the factors of two to four at which remote sensing super-resolution is usually developed. We study this real-world $\times 10$ problem using YREB-SR, which pairs 2022 Sentinel-2 RGB composites with 2022 Jilin-1 RGB references resampled to a 1-m grid over the Yangtze River Economic Belt. The benchmark contains 11,559 geographically matched scene pairs, with 6,409, 2,135, and 3,015 assigned to training, validation, and testing, respectively. We develop CrossVAR, a visual autoregressive model that predicts the 1-m latent as a nine-scale hierarchy conditioned on the Sentinel-2 input and estimates the remaining quantization residual with a diffusion refiner run without injected noise. Complete 1600 $\times$ 1600 scenes are reconstructed in overlapping windows that start on the Sentinel-2 pixel grid, as every training patch does, and every window is decoded at the size on which the decoder was trained before the windows are blended with Gaussian weights. On all 3,015 test scenes, CrossVAR achieves 27.60 dB PSNR, 0.7725 SSIM, 0.1451 LPIPS, and 7.15 FID, the best value of each metric among the compared methods. These results establish a strong autoregressive baseline for large-scale cross-sensor reconstruction. To facilitate reproducibility, the source code and implementation are publicly available at https://github.com/SHANOAL/RSSR.

</details>


### [150] [Efficient Multi-modal Video Fusion Network with Dynamic Region-Guided Temporal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34765)
*Jingxue Huang,Xiaosong Li,Yi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种高效多模态视频融合网络，用动态区域引导的时空交互替代光流/特征对齐，以线性复杂度实现时序建模，并引入MAE特征级时序一致性约束与新时序评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频融合方法依赖光流估计与特征warping做帧间对齐和跨帧聚合以维持时序一致性，计算开销大且对光流误差敏感、鲁棒性差。

Method: 1) 时空交互模块采用Test-Time Training范式建模时序依赖，复杂度为线性；2) 仅对视频中关键动态区域做时序建模，剔除低变化token以减少无效交互；3) 基于预训练MAE特征的全局结构建模能力，提出特征级时序一致性约束，通过对齐融合视频与源视频的帧间变化方向抑制局部闪烁；4) 提出基于MAE的时序指标，在动态区域内量化时序变化的方向距离。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，融合质量与时序一致性优于现有SOTA方法；在边缘设备部署场景下具备较高的计算效率。

Conclusion: 无需光流即可实现高效、时序一致的视频融合，动态区域选择与MAE特征约束共同提升融合质量与时间稳定性，适合边缘端部署。

Abstract: Multi-modal video fusion aims to generate temporally coherent videos with salient targets and rich structural details by integrating complementary information from heterogeneous sensors. However, existing video fusion methods commonly rely on optical flow estimation and feature warping for inter-frame alignment and cross-frame information aggregation to maintain temporal consistency, thereby introducing considerable computational overhead and making the fusion performance vulnerable to flow estimation errors. To address these limitations, this paper proposes an efficient multi-modal video fusion network with dynamic region-guided temporal interaction. Specifically, the proposed spatio-temporal interaction module leverages the Test-Time Training paradigm to model temporal dependencies with linear complexity. Building on this design, the module restricts temporal modeling to key dynamic regions in the video sequence, as static background regions provide limited temporal cues. By excluding low-variation tokens from unnecessary temporal interaction, this selective strategy contributes to improved fusion quality and temporal consistency. In addition, leveraging the strong ability of pretrained MAE features to capture global structural dependencies, we introduce a feature-level temporal consistency constraint to suppress local flickering by aligning the inter-frame variation directions of the fused and source videos. We further propose a new MAE-based temporal metric that quantifies directional distance between their temporal variations within dynamic regions. Experimental results demonstrate that, compared with existing state-of-the-art methods, our method achieves superior fusion quality and temporal consistency on multiple benchmark datasets, while exhibiting high computational efficiency under edge-device deployment settings.

</details>


### [151] [Factorized Gradients for Scalable Highly-expressive Parametric Diffeomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34766)
*Amit Aflalo,Eran Treister,Chaim Baskin,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FG-CPAB：将CPAB变换的轨迹积分与参数空间投影解耦，梯度复杂度从O(dTN)降至O(TN+dC)，实现最高约10^4倍加速与约500倍显存节省，使细网格CPAB在2D/3D中首次实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPAB通过分段仿射连续速度场参数化微分同胚，参数维度d仅取决于网格精细度而非数据分辨率，具备紧凑参数化优势；但其优化长期受限于轨迹积分与θ梯度计算的紧耦合，导致高复杂度、高开销，难以使用细网格以获得强表达力。

Method: 提出因子化梯度（factorized-gradient）公式FG-CPAB：将积分过程与参数空间投影分离，先计算与参数无关的积分量，再通过投影映射到参数空间，从而将梯度计算复杂度由O(dTN)降为O(TN+dC)（T为积分步数，N为变换点数，C为网格单元数）。

Result: 最高四个数量级加速（2D约10^4×），显著内存节省（2D峰值GPU显存约降低500×），表达力大幅提升；首次使细网格CPAB在2D与3D中实际可用。

Conclusion: FG-CPAB通过解耦积分与参数投影，解决了CPAB的可扩展性瓶颈，使高表达力的参数化微分同胚在大规模2D/3D问题中可行，可推广至依赖CPAB形变建模的各类科学应用。

Abstract: Diffeomorphisms provide a flexible, topology-preserving mathematical tool for modeling complex spatial or temporal deformations and are used in various scientific fields. However, making these deformations simultaneously highly expressive and computationally efficient remains challenging. Continuous Piecewise-Affine Based (CPAB) transformations (Freifeld et al., ICCV 2015; IEEE TPAMI 2017) offer an attractive solution by parameterizing a family of diffeomorphisms via continuous velocity fields that are piecewise affine with respect to a chosen tessellation of the domain. Importantly, $d=\dim(θ)$, the dimension of the parameter vector $θ$, depends on the fineness of the tessellation rather than on the data resolution. Despite this compact parameterization, CPAB optimization has long been hindered by the tight coupling between trajectory integration and gradient computation with respect to $θ$. We introduce FG-CPAB, a factorized-gradient formulation for scalable CPAB transformations. By separating integration from parameter-space projection, our method reduces gradient computation complexity from $\mathcal{O}(dTN)$ to $\mathcal{O}(TN+dC)$, where $T$ is the number of integration steps, $N$ is the number of transformed points, and $C$ is the number of tessellation cells. This reformulation yields speedups of up to four orders of magnitude (e.g., $\approx 10^4\times$ in 2D), substantial memory savings (e.g., $\approx 500\times$ reduction in peak GPU memory usage in 2D), and dramatically higher expressiveness. This makes fine-tessellation CPAB practical in 2D and 3D for the first time, enabling highly expressive parametric diffeomorphisms.

</details>


### [152] [ToDoPro: Faster and Better Reference-Based Generation: Early Token Reduction via Downsampled Self-Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34782)
*Caiyi Sun,Yujing Sun,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ToDoPro，通过对2×下采样参考图执行少量去噪步进行自探测，利用DiT自身评估参考token重要性，并在主去噪前做分层token削减，实现早期加速与显存节省且无需额外参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用参考图生成模型将多参考图与文本token拼接为联合序列由单一DiT处理，多参考导致token爆炸与O(N²)复杂度；DiT内部加速方法难以降低空间冗余和峰值显存，模型前置token削减方案又需引入外部模型。因此希望在无需额外参数、兼容模型内技术的前提下同时实现加速与显存节省。

Method: ToDoPro：对空间2×下采样的参考图执行少量去噪步作为"廉价探测"，由DiT自身提取参考token重要性；据此在主去噪前分层削减——完全丢弃低重要性token、用下采样对应token替换中重要性token以保留全局上下文、保留高重要性token。

Result: 在3个基础模型与2个基准上的实验表明，早期削减显著加速推理并降低峰值GPU显存；同时通过安全过滤噪声、无信息参考token可提升生成质量，使模型将有限注意力集中于真正有用的token。

Conclusion: 早期基于自探测的token削减是一种无需额外参数、与模型内技术兼容的有效加速与显存优化范式，并兼具质量增益。

Abstract: Recent generic reference-based image generation models natively support multiple conditions by concatenating all reference image and text tokens into a joint sequence processed by a single Diffusion Transformer (DiT). However, this versatile paradigm comes at an efficiency cost: accommodating multiple references leads to a token explosion, incurring massive $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ computational complexity. Most acceleration methods operate inside the DiT, showing limited capability in reducing spatial redundancy and failing to alleviate peak VRAM. While some pre-model token reduction alternatives exist, they inevitably introduce external models. This raises a question: Is it possible to achieve acceleration and memory savings while preserving compatibility with in-model techniques and requiring no additional parameters? In this work, we realize this goal by proposing the early Token reduction via Downsampled self-Probing (ToDoPro). By executing a few denoising steps on spatially $2\times$ downsampled reference images, this cheap probing stage utilizes the DiT model itself to effectively and efficiently extract reference token importance. Using the probed importance, we apply a hierarchical reduction before the main denoising stage: we completely discard low-importance tokens, substitute mid-importance tokens with their downsampled counterparts to cheaply preserve global context, and keep high-importance tokens. Experiments across three base models and two benchmarks demonstrate that our early reduction accelerates inference significantly and mitigates peak GPU memory. Furthermore, we can improve generation quality by safely filtering out noisy, uninformative reference tokens, enabling the model to concentrate its limited attention capacity on genuinely useful tokens. The code will be released at https://github.com/scy639/ToDoPro

</details>


### [153] [Natural Jammers: When Non-Robust Features Become Antagonistic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34785)
*Jiaming Zhang,Fuyao Zhang,Pengjun Xie,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"自然干扰器"（Natural Jammers）概念：非鲁棒线索并非仅是被动捷径，而会主动误导标准模型；其机制为"谱-空间锁定"，并在小变换下呈现"替代悖论"——干扰总量近似守恒、仅被重新分配，从而把自然非鲁棒性重构为模型-数据几何上的"失效场"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作将非鲁棒特征视为帮助标准模型在干净数据上取得好表现的机会主义捷径（被动、有益）。作者质疑这一被动观点，希望检验非鲁棒线索是否可能具有对抗性，在未经修改的干净图像上主动导致标准模型误判。

Method: 以标准模型与鲁棒模型之间的预测分歧作为分析透镜，界定出鲁棒模型优于标准模型的"Jam​​med Set"；随后通过因果实验（谱滤波、小幅几何位移）检验高频成分与特定空间结构的对齐关系，即"谱-空间锁定"假说，并考察干扰在各变换下的聚合分布行为。

Result: 发现存在 Jam​​med Set，其中鲁棒模型显著优于标准模型；提供因果证据表明干扰仅在特定高频成分与特定空间结构精确对齐时出现，破坏该锁定即可恢复正确预测；同时揭示"替代悖论"：单个干扰器对几何位移脆弱，但干扰的总体发生率在小变换下近似稳定，仅被重新分配而非消除。

Conclusion: 自然非鲁棒性应被理解为定义在经验模型-数据几何上的"失效场"（failure field），而非孤立的捷径特征；该失效场在小变换下不会消失，只会重新分配，这对鲁棒性评估与防御设计具有重要含义。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are known to rely on non-robust features that align poorly with human perception. Prior work has largely framed these features as opportunistic shortcuts that help standard models on clean data. We show instead that non-robust cues can be antagonistic: they can act as Natural Jammers that actively mislead standard models on clean, unmodified images. Using disagreement between a standard model and robust models as an analysis lens, we identify a Jammed Set on which robust models outperform standard models. We provide causal evidence for Spectral-spatial Locking: jamming arises only when high-frequency components are precisely aligned with specific spatial structures. Disrupting this lock through spectral filtering or small geometric shifts can restore correct predictions. Crucially, we uncover a Substitution Paradox: while individual jammers are fragile to geometric shifts, the aggregate prevalence of jamming remains approximately stable and is redistributed rather than eliminated under such transformations. Our results reframe natural non-robustness as a failure field over the empirical model-data geometry: one that redistributes rather than disappears under small transformations.

</details>


### [154] [Towards Spatio-Temporal Intelligence Systems in Urban Space: A Twin-Agent Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34795)
*Zhen Dong,Haiping Wang,Wei Tu,Yuan Liu,Bing Wang,Xuan Sun,Jinzhou Cao,Ronald Clark,Zhiheng Fu,Mohammed Bennamoun,Danfeng Hong,Yuanzheng Cui,Gemine Vivone,Pedram Ghamisi,Bisheng Yang,Qingquan Li,Deren Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从"孪生—智能体"视角综述城市时空智能（STI）系统的系统级集成，回顾孪生对城市环境的持久化重建与表征、智能体基于孪生接口的理解—推理—行动—演化机制，提出"内循环（接地—推理—校验）+外循环（数据—孪生—行动—反馈—更新）"的双环蓝图，并据此构建STI研究议程、展示共享原型对多城市领域的支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市需协调基础设施、出行、环境管理、应急响应与公共服务等多领域以应对动态变化，而现有各类技术（城市孪生、智能体AI等）相对分散。作者希望给出系统级视角，说明这些异构技术如何被一致地整合为可监控、可分析、可干预、可反馈自适应的城市时空智能系统，从而提升治理的及时性、有效性与透明度。

Method: 综述（survey）方法：以"孪生—智能体"（twin-agent）为分析框架，系统梳理孪生侧如何重建并表征持久、信息富集的城市环境；智能体侧如何通过接口理解孪生、在其上进行推理、可靠行动并随城市持续演化。在此基础上综合归纳出双环蓝图，并以该蓝图组织研究议程与原型验证。

Result: 形成两点主要产出：一是提出内循环（grounding—reasoning—verification）与外循环（数据—孪生—智能体行动—反馈—更新）的双环蓝图；二是以此结构化STI研究议程，并说明一个共享原型可支撑多样化城市领域的应用。

Conclusion: 城市时空智能应被视为孪生与智能体协同的双环闭环系统，而非孤立技术的堆叠；该框架为跨领域城市治理提供了统一集成范式与后续研究路线。

Abstract: Cities must coordinate domains like infrastructure, mobility, environmental management, emergency response, and public services as conditions evolve. An urban Spatio-Temporal Intelligence (STI) system helps the city monitor citywide states, analyze problems, plan and execute interventions, and adapt through feedback, making urban governance more timely, effective, and transparent. Recent urban twinning and agentic AI techniques provide foundations for such systems. From a twin-agent perspective, this survey takes a system-level view of how these disparate techniques can be coherently integrated into an urban STI system. Specifically, we review how the twin reconstructs and represents a persistent, information-enriched urban environment, and how the agent understands the twin via interfaces, reasons over it, acts reliably, and continuously evolves with the city. Built upon this synthesis, we summarize a two-loop blueprint, where an inner agent loop for grounding, reasoning, and verification, and an outer system loop linking data, twin, agentic action, feedback, and update. We then use this to structure an STI research agenda and show how a shared prototype supports diverse urban domains.

</details>


### [155] [Selective Monocular Precession Estimation under Geometric and Evaluation Ambiguity: An Auditable Synthetic Study with ORBIT-SCOUT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34819)
*Carlos Eduardo Sanoja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ORBIT-SCOUT：将单目图像中的线与轮廓几何耦合到恒锥、无扭矩三轴轨迹族，仅在多重校验通过时输出动量轴与平均进动率估计；并在96个合成场景、两轮冷处理（192次尝试）上开展失败保留式评估，结果为38例联合正确、0例错误、57例保留，注册目标均未达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目观测翻滚目标时，可以拟合出精确轨迹而未证明恢复的物理轴正确；作者要澄清"轨迹拟合"与"物理轴正确"的区分，并为测量—决策流程提供可审计的评估范式。

Method: 针对标定静止相机观测的细长近圆柱目标，将图像导出的直线与轮廓几何与恒锥、无扭矩三轴轨迹族耦合；通过数值、几何、时间及竞争假设检验后，才返回关联的动量轴与平均进动率；采用内部预注册的96个合成场景、两轮冷处理、保留全部192次尝试的评价协议。

Result: 主轮中38个场景给出联合正确估计，0个给出错误估计，57个被保留，1次执行不完整；64个被支持场景上的正确覆盖为25/64；95对重复实验在冻结比较策略下科学等价，另1对不完整；注册的覆盖率、无失败执行与全案例可重复性目标均未达成。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一个有记录的"测量到决策"实现与失败保留式评估案例研究，而非新的刚体理论或SOTA精度证据；零观测错误不等于校准风险，条件性时间检验不能消除共享图像特征偏差，真实图像迁移与外部复现仍待完成。

Abstract: Monocular observations of a tumbling object can support a precise fitted trajectory without establishing that the recovered physical axis is correct. We study this distinction for elongated, approximately cylindrical targets observed by a calibrated stationary camera. ORBIT-SCOUT couples image-derived line and rim geometry to constant-cone and torque-free triaxial trajectory families, then returns a linked momentum-axis and mean-precession-rate estimate only when numerical, geometric, temporal and competing-hypothesis checks pass. We describe the measurement objective, selection policy and limitations, and situate them within spacecraft pose estimation, unknown-target reconstruction, rigid-body identification and selective prediction. An internally preregistered population of 96 new synthetic scenes was processed in two cold passes, retaining all 192 attempts. In the primary pass, 38 scenes produced jointly correct estimates, none produced an incorrect estimate, 57 were withheld and one execution was incomplete. Correct coverage on the 64 supported scenes was 25/64. Ninety-five repeated pairs were scientifically equivalent under a frozen comparison policy; the remaining pair was incomplete. The registered coverage, failure-free execution and all-case repeatability targets were not met. Historical audits also exposed an overly permissive axis metric and motion-dependent acquisition cadence. The contribution is a documented measurement-to-decision implementation and a failure-preserving evaluation case study, not a new rigid-body theory or evidence of state-of-the-art accuracy. Zero observed errors does not establish calibrated risk, and conditional temporal checks do not remove shared image-feature bias. Real-image transfer and external reproduction remain open.

</details>


### [156] [Interpretable Brain Age Prediction Using White Matter Fiber Orientation Distributions with Rotation-Equivariant Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34825)
*Chenyang Fan,Robert D. Sanders,Elie Matar,Yixuan Yuan,Xiang Li,Sharon L. Naismith,Fernando Calamante,Jinglei Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结合白质纤维取向分布（WM-FOD）与SE(3)等变CNN的可解释脑龄预测框架，在HCP-Aging 692人上MAE 3.54年、r=0.958。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑龄预测多用DTI标量指标，丢失取向信息；常规CNN也未显式尊重取向相关特征的几何结构（旋转性质）。

Method: 以FOD的球谐（SH）系数为网络直接输入，利用SE(3)等变卷积网络保持旋转下的变换性质；在HCP-Aging数据集（n=692，36–90岁）上评估，采用五折交叉验证模型集成预测，并用逐束遮挡（tract-level occlusion）进行敏感性分析，考察SH阶数Lmax的影响。

Result: 整体优于DTI基线与常规CNN；五模型平均MAE=3.54岁、Pearson r=0.958；性能优势依赖WM-FOD与等变处理的结合；Lmax=2时精度最高，更高阶下降；遮挡分析显示随年龄变化的敏感通路——36–44岁额叶投射束、45–54岁穹窿、55–64岁胼胝体峡部、65–74岁小脑中脚、75–90岁胼胝体膝部。

Conclusion: 支持将旋转等变的WM-FOD处理用于高精度且具解剖可解释敏感性的脑龄预测。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI (dMRI) provides information about white matter microstructure that can support brain age prediction. However, many existing prediction models use diffusion tensor imaging (DTI) scalar metrics that omit orientation information. Deep learning is increasingly used for brain age prediction, but conventional CNNs do not explicitly respect the geometric structure of orientation-dependent features. We present an interpretable brain age prediction framework that combines white matter fiber orientation distributions (WM-FODs) with an SE(3) (Special Euclidean group in 3 dimensions) equivariant CNN. The network operates directly on spherical harmonic (SH) coefficients of the FOD, preserving their transformation properties under rotation. Evaluation in 692 participants aged 36-90 years from the Human Connectome Project Aging dataset showed that the combined framework outperformed the evaluated DTI-based models and conventional CNNs. Averaging predictions from five cross-validation models yielded a mean absolute error of 3.54 years and Pearson's correlation of 0.958. Our results indicated that the performance advantage depended on combining WM-FODs with equivariant processing. Prediction accuracy was highest at a maximum SH order of Lmax = 2 and declined at higher orders. Tract-level occlusion, where each segmented tract was masked in turn, revealed age-dependent patterns of model sensitivity, with importance peaks in frontal projection pathways at 36-44 years, the fornix at 45-54 years, the callosal isthmus at 55-64 years, the middle cerebellar peduncle at 65-74 years, and the callosal genu at 75-90 years. These findings support rotation-equivariant processing of WM-FODs for accurate brain age prediction with anatomically interpretable model sensitivity.

</details>


### [157] [PosePlaner: Denoising Any Feed-Forward Pose Predictor from Pairwise Planar Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34845)
*Yihang Chen,Bin Tan,Yujun Shen,Yinghao Xu,Nan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PosePlaner，用 flow matching 把位姿精化建模为以粗几何匹配与初始位姿为条件的生成问题，并对生成的位姿假设做融合，实现类似 RANSAC 投票的连续数据驱动版本；仅用双视图数据训练，可作为流式 n 视图位姿估计器的周期精化模块且无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期多视图 Transformer（DUSt3R、VGGT 等）将 SfM/SLAM 的提取-匹配-位姿估计流程压缩为单次前馈推理，但各阶段接口（对应、几何、位姿）被纠缠在一起，导致改进只能依赖有监督微调，且测试时缺乏利用几何证据纠错的手段。

Method: 将位姿精化视为条件生成问题：以粗糙几何匹配和初始位姿为条件，用 flow matching 重建对应与位姿的经典耦合，生成多个位姿假设并融合为单一位姿估计，形成 RANSAC 投票的连续数据驱动类比；在双视图数据上高效训练，无需重训练即可作为流式 n 视图位姿估计器的周期性精化器。

Result: 可修正前馈预测器的失败案例，显著提升基于对应关系的求解器性能，并减少长流式序列中的累积漂移。

Conclusion: 通过 flow matching 重新显式耦合对应与位姿，PosePlaner 恢复了测试时基于几何证据的纠错能力，兼具训练高效（双视图）与部署通用（n 视图流式精化）的优势。

Abstract: Recent multi-view Transformers (e.g., DUSt3R and VGGT) have advanced 3D reconstruction by scaling training data and model capacity from two views to many views and long sequences, subsuming the classical SfM/SLAM pipeline of feature extraction, matching, and pose estimation in a single feed-forward pass. Yet this subsumption hides the interfaces between pipeline stages---correspondences, geometry, and pose are entangled in a single forward pass, leaving supervised fine-tuning as the major path to improvement and offering no mechanism to correct predictions from geometric evidence at test time. We propose PosePlaner to close this gap by reviving the classical coupling between correspondences and pose via flow matching, treating pose refinement as a conditional generation problem that takes coarse geometric matches and initial pose as input condition and fuses the resulting pose hypotheses into a single refined estimate, providing a continuous, data-driven analog of RANSAC voting. Our method is trained efficiently on two-view data, yet serves as a periodic refiner for streaming n-view pose estimators with no retraining. Empirically, it corrects failures of feed-forward predictors, substantially improves correspondence-based solvers, and reduces accumulated drift in long streaming sequences.

</details>


### [158] [Seeing Before Naming: Context-Grounded Learning of Novel Visual Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34846)
*Gabriel Adineera,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SynerNet通过"上下文接地的语义锚定"框架学习概念专属名称嵌入并使其表征受视觉证据调制，在VISTA-Beyond四域16-shot平均准确率74.35%（较few-shot names learning的72.45%提升），在name-masked transfer的14个域上由45.17%升至46.74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习陌生视觉类别不止是适配分类器：模型还需把视觉证据与有用的语义名称连接起来。预训练视觉-语言模型可保留判别性图像特征，但对目标概念仅提供弱文本锚点，视觉与语义之间存在不平衡。

Method: 提出SynerNet：学习概念专属名称嵌入，将其暴露于兼容的语言上下文中，并以视觉证据为条件调制其表征；辅以难度感知处理与自适应目标权重来协调适配过程，同时保留预训练骨干网络。

Result: VISTA-Beyond四个域上16-shot平均准确率74.35%，优于few-shot name learning的72.45%；name-masked transfer设置下14个域平均准确率从45.17%提升至46.74%。消融实验表明概念专属名称嵌入与跨模态上下文整合为关键组件。

Conclusion: 结果支持将概念获取视为"构建视觉接地的语义锚"这一观点，即概念学习的核心在于名称语义与视觉证据的协同锚定，而非仅适配分类边界。

Abstract: Learning an unfamiliar visual category requires more than adapting a classifier: the model must connect its visual evidence to a useful semantic name. A pre-trained vision-language model can retain discriminative image features while providing a weak textual anchor for the target concept. We address this imbalance through context-grounded semantic anchoring. Our framework, SynerNet, learns concept-specific name embeddings, exposes them to compatible linguistic contexts, and conditions their representations on visual evidence. Difficulty-aware processing and adaptive objective weighting coordinate this adaptation while retaining the pre-trained backbone. On four VISTA-Beyond domains, SynerNet reaches a mean 16-shot accuracy of 74.35%, compared with 72.45% for few-shot name learning. Across 14 domains in the name-masked transfer setting, it improves mean accuracy from 45.17% to 46.74%. Ablations identify concept-specific name embeddings and cross-modal context integration as important components. These results support treating concept acquisition as the construction of visually grounded semantic anchors.

</details>


### [159] [Recovering Black-Box Point Cloud Models from Pose Shifts via Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34850)
*Seonghwan Park,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CanDO：一种仅依赖前向查询的黑盒测试时规范化方法，用于恢复点云模型在旋转分布偏移下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云模型日益以仅输出分数的黑盒接口部署，测试时出现训练分布外的旋转会显著降低性能；等变重训练与基于梯度的自适应虽有效，但需要模型权重或梯度，在此访问模型下不可行。

Method: CanDO仅使用前向查询，寻找使模型置信度最大的输入变换，并采用无导数优化完成搜索而不依赖梯度；同时指出成功恢复的三个条件：训练增广间隙、与准确率对齐的置信度代理、对多模态目标的全局搜索。

Result: 在多种架构与基准上，对象部件分割任务中，方位角错位下恢复76%的oracle差距，任意SO(3)旋转下恢复51%。

Conclusion: 冻结的、仅输出分数的点云模型可仅通过部署后规范化，从旋转引起的分布偏移中恢复性能。

Abstract: Point cloud models are increasingly deployed through score-only black-box interfaces. Under this access model, test-time rotations outside the training distribution can substantially degrade performance. Although existing mitigations such as equivariant retraining and gradient-based adaptation are effective, they are infeasible without access to model weights or gradients. To address this limitation, we propose CanDO, a test-time canonicalization method that recovers lost performance using only forward queries. Specifically, CanDO searches for an input transformation that maximizes model confidence and uses derivative-free optimization to perform this search without gradients. We further identify three conditions for successful recovery: a training augmentation gap, an accuracy-aligned confidence surrogate, and global search over a multi-modal objective. Across multiple architectures and benchmarks, CanDO recovers 76% of the oracle gap under azimuthal misorientation and 51% under arbitrary SO(3) rotations for object part segmentation. These results show that frozen score-only point cloud models can recover from rotation-induced distribution shifts through post-deployment canonicalization alone.

</details>


### [160] [Learning Dual-Camera Smooth Zoom via Decoupled Geometric-Photometric Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34854)
*Junjie Fan,Zhilu Zhang,Renlong Wu,Dazhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双摄平滑变焦(DCSZ)任务，构建ZoomGS v2数据合成框架并设计ZoomFI插帧网络，实现双摄变焦的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机双摄变焦时，几何透视与色彩渲染存在显著不连续，损害视觉体验；而直接套用现有视频插帧(VFI)模型受域差异大、真实连续真值难以采集两大障碍限制。

Method: 1) 定义DCSZ任务，合成连续中间帧实现平滑变焦；2) 提出ZoomGS v2数据合成框架，将几何变换与色彩变换从3D场景表示建模中显式解耦，从而可由易获取的单摄3D场景生成高保真训练序列；3) 设计专用VFI网络ZoomFI，利用互补的双向warping全面融合双摄信息。

Result: 在两部手机采集的测试集上，基于所构建DCSZ数据训练可显著提升多种VFI模型性能；ZoomFI在定量指标与视觉质量上均达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 数据合成解耦策略有效弥补域差距，专用双向warping网络实现双摄平滑变焦，数据集、代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: When zooming between dual cameras on smartphones, prominent discontinuities in both geometric perspective and color rendition inevitably degrade the visual experience of users. To address this issue, we introduce the dual-camera smooth zoom (DCSZ) task, aiming to synthesize continuous intermediate frames for fluid zooming. However, directly applying existing video frame interpolation (VFI) models to this task is severely hindered by substantial domain gaps and the practical intractability of capturing real-world continuous ground-truth. To this end, we propose a novel data synthesis framework to generate high-fidelity DCSZ training sequences, named ZoomGS v2. It explicitly decouples geometric and color transformations from 3D scene representation modeling, thereby enabling extensive data generation from easily accessible single-camera 3D scenes. Furthermore, we develop ZoomFI, a specialized VFI network tailored to the DCSZ task, which leverages complementary bidirectional warping for comprehensive dual-camera information fusion. Extensive experiments on test datasets captured using two mobile phones demonstrate that training with the constructed DCSZ data significantly improves the performance of various VFI models. Moreover, ZoomFI achieves state-of-the-art results in both quantitative metrics and visual quality. The datasets, code, and pretrained models are available at https://hitjunjief.github.io/DCSZ-v2

</details>


### [161] [CoDeRNet: Selective Cross-Task Routing under Heterogeneous Supervision for Change Detection and Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34876)
*Eunki Cho,Hyeon Bae Kim,Seong Tae Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将变化检测与描述（CDC）重构为异构任务分支间的选择性信息路由问题，提出CoDeRNet框架，通过Confidence-Decomposed Routing（CoDeR）机制实现双向互补信息交换，在LEVIR-MCI与WHU-CDC上取得强整体性能且显著提升描述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一建模方法主要依赖共享表示或联合优化架构，未充分探索异构监督下任务间的通信；检测与描述在监督粒度上存在本质差异，导致表示异构且在不同任务与空间位置上可靠性不均，因此需要决定"何时、何处"传递信息。

Method: 提出CoDeRNet统一框架，保留任务专属表示的同时支持双向互补信息交换；核心为Confidence-Decomposed Routing（CoDeR）机制，将路由条件分解为接收端的支持需求与发送端的可靠性线索，以引导选择性迁移。

Result: 在LEVIR-MCI和WHU-CDC数据集上，CoDeRNet取得较强的整体CDC性能，尤其在描述生成上有明显提升，同时保持有竞争力的变化定位能力；分析表明收益并非源于简单联合学习或通用跨任务通信，而是来自显式的选择性跨任务信息路由。

Conclusion: 条件化信息传递对异构监督下的统一CDC至关重要，选择性路由是统一建模的关键设计要素。

Abstract: Change Detection & Captioning (CDC) aims to jointly localize changed regions and describe their semantic content from bi-temporal remote sensing images. While recent approaches have demonstrated the effectiveness of unified modeling, they primarily rely on shared representations or jointly optimized architectures, leaving task-specific communication under heterogeneous supervision less explored. However, detection and captioning differ fundamentally in their supervision granularity, leading to heterogeneous representations with uneven reliability across tasks and spatial locations. In this paper, we reformulate CDC as a selective information routing problem between heterogeneous task branches, where the key challenge is to determine when and where information should be transferred. To this end, we propose CoDeRNet, a unified framework that preserves task-specific representations while enabling bidirectional exchange of complementary information. CoDeRNet introduces a Confidence-Decomposed Routing (CoDeR) mechanism, which decomposes the routing condition into receiver-side need for support and sender-side reliability cues to guide selective transfer. Experiments on LEVIR-MCI and WHU-CDC show that CoDeRNet achieves strong overall CDC performance, particularly improving caption generation while preserving competitive change localization. Extensive analyses further show that the benefits arise not merely from joint learning or generic cross-task communication, but from explicitly modeling selective cross-task information routing. These results highlight the importance of conditional information transfer for unified CDC under heterogeneous supervision.

</details>


### [162] [LayoutMamba: Efficient Document Layout Analysis with Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34879)
*Kehua Chen,Mingyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LayoutMamba：稀疏注意力Transformer + Mamba状态空间的混合框架，实现O(n)复杂度的高效文档版面分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档版面分析（DLA）存在长期两难：Transformer类方法能捕捉丰富局部细节但计算复杂度为二次方、代价高昂；状态空间模型全局建模高效却牺牲细粒度结构感知。该权衡阻碍了在资源受限场景（边缘计算）中的部署。此外，现有DLA基准存在被忽视的布局对象低重叠分布偏置问题。

Method: 提出LayoutMamba混合框架，采用两阶段范式：第一阶段用轻量级稀疏注意力Transformer编码器（O(n)复杂度）分层提炼显著局部语义；第二阶段用Mamba的循环状态空间机制在2D文档画布上进行无稀释的长程依赖建模。同时提出几何重叠惩罚损失（GOPLoss），作为双重优化目标，最小化冗余边界框重叠并鼓励几何一致的预测，以缓解低重叠分布偏置。

Result: 在PubLayNet和D4LA上实验：以少8倍的参数量超越PubLayNet上的SOTA AP；在D4LA上保持领先性能，在已发表方法中排名前三，实现精度、效率与可扩展性的空前平衡。

Conclusion: 该工作提出受认知启发的文档分析新范式，兼顾精度、效率与可扩展性，推动在边缘计算和资源受限环境中的实际部署。

Abstract: Document layout analysis (DLA) faces a long-standing dilemma: existing methods either capture rich local details at prohibitive computational cost (e.g., Transformer-based approaches with quadratic complexity) or achieve efficient global modeling but sacrifice fine-grained structural awareness (e.g., state-space models). This trade-off hinders deployment in resource-constrained scenarios. We present LayoutMamba, a hybrid framework that synergistically combines sparse-attention Transformers for localized feature extraction (O(n) complexity) and the recurrent state-space mechanism of Mamba for dilution-free long-range dependency modeling across the 2D document canvas. Our two-stage paradigm first uses a lightweight Transformer encoder to hierarchically distill salient local semantics; second, Mamba propagates global contextual dependencies without attention dilution, enabling precise, efficient, and scalable layout understanding. We further identify a critical yet overlooked issue in existing DLA benchmarks: the inherent low-overlap distribution bias of layout objects, which we mitigate through a novel Geometric Overlap Penalty Loss (GOPLoss), a dual-optimization objective that minimizes redundant bounding box overlaps and encourages geometrically consistent predictions. Extensive experiments on PubLayNet and D4LA show that LayoutMamba surpasses state-of-the-art AP on PubLayNet with 8x fewer parameters and maintains leading performance on D4LA, ranking top-3 among published methods, achieving an unprecedented balance between accuracy, efficiency, and scalability. Our work pioneers a cognition-inspired paradigm for document analysis, advancing practical deployment in edge computing and resource-limited environments.

</details>


### [163] [MedAdaptAgent: An Automatic Harness for Adapting Medical Imaging Models to New Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34884)
*Hanxue Gu,Zhonghao Liu,Xingrui Gu,Kang Wang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedAdaptAgent 是一个自动化的医疗影像跨站点适配框架，将给定模型、目标数据和自由文本需求转为可运行的适配工作流，通过 LLM 规划器与证据驱动的选择机制改善迁移性能并减少有害替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用预训练医疗影像模型可替代从零训练，但同一任务的模型在新医院可能因成像协议与预处理差异而表现不佳；有效适配取决于模型架构、域偏移、可用标签与算力，且适配可能在小规模标注集上提分却在未见数据上退化。因此需要一个能自动完成跨站点适配并规避有害替换的流程。

Method: 提出 MedAdaptAgent 自动适配框架：输入为已有模型、目标数据与自由文本请求，输出为新站点适配工作流；其 LLM 驱动的 Adaptation Planner（适配规划器）利用模型—站点证据选择兼容实验并迭代修正后续调整；框架要求候选方案必须相对预训练模型有提升证据，最终以完整可运行包形式交付选定模型。评估覆盖 26 项分割与分类迁移任务，跨多种架构与算力配置，分割标注预算从零样本迁移到少样本适配。

Result: 使用 20 个本地标注时，10 项分割迁移的平均 Dice 从 0.483 提升至 0.774（相对提升 60.1%），且无有害替换；13 项分类迁移的平均 AUROC 从 0.810 提升至 0.837（相对提升 3.4%）。在相同候选集下，与"按可用标签最高分选模型"相比，以预训练模型为参照的适配模型选择将有害分类替换减少 30.8%。七项公开模型部署验证了在对比度与模态偏移下的适配及可复现包交付；CT-to-MRI 研究将评估扩展到四种 LLM 规划器（含两个开放权重模型），代码已开源。

Conclusion: MedAdaptAgent 以证据驱动的自动规划与以预训练模型为基线的安全选择准则，在分割与分类跨站点迁移中实现稳定增益并降低有害替换风险，同时支持可复现的模型交付；但其结论主要基于本文设定的 26 项迁移与特定数据集，泛化性及对规划器性能的依赖仍需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Reusing pretrained medical-imaging models offers an alternative to training from scratch. A model trained for the same task may perform poorly at a new hospital because imaging protocols and preprocessing differ. Effective adaptation depends on the model architecture, domain shift, and available labels and compute. Adaptation can improve scores on a small labeled set yet reduce performance on unseen data. We present MedAdaptAgent, an automatic harness that turns a supplied model, target data, and a free-text request into a new-site adaptation workflow. Its LLM-driven Adaptation Planner uses model-site evidence to choose compatible experiments and revise subsequent adjustments. The harness requires evidence of improvement over the pretrained model, then delivers the selected model as a complete, runnable package. Evaluation spans 26 segmentation and classification transfers across architectures and compute settings, with segmentation annotation budgets ranging from zero-shot transfer to few-shot adaptation. With 20 local annotations, mean segmentation Dice rises from 0.483 to 0.774 across 10 transfers, a 60.1% relative gain, with no harmful replacements. Mean classification AUROC rises from 0.810 to 0.837 across 13 transfers, a 3.4% relative gain. Comparing adapted models with the pretrained model reduces harmful classification replacements by 30.8% versus selecting the highest-scoring model on the available labels, using identical candidates. Seven public-model deployments demonstrate adaptation and reproducible package delivery across contrast and modality shifts. A CT-to-MRI study extends the evaluation to four LLM planners, including two open-weight models. We release the code on GitHub.

</details>


### [164] [SGDB: Semantic-Geometric Decoupling and Evidence Binding for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34897)
*Chenrui Ji,Jiafan Lu,Zilu Luo,Feng Wan,Qiuhui Chen,Weimin Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义-几何解耦与证据绑定框架（SGDB），将物体身份识别与几何测量分离，并通过部分一对一关联、支持认证读取器与可执行检查实现可解释的逻辑异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑异常检测需同时识别场景部件并判断其配置是否满足任务约束；但正确身份可能与不准确几何共存，干净掩码片段也可能给出误导性长度，需将身份与几何测量解耦。

Method: 语义解码器从部件标注的正常样本与事实性编辑中学习物体身份；独立读取器提供最终测量；部分一对一关联将几何见证绑定到身份并保留未匹配物体计数；支持认证读取器依据线条质量与部件完整归属筛选掩码派生轴；以显式任务要求与正常数据参考定义可执行检查，阈值由独立正常校准固定；执行级解释回溯规则贡献，提取紧凑充分告警凭证；冻结语言模型将选中结果表述为带引用主张，数值证据由执行器保留。

Result: 在 MVTec LOCO 五个逻辑类别上达 98.19% AUROC 与 94.52% 固定阈值 F1；在全部 50 个 VID-AD 任务上达 93.56% AUROC 与 92.76% F1；并以冻结输出对照评估几何读取与解释的充分性、紧凑性。

Conclusion: 解耦语义身份与几何证据、并以可执行规则与校准阈值绑定判断，可在保持解释充分与紧凑的同时取得强逻辑异常检测性能；报告同时呈现观测偏差与不可用证据。

Abstract: Logical anomaly detection requires recognizing scene components and measuring whether their configuration satisfies task constraints. Correct identities can coexist with inaccurate geometry; clean mask fragments can yield misleading lengths. We propose Semantic-Geometric Decoupling and Evidence Binding (SGDB). A semantic decoder learns object identities from component-annotated normal sources and factual edits; a separate reader supplies final measurements. Partial one-to-one association binds geometric witnesses to identities while retaining unmatched objects in the count. A support-certified reader selects mask-derived axes using line quality and complete component ownership. Explicit task requirements and normal-data references define executable checks, with decision thresholds fixed by independent normal calibration. Execution-grounded explanation traces rule contributions to source objects and measurements, extracting compact sufficient alarm certificates. A frozen language model expresses selected outcomes as cited claims, with numeric evidence retained by the executor. Reports preserve observed deviations and unavailable evidence. SGDB achieves 98.19% AUROC and 94.52% fixed-threshold F1 on five MVTec LOCO logical categories, and 93.56% AUROC and 92.76% F1 across all 50 VID-AD tasks. Frozen-output controls evaluate geometric readout and explanatory sufficiency and compactness.

</details>


### [165] [LE-GDA: Layer Ensemble Gaussian Discriminant Analysis for Training-Free Few-Shot CLIP Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34901)
*Pavodi Maniamfu,Vogel Kiketa,Zezina Kuzulu Kuetu,Jakin Mukendi Kabongo,David Mayoya Kutangila,Pescie Honore Kinkani Mampuya,L'Or Wolo Sieka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练少样本适应方法LE-GDA，通过组合CLIP中间层与最终层表示提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练少样本方法仅用CLIP最终投影表示构建分类器，中间表示被闲置；而不同表示的分类得分尺度相差数量级，直接平均会按数值大小而非预测信息分配影响。

Method: 在单次冻结编码前向中提取各中间层与最终层表示，分别拟合正则化共享协方差高斯判别分析（GDA）判别器；对每个查询对各判别器的类得分做逐查询标准化；在带标签验证集上选择少量表示组合，并与零样本分类器融合，全程无梯度优化。

Result: 在11个数据集、4个CLIP骨干、5种shot数与3组独立采样的K-shot训练集上，LE-GDA在所有骨干上取得最高平均精度；16-shot均值由78.01%提升至79.54%，且对每个骨干-shot组合均有改进；1-shot时仍保持1.20点平均增益。单表示内更丰富的类结构对照无法复现该增益，表明收益来自表示组合。

Conclusion: CLIP中间表示蕴含最终嵌入之外的信息，组合多层表示可有效改进免训练少样本适应。

Abstract: Training-free few-shot adaptation of vision-language models typically constructs a classifier from CLIP's final projected image representation, leaving intermediate representations unused. Combining them is not straightforward. Class scores from different representations differ in spread by orders of magnitude for the same query. Direct averaging allocates influence by numerical scale rather than predictive information. We propose Layer-Ensemble Gaussian Discriminant Analysis (LE-GDA), a method that fits regularized shared-covariance discriminants independently to intermediate and final representations extracted in one frozen encoder pass. LE-GDA standardizes each discriminant's class scores per query, selects a small representation multiset on labeled validation data, and combines it with the zero-shot classifier without gradient-based optimization. Across 11 datasets, four CLIP backbones, five shot counts, and three independently sampled K-shot training sets, LE-GDA achieves the highest mean accuracy of all evaluated methods on every backbone, raising the 16-shot mean from 78.01% to 79.54% over a matched GDA-CLIP (final-feature) baseline and improving that baseline for every backbone-shot combination. At one shot, LE-GDA retains a 1.20-point mean gain over the baseline even though covariance estimation is unavailable and per-query standardization is neutral. A control with richer per-class structure inside a single representation does not reproduce the gain, indicating that the benefit comes from combining representations. These results show that intermediate CLIP representations carry information beyond the final embedding and can improve training-free few-shot adaptation. Code and evaluation resources are available at https://github.com/pavodi-NM/le-gda.

</details>


### [166] [DiffAuction: An Auction-Driven Framework for Generative Image Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34917)
*Yijie Su,Ruiyang Si,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiffAuction框架，将可控生成（RSC+SCP）、基于VLM/LLM的点击意图评估（CTI）与可学习仿射极大化拍卖（AMA）结合，使多广告主在多目标份额配置下竞争共享生成图像中的曝光，并保证DSIC与IR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在目标份额变化时生成图像计算代价高，且对多广告主共享生成图像的结果缺乏评估与定价机制。

Method: 1) 免训练残差份额校准（RSC）：将联合注意力更新分解为各产品残差并按目标份额重缩放；2) 份额条件提示（SCP）适配视角；3) 评估器：VLM提取视觉证据、LLM从产品显著性、品牌可识别性、场景融合、语境价值传达四维评估CTI，并与10名受训标注者在1000张图像上的判断对比验证；4) 将广告场景建模为对联合可行生成配置的拍卖，配置具有公开期望CTI向量，广告主持有每单位CTI的私有价值，采用可学习仿射极大化拍卖（AMA）联合选择配置并确定支付以最大化期望平台收益。

Result: 生成方法相较基线强化了目标份额与CTI之间的单调关系；学得的AMA在对应拍卖环境中取得更高平台收益；分配与支付规则满足DSIC与IR。

Conclusion: DiffAuction将可控多广告主生成、意图评估与机制设计统一，验证了份额可调生成的有效性与拍卖机制在收益和理论性质上的优势。

Abstract: Diffusion models enable multiple advertisers to compete for exposure within a shared generated image. Yet existing approaches are computationally costly when generating images under varying target-share configurations, and it remains unclear how the resulting outcomes should be evaluated and priced. To address these gaps, we propose DiffAuction, a framework integrating controllable generation, click-intent evaluation, and auction design. First, our training-free Residual Share Calibration (RSC) decomposes joint-attention updates into product-specific residuals and rescales them according to target shares. We further introduce Share-Conditioned Prompting (SCP) to adapt the viewpoint to each desired allocation. Second, we develop an evaluator that uses a VLM to extract visual evidence and an LLM to assess Click-Through Intent (CTI), a proxy for click propensity, along four dimensions: Product Prominence, Brand Recognizability, Scene Integration, and Contextual Value Communication. We validate its assessments against judgments from 10 trained annotators on 1000 images. Third, we formulate this advertising setting as an auction over jointly feasible generation configurations. Each configuration has a public expected CTI vector, while advertisers hold configuration-specific private values per unit of CTI that map click intent into commercial value. We adopt a learnable affine maximizer auction (AMA), which jointly selects a shared generation configuration and determines advertiser-specific payments to maximize expected platform revenue. The resulting allocation and payment rules guarantee dominant-strategy incentive compatibility (DSIC) and individual rationality (IR). Experiments show that our generation method strengthens the monotonic relationship between target shares and CTI relative to baselines, while the learned AMA achieves higher platform revenue in the resulting auction setting.

</details>


### [167] [Rethinking Data Imbalance in Class Incremental Surgical Instrument Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34925)
*Shifang Zhao,Long Bai,Kun Yuan,Feng Li,Jieming Yu,Wenzhen Dong,Guankun Wang,Mobarakol Islam,Nicolas Padoy,Nassir Navab,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用框架 SurgCSS，通过修补生成背景、类别感知数据混合、类别脱敏损失与新旧模型权重动态融合，缓解手术器械增量分割中的数据不平衡与灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术器械种类随时间不断增加，现有神经网络难以有效学习增量任务且存在灾难性遗忘，学习新数据后旧数据性能骤降。已有手术场景持续学习方法又受数据不平衡影响，导致分割头产生强偏置：一是新旧数据间的类别不平衡，二是同一时间点内的类别不平衡，主导类别会主导持续语义分割（CSS）训练过程。

Method: 1) 通过图像修补（inpainting）生成逼真的手术背景，并以类别感知方式将器械前景与生成背景混合，以在多种场景下平衡数据分布；2) 提出类别脱敏损失（Class Desensitization Loss），利用对比学习纠正数据不平衡导致的边缘偏置；3) 动态融合新旧模型的权重参数，在偏置与非偏置权重之间取得更优折中；4) 整合多个数据集，构建手术器械 CSS 数据不平衡新基准。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架有效，相较现有基线取得显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: SurgCSS 作为即插即用框架，可有效应对手术器械持续语义分割中的数据不平衡问题，缓解灾难性遗忘并提升分割性能；所构建的基准为后续研究该问题提供了评测基础。

Abstract: In surgical instrument segmentation, the increasing variety of instruments over time poses a significant challenge for existing neural networks, as they are unable to effectively learn such incremental tasks and suffer from catastrophic forgetting. When learning new data, the model experiences a sharp performance drop on previously learned data. Although several continual learning methods have been proposed for incremental understanding tasks in surgical scenarios, the issue of data imbalance often leads to a strong bias in the segmentation head, resulting in poor performance. Data imbalance can occur in two forms: (i) class imbalance between new and old data, and (ii) class imbalance within the same time point of data. Such imbalances often cause the dominant classes to take over the training process of continual semantic segmentation (CSS). To address this issue, we propose SurgCSS, a novel plug-and-play CSS framework for surgical instrument segmentation under data imbalance. Specifically, we generate realistic surgical backgrounds through inpainting and blend instrument foregrounds with the generated backgrounds in a class-aware manner to balance the data distribution in various scenarios. We further propose the Class Desensitization Loss by employing contrastive learning to correct edge biases caused by data imbalance. Moreover, we dynamically fuse the weight parameters of the old and new models to achieve a better trade-off between the biased and unbiased model weights. To investigate the data imbalance problem in surgical scenarios, we construct a new benchmark for surgical instrument CSS by integrating datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, achieving significant performance improvement against existing baselines.

</details>


### [168] [Supervising the Visible State: Separating Representation from View Selection in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34926)
*Yihao Hu,Pan Wang,Yitong Lin,Zhihao Wen,Deyuan Liu,Hang Wang,Mingda Wan,Yiwen Zhao,Yutong Li,Yuchen Mao,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出流式视频问答存在"状态欠定"问题：相同答案在事件顺序、累积或关系不同的历史下未受约束。提出ViSS监督（基于源锚定的探针）与TVS测试时因果视图搜索，分别改进表征与证据访问，在StreamingBench与OVO-Bench上以更少帧数达到最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作识别式标注使"看一次"与"看三次"获得同一标签，监督不要求模型计数；共享答案掩盖了事件顺序、累积与关系差异，导致这些区分未被约束，即"状态欠定"（under-specification）。

Method: 1) Visible-State Supervision (ViSS)：对事件身份、顺序、累积、关系变化与答案充分性进行源锚定探针训练，在相同的近期帧接口下进行监督；2) Test-Time Causal View Search (TVS)：保持模型固定，仅自适应调整证据访问，按需从近期帧扩展到稀疏事件视图与紧凑历史视图。

Result: 在严格匹配的通用时序监督基线对比下，ViSS在OVO-Bench上提升、在StreamingBench上保持可比，幻觉检测增益最大；TVS在事件召回与前向推理上收益最显著；二者结合在所比较的已发表系统中取得StreamingBench与OVO-Bench最优精度，且处理帧数仅为穷举选择的一小部分；严格重放显示与三视图oracle的剩余差距主要是"正确但随后被替换"的答案。

Conclusion: 流式准确率取决于三点：监督要求模型保留什么、推理暴露哪些证据、以及模型在得到正确答案后能否保持它；表征监督与视图选择应解耦处理。

Abstract: An action seen once and the same action seen three times receive the same action-recognition label, so that supervision does not require a model to count. Answers shared across histories that differ in event order, accumulation, or relations leave those distinctions unconstrained; we call this state under-specification. Visible-State Supervision (ViSS) addresses it by training on source-grounded probes of event identity, order, accumulation, relation change, and answer sufficiency; under the same recent-frame interface it improves OVO-Bench over a strict-matched generic temporal supervision baseline while remaining comparable on StreamingBench, with the largest gain in hallucination detection. Test-Time Causal View Search (TVS) then adapts evidence access while keeping the learned model fixed, expanding from recent frames to sparse event and compact history views on demand. The two interventions separate representation from view selection: supervision particularly benefits hallucination detection and causal reasoning, access particularly benefits event recall and forward reasoning. Together they reach the best reported accuracy among the published systems compared here on StreamingBench and OVO-Bench at a fraction of the processed frames of exhaustive selection, and strict replay shows that most of the remaining gap to a three-view oracle is a correct answer that was later replaced. Streaming accuracy depends on what supervision asks the model to keep, what evidence inference exposes, and whether the model keeps a correct answer once it has one.

</details>


### [169] [Fed-MAPTrack: Federated Modality-aware Prompt Tuning for RGB-X Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34939)
*Xiangyu Liu,Yijie Shen,Yue Yin,Zhitong Yin,Yongqi Pan,Wentao Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fed-MAPTrack，一种面向RGB-X跟踪的模态感知联邦提示微调框架：冻结主干与预测头，仅交换提示相关参数，服务器对RGB参数全局聚合，对热红外/事件/深度参数分设独立参数库，并用选择性参与规则使纯RGB客户端只贡献RGB共享参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-X跟踪依赖多模态视频，集中式训练需在同一地点采集数据，隐私与成本受限；联邦训练虽保留数据本地性，但传感器异构性导致"参数共享域"与"实际贡献客户端"不匹配。尤其是纯RGB客户端与传感器客户端共存时，前者的RGB观测仍有用，却无法提供学习传感器相关交互所需的辅助模态测量。

Method: 1) 冻结预训练跟踪主干与预测头，客户端仅优化并交换提示（prompt）相关参数，降低通信与个性化冲突；2) 服务器端将参数分组：RGB共享参数做全局聚合，热红外、事件、深度各自维护独立参数库（含模态专用、融合与归一化参数）；3) 选择性参与机制：纯RGB客户端参与RGB全局库聚合，但其融合与归一化更新被排除在模态特定聚合之外；4) 在固定客户端划分下进行三次匹配训练重复实验，并做受控消融（如无兼容参与规则地扩大分桶范围）。

Result: 在三次匹配重复下：LasHeR热红外AUC较FedAvg平均提升2.35点，VisEvent的SR提升3.45点，DepthTrack的F-score提升2.11点。九种对比方法中，其热红外平均AUC与深度平均F-score均为最高；事件SR均值仅比Independent FL低0.07点。消融显示，在缺乏兼容参与规则时扩大分桶范围会带来性能下降。

Conclusion: 传感器异构下的联邦多模态跟踪，关键在于为每组参数同时界定"共享域"与"合格贡献者"。参数分桶与参与规则需协同设计，单纯扩大共享范围而无匹配的参与约束反而有害；所提框架在冻结主干、仅调提示的低开销设定下取得一致增益。

Abstract: RGB-X tracking complements RGB appearance with thermal, depth, or event observations, but centralized training requires collecting multimodal videos at a single location. Federated training keeps raw videos local, but sensor heterogeneity can create a mismatch between parameter-sharing domains and the clients contributing to them.
