This mismatch is particularly relevant when RGB-only clients coexist with sensor-equipped clients: their RGB observations remain useful, but they provide no auxiliary measurements for learning sensor-dependent interactions. We propose Fed-MAPTrack, a modality-aware federated prompt tuning framework for RGB-X tracking. The pretrained tracking backbone and prediction head are frozen, and clients optimize and exchange only prompt-related parameters. The server aggregates RGB-shared parameters globally and maintains separate thermal, event, and depth banks for auxiliary-specific, fusion, and normalization parameters. Selective participation retains RGB-only clients' contributions to the global RGB bank while excluding their fusion and normalization updates from modality-specific aggregation. Across three matched training repetitions under a fixed client partition, Fed-MAPTrack improves the mean FedAvg scores by 2.35 AUC points on LasHeR, 3.45 SR points on VisEvent, and 2.11 F-score points on DepthTrack. Among the nine evaluated methods, it achieves the highest mean thermal AUC and depth F-score, while its mean event SR is 0.07 points below that of Independent FL. Controlled ablations show that extending the bucketing scope without a compatible participation rule can reduce performance. These results highlight the need to jointly define the sharing domain and eligible contributors for each parameter group.

</details>


### [170] [DimFlow: Bidirectional Grid-Expert Learning for Dynamic Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34952)
*Gabriel Adineera,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DimFlow 通过多分辨率网格与坐标专家分支的双向交互学习紧凑动态场景特征，聚合 benchmark 上达 39.73 dB PSNR，较 4D-GS 提升 1.92 dB，同时保持 85 FPS 与相近存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态高斯重建需要紧凑的场景特征以支撑几何与外观的协同变化。因子化网格支持高效局部查询，但仅靠精确插值无法保证所得动态属性在空间与时间上保持兼容一致。

Method: 提出 DimFlow：以多分辨率网格与基于坐标的专家分支进行双向交互来学习特征。具体包含特征级对抗对齐（对齐两者分布）、自适应融合（传递互补信息）、激活掩码残差反馈（训练中精修网格）；随后由四个属性专家分别组织形变、旋转、不透明度与时间信息，再进行高斯渲染。

Result: 在 D-NeRF、HyperNeRF、DAVIS 的聚合比较中达到 39.73 dB PSNR，较 4D-GS 提升 1.92 dB，并保持其报告的 85 FPS 渲染速度；模型占用 18.9 MB，4D-GS 为 18.3 MB。组件与属性专家消融实验表明特征对齐、自适应交换与时间处理具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: 通过特征级分布对齐与双向自适应交互，可在近乎不增加存储与计算开销的前提下显著提升动态高斯重建质量，验证了网格与坐标专家分支互补融合的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian reconstruction requires compact scene features to support coordinated changes in geometry and appearance. A factorized grid provides efficient local queries, but accurate interpolation alone does not ensure that the resulting dynamic attributes remain compatible across space and time. We introduce DimFlow, a framework that learns these features through bidirectional interaction between a multi-resolution grid and a coordinate-based expert branch. Feature-level adversarial alignment connects their distributions, adaptive fusion transfers complementary information, and activation-masked residual feedback refines the grid during training. Four attribute experts organize deformation, rotation, opacity, and temporal information before Gaussian rendering. In the aggregate comparison across D-NeRF, HyperNeRF, and DAVIS, DimFlow achieves 39.73 dB PSNR, improving on 4D-GS by 1.92 dB while matching its reported 85 FPS rendering speed. The model occupies 18.9 MB, compared with 18.3 MB for 4D-GS. Component and attribute-expert ablations reveal complementary contributions from feature alignment, adaptive exchange, and temporal processing.

</details>


### [171] [GUIFlow: Temporal Redundancy-Aware Incremental Inference for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34959)
*Ye Li,Haoran Ding,Huanan Liu,Yuan Meng,Yutian Shen,Yuansong Wang,Kangye Ji,Hanyun Cui,Hongbo Duan,Chen Tang,Jiawei Li,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUIFlow 提出全流程增量推理框架，通过在视觉编码、预填充与解码三个阶段跨步骤复用计算，实现多图像 GUI 交互最高 3.39x（离线）/3.49x（OSWorld 在线闭环）加速且性能与原模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉语言模型的 GUI 智能体需与持续变化的界面多步交互，而常规推理对高度重叠的观测与历史反复重算表示，导致多步交互代价高昂；同时上下文、界面内容与动作参数的变化使简单缓存复用不可行。

Method: 核心观点是推进一次决策无需重建全部支撑表示。机制包括：（1）历史 KV 状态按位置对齐注入当前上下文；（2）利用变化信号引导视觉编码器与语言模型对当前观测状态做选择性更新；（3）以先前动作响应作为推测草稿并由目标模型验证。无需额外训练、独立草稿模型，也不改动模型可见输入与动作接口。

Result: 离线多图像基准上取得最高 3.39x 几何平均加速，OSWorld 在线闭环交互中最高 3.49x，任务性能与稠密基线总体相当。

Conclusion: 跨步骤计算复用可贯穿视觉编码、预填充与解码全流程，在保持精度的前提下显著降低 GUI 多步交互的推理开销。

Abstract: GUI agents built on vision-language models interact with continuously evolving interfaces, yet conventional inference repeatedly recomputes representations of largely overlapping observations and histories. This mismatch makes multi-step interaction costly, while changes in context, interface content, and action arguments prevent straightforward cache reuse. We introduce GUIFlow, a framework for full-pipeline incremental inference in multi-image GUI interaction. Our central insight is that advancing a decision need not require rebuilding all supporting representations: each interaction step can instead selectively update computational states maintained across the trajectory. To support new decisions as the context evolves, GUIFlow adapts how past computation is reused: historical KV states are position-aligned and injected into the current context; change signals guide selective updates of current-observation states in the vision encoder and language model; and earlier action responses serve as speculative drafts verified by the target model. Together, these mechanisms enable cross-step reuse throughout visual encoding, prefill, and decoding without additional training, a separate draft model, or changes to the model-visible inputs and action interface. Experiments demonstrate up to 3.39x geometric-mean speedup across offline multi-image benchmarks and up to 3.49x in online, closed-loop interaction on OSWorld, while maintaining task performance broadly comparable to the dense baseline.

</details>


### [172] [Sparse Recovery Bird's-Eye View Model Soup for Robust Camera-Based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34976)
*Jinwoo Chung,Sangho An,Hyunsung Park,Jangho Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SR-BEVS（稀疏恢复BEV模型汤），通过分层权重幅值确定剪枝候选、随机剪枝生成多样稀疏模型、以随机采样学习率重训后平均成单一稠密模型，在nuScenes上将Fast-BEV的干净精度提升1.8%、损坏鲁棒性最高提升33.8%，且零推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机BEV感知方法（如Fast-BEV）推理快、精度有竞争力，但其对真实世界corruption的鲁棒性研究不足，而自动驾驶部署中环境变化不可避免。现有鲁棒性提升通常依赖额外数据或结构改动，作者希望在无需额外数据与架构修改的前提下同时提升干净精度与损坏鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 基于逐层权重幅值确定剪枝候选；2) 对候选执行随机剪枝，生成多个多样化的稀疏模型；3) 对每个稀疏模型使用随机采样的学习率进行重训（sparse recovery）；4) 将多样化的恢复模型权重平均，合并为单一稠密模型；5) 通过模式连通性（mode connectivity）分析论证剪枝驱动的多样化仍保留权重平均所需的连通低损失盆地。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，相对Fast-BEV基线，损坏鲁棒性最高提升33.8%，干净精度提升1.8%，并在传感器噪声、天气畸变与光照变化下取得一致性提升；推理阶段无额外开销。

Conclusion: SR-BEVS是一种简单而有效的方法，可在不增加数据与不改动架构的条件下同时改善BEV感知的干净精度与损坏鲁棒性，且可推广至其他BEV模型。

Abstract: Camera-based bird's-eye view (BEV) perception methods such as Fast-BEV have demonstrated promising results with fast inference speed and competitive accuracy. However, their robustness to real-world corruptions remains insufficiently explored, posing risks for autonomous driving deployment where environmental variations are inevitable. In this paper, we propose Sparse Recovery Bird's-Eye View Model Soup (SR-BEVS), a sparse recovery learning approach that simultaneously improves clean accuracy and corruption robustness without requiring additional data or architectural modifications. Our method identifies pruning candidates based on layer-wise weight magnitudes, applies random pruning to create diverse sparse models, and retrains each model with a randomly sampled learning rate. By averaging these diverse recovered models into a single dense model, we achieve enhanced robustness against various corruptions while introducing zero inference overhead. We further provide a mode connectivity analysis demonstrating that our pruning-based diversification preserves the connected low-loss basin necessary for stable weight averaging. Experiments on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate up to 33.8% improvement in corruption robustness and a 1.8% gain in clean accuracy over the Fast-BEV baseline, with consistent improvements across sensor noise, weather distortions, and illumination changes. Our simple yet effective approach may be applicable to other BEV models.

</details>


### [173] [Reason or Recite: Complementary Specialists with an LLM Judge for Traffic Anomaly Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34993)
*Mayur Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出双专家框架 Reason-or-Recite (RoR)，针对交通异常视频理解中“闭式问答需精确推理、开放式回答受标注风格支配”的矛盾，分别训练一个推理专家和一个风格吸收专家，并用无训练 LLM 裁判逐题选择，在 2026 AI City Challenge 交通异常推理赛道排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通异常（碰撞、违规、突发危险）场景的实用系统不能只做检测，还需验证事件、描述场景、解释成因。然而视觉语言模型在这两类能力上存在张力：闭式问题奖励精确、有证据支撑的推理，开放式回答按与参考文本的相似度评分，匹配标注风格与推理正确性同等重要。单一模型难以同时兼顾两种目标。

Method: 采用两专家（two-specialist）框架：(1) 将官方思维链（chain-of-thought）标注重写为结构化、时间对齐（temporally grounded）的模式，并在增强轨迹上对 9B 视觉语言模型进行 LoRA 微调，形成推理专家；(2) 仅用答案对 4-bit 量化的 27B 模型微调，吸收标注风格，形成复述专家；(3) 通过无需训练的 LLM 裁判对每题在两个候选回答间进行选择。

Result: 在 2026 AI City Challenge 交通异常推理赛道 in-domain 排行榜上以平均分 0.6788 位列第一。

Conclusion: 将“推理”与“复述/风格匹配”解耦为两个专门化模型，并以轻量 LLM 裁判做实例级路由，可有效缓解闭式推理精度与开放式风格相似度评分之间的目标冲突，是一种低成本、可落地的竞赛方案。

Abstract: Road anomalies such as collisions, violations, and sudden hazards demand more from video understanding than detection: a practical system must verify what happened, describe the scene, and explain the cause. We find these competences pull in opposite directions for vision-language models: closed-form questions reward precise, evidence-grounded reasoning, while open-ended answers are scored by similarity to reference text, where matching the annotation style matters as much as reasoning correctly. We present Reason-or-Recite (RoR), a two-specialist framework for the Traffic Anomaly Reasoning track of the 2026 AI City Challenge. RoR (1) rewrites the official chain-of-thought annotations into a structured, temporally grounded schema and LoRA-tunes a 9B vision-language model on the enhanced traces; (2) fine-tunes a 4-bit-quantized 27B model on answers alone, absorbing the annotation style; and (3) selects between the two candidates per question with a training-free LLM judge. RoR ranks first on the in-domain leaderboard with mean score 0.6788.

</details>


### [174] [More Memory, Better Videos? A Controlled Study of Visual Memory in Long-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35001)
*Yuzhuo Chen,Huafeng Shi,Xinyu Wang,Yulong Xu,Yucheng Wang,Haoqin Hong,Guoxin Zhang,Zehua Ma,Weiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VMem-Bench诊断性基准，区分"历史忠实"与"当前故事正确"，证明显式视觉记忆检索并非总是有益


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成器普遍引入对过去视觉观测的检索以抑制身份漂移，隐含假设是"历史访问越多→一致性越好"；但"忠实于过去"的图像可能"错误于当前"（如故事已改变外观），故需检验视觉记忆是否真能提升生成故事质量

Method: VMem-Bench双轨诊断：Track-A在生成前、排除生成器混杂，衡量从92部真实影片（16,548段）中选取哪些参考；Track-B在共享生成器下，于30个因果生成故事（2,824段）上比较4种检索策略与无记忆、gold-informed last-seen两类对照，并提出appearance-stripping分析以剥离提示词自带外观信息的混杂

Result: 检索使实体召回由0.559升至0.628–0.693，并提升与过往外观的相似度；但精确率由0.977降至0.915–0.938，禁用实体规避率由0.968降至0.845–0.898；故事改变外观后检索还会强化过时外观；长间隔高召回可在无记忆时由提示自身供给外观而出现

Conclusion: 视觉记忆存在历史忠实与当前正确性的权衡，检索相关性或最终视频一致性本身不能证明记忆有效，需以当前故事正确性为判据

Abstract: Long-video generators increasingly retrieve past visual observations to prevent identity drift. The intuition is compelling: more access to history should make future video more consistent. But an image can be faithful to the past and wrong for the present. Does visual memory always improve the generated story? We investigate this question through VMem-Bench, a two-track diagnostic study. Track-A measures which references are selected from 92 real films (16,548 segments), before generation and without generator confounds. Track-B compares four retrieval policies with no-memory and gold-informed last-seen controls on 30 causally generated stories (2,824 segments) using a shared generator. Retrieval increases entity recall from 0.559 to 0.628-0.693 and improves similarity to past appearances, but decreases precision from 0.977 to 0.915-0.938 and forbidden-entity avoidance from 0.968 to 0.845-0.898. It can also reinforce obsolete appearances after the story changes them. Conversely, high long-gap recall can arise without memory when the prompt itself supplies the appearance; an appearance-stripping analysis exposes this confound. These results distinguish historical fidelity from current-story correctness and show why retrieval relevance or final-video consistency alone does not establish memory utility.

</details>


### [175] [DUET4D: Self-Corrective Coupled Guidance for Sparse-View 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35002)
*Beizhen Zhao,Nanjie Yao,Boyi Fu,Qingsong Yan,Haofan Ren,Kaiyong Zhao,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DUET4D自校正耦合引导框架，通过4D高斯表征引导生成先验产生时空一致的监督信号，实现稀疏视角4D重建的质量与效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下4D重建严重欠约束。视频生成模型计算代价高，不适用于迭代重建；单视角模型生成的伪观测在时间上不一致；且直接将不确定的生成输出纳入高斯优化易产生冗余基元与漂浮伪影。

Method: 构建自校正耦合引导框架：(1) 从当前高斯证据构建序列对齐，引导潜空间复原；(2) 采用Sequential Fidelity Resampling将生成监督提升到高斯空间，选择性区分被反复支持的基元；(3) 校正后的表征为下一轮复原提供更可靠的高斯证据，形成闭环迭代。

Result: 大量实验表明DUET4D显著提升了渲染质量与渲染效率。

Conclusion: 以4D表征与生成先验双向耦合、自校正迭代的方式，可在稀疏视角条件下获得时空一致且高效的4D高斯重建。

Abstract: Sparse-view 4D reconstruction is highly under-constrained when only a few cameras are available. Generative priors can recover missing appearances, but video models are computationally impractical for iterative reconstruction, whereas single-view models produce temporally inconsistent pseudo-observations. Moreover, directly absorbing uncertain generative outputs into Gaussian optimization often creates redundant primitives and floating artifacts. To address these challenges, we propose DUET4D, a self-corrective coupled guidance framework for sparse-view 4D Gaussian splatting. The core of our approach is a self-corrective framework where the 4D representation guides generative prior for spatio-temporal consistent supervisions. We first construct sequential alignments from current Gaussian evidence to steer the latent restoration. Then we use Sequential Fidelity Resampling to lift generative supervisions into Gaussian space to selectively distinguish repeatedly supported primitives. The corrected representation then provides more reliable Gaussian evidence for the next restoration cycle. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DUET4D significantly improves rendering quality and rendering efficiency.

</details>


### [176] [Shifted Window Temporal Transformer for YOLO-Guided Phase Recognition in Laparoscopic Cholecystectomy Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35005)
*Sheekar Banerjee,Md. Shahabuddin,Md. Mahbub Hasan Akash,Nabilah,Mohammad Arafat Hussain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合Swin Transformer与时间Transformer编码器(TTE)的手术阶段识别框架，并并行YOLOv11s实现器械定位，配合因果时间平滑抑制阶段闪烁，在Cholec80上达92%准确率、宏平均F1=0.86，器械mAP@0.5=0.863。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手术室需要准确的手术流程感知以支持术中决策、团队协作与手术记录，但现有AI方法在阶段转换处预测波动明显（阶段闪烁），限制了临床应用。

Method: Swin Transformer + Temporal Transformer Encoder用于阶段识别；并行YOLOv11s网络用于实时器械定位；引入因果时间平滑（causal temporal smoothing）以获得稳定流程预测；在Cholec80数据集上同步评估阶段与器械识别。

Result: 阶段识别总体准确率92%，宏平均F1为0.86，优于EndoNet(81.7%)、SV-RCNet(85.3%)、TeCNO(88.6%)、Trans-SVNet(90.9%)、LoViT(91.5%)；器械检测mAP@0.5=0.863。

Conclusion: 结果验证了框架在高流程识别率与高器械定位精度上的可靠性，可作为智能手术辅助与提升态势感知的基础，并具备机器人手术应用潜力；代码将公开。

Abstract: Intelligent operating rooms require accurate awareness of surgical workflow for better intra-operative decisions, teamwork, and surgical documentation. Therefore, accurately recognizing surgical phase during surgery is crucial. However, previous artificial intelligence-based methods provide fluctuating predictions for the transitions, restricting their clinical use. To address this challenge, we propose a clinically meaningful framework that integrates a Swin Transformer and Temporal Transformer Encoder (TTE) for effective surgical phase recognition and a parallel YOLOv11s network for real-time surgical instrument localization. To avoid phase flickering and to produce stable workflow predictions, a causal temporal smoothing is applied. The proposed framework is tested on the Cholec80 dataset for the simultaneous recognition of the surgical phases and instruments. The overall phase recognition accuracy of this model was 92%, and the macro average F1-score was 0.86. This performance surpasses CNN based methods such as EndoNet (81.7%) and SV-RCNet (85.3%), as well as temporal convolutional and transformer-based approaches including TeCNO (88.6%), Trans-SVNet (90.9%), and LoViT (91.5%). Besides, the module of instrument detection achieved mAP@0.5 = 0.863. The reliability of the results is confirmed, obtaining a high recognition rate of the workflows, and a high accuracy of the localization of the instruments. The proposed framework can serve as a foundation for intelligent surgical assistance, enriching situation awareness, and potential robotic surgery applications. Code will be made publicly available at: https://github.com/ac005sheekar/LapChol-Surgery-Swin-Cholec80

</details>


### [177] [When Pointing Becomes Profiling: Social Bias and Refusal in Text-Steerable Visual Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35017)
*Seyedarmin Azizi,Parsa Razmara,Sajjad Ghiasvand,Ardalan Aryashad,Tina Khezresmaeilzadeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 审计发现 SteerViT 的文本引导定位接口会把语言指向转化为人口统计画像（性别/种族路由），且模型无法拒绝；提出 SafeSteer，用学习式弃答输出+反事实不变性训练修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SteerViT 类文本可分引导的视觉编码器在自然语言提示下输出提示条件热图与特征，但其"必须把 softmax 质量放到某处"的机制不允许拒绝，可能把无法落地的请求（缺席目标、需刻板印象推断的请求）强行路由到敏感人群。作者希望审计该风险并给出修复方案。

Method: 在反事实人脸拼贴与真实多人照片上对已发布 SteerViT 做行为审计（对照基础率、对比职业词、对照 backbone 无引导显著性、对照冻结文本编码器、随引导强度与跨注意力层的消融，并横向比较 CLIPSeg、Grounding-DINO、SAM 3、Qwen2.5-VL）。提出 SafeSteer：在同样 23M 引导参数上增加可学习弃答输出，并施加反事实不变性训练。

Result: 审计：'the person' 选中男性高于基础率；犯罪词比职业词更常选中黑人；四女图中的 'the man' 有 75.6% 选中黑人女性；'the monkey' 选中黑人。这些效应不在 backbone 无引导显著性中，也不在冻结文本编码器中，随引导强度增长，且仅由最后两层交叉注意力携带。四个对比模型无犯罪路由、种族路由更弱。原模型区分可定位请求与应弃答请求仅达随机水平（AUROC 0.52）。SafeSteer：可分提示 AUROC 0.997，泛化到留出敏感/缺席词 0.998；0.5 阈值下拒绝 99.8% 训练敏感词、62% 留出敏感词；对可见人物无过度拒答；大幅削弱种族路由，定位与检索性能在 1 点内。

Conclusion: 文本引导定位中的"必须指向"约束构成安全漏洞，可被转化为人口统计画像且模型不能拒答；该效应源于引导侧交叉注意力而非视觉骨干或文本编码器，因此可在引导参数上以弃答+反事实不变性训练低成本修复，且性能损失可忽略。

Abstract: Text-steerable visual encoders condition a frozen Vision Transformer on a natural-language prompt and point at the matching image region, producing prompt-conditioned heatmaps and features. We audit the released SteerViT model and find that its steering interface turns language-driven pointing into demographic profiling. On counterfactual face mosaics and real multi-person photographs, "the person" selects a man more often than the base rate, crime words select Black people more often than occupation words do, "the man" among four women selects the Black woman 75.6% of the time on SocialCounterfactuals, and "the monkey" selects Black people. None of them is in the backbone's unsteered saliency, and the crime association is not in the frozen text encoder; the race effects grow with steering strength and enter through the last two cross-attention layers: giving the prompt to those layers alone reproduces them. Four other grounding models, CLIPSeg, Grounding-DINO, SAM 3, and Qwen2.5-VL, show no crime routing and weaker race routing. The model cannot refuse. Its patch softmax must place its mass somewhere, and its heatmap separates a groundable request ("the police officer") from one that is absent ("the pizza") or that would require a stereotyped demographic inference ("the criminal") only at chance (AUROC 0.52). We introduce SafeSteer, which adds a learned abstention output and counterfactual-invariance training on the same 23M steering parameters. SafeSteer separates should-point from should-abstain prompts at AUROC 0.997, generalizes to held-out sensitive and absent words (0.998; at a 0.5 threshold it refuses 99.8% of trained and 62% of held-out sensitive words) with no over-refusal of visible people on the benchmark, and removes most race routing while keeping localization and retrieval within 1 point of the released model. Code, benchmarks, and the curated mosaic sets will be released.

</details>


### [178] [Zero-Shot Monocular Depth Estimation for Visual Perception in Lower-Limb Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35018)
*Ishi Vijayvergiya,Marc Hölle,Youssef Dawoud,Amir El-Ghoussani,Michele De Vita,Rohan Asthana,Philipp Beckerle,Vasileios Belagiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出下肢假肢（LP）数据集，用于从模拟膝部假肢视角评估单目深度估计基础模型的感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计基础模型零样本泛化能力强，但其在下肢假肢视觉感知中的适用性尚未被探索；而可靠深度感知对假肢使用者应对楼梯、不平地形等复杂环境至关重要。

Method: 构建Lower-Limb Prosthesis (LP)基准数据集，采集模拟膝部高度假肢视角的图像，并在其上系统评测四种最先进单目深度估计基础模型。

Result: 给出了首个针对假肢视角的单目深度估计系统性基准评测结果，揭示了基础模型在该场景下的挑战与潜力。

Conclusion: 将基础模型部署于假肢感知系统具有可行性但仍面临挑战，LP数据集为该方向提供了标准化评测基准，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has advanced rapidly with the development of foundation models that demonstrate strong zero-shot generalisation across diverse visual domains. However, the applicability of these models to visual perception for lower-limb prosthetics remains largely unexplored. Reliable depth perception is particularly important for prosthesis users, who often experience significant challenges when navigating stairs, uneven terrain, and other complex environments. To address this gap, we introduce the Lower-Limb Prosthesis (LP) dataset, a benchmark designed to evaluate depth perception from an emulated knee-level prosthetic viewpoint. Using this dataset, we evaluate four state-of-the-art monocular depth estimation foundation models. Our results provide the first systematic benchmark of monocular depth estimation performance from a prosthetic viewpoint, while highlighting both the challenges and the potential of deploying foundation models in prosthetic perception systems. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Ishivijay/lp_dataset.

</details>


### [179] [Revealing Imaging Characteristics of Thermal Wakes from Underwater Vehicles Without Cooling-Water Discharge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35038)
*Guoyi Zhang,Yanjin Du,Guangsheng Xu,Zhengyao Zhao,Siyang Chen,Jingjing Gao,Yuezheng Zhou,Yiyang Wu,Bin Wu,Tongsu Zhang,Lejia Ye,Donghe Wang,Jie Wang,Kaiye Hu,Zhipeng Ren,Han Wang,Boo Cheong Khoo,Xiangpeng Xu,Xiaohu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出首个大规模真实红外热尾迹基准 AVP-IR-Wake（161,485帧，历时三年），揭示小uuv热尾迹具有"局部不可分、全局形态可分"的特性，主张检测范式应从局部特征提取转向全局轨迹约束的物理建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人水下航行器扩散威胁海上安全，却能规避为大型潜艇设计的声呐与磁传感器。红外热尾迹提供了非声非磁的替代探测途径（即使无冷却水排放也因流体位移调制冷皮层而存在），但现有研究依赖理想化仿真，缺乏真实背景，难以指导目标-背景判别。

Method: 构建 AVP-IR-Wake 基准数据集：首个大规模、真实世界、物理多样性的红外热尾迹数据，共161,485帧，历时三年采集。在此基础上分析尾迹的时空、频率与稀疏域统计特性，并将其与风致背景涡旋进行对比。

Result: 热尾迹是由离散涡旋构成的延展结构，违背常规目标的紧凑性假设；局部层面存在不可分性——单个尾迹涡旋在时间域、频率域和稀疏域与风致背景涡旋统计上无法区分，共享低频信息与长程相关性，挑战了"紧凑、瞬态、高频目标"的传统检测假设；但全局层面存在形态可分性——尾迹整体由航行器轨迹组织，而背景杂波由风驱动热锋组织。这解释了代表性检测器失效的原因。

Conclusion: 可靠检测必须从局部特征提取转向全局轨迹约束的物理建模。由于冷皮层存在全球与季节变化，预测尾迹可观测性需融合物理海洋学与大气科学，从而将水下监视与变化的海表环境联系起来。

Abstract: Small uncrewed underwater vehicles are proliferating and threaten maritime security, yet evade sonar and magnetic sensors built for larger submarines. Infrared thermal wakes, generated by hydrodynamic displacement that modulates the cold-skin layer, offer a non-acoustic, non-magnetic alternative even without cooling-water discharge. Existing studies, however, rely on idealized simulations that fail to capture real-world backgrounds, offering limited guidance for target-background discrimination. We establish AVP-IR-Wake, the first large-scale, real-world, physically diverse benchmark of 161,485 frames collected over three years. We show that thermal wakes are extended structures of discrete vortices, violating the compactness assumption of conventional targets. We reveal local indistinguishability: individual wake vortices are statistically identical to wind-induced background vortices in temporal, frequency, and sparse domains, sharing low-frequency information and long-range correlations. This challenges the assumptions of compact, transient, high-frequency targets in conventional detection. Yet we establish global morphological separability: although locally inseparable, the wake as a whole is organized by vehicle trajectory, whereas background clutter is organized by wind-driven thermal fronts. This explains why representative detectors fail and shows that reliable detection must shift from local feature extraction to global trajectory-constrained physical modeling. Because the cold-skin layer varies globally and seasonally, forecasting wake observability will require integrating physical oceanography and atmospheric science, linking underwater surveillance to the changing ocean surface.

</details>


### [180] [DriveUniTok: A Unified Multimodal Tokenizer for Driving World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35044)
*Jingbo Xu,Hanchao Leng,JiaHui Huang,Hanbing Li,Jingwei Zhao,Kuiyuan Yang,Chen Gao,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一多模态分词器 DriveUniTok 与扩散式驾驶世界动作模型 DriveUniWAM，将外观、投影LiDAR深度与自车运动编码为单一连续隐变量，在NAVSIM上实现更优预测与规划并达到91.54 PDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型对外观、度量几何（投影LiDAR深度）与自车运动部分建模或独立建模，忽视了它们是同一底层世界状态的不同投影，导致信息损失与跨模态不一致，限制下游性能。

Method: 提出统一多模态分词器DriveUniTok，将三种模态编码为单一可解码的连续隐空间；冻结该分词器并适配预训练扩散模型DriveUniWAM预测未来统一隐变量，再由分词器解码出未来外观、投影LiDAR深度与自车轨迹；并采用强化学习后训练。

Result: 在NAVSIM上，统一设计相比分离流方案取得更好的未来预测与规划性能，且下游计算开销更低；经强化学习后训练后PDMS达91.54，在被评估的WAM类规划器中达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 以"共享世界状态的不同投影"为统一视角建模多模态，可在降低计算成本的同时提升驾驶世界动作模型的预测与规划能力。

Abstract: World models are emerging as a fundamental component of policy learning for real-world agents, enabling agents to predict future world states and plan actions within a learned representation of the dynamic environment. Existing approaches, however, often model appearance, metric geometry represented by projected LiDAR depth, and ego motion either partially or independently, overlooking the fact that these modalities are different projections of a shared underlying world state. This separation can lead to information loss and cross-modal inconsistency, ultimately limiting downstream performance. Motivated by this unified view of the world, we propose DriveUniTok, a unified multimodal tokenizer for driving world-action models (WAMs) that encodes these modalities in a single decodable continuous latent. We freeze DriveUniTok and adapt a pretrained diffusion model, DriveUniWAM, to predict future unified latents; the tokenizer then decodes them into future appearance, projected LiDAR depth, and ego trajectories. Experiments on NAVSIM demonstrate that our unified design achieves better future prediction and planning performance at lower downstream computational cost than separate-stream alternatives. With reinforcement learning post-training, DriveUniWAM achieves a PDMS of 91.54, reaching state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated WAM-based planners.

</details>


### [181] [LOCUS: Locate Once, Check Until Settled. Draft-and-Verify Visual Grounding with a Box Head and Coordinate-Token Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35053)
*Muhammad Talha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Locus将回归头作为坐标token的投机drafter，用Jacobi迭代在共享图像前缀上验证，以约5.60次顺序前向替代18.46步自回归解码，兼顾精确度与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM定位referring expression有两种方式：坐标token解码精确但逐token串行耗时；回归头一步出框但高IoU下精度下降。二者的失效模式互补——回归头通常找对目标但边界松散，坐标token边界精确。作者据此希望用回归头为模型自身的坐标token做投机起草，兼得两者优势。

Method: 把回归头输出的框写成坐标token作为投机草稿，用同一backbone在共享图像前缀上通过Jacobi迭代并行验证；不动点即模型的贪心坐标token答案；drafter与verifier共享一套权重并联合训练。

Result: 在8个RefCOCO/RefCOCO+/RefCOCOg划分上，验证将Locus-2B的Acc@0.9从自身头的54.87提升到59.13；单次前向头在Acc@0.5上超过除Florence-2-L外所有≤3B参数的已发表模型。与同规模同训练预算的Jag相比，Acc@0.5持平，典型接受率下Acc@0.9提升+4.52（配对显著性检验）。精确答案生成比vLLM贪心解码相同坐标token快1.5倍，但纯回归头仍更快。

Conclusion: 回归头可作为坐标token的有效投机drafter，验证机制显著提升高IoU精度并把串行解码步数降至约5.60次；在以精度换速度的权衡中，Locus在精确答案上快于自回归解码，但纯回归头在速度上仍占优。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) ground a referring expression in one of two ways. They decode the box as coordinate tokens, which is precise but takes one sequential step per token, or they regress it from a hidden state with a small head, which takes one step but loses accuracy at high IoU. The two fail differently: a head usually finds the right object but places its edges loosely, while coordinate tokens place edges precisely. We therefore use the regression head as a speculative drafter for the model's own coordinate tokens. Locus writes the head's box as coordinate tokens and checks them with the same backbone by Jacobi iteration over a shared image prefix. The fixed point is the model's greedy coordinate-token answer, reached in about 5.60 sequential passes on average instead of 18.46 decoding steps; head and verifier are one set of weights, trained jointly. On the 8 RefCOCO, RefCOCO+ and RefCOCOg splits, verification raises Locus-2B's Acc@0.9 from 54.87 for its own head to 59.13, and the one-pass head is ahead at Acc@0.5 of every published model with at most 3B parameters except Florence-2-L. Against Jag, the strongest open regression-head model, at its own size and training budget, Locus ties at Acc@0.5 and, with typical acceptance, gains +4.52 points at Acc@0.9, with paired significance tests. Exact answers come 1.5x faster than vLLM greedy-decoding the same coordinate tokens with the same weights; a pure regression head remains faster still.

</details>


### [182] [Kronecker-Structured Video Completion with Cross-Fitted Self-Supervised Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35065)
*Qi Qi,Yanfei Wang,Yanjun Cui,Jun-e Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段管状视频补全框架：先用正则化掩码Kronecker积分解(KPD)从观测项估计紧凑结构骨架，再用交叉拟合的自监督残差学习（3D CNN）修正缺失管的预测误差，并保持观测数据一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管状缺失（某些空间位置在所有帧均未观测）使传统帧内/低秩方法失效；同时骨架模型的样本内拟合误差与缺失管处的真实预测误差存在失配，需要显式校正。

Method: 第一阶段：对观测元素做正则化掩码KPD，采用循环分量最小二乘更新、空间与时间因子正则化及正则项最优尺度平衡，保证目标非增。第二阶段：将观测管划分为折叠，用排除目标管的骨架模型构造残差目标，训练轻量3D CNN依据骨架、可见残差上下文、观测掩码和局部细节特征预测修正；最后施加精确数据一致性约束。

Result: 灰度基准高采样率下平均PSNR与SSIM最高；在8个DAVIS RGB序列上，各采样率下缺失区域平均PSNR、全帧SSIM最高，缺失管时间梯度MAE最低；并含消融、敏感性分析及结构化缺失实验。

Conclusion: 正则化KPD骨架加交叉拟合自监督残差学习可有效解决管状视频补全中的模型失配问题，在灰度与彩色视频及结构化缺失模式下均优于对比方法。

Abstract: Tube-wise video completion reconstructs temporal trajectories at spatial locations unobserved in every frame. We propose a two-stage framework combining regularized masked Kronecker product decomposition (KPD) with cross-fitted self-supervised residual learning. First, we estimate a compact structural backbone directly from observed entries using cyclic component-wise least-squares updates, spatial and temporal factor regularization, and regularizer-optimal scale balancing. The exact updates yield a nonincreasing objective. Second, we address the mismatch between in-sample fitting errors and the prediction errors encountered at missing tubes. We partition observed tubes into folds and construct residual targets using backbone models fitted without the corresponding target tubes. A lightweight 3-D convolutional neural network then predicts corrections from the backbone, visible residual context, observation mask, and local detail features. Exact data consistency preserves observed measurements. Experiments include ablations, sensitivity analysis, grayscale and RGB video completion, and structured missing patterns. Our method achieves the highest mean PSNR and SSIM at the higher tested sampling rates on the grayscale benchmark. On eight selected DAVIS RGB sequences, it achieves the highest mean missing-region PSNR and full-frame SSIM and the lowest mean missing-tube temporal-gradient MAE at every evaluated sampling rate.

</details>


### [183] [MemVLN: Streaming Episodic Memory for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35066)
*Yan Wang,Jiangyong Huang,Baoxiong Jia,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEMVLN：面向流式视觉语言导航的有限容量情景记忆框架，通过压缩感知特征+记忆token实现长程记忆，在VLN-CE多指标达SOTA并完成真机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM驱动的流式智能体在长时序3D导航中难以维持持续的情景记忆（episodic memory），缺少对历史观测的有效压缩与检索机制，限制了长程指令跟随能力。

Method: 构建有界（bounded）记忆库，存储带时间戳与动作推导相对位姿的压缩感知特征，经交叉注意力生成紧凑记忆token；与冻结的预训练几何编码器配合；设计基于掩码的轨迹摘要任务，采用多粒度子轨迹分解，迫使路线重建只能依赖情景记忆，从而训练记忆模块。

Result: 导航训练仅使用官方R2R/RxR轨迹，在标准VLN-CE基准多数指标上取得SOTA；长程分析刻画了情景记忆的有效条件；真机在单场景30条指令条件episode（24条示范后训练、6条测试）中，成功率由VLM基线17%提升至40%，后训练进一步提升至47%。

Conclusion: 有界压缩情景记忆可有效支撑VLM流式长程导航，掩码轨迹摘要是一种有效的记忆学习信号，且仿真性能可迁移到真实环境，后训练进一步提升实机表现。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to follow language instructions through 3D environments. Maintaining persistent episodic memory remains a challenge for VLM-based streaming agents in long-horizon navigation. We present MEMVLN, a streaming episodic memory framework that maintains a bounded bank of compressed perceptual features annotated with timesteps and action-derived relative poses, and produces compact memory tokens via cross-attention. We pair the proposed memory with a frozen pretrained geometry encoder. To learn the memory module, we design a masking-based trajectory summarization task with multi-granularity sub-trajectory decomposition, encouraging route reconstruction exclusively from episodic memory. Under navigation training restricted to the official R2R/RxR trajectories, MEMVLN achieves state-of-the-art performance on most reported metrics on standard VLN-CE benchmarks. Long-horizon analyses further characterize when episodic memory is useful. For physical evaluation, we collect 30 instruction-conditioned episodes in one physical scene, use 24 as demonstrations for post-training, and hold out six for testing. On the held-out episodes, MEMVLN improves success over the VLM baseline from 17% to 40%, and post-training further raises it to 47%.

</details>


### [184] [Blendshape Generation for Models with Disconnected Parts: Adding ARKit/MediaPipe Blendshapes to Anime-Style Character Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35076)
*Pramook Khungurn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出针对多分离部件动漫风格3D角色的固定表情Blendshape生成案例研究，通过多网络分别处理各部件并以锚定部件协调，实现拓扑无关的形变场生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Blendshape生成研究多针对单一连通网格，极少处理含多个不连通部件的模型；商业动漫风格角色脸部常多达11个部件，用户社区有真实需求。作者希望方法对拓扑不敏感、且比近期神经网络方案更简单。

Method: 训练神经网络在输入网格周围生成形变场（deformation fields），对每个部件至少用一个网络独立处理；每个网络同时处理一个锚定部件（anchor part）的几何，以协调各部件输出的相对位置。两项精度提升机制：预测位移是否为零（zero/non-zero displacement 分类）与按尺寸分组、每组专用一个网络。另引入提升双侧对称性以改进Blendshape质量的方法。

Result: 方法可处理每个面部最多11个部件的商业动漫模型，生成固定表情Blendshape；通过对齐锚定部件实现部件间空间一致，零位移预测与按尺寸分组提升精度，对称性增强进一步改善质量。原文未给出定量指标。

Conclusion: 多网络+锚定部件协调的形变场框架可拓扑无关地解决多分离部件模型的Blendshape生成；方法较现有神经网络工作更简单，并解决了该类模型用户社区的实际问题。

Abstract: Blendshape generation for 3D models has been extensively researched, but very few works address generating blendshapes for models with multiple disconnected parts. We present a case study of generating fixed facial expression blendshapes for commercial anime-style 3D character models with up to 11 parts per face. To make sure that our system can handle models in a way that is agnostic to topology, we train neural networks to generate deformation fields around the input meshes. We employ at least one network to independently process each part, and the relative positions of their outputs are coordinated by having each network also process the geometry of an anchor part. We use two mechanisms to improve accuracy: having the networks predict whether the output displacement is zero or not, and classifying blendshapes into different size groups and dedicating a network to each group. We also introduce a method to improve blendshape quality by enhancing bilateral symmetry. Our method is simpler than recent neural-network-based works and solves a real problem of the user community of these models.

</details>


### [185] [Improving VLMs on Scientific Chart Analysis via Autonomous Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35077)
*Abhinav Sudhakar Dubey,Vidushi Goel,Kiran Rana,Shikhar Mathur,Vaggos Chatziafratis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过自主进化搜索推理程序与代码，在不改变"冻结"视觉语言模型的前提下，将其在科学图表问答上的精确匹配准确率从平均16.6%提升至41.3%，且推理速度提升3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源视觉语言模型在基于科学图表的问答任务上表现较差，但作者初步观察发现，许多错误源于"答案不精确"而非理解失败。由此提出核心问题：在不改变模型本身的情况下，能通过优化推理流程挽回多少准确率？

Method: 借鉴AlphaEvolve式的自主进化思路，以"冻结"模型为中心搜索推理程序和外围代码。从基线推理程序出发，每轮让LLM评判器审视代码及训练问题上的若干失败案例，提出改进的进化编辑；候选程序维护在一个小规模程序池中并按训练准确率排序，后续编辑只针对池中的高分程序进行。在3个VLM和4个科学图表基准上评测描述性问题的精确匹配率。

Result: 所有实验设置下准确率均有提升：平均从起始程序的16.6%升至进化后的41.3%，单项最大提升达55.2个百分点；副作用是进化后的程序比起始程序快3.8倍（短答案更易评分、生成更快）。

Conclusion: 观测到的大量错误源自推理流水线本身，而非模型能力上限；仅优化推理程序即可在无需重新训练的情况下显著提升"冻结"VLM的性能，同时获得效率收益。

Abstract: Understanding and operating with scientific charts is a standard approach in data analysis, and while humans are good at interpreting such charts, open vision-language models (VLMs) are known to perform poorly when it comes to question answering based on such data types. Motivated by an initial observation that many of their errors are due to inexact answering, we raise the following natural question: how much accuracy can we recover without changing the model at all? In this paper, we study this question by searching over inference programs and the code around a "frozen" version of the model. Using ideas from autonomous evolution (famously used in AlphaEvolve), we first start from a baseline inference program, and in each round we show an LLM judge the code together with a few failures on training questions, and ask for an improving evolutionary move. As usual in autonomous evolution approaches, the candidate programs are kept in a small pool ranked by training accuracy, and the next edit is performed on top-scorers from this pool. We run this approach on three VLMs and four popular benchmarks on scientific charts, and we measure the fraction of descriptive questions whose answer exactly matches the ground truth. The accuracy improves in all experimental settings. On average, it rises from 16.6% with the starting program to 41.3% with the evolved one, and the largest single increase is 55.2 percentage points. As a side benefit, our evolved program is 3.8x faster than the starting program, because short answers are easier to grade and faster to generate. Our results demonstrate that a large part of the observed errors arise due to the inference pipeline, and that optimizing it can improve the "frozen" VLM without the need to retrain it.

</details>


### [186] [Reading the Registers of Honorius III without Manual Line Transcription: Forced-Aligned HTR, End to End, Modular or Hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35085)
*Tamas Kovacs,Daniel Luger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对教宗Honorius III登记册（Reg. Vat. 9，5000+封仅存手稿图像的信件），利用免行切分的专家解稿借助强制对齐自动引导13122条训练行，训练端到端的混合Conformer HTR模型，联合完成转写与缩写展开。在测试行上达到4.7% CER、8.7% WER（ROVER 8.4%），显著优于基线（18.9% CER）与模块化管线（15.6% WER）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模解锁中世纪档案的瓶颈在于行级别外交体（diplomatic）转写与人工缩写展开成本极高。本文希望在不增加人工行切分与对齐成本的前提下，利用已有的、无行断标记的专家解稿（edition drafts）训练端到端的HTR，直接联合完成重缩写拉丁文的转写与展开。

Method: 1) 在Reg. Vat. 9的394页、860条未对齐解稿条目上，用强制对齐自动生成13122条训练行，无需人工行分割与对齐；2) 从零训练混合Conformer模型：CTC + 双向子词注意力解码器，结合迭代自对齐（self-realignment）与n-best语言模型重打分；3) 对比CATMuS通用基线与无空格HTR+分词模块化管线；4) 研究保真与编辑规范化间的目标选择张力（保留抄写员姓名首字母）；5) 消融辅助外交体CTC头、copy机制、置换解码器、掩码图像预训练，并考察padding-mask传播与逐行beam终止对批量不变性的影响。

Result: CATMuS基线CER 18.9%，模块化管线WER 15.6%，本文混合模型CER 4.7%、WER 8.7%（ROVER组合后8.4%），句末词错误率从22.7%降至12.2% WER（以上基于能与解稿对齐的测试行，最佳模型覆盖86%测试行）。保留抄写员姓名首字母的目标使对原稿文本的WER在31条相关测试行上由13.3%降至9.2%，代价是相对展开解稿仅WER +0.3、CER +0.4。消融显示辅助外交体CTC头、copy机制、置换解码器、掩码图像预训练均不优于直接端到端监督；严格的padding-mask传播与逐行beam终止可保证推理的批量不变性。

Conclusion: 免行断的专家解稿经强制对齐足以支撑端到端HTR，联合转写与缩写展开展现在中世纪登记册上可大幅降低错误率；混合CTC+注意力Conformer配合自对齐与n-best LM重打分是有效方案，而多种额外结构（辅助头、copy、置换解码器、预训练）并无增益。同时需在古文字保真与编辑规范化之间做权衡，且工程上需保证批量不变的推理。

Abstract: Unlocking medieval archives at scale is bottlenecked by the cost of line-level diplomatic transcriptions and manual abbreviation expansion. Focusing on the registers of Pope Honorius III (1216-1227) over 5,000 letters surviving in full only as manuscript images - we show that existing, line-break-free expert transcriptions (edition drafts) suffice to train end-to-end hand-written text recognition (HTR) that jointly transcribes and expands heavily abbreviated Latin. Using forced alignment on 860 unaligned edition entries across 394 pages of Reg. Vat. 9, we automatically bootstrap 13,122 training lines without manual alignment of segmented lines. While the generic CATMuS baseline struggles on this domain (18.9% CER) and a modular space-free HTR + word-segmentation pipeline (cf. Camps et al. 2021) plateaus at 15.6% WER, our from-scratch hybrid Conformer - combining CTC with bidirectional subword attention decoders, iterative self-realignment and n-best LM rescoring - achieves 4.7% CER and 8.7% WER (8.4% WER with ROVER system combination), nearly halving errors on clause-final words (22.7% to 12.2% WER). These figures are measured on the test lines that align with the transcription (86% of all test lines for the best hybrid). Furthermore, we address a tension between paleographic fidelity and editorial normalization: training on targets that preserve scribal name initials reduces the WER against the manuscript text on the 31 affected test lines from 13.3% to 9.2%, at a small cost against the expanded transcription (WER +0.3 points, CER +0.4). Finally, extensive ablations show that none of an auxiliary diplomatic CTC head, a copy mechanism, a permutation decoder or masked-image pretraining improves on direct end-to-end supervision, while strict padding-mask propagation and per-line beam termination make inference batch-invariant.

</details>


### [187] [Test-Time Prompt-Agnostic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35090)
*Junze Wang,Lei Fan,Dezheng Zhang,Donglin Di,Yang Song,Sidong Liu,Cong Cong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出测试时提示无关分解TPD，从动力系统视角诊断提示更新轨迹的幅度膨胀与方向漂移，并以AKC（自适应Koopman控制）与HUR（Hankel更新路由）稳定更新，作为即插即用框架提升视觉与文本TPT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Test-Time Prompt Tuning逐步骤局部优化提示，当前步更新后的提示被直接用于对应预测与后续更新，使无标签目标中的噪声与偏差累积，导致提示更新轨迹失稳；作者识别出幅度膨胀（更新使提示移动过远）与方向漂移（连续更新方向不一致）两类失效模式。

Method: 提出Test-Time Prompt-Agnostic Decomposition (TPD)：用动力系统视角刻画观测到的提示更新轨迹，将基础提示更新分解为幅度与方向；以谱半径度量更新膨胀，并将更新方向分解为持续、振荡与残差分量。在分解基础上构建Adaptive Koopman Control (AKC)，在近期动态呈扩张时收缩更新以调控幅度；构建Hankel Update Router (HUR)，保留持续分量、抑制振荡与残差分量以精炼方向。二者联合生成稳定化提示更新，且无需修改骨干网络、提示结构或适配损失。

Result: 在15个数据集上，TPD一致提升10个基线方法；视觉提示精度提升3–8%，文本提示提升超过2%。

Conclusion: TPD作为提示无关框架，可即插即用地嵌入视觉与文本TPT方法，有效抑制测试时更新失稳并带来稳定且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Test-Time Prompt Tuning (TPT) adapts large pretrained models to unseen target distribution shifts by updating only lightweight prompt tokens. Existing TPT methods are effective, but they mainly optimize each prompt update locally. Across test-time steps, prompts updated at the current step are reused for the corresponding predictions and updates, so noisy or biased unlabeled objectives can accumulate and destabilize the prompt-update trajectory. We identify two failure modes: magnitude expansion, where updates move the prompt too far, and directional drift, where consecutive updates point in inconsistent directions. To analyze and control these failures, we propose Test-Time Prompt-Agnostic Decomposition ($\mathtt{TPD}$), which characterizes observed prompt-update trajectories from a dynamical-systems view and decomposes base prompt updates into magnitude and direction. $\mathtt{TPD}$ computes a spectral radius to measure update expansion and decomposes update directions into persistent, oscillatory, and residual components. Building on this decomposition, Adaptive Koopman Control ($\mathtt{AKC}$) regulates update magnitude by shrinking updates under expansive recent dynamics, while Hankel Update Router ($\mathtt{HUR}$) refines update direction by preserving persistent components and suppressing oscillatory and residual components. Together, $\mathtt{AKC}$ and $\mathtt{HUR}$ produce a stabilized prompt update. As a prompt-agnostic framework, $\mathtt{TPD}$ can be plugged into visual and text TPT methods without modifying the backbone, prompt architecture, or adaptation loss. Experiments on 15 datasets show that $\mathtt{TPD}$ consistently improves 10 baselines, with accuracy gains of $3$--$8\%$ for visual prompts and over $2\%$ for text prompts.

</details>


### [188] [Uncertainty Evaluation to Support Safety of the Intended Functionality Analysis for Determining the Applicability of Performance Targets Across Triggering Conditions in ML-Based LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35092)
*Milin Patel,Rolf Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将不确定性阈值转化为触发条件，用统计检验判定性能目标在该条件下是否适用，并按独立记录序列计数给出下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISO 21448 SOTIF 分析需覆盖所有触发条件，而在某一条件数据上选出的阈值仅对同分布数据有统计保证。

Method: 在 KITTI 与 nuScenes 上对 PointPillars 集成模型迁移不确定性阈值，进行三态（适用/不适用/未判定）误差概率检验，并以独立单元下界计数。

Result: 按记录序列计数时两个数据集均无足够独立单元支撑任何目标限值，且无"不适用"结论；按帧计数则出现"不适用"结果，迁移后达标阈值会拒绝大量正确检测，雨天 AUC 未下降。

Conclusion: 单一条件下达标只构成证据，须在触发条件处检验并同时报告接受正确检测占比与独立序列数。

Abstract: For safety of the intended functionality (SOTIF) analysis according to ISO 21448, a machine learning object detector for light detection and ranging (LiDAR) point clouds can meet a performance target, a limit on the mean per-frame share of wrong detections among accepted detections, by rejecting detections above an uncertainty threshold. A threshold selected on data of one condition carries a statistical guarantee only for data distributed like its selection data, whereas SOTIF analysis covers every triggering condition. This paper presents an uncertainty evaluation in which the threshold is transferred into a triggering condition, a test decides at a stated error probability whether the target is applicable there, not applicable or not determined, and a lower bound counts the independent units an applicable outcome requires. For PointPillars ensembles on KITTI and nuScenes, counted in recorded sequences, as the dependence between frames requires, neither dataset held enough independent units for any target limit, and no transfer had a not applicable outcome. Counted in frames, the same data gave not applicable outcomes at limits fixed in advance after transfers into far distance, into rain and between two separately trained detectors, thresholds that met the target limit after a transfer rejected most correct detections, and in rain the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve did not decrease. A performance target met under one condition therefore counts as evidence under a triggering condition only after a test there, reported with its share of accepted correct detections and its number of independent recorded sequences.

</details>


### [189] [Understanding and Repairing Cooperative Perception under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35110)
*Mengxu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文首次提出并受控研究了"天气诱导的上下文干扰"（Weather-Induced Context Interference）：在目标本体LiDAR点完全不变的情况下，仅外部（目标邻域）天气引发的变化即可使协同3D检测漏检；据此提出"恶劣天气协同修复"（Adverse-Weather Cooperative Repair, AWCR）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同3D检测依赖路侧与车侧的互补信息，但现有研究默认协同收益稳定可靠。作者发现在恶劣天气下，即使目标本体的点云完全没变，检测器仍会漏检——即天气引起的外部（目标邻域）变化破坏了协同收益。作者希望首次以受控实验方式隔离并刻画这一失效机制，并给出修复方案。

Method: 受控实验：在仿真与真实路侧输入中，仅移除/恢复目标邻域的外部点云，保持标注目标内所有点不变；并与等面积的外部对照（three equal-area exterior controls）及第二个融合模型做对照验证。修复方案AWCR按可用监督分两套实现：(1) 点分类器 Point Classifier，用于仿真中过滤新增天气回波；(2) 真实输入下采用局部校正 Local Correction 精修候选 + 质量选择 Quality Selection，依据当前测量校验候选，无需自然配对的清晰/恶劣天气录制成对数据。

Result: - 真实输入上，恢复目标邻域特征可修复31例诱导失效中的30例；三个等面积外部对照修复0例，证明失效源于邻域上下文而非外部点的数量效应。
- 失效条件在第二个融合模型上同样复现，表明其非单一模型特异现象。
- 候选追踪显示，局部校正能为部分在原候选定位阈值下无法定位的目标提供准确几何。
- 自然恶劣天气 CoInfra 开发集上，AWCR使车类BEV AP@0.7提升4.66点（V2X-ViT）、5.56点（AttFuse）；仿真 V2X-R 雾天场景下，在固定4,096候选预算下使车辆BEV AP@0.7提升4.47点。

Conclusion: 协同检测的收益在恶劣天气下是脆弱的：目标外部上下文的变化即可在融合前改变目标表征、削弱协同帮助，这构成一类此前被忽视的失效模式。AWCR通过分离的仿真与真实实现（过滤天气回波 / 局部校正+质量选择）有效恢复性能，并在真实与仿真基准上均取得稳定提升，为恶劣天气下的协同感知鲁棒性提供了可复现的分析与修复思路。

Abstract: A cooperative 3D detector can miss a vehicle even when the LiDAR points on it remain unchanged. We study this failure under adverse weather as Weather-Induced Context Interference. To our knowledge, this is the first controlled study of how weather-related exterior changes remove cooperative benefits while preserving all points inside annotated objects. Controlled experiments on simulated and real roadside inputs show that exterior loss changes object representations before fusion and removes previously useful assistance. On real inputs, restoring object-neighborhood features recovers 30 of 31 induced failures; three equal-area exterior controls recover none. The failure condition also appears with a second fusion model. These findings motivate Adverse-Weather Cooperative Repair (AWCR), with separate implementations for the available supervision. Point Classifier filters added weather returns in simulation. On real inputs, Local Correction refines candidates and Quality Selection checks them against current measurements, without naturally paired clear/adverse-weather recordings. Candidate tracing shows that correction supplies accurate geometry for some targets that original candidates cannot localize at the evaluation threshold. On natural adverse-weather CoInfra development data, AWCR improves car bird's-eye-view (BEV) AP@0.7 by 4.66 points with V2X-ViT and 5.56 with AttFuse. On simulated V2X-R fog, it improves vehicle BEV AP@0.7 by 4.47 points at a fixed 4,096-candidate budget.

</details>


### [190] [Domain-Adapted Latent Diffusion Models for CT-to-PET Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35132)
*Yann Bourdé,Lea Bogensperger,Arnela Hadzic,Michael Krauthammer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出域自适应潜空间扩散框架，用21,930对CT/PET切片迁移自然图像先验，结合符号幂变换解决PET极端强度稀疏问题，实现CT→PET翻译；并指出全图指标对稀疏PET不可靠，提出背景滤波与掩膜限制指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET代谢信息对肿瘤检测与分期有价值，但成本高、基础设施要求高、依赖放射性示踪剂，可及性远低于CT；基于扩散的CT-to-PET翻译受制于配对数据稀缺。

Method: 域自适应潜空间扩散框架，利用相对小规模配对数据（21,930对CT/PET切片）迁移自然图像先验；引入符号幂变换（signed power transform）处理PET数据中的极端强度稀疏性。

Result: 相较SOTA基线CPDM，在逐像素重建与局部肿瘤保真度上均取得更优表现，验证自然图像先验可有效迁移至医学域；系统分析显示标准全图指标对稀疏PET具有误导性。

Conclusion: 域自适应潜扩散结合强度变换可有效提升CT-to-PET翻译质量；建议以背景滤波与掩膜限制指标替代全图指标，作为更具临床意义的评估方案。

Abstract: Positron Emission Tomography (PET) provides important metabolic information for tumor detection and staging but remains significantly less accessible than Computed Tomography (CT) due to its high cost, infrastructure requirements, and reliance on injected radioactive tracers. CT-to-PET image translation using generative models aims to address this disparity, yet state-of-the-art diffusion-based methods are limited by the scarcity of paired training data. To overcome this, we propose a domain-adapted latent diffusion framework that transfers natural-image priors using a relatively small dataset of 21,930 paired CT/PET slices, incorporating a signed power transform to resolve extreme intensity sparsity in PET data. Compared to CPDM, a state-ofthe-art baseline for CT-to-PET image translation, our approach yields superior performance in pixel-wise reconstruction and localized tumor fidelity, demonstrating effective transfer of natural-image priors to the medical domain. We further provide a systematic analysis of evaluation metric reliability for sparse PET data, showing that standard full-image metrics can be misleading indicators of translated image quality, and propose background-filtered and mask-restricted metrics as more clinically meaningful alternatives.

</details>


### [191] [Sexual dimorphism and sex-specific features of pediatric hand bone shapes via statistical shape modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35143)
*Edwin Tay,Amir A. Zadpoor,Nazli Tümer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段检测-分割框架从影像数据中提取骨骼掩膜，结合统计形状模型(SSM)分析分层形状参数，量化骨骼的性二态性与性别特异的形状发育变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活体组织中形状变异同时涵盖性二态性与生长相关变化，刻画其变化包络（envelope）是困难任务，缺乏明确的数据驱动方法来表征组织形状及其群体尺度变异。

Method: 开发两阶段检测-分割框架，在1456名不同年龄与性别受试者的放射影像数据集中分割出27664个独立骨骼掩膜；随后进行SSM拟合，并按人口学分层分析形状参数以识别各骨骼的性二态形态及性别特异的形状发育差异。

Result: 分割性能优异：HD95=1.512±0.005、ASSD=0.449±0.001、Dice=0.987±0.00004、IoU=0.975±0.0001；分层形状参数分析揭示了各骨骼的性二态形态及性别特异的形状发育差异，结果与既有文献一致。

Conclusion: 提供了一种明确、数据驱动的方法来刻画组织形状及其群体尺度变异，为构建基于群体的解剖变异模型奠定方法学基础，并可扩展用于研究组织生长与形状随时间的变化；未来需在其它解剖结构与成像模态上验证，并用纵向数据验证生长轨迹。

Abstract: Understanding the envelope of shape variation encompassing both sexual dimorphism and growth-related changes in living tissue is a non-trivial task. In this work, we use statistical shape models (SSMs) and analyze demographic-stratified shape parameters to quantify sexual dimorphism and sex-specific shape changes. To this end, we developed a two-stage detection-segmentation framework that accurately segmented $n = 27664$ unique bone masks from a radiographic dataset of $n = 1456$ subjects with diverse ages and sexes (HD95 = $1.512 \pm 0.005$, ASSD $0.449 \pm 001$, Dice = $0.987 \pm 0.00004$, IoU = $0.975 \pm 0.0001$). Following SSM fitting, our follow-up analyses of stratified shape parameters highlighted the morphology of sexual dimorphisms for each bone and demonstrated sex-specific differences in shape development. Our results were consistent with existing literature and provided an unambiguous, data-driven methodology for characterizing tissue shape and its population-scale variation. This approach provides a potential methodological foundation for developing population-based models of anatomical variation and could be extended to investigate tissue growth and shape changes over time. Future work should evaluate the applicability of this methodology on alternative anatomical structures and imaging modalities, with the goal of validating these growth trajectories on longitudinal data.

</details>


### [192] [Temporal Slice Learning for AI-Generated Video Detection with 400$\times$ Fewer FLOPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35148)
*Wei Shang,Zhiyuan Li,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出从"表示"视角重思AI生成视频检测，将整段视频重构为紧凑的时间切片表示，用频率感知的时间切片一致性学习实现轻量高效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成视频检测器依赖重型骨干网络对完整视频建模，而生成伪影信号微弱且空间局部化，视频大部分内容冗余，造成计算开销大、可扩展性差的根本性错配。

Method: 沿时间聚合信息量高的空间行/列构成切片域输入，构建高频与低频切片以暴露互补时间伪影（细节不稳定、运动过于平滑）；实例化为Temporal Slice Consistency Network，含任务驱动的切片定位、层级位置编码与轻量Transformer建模跨切片与跨频率依赖；并构建AI-Artist基准。

Result: 在GenVideo与AI-Artist上取得强性能，模型前向FLOPs比ReStraV少400倍以上，模型推理快3.6倍，端到端（含预处理）快2.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过切片域重参数化抑制冗余外观、保留判别性时间动态，实现可扩展、可部署的AI生成视频检测新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models have improved visual realism, making detection increasingly dependent on subtle temporal inconsistencies. However, these signals are weak and spatially localized, while most of the video content is redundant. This creates a fundamental mismatch: existing detectors rely on heavy backbones to model full videos, which leads to high computational cost and limited scalability. In this work, we rethink AI-generated video detection from a representation perspective. Instead of modeling entire videos, we restructure them into compact temporal slice representations that isolate informative temporal evolution. We introduce a lightweight framework using frequency-aware temporal slice consistency learning, where informative spatial rows and columns are aggregated over time to form slice-domain inputs. This reparameterization suppresses redundant appearance while preserving discriminative temporal dynamics, enabling efficient and targeted detection. By constructing slices from both high- and low-frequency regions, our method exposes complementary temporal artifacts, including unstable details and abnormal motion smoothness. We instantiate this idea in a Temporal Slice Consistency Network, which integrates task-driven slice localization, hierarchical positional encoding, and a lightweight Transformer to model cross-slice and cross-frequency dependencies. We further introduce AI-Artist, a new benchmark with artist-curated videos from recent high-fidelity generators, including Seedance and Kling. Experiments on GenVideo and AI-Artist show that our method achieves strong performance while requiring over 400$\times$ fewer model-forward FLOPs and 3.6$\times$ faster model inference than the strong video-based baseline ReStraV (2.9$\times$ faster end to end, including preprocessing), enabling scalable and practical deployment.

</details>


### [193] [Complexity-guided Regularization for Generalizable Human Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35152)
*Gun Ryu,Gi-Mun Um,Won-Sik Cheong,Wonjun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出复杂度引导的正则化方案（CGR-GS），利用基于局部熵的复杂度先验约束高斯放置密度并指导局部块采样，从而提升可泛化人体高斯泼溅对精细细节的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化人体高斯泼溅方法难以表示人体的细粒度细节（几何与纹理复杂度高），原因是未对高斯放置密度施加约束，导致复杂区域渲染模糊。

Method: 提出复杂度引导正则化：通过计算着装人体几何与纹理特征的局部熵得到复杂度先验；用该先验阻止高斯在复杂区域被粗放放置；并据此优先从复杂区域采样局部补丁进行充分监督。

Result: 在基准数据集上取得state-of-the-art性能，同时保持可泛化人体高斯泼溅的实时推理速度。

Conclusion: 以局部熵复杂度先验引导高斯放置与采样监督，可有效忠实重建复杂结构，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Recent studies on generalizable human Gaussian splatting have achieved the notable progress in synthesizing novel views of unseen human subjects from multi-view images. Despite this progress, existing methods still struggle to represent fine-grained details of human subjects, which inherently involve high geometric and textural complexity. This is because previous methods do not impose a constraint on how densely Gaussians are placed, which often leads to blurry rendering results in complex regions. To address this problem, we propose a complexity-guided regularization scheme. The key idea is to leverage a complexity prior computed via the local entropy in geometric and textural characteristics of a clothed human. This effectively prevents Gaussians from being coarsely placed in complex regions, which facilitates the faithful reconstruction of intricate structures. Furthermore, we sample local patches predominantly from complex regions according to this complexity prior, which allows the model to be sufficiently supervised by local details. Experimental results on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method delivers state-of-the-art performance while maintaining the real-time inference speed in generalizable human Gaussian splatting. The code and model are publicly available at https://github.com/DCVL-3D/CGR-GS_release.

</details>


### [194] [OrthoRF: Exploring Orthogonality in Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35203)
*Despoina Touska,Bastiaan Onne Fagginger Auer,Alexandru Onose,Tejaswi Kasarla,Luis Armando Pérez Rey,Maximilian Lipp,Lyubov Amitonova,Martin R. Oswald,Pascal Cerfontaine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Orthogonal Rotating Features (OrthoRF)，通过内积损失与架构修改在方向空间强制正交性，获得更清晰的相位对齐与更可靠的物体分组，在无监督物体发现中匹配或超越现有方法，且无需后处理聚类并能恢复被遮挡物体部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经同步假说认为大脑通过同步性将视觉场景组织为结构化的多物体表示；机器学习中基于同步性的模型以复数特征相位存储绑定信息，Rotating Features (RF) 用矢量激活实现该思想（幅值编码物体存在、方向编码归属）。但 RF 的方向空间缺乏约束，相位对齐不够锐利、分组可靠性有限，且许多同步性方法依赖后处理聚类。

Method: 在 RF 的基础上提出 OrthoRF：通过一个内积损失（inner-product loss）并结合架构修改，在 RF 的方向空间中强制正交性，从而作为归纳偏置强化相位对齐与分组。

Result: 在无监督物体发现评估中（含重叠物体、噪声与分布外测试），OrthoRF 匹配或超越当前模型，表示更具可解释性，且无需许多同步性方法所要求的后处理聚类；与现有模型不同，OrthoRF 还能恢复被遮挡物体的部分，表明在遮挡下具有更强的分组能力。

Conclusion: 正交性是一种简单而有效的归纳偏置，可用于基于同步性的物体中心学习。

Abstract: Neural synchrony is hypothesized to help the brain organize visual scenes into structured multi-object representations. In machine learning, synchrony-based models analogously learn object-centric representations by storing binding in the phase of complex-valued features. Rotating Features (RF) instantiate this idea with vector-valued activations, encoding object presence in magnitudes and affiliation in orientations. We propose Orthogonal Rotating Features (OrthoRF), which enforces orthogonality in RF's orientation space via an inner-product loss and architectural modifications. This yields sharper phase alignment and more reliable grouping. In evaluations of unsupervised object discovery, including settings with overlapping objects, noise, and out-of-distribution tests, OrthoRF matches or outperforms current models while producing more interpretable representations, and it eliminates the post-hoc clustering required by many synchrony-based approaches. Unlike current models, OrthoRF also recovers occluded object parts, indicating stronger grouping under occlusion. Overall, orthogonality emerges as a simple, effective inductive bias for synchrony-based object-centric learning.

</details>


### [195] [Multi-Agent Relay Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35219)
*Xu Liu,Jungang Qiao,Guikun Chen,Xiaojuan Qi,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MACR框架，用置信度引导的中继式多智能体协作替代传统的群思式（group thinking）协作拓扑，在多个基准上提升准确率并显著降低token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体协作（MAC）方法主要采用"群思式"拓扑，即各智能体独立生成解再聚合输出，缺乏推理轨迹的连续传递与精炼；作者希望探索一种推理所有权动态转移的"中继式"拓扑。

Method: 提出Multi-Agent Confidence-guided Relay（MACR）框架，实现智能体间的动态中继协作。核心在于确定"何时中继"与"由谁接续"：通过置信度引导的中继触发（confidence-guided relay triggering）判断是否转移推理，并通过基于置信度的竞价机制（bidding mechanism）选择下一个接续智能体，使其继承并精炼演进的推理轨迹。

Result: 在四个基准上优于最强单模型策略，如MathVista +3.3%、MMStar +3.9%；与多智能体策略效果相当，但在HallusionBench和MMStar上token开销降低6.0倍。进一步分析表明，中继协作使异构智能体在推理各阶段逐步整合互补洞见与异构能力。

Conclusion: 中继式（relay thinking）拓扑是群思式拓扑的有效替代方案，能够在保持或提升精度的同时显著降低计算开销，并展现异构智能体能力互补的潜力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent collaboration (MAC) methods have primarily explored a group thinking collaboration topology, where agents independently generate solutions and aggregate their outputs. In this work, we investigate an alternative relay thinking topology, where reasoning ownership is dynamically transferred across agents, allowing subsequent agents to inherit and refine an evolving reasoning trajectory. We introduce Multi-Agent Confidence-guided Relay (MACR), a framework that enables dynamical relay collaboration among agents. The key challenge is determining when reasoning should be relayed and which agent should continue the process. method addresses this challenge through confidence-guided relay triggering and a bidding mechanism that selects the next agent based on its confidence. Experiments on four benchmarks show that method outperforms the strongest single-model strategies in accuracy, e.g., +3.3% on MathVista and +3.9% on MMStar, and remains on par with multi-agent strategies while consuming 6.0x less token overhead on HallusionBench and MMStar.Further analysis demonstrates that relay collaboration enables heterogeneous agents to progressively integrate complementary insights, combining heterogeneous capabilities across reasoning stages. Our source code is available at https://github.com/67L1/MACR.

</details>


### [196] [O2D-MedNet: An Optimization-Aware Dual-Domain Framework for Robust Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35220)
*Shipeng Hu,Miaohui Wang,Jing Qin,Sukun Tian,Yuchun Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出优化感知双域框架O2D-MedNet，用于医学图像分割，包含DDI双域交互、CGF跨骨干融合与GCO梯度优化三模块，在六个基准上稳定超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割受复合退化与多尺度几何变化影响，损害病灶-背景可分性、结构一致性与优化稳定性；现有单流框架混淆异质线索，混合流方法多用通用或静态融合，难以跨域协同且未显式处理优化不稳定问题。

Method: (1) 退化感知双域交互(DDI)模块：融合傅里叶域与小波域特征，抑制退化敏感响应、保留结构与细节；(2) 跨骨干轮廓引导融合(CGF)模块：在CNN局部细节建模与视觉状态空间(VSS)全局结构建模之间进行选择性交互；(3) 梯度上下文优化(GCO)策略：依据损失趋势与梯度统计自适应调整学习动态以稳定训练。

Result: 在六个基准、多种成像模态上取得相对SOTA的一致提升；进一步分析验证了灵敏度、结构一致性与优化稳定性的改善。

Conclusion: O2D-MedNet通过双域特征交互、跨骨干轮廓引导融合与优化感知训练策略，可在异质条件下实现更稳健的医学图像分割。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation (MIS) remains challenging due to composite degradations and multi-scale geometric variations, which jointly impair lesion-background separability, structural consistency, and optimization stability. Existing single-stream frameworks entangle heterogeneous cues within a single representation pathway, while hybrid-stream approaches often rely on generic or static fusion, limiting effective coordination across domains and failing to explicitly address optimization instability. To overcome these limitations, we propose a new optimization-aware dual-domain framework for MIS (O2D-MedNet). First, a degradation-aware dualdomain interaction (DDI) module integrates Fourier - and Wavelet-domain features to suppress degradation-sensitive responses while preserving structural and fine-detail information. Second, a cross-backbone contour-guided fusion (CGF) module enables selective interaction between CNN-based local detail modeling and visual state-space-based global structure modeling, improving boundary accuracy and representation consistency. Third, a gradient-context optimization (GCO) strategy stabilizes training by adaptively adjusting learning dynamics based on loss trends and gradient statistics, mitigating instability caused by class imbalance and heterogeneous sample difficulty. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art MIS methods across multiple imaging modalities. Further analyses confirm enhanced sensitivity, structural consistency, and optimization stability, validating the effectiveness of the proposed O2D-MedNet for robust MIS under heterogeneous conditions.

</details>


### [197] [Joint Latent Reasoning and Observation for Agentic Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35229)
*Junzhang Liu,Hao Yuan,Qi Zhang,Yiming Wu,Long Chen,Zhecan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LARVA框架，通过联合学习潜在推理与视觉观测蒸馏多轮视频智能体，以连续表示替代文本描述在观察者与推理者之间传递信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效视频智能体不仅要会回答，还要决定"下一步看哪里"。将其能力迁移到小模型困难在于每次观测承担双重用途——支撑回答与引导后续证据搜索；而文本描述可能只捕捉当前可见内容，却丢失后续获取所需的线索。

Method: 观察者向推理者传递紧凑连续表示，推理者据此选择下一个获取动作或生成最终答案；动作与答案监督通过该接口反向传播，使观察者学会传递对下游决策有用的信息；并利用观测重建与推理分布对齐，从文本教师轨迹中提供辅助监督。

Result: 在五个视频理解基准上，4B与7B骨干均取得强劲性能；在VideoHolmes、Video-MME、LVBench上，7B模型分别比同教师轨迹训练的文本CoT学生高出5.1、2.5、3.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在蒸馏视频理解能力时，联合学习智能体如何表示证据与如何基于证据行动具有重要价值。

Abstract: Effective video agents must learn not only how to answer, but also where to look next. Transferring this capability to smaller models is challenging because each observation serves two purposes: supporting an answer and guiding the search for further evidence. Textual descriptions may capture what is currently visible while omitting cues needed for subsequent acquisition. We introduce Latent Agentic Reasoning for Video Analysis (LARVA), a framework that distills multi-round video agents through jointly learned latent reasoning and visual observations. Rather than communicating through captions, an observer passes compact continuous representations to a reasoner that selects the next acquisition action or produces the final answer. Crucially, action and answer supervision propagate through this interface, training the observer to communicate information useful for downstream decisions. Observation reconstruction and reasoning-distribution alignment provide auxiliary supervision from textual teacher trajectories. Across five video-understanding benchmarks, LARVA achieves strong performance with 4B and 7B backbones. On VideoHolmes, Video-MME, and LVBench, the 7B model outperforms a textual CoT student trained on the same teacher trajectories by 5.1, 2.5, and 3.7 percentage points, respectively. These results highlight the value of jointly learning how agents represent evidence and act on it when distilling video-understanding capabilities.

</details>


### [198] [Reading Beyond OCR Noise: Robust Cross-Lingual RAG for Legislative Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35240)
*Radha Krishna Deshpande,Ajoy Mondal,Akash Manna,C V Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个端到端框架，将基于视觉的版面分割、多文字OCR与智能体式RAG流水线结合，用于处理印度历史公共档案中退化扫描页的信息检索，并在20万页旁遮普邦议会档案上验证了优于纯文本基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度历史公共档案以退化的多栏扫描页形式存在，夹杂多种文字、复杂版面和密集视觉噪声，OCR失效成为信息检索的主要瓶颈；需要明确视觉退化与版面碎片化如何传导为文本抽取错误，并在此基础上实现可用的检索问答。

Method: 提出端到端框架：视觉驱动的版面分割 + 多文字OCR + 智能体式检索增强生成（RAG）流水线；利用迭代式智能体回路，在生成答案前规避OCR噪声与版面歧义。

Result: 在旁遮普邦议会（Punjab Vidhan Sabha）20万页档案上评估，系统对历史文档退化表现出稳健处理能力，优于标准纯文本基线。

Conclusion: 将视觉版面理解、多文字OCR与智能体式RAG耦合，可有效缓解OCR噪声与版面歧义对下游检索问答的传导影响，为退化历史档案的信息获取提供可行方案。

Abstract: India's historical public archives present severe computer vision and document image analysis challenges: decades of parliamentary records survive only as degraded, multi-column scanned pages featuring mixed scripts, complex layouts, and dense visual noise where OCR failure is the primary obstacle to information retrieval. We present an end-to-end framework that couples vision-based layout segmentation and multi-script OCR with an agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline. Our approach specifically addresses how visual degradation and layout fragmentation propagate into text extraction errors, using an iterative agentic loop to navigate around OCR noise and layout ambiguities before generating answers. Evaluated on the 200,000-page Punjab Vidhan Sabha archive, our system demonstrates robust handling of historical document degradation, outperforming standard text-only baselines.

</details>


### [199] [Beyond Vision-Language Models: Visual-Semantic Affect Graphs for Emotion Recognition in Artworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35244)
*Ngoc-Duy Mai,Ha-Thanh Nguyen,Hideaki Takeda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉-语义情感图（VSAG）框架，以可解释的视觉特征节点与实体节点构建图结构，学习特征-情感关联来预测艺术作品主导情感，并通过层级化效价-唤醒辅助模块实现粗略到精细的情感建模，在EmoArt-5k上优于直接VLM推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术作品情感识别困难：情感意义主观、隐晦，依赖视觉形式与语义联想之间的关系。现有视觉语言模型虽具强多模态表示能力，但直接"图像→情感"预测可能忽略形式、语义与情感线索之间的显式关系，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 构建VSAG图框架：节点为可解释特征（色彩、笔触、构图、明暗、线条质量）及抽取的视觉实体，边学习特征-情感关联；可选层级辅助情感模块建模效价-唤醒结构。提出VSAG-TopK变体，与Qwen3-VL、InternVL3.5的直接推理在EmoArt-5k数据集上对比。

Result: 直接VLM给出有用但有限的基线；图模型提升主导情感预测。层级效价-唤醒版本的VSAG取得最佳整体性能，在准确率、macro-F1与weighted-F1上均超过直接VLM推理和仅情感VSAG基线。逐类结果显示图模型与VLM捕获部分互补信号；效价-唤醒结构作为粗到细情感层级整合时最有效，而非简单的事后校正。

Conclusion: 显式视觉-语义结构可同时支撑艺术作品情感识别的预测与解释，层级化情感建模优于直接推理与简单后处理。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in artworks remains challenging because affective meaning is often subjective, implicit, and shaped by relationships between visual form and semantic associations. While vision-language models provide strong multimodal representations, direct image-to-emotion prediction may overlook explicit relations among formal, semantic, and affective cues that support emotional interpretation. This paper proposes Visual-Semantic Affect Graphs (VSAG), a graph-based framework that represents artworks through interpretable feature nodes, including color, brushstroke, composition, light-shadow, line quality, and extracted visual entities. The graph learns feature-emotion associations for dominant emotion prediction, with valence-arousal structure modeled through an optional hierarchical auxiliary affect module. We evaluate VSAG and VSAG-TopK against direct VLM inference using Qwen3-VL and InternVL3.5 on EmoArt-5k. Results show that direct VLMs provide useful but modest baselines, while graph-based models improve dominant emotion prediction. In particular, the hierarchical valence-arousal variant of VSAG achieves the best overall performance, improving accuracy, macro-F1, and weighted-F1 over both direct VLM inference and the emotion-only VSAG baseline. Per-class results indicate that graph-based models and VLMs capture partly complementary signals, while valence-arousal structure is most useful when integrated as a coarse-to-fine affect hierarchy rather than as a simple post-hoc correction. These findings suggest that explicit visual-semantic structure can support both prediction and interpretation in artwork emotion recognition.

</details>


### [200] [Low-rank MERA Decomposition of 3D OCT Image Volume for Compression, Denoising, Feature Extraction and Diagnosis of Retinal Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35271)
*Ivica Kopriva,Fei Shi,Marija Štanfel,Qin Peng,Jianhao Bao,Gaowei Li,Xinjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于低秩多尺度纠缠重正化拟设(MERA)张量网络的3D OCT体数据表示，将体数据表示为全局特征顶层核心与多个相互作用的低秩正交/半正交因子的收缩，可同时实现压缩、去噪与特征提取；方法无需训练、逐个体数据处理。在两种扫描仪数据上视觉质量与视网膜层分割误差优于对比方法，并在小样本临床数据集上结合Grassmann流形最近子空间分类器取得有竞争力的疾病分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT体数据受散斑噪声影响，降低对比度并掩盖精细结构；同时大视场高分辨率OCT体数据在存储与远程眼科传输中负担沉重。现有深度学习方案依赖大量标注且缺乏可解释性，需要一种结构感知、免训练、面向小样本场景的表示方法，同时兼顾压缩、去噪与特征提取。

Method: 对3D OCT体数据做低秩MERA张量网络近似：以顶层核心因子编码全局特征，以多个相互作用的低秩正交或半正交因子建模B-scan间与B-scan内相关性，通过张量收缩表示图像。该表示统一支持压缩、去噪与特征提取；方法免训练、逐体独立处理。特征提取后使用Grassmann流形上的最近子空间分类器，仅以子空间维数为超参数，理论上证明其在小样本下稳健。

Result: 在两种扫描仪采集的OCT体数据上，相比多个对比方法，所提框架获得更优的视觉质量并降低视网膜层分割误差。在两个临床OCT体数据集上提取MERA特征并以Grassmann最近子空间分类，在小样本设置下取得有竞争力的视网膜疾病分类精度，而深度学习方法常受标注稀缺限制。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构感知、模型驱动的表示学习可在有限监督下处理高维数据，兼顾良好泛化性能与高可解释性，适用于OCT压缩、去噪、特征提取及小样本疾病分类。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography (OCT) is a non-invasive imaging modality used for visualization of retinal tissue. However, OCT volumes are affected by speckle noise, which degrades image contrast and obscures fine structural details. Furthermore, modern clinical practice requires large field-of-view, high-resolution OCT volumes, whose storage and transmission are demanding for archiving and teleophthalmology applications. To address aforementioned challenges, we propose a low-rank multi-scale entanglement renormalization ansatz (MERA) tensor network approximation of 3D OCT image volume, where the image volume is represented as contractions of a top core factor encoding global features and multiple interacting low-rank orthogonal or semi-orthogonal factors modeling inter- and intra-B scan correlations. This representation jointly enables compression, denoising, and feature extraction. Unlike deep learning approaches, the proposed approach is training-free and processes each OCT volume independently. We evaluate the proposed framework on OCT image volumes acquired by two different scanners and demonstrate improved visual quality and reduced retinal layer segmentation errors in comparison with several competing methods. We also extract MERA features from two clinical OCT volume datasets. They are subsequently classified using a Grassmann-manifold nearest-subspace classifier. With the subspace dimension as its only hyperparameter, the classifier is theoretically shown to be robust in small-sample clinical scenario. We demonstrate competitive accuracy of classification of retinal diseases in these small-sample settings, where deep learning methods are often limited by the scarcity of labeled data. The proposed structure-aware model-driven representation learning for high-dimensional data under limited supervision provides good generalization performance while maintaining high interpretability.

</details>


### [201] [OVM-Occ: Open-Vocabulary Monocular Occupancy Prediction via Geometry-Aware Vision-Language Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35312)
*Shuyang Yu,Haibo Qin,Shaohan Hu,Wei Shan,Xiaoqing Guan,You Wang,Guang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开放词汇单目室内占据预测任务及OVM-Occ框架，并构建OpenOcc-ScanNet/OpenOcc-Replica基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目室内占据预测受限于预定义类别的闭集设定，难以泛化到未见类别；而室内物体语义类别高度多样且细粒度，闭集限制尤为突出。

Method: 采用Language-Embedded Gaussians作为中间场景表示；设计几何感知可变形注意力模块精炼高斯语义特征；构建实例级视觉-语言嵌入提供可靠语义监督；提出几何感知解耦语义蒸馏以缓解几何学习与语义学习的相互干扰。

Result: 在Occ-ScanNet上较此前最优方法提升12.2 mIoU与0.6 IoU，并在新开放词汇评测协议下取得一致增益，达到室内开放词汇单目占据预测的SOTA。

Conclusion: OVM-Occ有效实现开放词汇室内单目占据预测，配合新提出的细粒度基准推动了该方向的评测与泛化能力研究。

Abstract: Monocular occupancy prediction in indoor scenes aims to recover a scene's 3D geometry and semantics from a single RGB image. Existing methods are restricted to a closed set of predefined categories, limiting their ability to generalize to unseen categories. This limitation is particularly critical in indoor scenes, where objects exhibit highly diverse and fine-grained semantic categories. We study open-vocabulary monocular occupancy prediction for indoor scenes and present OVM-Occ, a Gaussian-based occupancy framework with an instance-aware vision-language teacher. Specifically, we adopt Language-Embedded Gaussians as the intermediate scene representation, propose a geometry-aware deformable attention module to refine Gaussian semantic features, and construct instance-level vision-language embeddings to provide reliable semantic supervision. We further introduce geometry-aware decoupled semantic distillation to mitigate the interference between geometry learning and semantic learning. To evaluate open-vocabulary occupancy prediction performance, we introduce OpenOcc-ScanNet and OpenOcc-Replica, two extended benchmarks with more diverse and fine-grained semantic categories. Experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary monocular occupancy prediction for indoor scenes, outperforming the previous state-of-the-art approach by 12.2 mIoU and 0.6 IoU on the Occ-ScanNet dataset, with consistent gains under new open-vocabulary evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [202] [Targeted Deep Regression - Initial Applications in Whole-Body Water-Fat MRI in UK Biobank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35339)
*Filip Marttala,Taro Langner,Johan Öfverstedt,Nouman Ahmad,Håkan Ahlström,Robin Strand,Joel Kullberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图像回归的方法，利用ResNet50处理颈部至膝部水脂MRI的2D多通道拼图，从3万余例扫描中估计肾脏体积、心脏体积与年龄，R²达0.731–0.984，并通过群体显著性图验证所利用区域的医学相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UK Biobank计划对10万名参与者采集详细MRI，需自动化手段扩充影像衍生表型（IDP）以供医学研究；直接对全身大视野高维影像做深度回归信息密度低、冗余大。

Method: 将手动定义的解剖子区域通过图像配准从参考扫描传播到>30,000例扫描，实现目标子区域采样；把多切片子区域组织为2D多通道拼图（如肾脏多切片），输入ResNet50风格网络做数值回归；并可进行群体水平的显著性分析以定位网络依赖的影像与解剖区域。

Result: 在肾脏体积、心脏体积和年龄三个回归终点上取得R²=0.731–0.984；群体显著性图突出医学相关区域。

Conclusion: 该方法可标准化并限定输入深度回归流程的信息，提高信息密度，为未来广泛的医学推断研究提供可行路径。

Abstract: UK Biobank is a British clinical study which, in addition to large amounts of medical data collected, plans to include detailed Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) examina- tions from 100,000 participants. The image data can be analyzed for example by auto- mated analysis with neural networks for image segmentation or classification to expand the collection of image-derived phenotypes for medical research. In this work, we pro- pose, present and evaluate a methodology that uses image-based regression to estimate numerical properties from the collected neck-to-knee water-fat MRI scans. It applies a ResNet50-style network to 2D multi-channel collages that represent targeted and study- specific anatomical sub-regions from the MRI scan. The targeted sub-region sampling is achieved by propagating manually defined subregions from a reference scan to the other >30,000 analyzed scans using image registration. The resulting collages can be compact while retaining high-resolution, for example by providing multiple slices of kidney tissue for a kidney volume regression network. This approach also enables group-wise saliency analysis that highlight image and anatomical regions used by the network for the pre- dictions. The method was applied for the regression endpoints of kidney volume, heart volume, and age. Predictive performance was found between R2 = 0.731-0.984 and the group-wise saliency maps were found to highlight medically relevant regions. We con- clude that the method can be used to standardize and restrict the information presented to a deep regression analysis pipeline, increasing information density and opening up a wide range of future medical inference studies.

</details>


### [203] [CVTSplat: Multi-view Reconstruction Using an Adaptive Centroidal Voronoi Tessellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35347)
*Briac Toussaint,Diego Thomas,Jean-Sébastien Franco,Edmond Boyer,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVTSplat 采用自适应质心 Voronoi 剖分（CVT），以演化中的 SDF 调制单元尺寸，保持近似规则的细胞形态，并配套 GPU 最近邻查询、近似 Voronoi 构造与专用光栅化器，实现单卡约 2.5 分钟训练、几何精度媲美最优 Gaussian-splatting 表面方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Voronoi 剖分已被用于多视图图像的自适应空间划分重建，但其不规则单元导致优化过程中反复重建剖分开销高昂，亟需兼顾自适应性与计算效率的离散化方案。

Method: 扩展标准 CVT 为尺寸非均匀的质心 Voronoi 剖分：以演化中的 signed distance field 调制单元尺寸，在零水平集附近加密站点而保持单元近似质心性与良态；针对该规则性专门优化整条管线，包括 GPU 最近邻查询、近似 Voronoi 构造，以及面向小型近各向同性单元的光栅化器。

Result: 在该单元形态区间内，Voronoi 构造比通用方法快 5–10 倍；在 DTU 与 BlendedMVS 上几何精度与最佳 Gaussian-splatting 表面方法相当，单 GPU 训练约 2.5 分钟。

Conclusion: 保持单元的规则性与近各向同性可在不牺牲自适应性的前提下大幅降低剖分与渲染开销，使基于 Voronoi 的表面重建在精度与效率上同时具备竞争力，为快速多视图重建提供可行路线。
