Abstract: Voronoi tessellations have recently emerged as an adaptive, space-partitioning discretization for shape and appearance reconstruction from multi-view images. However, the irregular cells they produce make the repeated reconstruction of the tessellation during optimization costly. We therefore explore adaptive centroidal Voronoi tessellations (CVTs), which extend standard CVTs with non-uniform cell sizes while preserving the regular, well-shaped cells that make them efficient to compute. In CVTSplat, cell size is modulated by the evolving signed distance field, concentrating sites near the zero level set while keeping cells nearly centroidal. This regularity lets us specialize the whole pipeline, with GPU algorithms for nearest-neighbor queries and approximate Voronoi construction, and a rasterizer tailored to small, nearly isotropic cells. Our Voronoi construction is 5 to 10 times faster than general-purpose alternatives in this regime. On DTU and BlendedMVS, CVTSplat reaches geometric accuracy competitive with the best Gaussian-splatting surface methods while training in about 2.5 minutes on a single GPU.

</details>


### [204] [Evidence-Guided Selection of Retinal Vessel Segmentation Pipelines: A Cross-Dataset Benchmark of Performance, Structure, Failure Modes, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35357)
*Ruoyu Yan,Yuejia Liu,Shixin Xu,Xiaoyan Li,Xi Luo,Yuhui Deng,Huaxiong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了五种可执行视网膜血管分割流水线在六个公开数据集244张图像上的表现，并从重叠度、跨数据集稳定性、细血管保留、结构度量、错误构成等多维度进行比较，主张按任务需求进行基准相对化的流水线选择，而非追求单一普适排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据聚合重叠分数（如Dice/IoU）进行模型选择，会忽视结构保真度、跨数据集鲁棒性以及应用相关的错误代价，可能导致选型与实际任务需求不匹配。

Method: 在6个公开数据集的244张图像上评估FSG-Net、SA-UNetv2、DCP-1024、GAVE、FR-UNet五种二值血管分割流水线；采用像素级一致性、跨数据集稳定性、细血管保留、结构指标（clDice、Skeleton F1）、错误构成及算力证据，并按数据集等权聚合。另在45张HRF图像上做了辅助分析：将分割掩膜转为11个环形血管特征用于留一法分类，并通过受控扰动检验局部掩膜误差在下游特征中的放大效应。

Result: 无任何流水线在所有设定下最优：FSG-Net取得最高等数据集平均Macro-F1（0.77）、IoU（0.63）、clDice（0.83）和Skeleton F1（0.78）；SA-UNetv2取得最高平均敏感度（0.85）与细血管敏感度（0.63）。数据集间差异常大于领先流水线之间的差异，五种方法共有的错误集中于标注血管边界附近及暗淡的周边血管。扰动实验显示局部掩膜误差会在下游特征中被放大，针对性稳定化可使5个特征的敏感度下降。

Conclusion: 应基于任务条件、以基准相对化的方式选择血管分割流水线，而非采用单一普适排名；并需在目标域上验证所选流水线，同时关注下游特征对掩膜局部误差的敏感性。

Abstract: Retinal vessel segmentation is widely used in retinal image analysis, yet model selection based only on aggregate overlap scores can overlook structural fidelity, cross-dataset robustness, and application-specific error costs. We evaluated five executable binary vessel-segmentation pipelines (FSG-Net, SA-UNetv2, DCP-1024, GAVE, and FR-UNet) on 244 images from six public datasets using pixel-level agreement, cross-dataset stability, thin-vessel retention, structural metrics, error composition, and available computational evidence. Results were aggregated with equal weighting across datasets. No pipeline was optimal in every setting. FSG-Net achieved the highest equal-dataset mean Macro-F1 (0.77), IoU (0.63), clDice (0.83), and Skeleton F1 (0.78), whereas SA-UNetv2 achieved the highest mean sensitivity (0.85) and Thin-Vessel Sensitivity (0.63). Differences across datasets were often larger than those among the leading pipelines, and errors shared by all five methods were concentrated near annotated vessel boundaries and faint peripheral vessels. In an ancillary analysis on 45 HRF images, segmentation masks were converted into 11 ring-wise vascular features for leave-one-out classification. Controlled perturbations showed that local mask errors can be amplified in downstream features, while targeted stabilization reduced sensitivity for five features. The results support task-conditioned, benchmark-relative pipeline selection rather than a single universal ranking and highlight the need to validate the selected pipeline on the intended target domain.

</details>


### [205] [SG-Splat: Stable Geometry Learning under Pose Uncertainty for Pose-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35361)
*Xiaohan Lu,Jiaqi Zhang,Weijia Jiang,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SG-Splat：一种对位姿不确定性更鲁棒的无位姿前馈3DGS框架，通过解耦几何与高斯属性分支、由粗到精的位姿与几何精化、以及混合真值/预测位姿的双路训练策略，稳定高斯基元学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无位姿前馈3DGS对残余位姿误差敏感：位姿误差会扰动规范空间下的高斯变换，从而对几何与属性学习引入噪声监督，影响重建质量与稳定性。

Method: 1) 将几何估计与高斯属性预测解耦为两个专用分支，降低位姿敏感几何与高斯属性间的误差耦合；2) 提出由粗到精的精化流程：Flow-Guided Pose Refinement（FGPR）利用光流相关性特征与可微捆绑调整精化相机位姿，Residual-Guided Geometry Correction（RGC）用FGPR导出的深度残差修正初始点图以获得更可靠的Gaussian中心；3) 采用混合真值与预测位姿的双路策略进行规范变换，为高斯属性提供干净监督并提升几何对残余位姿误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在DL3DV与RealEstate10K数据集上，SG-Splat在新视角合成与位姿估计两项任务上均达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 通过分支解耦、位姿-几何协同精化与双路规范变换监督，SG-Splat有效缓解了前馈3DGS对残余位姿误差的敏感性，兼顾重建质量与位姿精度。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a promising representation for real-time novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. For practical deployment, recent studies have explored pose-free feed-forward 3DGS, which reconstructs Gaussian representations directly from unposed input images. However, these methods remain sensitive to residual pose errors, which perturb canonical-space Gaussian transformation, introducing noisy supervision for geometry and attribute learning. To address this problem, we present SG-Splat, a pose-free feed-forward 3DGS framework that improves robustness to pose uncertainty and stabilizes the learning of Gaussian primitives. SG-Splat decouples geometry estimation and Gaussian attribute prediction into two dedicated branches, reducing error coupling between pose-sensitive geometry and Gaussian attributes. We further introduce a coarse-to-fine refinement pipeline, consisting of Flow-Guided Pose Refinement (FGPR) and Residual-Guided Geometry Correction (RGC), to improve geometric alignment before Gaussian attribute reconstruction. Specifically, FGPR refines camera poses with flow-based correlation features and differentiable bundle adjustment. RGC then corrects initial point maps using depth residuals derived from FGPR, producing more reliable Gaussian centers. To stabilize training, we introduce a dual-path strategy that mixes ground-truth and predicted poses for canonical transformation. It provides clean supervision for Gaussian attributes while improving geometry robustness to residual pose errors. Extensive experiments on DL3DV and RealEstate10K show that SG-Splat achieves state-of-the-art performance in both novel view synthesis and pose estimation.

</details>


### [206] [Shared-Factor-Conditioned Diffusion for Low-Rank Tensor Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35368)
*Zhengyun Cheng,Guanwen Zhang,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiffTR 是一个无监督的坐标条件扩散框架，用共享因子统一处理张量补全、去噪与高光谱超分辨，实验显示其在多模态、多任务上具有竞争力的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量恢复涉及异构的数据结构与观测过程，亟需一种能跨模态、跨任务自适应的统一表示；现有方法通常针对单一任务或单一损坏模型设计，缺乏通用性。

Method: 基于坐标条件的扩散模型：由张量坐标索引的共享因子调节一个紧凑的分数网络，通过分数匹配从可用观测中联合学习因子与隐式张量表示。输出可为标量或向量，从而同时覆盖单个元素与整条 tube，统一表格张量、图像与视频。框架将表示学习与任务相关观测建模解耦：反向扩散用于补全缺失值；基于 Tweedie 公式、软阈值与可选空间一致性进行混合噪声去噪；测量引导采样把从低分辨率高光谱学到的先验迁移至高分辨率重建，并以数据一致性与光谱子空间正则做采样后精修。

Result: 在多种张量模态与恢复任务上实验表明，重建质量具有竞争力，并验证了该统一条件生成表示的灵活性。

Conclusion: 将表示学习与观测建模分离使同一表示可兼容不同损坏模型，并可在同一恢复框架内融入任务特定约束，为异构张量恢复提供了统一的条件生成式解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor recovery spans heterogeneous data structures and observation processes, calling for a representation that can adapt across modalities and tasks. We propose DiffTR, an unsupervised framework that unifies tensor completion, denoising, and hyperspectral super-resolution through coordinate-conditioned diffusion. Shared factors indexed by tensor coordinates condition a compact score network, and score matching jointly learns the factors and an implicit tensor representation from the available observations. Scalar and vector-valued outputs accommodate individual entries and entire tubes, enabling a common formulation for tabular tensors, images, and videos. The framework separates representation learning from task-specific observation modeling: reverse diffusion recovers missing values; estimation via Tweedie's formula, soft thresholding, and optional spatial consistency support mixed-noise denoising; and measurement-guided sampling transfers a prior learned from low-resolution hyperspectral observations to high-resolution reconstruction, followed by post-sampling refinement with data consistency and spectral subspace regularization. This separation makes the representation compatible with different corruption models and permits task-specific constraints to be incorporated within the same recovery framework. Experiments across multiple tensor modalities and recovery tasks demonstrate competitive reconstruction quality and the flexibility of a unified conditional generative representation. The code is available at https://github.com/CZY-Code/DiffTR

</details>


### [207] [GIFT: Representation Geometry Matters for Single-Domain Generalized Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35374)
*Yin Zhang,Yaoyue Zheng,Yongqiang Zhang,Bogdan Raducanu,Dan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何不变微调方法GIFT，通过向预训练权重引入可学习的Householder反射保持表示几何结构，在S-DGOD基准上以不足1%的额外可训练参数显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单源域泛化目标检测研究主要依赖模拟未见域或抑制域特定因素，忽视了视觉基础模型（VFMs）自身蕴含的内在泛化能力；且无约束微调会破坏预训练表示中编码域不变语义的几何结构。

Method: 向预训练权重引入一组可学习的Householder反射，促使在域偏移下实现几何一致的适应；同时提出轻量的通道级缩放以支持有效的任务特定适应；主干网络保持冻结，仅增加极少可训练参数。

Result: 在两个S-DGOD基准上持续优于SOTA；以不足1%的额外可训练参数，在Cityscapes-C和Diverse Weather Dataset上mPC分别提升+7.9%和+2.9%。

Conclusion: 保持表示几何结构对鲁棒的跨域泛化至关重要，GIFT为域偏移问题提供了一种有原理依据且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Single-Domain Generalized Object Detection (S-DGOD) aims to generalize from a single source domain to unseen domains under distribution shifts. Existing efforts mainly focus on simulating unseen domains or suppressing domain-specific factors. However, they largely overlook the intrinsic generalization capability embedded in Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). In this work, we show that unconstrained fine-tuning disrupts the geometric structure of pre-trained representations, which encodes domain-invariant semantics. Motivated by this, we propose Geometric-Invariance Fine-Tuning (GIFT), a geometric-aware fine-tuning method for VFMs. Specifically, we introduce a series of learnable Householder reflections to the pre-trained weights to encourage geometry-consistent adaptation under domain shift. In addition, we propose a lightweight channel-wise scaling to facilitate effective task-specific adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GIFT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches on two S-DGOD benchmarks. Notably, by introducing fewer than 1% additional trainable parameters into the frozen backbone, the proposed method achieves improvements of +7.9% and +2.9% in mPC over the previous SOTA on the Cityscapes-C and Diverse Weather Dataset, respectively. This finding highlights that preserving representation geometry is crucial for robust cross-domain generalization, establishing GIFT as a principled and efficient solution to domain shift.

</details>


### [208] [ACSAN: All-Convolutional Self-Attention Network Integrating Convolutional Operations with Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35375)
*Zimian Wei,Tianqi Zhang,Hengyue Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全卷积自注意力网络ACSAN，用卷积替换ViT中的全连接矩阵乘法，以降低参数依赖、提升学习效率，在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet-1k上取得优于标准ViT的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT参数量庞大、严重依赖大规模训练数据，学习效率低，难以适用于资源受限场景。作者希望以卷积替代全连接矩阵乘法，降低参数与数据依赖。

Method: 设计动态卷积自注意力（DCSA）模块：用多尺度空洞卷积生成Query、Key、Value；分别构造空间自注意力与通道自注意力生成注意力特征，并将二者融合为DCSA输出。基于DCSA构建ACSAN基本块，以DCSA替换常规自注意力、以卷积层替换MLP。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100与ImageNet-1k上从头训练，ACSAN在参数量相当或更少的情况下分类精度优于标准ViT及其他先进方法；消融实验表明各组件均有正向贡献。

Conclusion: 以卷积全替代ViT矩阵乘法可有效缓解参数与数据依赖问题，在可比特参数规模下实现更优图像分类性能，且各设计组件均有效。

Abstract: In the past few years, Vision Transformer (ViT) has achieved excellent performance in the field of computer vision. However, there still some problems remain. For instance, ViTs heavily rely on large-scale training data since they simply contain a large number of trainable parameters, which slows down their learning efficiency and limits their applicability in resource-constrained scenarios. In this paper, we propose a novel deep learning model, namely All-Convolutional Self-Attention Network (ACSAN), which applies convolution operations to replace fully-connected matrices multiplications in the ViT framework. To implement ACSAN, we firstly design Dynamic Convolutional Self-Attention (DCSA) as the basic module, which employs multi-scale dilated convolutions to generate Query, Key, and Value. We defined spatial self-attention and channel self-attention to generate attention features respectively. The two attention features are finally merged as the output of the DCSA module. Based on DCSA, we then construct the basic ACSAN block. Comparing with ViT, we use DCSA to replace the regular self-attention operations, and use convolution layers to replace MLPs. To evaluate the performance of the proposed method, we train ACSAN on three widely-used image classification databases, i.e. CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1k, from scratch. Experimental results show that ACSAN achieves excellent classification accuracy comparing with standard ViT with comparable or fewer parameters, as well as other state-of-the-art methods. Ablation studies verify that each component contributes positively to the final performance.

</details>


### [209] [Toward Clinical Translation of Deep Learning-Based Intracranial Super-Resolution 4D Flow MRI Using Multi-Cohort Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35396)
*Oliver Welin Odeback,Susanne Schnell,Patrick Winter,Johan Lundberg,Fredrik Piehl,Tobias Granberg,Alexander Fyrdahl,David Marlevi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估合成预训练与多队列迁移学习用于颅内4D Flow MRI深度学习超分辨率重建，在异质神经队列中比较插值、合成预训练、队列特异及多队列合并迁移学习的重建精度与血管描绘效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D Flow MRI空间分辨率受限，深度学习超分辨率在异质神经队列（健康、烟雾病、多发性硬化）中的泛化性与跨队列迁移策略尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 混合前瞻-回顾研究，纳入18名受试者（12健康、3烟雾病、3多发性硬化），均具有配对的1.2 mm与0.6 mm 4D Flow MRI。采用在合成脑血管数据上预训练的CNN，再用队列特异或合并多队列的在体数据微调。以高分辨率衍生输入进行体素级评估，用原生标准分辨率扫描做血管造影与血流动力学分析；评价指标包括速度归一化均方根误差（vNRMSE）与Bland-Altman 95%一致性界限宽度，以及相位对比血管造影的PR-AUC。

Result: 合成预训练较插值降低平均vNRMSE（0.147 vs 0.166）；多队列合并迁移学习获得最低vNRMSE（0.119，队列特异模型0.127，合成预训练0.147），且在所有测试病例中95%一致性界限宽度更窄（0.109–0.222 vs 0.154–0.278 m/s）。超分辨相位对比血管造影在各队列的PR-AUC均高于原生标准分辨率（0.848–0.896 vs 0.486–0.805）。

Conclusion: 多队列合并迁移学习可提升跨队列重建一致性，超分辨率能改善血管描绘，支持进一步开展多中心验证。

Abstract: We evaluated synthetic pretraining and multi-cohort transfer learning for deep learning-based super-resolution of intracranial 4D Flow MRI across heterogeneous neurologic cohorts. This mixed prospective-retrospective study analyzed 18 participants (12 healthy participants, 3 with moyamoya disease, and 3 with multiple sclerosis) with paired 1.2-mm and 0.6-mm 4D Flow MRI scans. A convolutional neural network pretrained on synthetic cerebrovascular data was fine-tuned using cohort-specific or pooled multi-cohort in vivo data. High-resolution-derived inputs enabled voxel-wise evaluation; native standard-resolution scans were used for angiographic and hemodynamic analyses. Synthetic pretraining reduced mean artery-wise velocity-normalized root mean square error (vNRMSE) versus interpolation (0.147 vs 0.166). Pooled transfer learning yielded the lowest mean vNRMSE (0.119 vs 0.127 for cohort-specific models and 0.147 for synthetic pretraining), with narrower 95% limits-of-agreement widths than synthetic pretraining across all test cases (0.109-0.222 vs 0.154-0.278 m/s). Super-resolved phase-contrast angiography achieved higher precision-recall area under the curve than native standard-resolution imaging in all cohorts (0.848-0.896 vs 0.486-0.805). These results indicate that pooled transfer learning improves cross-cohort reconstruction agreement and that super-resolution can improve vessel depiction, supporting further multi-center validation for cerebrovascular imaging.

</details>


### [210] [Unveil the Mask: Jointly-Optimized Diffusion Masked Pretraining for Appearance-Agnostic Operator Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35414)
*Katsuya Ogata,Ryoya Yoshiyama,Kawachi Hodaka,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个无需外部预训练模型的说话人验证框架，利用面部关键点序列与视频帧两种模态学习对头像外观不敏感的运动表征，融合后在WBA2026基准上取得0.769的AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 照片级真实头像视频将渲染人脸的外观与实际操作者解耦，使传统人脸识别无法识别操作者，因此需要一种对头像外观不敏感、仅表征"人脸如何运动"的表示来完成操作者验证。

Method: 框架不依赖任何外部预训练模型。关键点模态：对分组关键点做扩散掩码预训练学习运动表示，并以预测运动强度、眼睛开合等行为线索的轻量辅助任务引导，再用对比式线性探针转换为操作者嵌入；为泛化到未见过的头像生成器，对预训练输入做随机变换以模拟生成器对操作者运动的改变。视频模态：通过连续帧差分捕捉细微面部运动。最后将两模态嵌入合并为统一表示。

Result: 在WBA2026基准上总体AUC达0.769。线性探针显示视频嵌入几乎不保留头像身份信息，而关键点嵌入保留部分信息；两模态融合可提升验证性能，尤其在说话与倾听模式下。

Conclusion: 基于运动而非外观的双模态表征可有效用于头像操作者验证；视频模态对外观泄漏更少，与关键点模态互补融合能带来进一步增益。

Abstract: Photorealistic avatar videos decouple the appearance of a rendered face from the person who operates it, which makes conventional face recognition unsuitable for identifying the operator. Verifying the operator therefore requires representations of how a face moves that are insensitive to what the avatar looks like. We propose a verification framework built without any external pretrained model. It uses two complementary modalities provided for each sample: facial landmark sequences and video frames. From the landmark modality, it learns motion representations by diffusion masked pretraining over grouped landmarks, guided by lightweight auxiliary tasks that predict behavioral cues such as motion intensity and eye openness, and turns them into operator embeddings with a contrastive linear probe. To generalize to unseen avatar generators, the pretraining input is randomly transformed to simulate how a generator may alter the operator's motion. From the video modality, it captures fine facial motion through differences between consecutive frames. The embeddings of the two modalities are combined into one. Our approach reaches an overall AUC of 0.769 on the WBA2026 benchmark. Linear probing shows that the video embedding retains little information about the avatar's identity, whereas the landmark embedding retains some, and fusing the two modalities improves verification in particular across speaking and listening modes.

</details>


### [211] [OBB-PBD: Extending Parallel Box Decoding to Oriented Bounding Boxes for Referring Expression Grounding in Aerial Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35450)
*Chang Woo Song,Yeong Jun Koh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将并行框解码（PBD）的四坐标水平框接口扩展为基于双角度参数化的六槽有向框表示，并通过消融与控制实验验证其真实依赖图像与文本，在AVVG基准上将rotIoU从0.1253提升到0.303±0.012。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俯视遥感图像中目标呈任意朝向，有向包围盒比水平框更贴合；然而PBD仅支持四坐标水平框的单次前向并行解码，且作者强调不应仅凭损失下降判定扩展正确，需排除模型依赖提示统计而非视觉/语言输入的伪增益。

Method: 把PBD的块对齐接口扩展为六槽有向表示，采用双角度（double-angle）参数化以避免角度周期性歧义；引入图像/提示打乱对照及可见性、信息泄漏探针检验模型是否真正依赖图像与提示；在AVVG全量测试集（120张图像、6139条记录）上以3个训练种子报告均值±标准差，并设置匹配对照实验区分“有向框学习”与“仅块扩展”的贡献，配以逐种子95%置信区间。

Result: rotIoU由零样本基线0.1253提升至0.303±0.012；相对匹配对照，学习有向框而非其轴对齐外包络额外带来0.055±0.008 rotIoU，且逐种子95%置信区间均不含零；仅扩大块槽位而不引入有向表示则无可辨别的rotIoU增益。

Conclusion: 并行框解码可被有效扩展至有向框形式，增益来源于有向几何建模本身而非接口扩容；结合扰动控制与探针验证的评估范式能更可靠地排除虚假改进。

Abstract: Referring expression grounding in overhead imagery requires oriented bounding boxes rather than horizontal ones, since aerial objects appear at arbitrary orientations. Most grounding models emit box coordinates sequentially; Parallel Box Decoding (PBD) instead predicts all geometry slots in one forward pass, but only for four-coordinate horizontal boxes. We extend this block-aligned interface to a six-slot oriented representation using a double-angle parameterization. Rather than assuming correctness from a decreasing loss curve, we verify the extension with image/prompt-shuffle controls and visibility/leakage probes, which show that the corrected model depends on both the image and the prompt rather than on prompt statistics alone. On the aerial vehicle visual grounding (AVVG) benchmark, whose targets are genuinely oriented, the extended model improves rotated intersection over union (rotIoU) from 0.1253 for the zero-shot baseline to 0.303 +/- 0.012 (mean +/- standard deviation over three training seeds) across the complete 6139-record test split spanning all 120 images. Learning oriented boxes rather than their axis-aligned envelopes adds 0.055 +/- 0.008 rotIoU beyond a matched control (per-seed 95% confidence intervals all excluding zero), whereas block expansion alone yields no rotIoU gain distinguishable from zero.

</details>


### [212] [TP-JSCC: Efficient Token Pruning and Joint Source-Channel Coding for LVLMs-based 3D-VQA in Cloud-Edge Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35489)
*Zhengxin Li,Chongzhen Tian,Hui Yuan,Hao Liu,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TP-JSCC，一种面向云边协同下基于 LVLM 的 3D-VQA 的高效 token 剪枝与联合信源信道编码方法，实现 180 倍数据量压缩与 90% 计算复杂度节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的具身智能体上部署大视觉语言模型（LVLM）进行 3D 视觉问答需要云边协同，但将海量视觉数据经带宽受限、噪声信道上传云端会带来严重时延与传输失真。

Method: 方法包含两个模块：(1) LGS-HTP（局部与全局语义混合 Token 剪枝），通过解耦前景-背景 token 并联合选择局部与全局语义，在保留推理关键 token 的同时降低冗余；(2) T-JSCC（Token 联合信源信道编码），采用轻量级级联线性层与 SNR 自适应机制，对剪枝后 token 进一步压缩并保障在易错信道上的鲁棒传输。

Result: 在 ScanQA 与 SQA3D 数据集上实验：ScanQA 验证集上 LGS-HTP 实现 90% 剪枝率且 CIDEr 损失小于 1%；T-JSCC 进一步将 token 压缩 18 倍，总体数据量降低 180 倍，同时为 LVLM 部署节省 90% 计算复杂度。

Conclusion: TP-JSCC 在保证 3D-VQA 性能的同时显著降低传输数据量与计算开销，验证了其在云边协同具身场景下的高效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deploying large vision-language models (LVLMs) on resource-constrained embodied agents for 3D visual question answering necessitates cloud-edge collaboration. However, offloading massive visual data to the cloud over bandwidth-limited and noisy channels introduces severe latency and transmission distortion. To address this, we propose TP-JSCC, an efficient token pruning and joint source-channel coding method for LVLMs-based 3D-VQA in Cloud-Edge Collaboration. First, we develop a Local and Global Semantics-based Hybrid Token Pruning (LGS-HTP) module to significantly reduce the visual token count. By decoupling foreground-background tokens and jointly selecting local and global semantics, LGS-HTP effectively reduces redundancy while preserving reasoning-critical tokens. Second, we introduce a lightweight Token Joint Source-Channel Coding (T-JSCC) module to further compress the pruned tokens and ensure their robust transmission over error-prone channels. By employing lightweight cascaded linear layers and Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR)-adaptive mechanism, this module adapts to fluctuating channel conditions while maintaining minimal computational overhead. Extensive experiments on ScanQA and SQA3D datasets indicate the exceptional efficiency and robustness of our approach. Specifically, on ScanQA validation set, the LGS-HTP module could achieve 90% pruning ratio while incurring less than a 1% CIDEr loss. Building on this, the T-JSCC further compresses the tokens by 18 times, resulting in an overall 180 times data volume reduction for robust transmission, while saving 90% in computational complexity for LVLM deployment. Our code is available at https://github.com/yuanhui0325/TP-JSCC.

</details>


### [213] [S$^2$-RL: Sample-Set Dual Reinforcement Learning for Generative Semantic Segmentation Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35491)
*Haoyu Wang,Fei Zhou,Qingqing Qiu,Lei Zhang,Wei Wei,Chen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S²-RL框架，用扩散模型文本到图像生成+GRPO强化学习实现语义分割数据集蒸馏，规避双层优化与像素级梯度展开的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割等密集预测任务的数据集蒸馏存在两大瓶颈：基于双层优化的方法因像素级梯度展开计算代价过高；基于代理的生成方法仅将视觉纹理注入真实数据衍生的固定语义掩码，无法将丰富语义知识压缩为紧凑且新颖的场景生成。

Method: 将SSDD建模为基于扩散模型的文本到图像生成范式，通过简洁文本提示生成具有信息量和新颖语义分布的图像；用GRPO微调扩散策略，并设计样本-集合双奖励范式：单样本层面约束与文本提示的语义对齐及类内特征多样性，样本集合层面通过求解上下文多臂老虎机问题最大化样本间语义分布多样性。

Result: 在ADE20K与COCO数据集上，S²-RL相较现有最优基线取得显著且一致的性能提升，为SSDD建立了新基准。

Conclusion: S²-RL无需像素级梯度展开即可将大规模语义分割数据集蒸馏为紧凑、语义信息丰富的生成样本集合，验证了强化学习驱动生成式蒸馏在密集预测任务上的有效性。

Abstract: While dataset distillation has witnessed significant advances in image classification, its extension to dense prediction tasks such as semantic segmentation is plagued by two core bottlenecks: bi-level optimization-based approaches suffer from prohibitive computational costs stemming from pixel-wise gradient unrolling, whereas proxy-based generative methods merely infuse visual textures into fixed semantic masks derived from real data, thus failing to condense rich semantic knowledge into a compact set of informative, novel scene generations. To address both bottlenecks simultaneously, we propose a novel Sample-Set Reinforcement Learning (S$^2$-RL) framework for generative semantic segmentation dataset distillation (SSDD). Specifically, S$^2$-RL formulates SSDD as a diffusion model based text-to-image generation paradigm, enabling flexible generation of images with informative, novel semantic distributions via a concise text prompt. Furthermore, we fine-tune a diffusion policy via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), leveraging a sample-set dual reward paradigm: for individual samples, it enforces semantic alignment with input text prompts and intra-class feature diversity; for sample sets, it maximizes inter-sample semantic distribution diversity by solving a contextual multi-armed bandit problem. These design choices enable S$^2$-RL to distill large-scale semantic segmentation datasets into a compact set of generated samples that encapsulate diverse, informative semantic knowledge without the need of pixel-wise gradient unrolling. Extensive experiments on the ADE20K and COCO datasets demonstrate that S$^2$-RL achieves substantial and consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, establishing a new benchmark for SSDD.

</details>


### [214] [Learning Scene Coordinate Reconstruction from Unposed Images via Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35501)
*Tze Ho Elden Tse,Jizong Peng,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将位姿图优化（PGO）与不确定性感知优化引入ACE-Zero，构建混合框架以提升无监督SfM的位姿估计鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACE-Zero等基于学习的SfM方法无需真值监督即可从无序图像集估计相机位姿与场景坐标，但缺少全局与多视图一致性约束，在复杂或歧义场景中易产生位姿漂移与错位。

Method: 从ACE-Zero预测的场景坐标中提取相对位姿约束直接构建位姿图；并基于对极一致性、光流一致性等几何先验估计置信度分数，实现不确定性感知的优化策略以抑制错误校正。

Result: 该方法提升了位姿估计的鲁棒性与精度，表明全局几何推理可有效补充基于学习的推理。

Conclusion: 全局几何推理（PGO+不确定性加权）能有效弥补学习式SfM的局部一致性缺陷，形成互补的混合SfM范式。

Abstract: Learning-based structure-from-motion methods such as ACE-Zero have demonstrated strong performance in estimating cameraposes and scene coordinates from unordered image collections without requiring ground truth supervision. However, the lack of global and multi-view consistency constraints in ACE-Zero can lead to pose drift and misalignment, particularly in complex or ambiguous scenes. In this work, we propose a hybrid framework that integrates pose graph optimization (PGO) into ACE-Zero to refine camera poses and suppress incorrect refinements. We construct pose graphs directly from ACE-Zero outputs by extracting relative pose constraints from predicted scene coordinates. Furthermore, we introduce an uncertainty-aware optimization strategy by estimating confidence scores using geometric priors, including epipolar and optical flow consistencies across views. Our approach improves the robustness and accuracy of pose estimation, demonstrating that global geometric reasoning can effectively complement learning-based inference in structure-from-motion.

</details>


### [215] [HiFiPix: Bringing High-Fidelity Real-World Super-Resolution Back to Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35515)
*Wenjie Li,Du Chen,Hui Wang,Linlong Fan,Youwei Lv,Zeyi Chen,Zehan Chen,Jinwei Chen,Heng Guo,Zhanyu Ma,Qingnan Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiFiPix：基于像素空间扩散模型的高保真真实世界图像超分框架，通过跨尺度小波判别器与Mixup对比自蒸馏抑制patch边界伪影，并提出选择性LoRA剪枝以平衡生成与保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE潜空间表示的信息损失限制了扩散式Real-ISR的重建保真度；像素空间扩散模型虽具高保真潜力，但存在patch边界伪影与生成能力退化两大障碍。

Method: 提出HiFiPix像素空间Real-ISR框架：1) Cross-Scale Wavelet Discriminator (CWSD)，在图像级监督中惩罚不真实的高频周期分量以抑制patch边界伪影；2) Mixup Contrastive Self-Distillation (MixCSD) 对中间表示进行正则化；3) 发现LoRA层存在异质性的生成-保真权衡，提出Selective LoRA Pruning (LoRA-SP) 自适应剪枝LoRA层。

Result: 大量实验表明HiFiPix在复原精度与感知质量两方面均大幅超越现有最先进潜空间方法。

Conclusion: 像素空间扩散模型经伪影抑制与LoRA剪枝适配后，可显著释放其在Real-ISR中的高保真与细粒度生成潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion-based real-world image super-resolution suffers from limited reconstruction fidelity due to information loss in VAE-based latent representations. Recently, pixel-space diffusion models offer a promising high-faithfulness alternative by avoiding latent compression, but adapting them to Real-ISR remains challenging due to inherent patch boundary artifacts and degraded generative capability. To address the issues and maximize the potential of pretrained pixel-space diffusion models, we propose HiFiPix, a pixel-space Real-ISR framework capable of ultra high-fidelity reconstruction and fine-grained visual generation effects. To suppress patch boundary artifacts, we penalize unrealistic high-frequency periodic components with a Cross-Scale Wavelet Discriminator (CWSD) for image-level supervision, and Mixup Contrastive Self-Distillation (MixCSD) for intermediate representation regularization. Furthermore, we uncover a heterogeneous generation-fidelity trade-off across low-rank adapters (LoRA) and propose Selective LoRA Pruning (LoRA-SP) to adaptively prune LoRA layers, enhancing generative capability with minimal deterioration of reconstruction fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HiFiPix consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art latent space methods in terms of both restoration accuracy and perceptual quality by a large margin. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [216] [Learning High-Quality Pseudo-Labels for Point-Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35516)
*Zhenying Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个融合"先验引导初始化"与"共识引导精炼"的伪标签学习框架用于点监督时序动作定位(PTAL)，在THUMOS14上取得68.5%平均mAP，较PseR提升3.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于伪标签的PTAL方法存在两点局限：(1)基于置信度的初始化会忽略定位良好但置信度不高的候选proposal；(2)训练过程中伪标签要么保持固定，要么在更新时未充分利用原始候选池中可替代的边界证据。这限制了监督质量与检测性能。

Method: 框架包含两个模块：Prior-Guided Initialization (PGI) 将proposal置信度与点级先验结合，建立实例级可靠性，用于初始伪标签选择与候选保留；Consensus-Guided Refinement (CGR) 在训练过程中持续利用被保留的原始候选构建边界目标，并将其共识与不断演化的检测器预测结合，迭代精炼伪标签。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上验证，方法性能与现有方法相比具有竞争力；在THUMOS14上，tIoU 0.1–0.7范围内的平均mAP达68.5%，比PseR高出3.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过先验引导的初始化与共识引导的持续精炼，可显著提升伪标签监督质量，从而改善点监督时序动作定位的检测性能。

Abstract: Point-supervised temporal action localization (PTAL) aims to recognize and localize action instances in untrimmed videos using only point-level annotations. Existing pseudo-label-based methods have made substantial progress in PTAL. However, confidence-based initialization can overlook well-localized proposals. Meanwhile, existing methods either keep pseudo-labels fixed during detector training or update them without fully exploiting alternative boundary evidence from the original proposal pool. These limitations constrain supervision quality, thereby hindering detection performance. To learn high-quality pseudo-labels, we propose a framework that integrates prior-guided initialization with consensus-guided refinement during detector training. Our framework comprises two modules: Prior-Guided Initialization (PGI) and Consensus-Guided Refinement (CGR). PGI combines proposal confidence with point-derived priors to establish instance-specific reliability for initial pseudo-label selection and candidate retention. CGR continually uses the retained original candidates to construct boundary targets, combining their consensus with evolving detector predictions to refine pseudo-labels throughout training. Experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves competitive detection performance compared with existing methods. In particular, our method achieves 68.5\% average mAP over tIoU thresholds 0.1--0.7 on THUMOS14, surpassing PseR by 3.7 percentage points. Code is available at https://github.com/zhenyingfang/LHQP

</details>


### [217] [Generative Structure from Motion with Native 3D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35522)
*Haobin Duan,Binbin Huang,Mochu Xiang,Yiqun Zhao,Zibo Zhao,Yi Ma,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenSfM 将基于图像的三维重建与原生三维扩散模型统一到一个多模态扩散 Transformer 中，联合去噪形状隐向量与逐视角位姿隐向量，在少量无标定视角下同时恢复几何、纹理与相机位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 SfM 只能恢复与输入图像一致的可见几何、无法补全未观测区域；原生生成方法虽能产生合理三维物体，却不保证与输入视图一致；简单串联"重建+生成"会导致误差级联，难以得到同时符合数据分布与输入的 3D 结果。因此需要一个统一框架，兼顾输入一致性(Object-level prior 补全)与多视图约束。

Method: 提出 GenSfM：1) 用多模态扩散 Transformer 在共享自注意力下联合去噪形状 latent 与每视角位姿 latent，使生成几何锚定于观测视图，并借助物体级先验推断未观测区域；2) 采用紧凑的三维原生 UV-volume tokenization，使联合序列长度随视图数量增加仍保持较小；3) 位姿感知精化阶段，在恢复的相机位姿下采样逐体素图像特征以细化几何与纹理。无需外部位姿估计骨干网络、后处理优化或已知内参。

Result: 在 Toys4k 与 GSO 数据集、4 个无标定视角设置下，相比强原生三维生成基线，新视角 PSNR 提升 3.2–4.5 dB，中位 Chamfer 距离降低 2.8–3.8 倍；输入视角位姿恢复达到 96% 以上 Acc@5°。

Conclusion: GenSfM 表明将图像驱动的三维重建与原生三维扩散生成在共享注意力中联合建模，可避免两阶段串联的误差级联，在无位姿、无内参先验的少视图场景下同时获得高保真新视角渲染、精确几何与准确位姿估计，为重建-生成一体化提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Recovering an object's 3D structure and camera motion from a few uncalibrated views is a fundamental problem in computer vision. Classical structure from motion methods recover 3D geometry consistent with the input images but cannot recover unseen regions; native generation methods produce plausible 3D objects which are related but not necessarily faithful to the input images. Methods simply concatenating reconstruction and generation allow error to cascade across the two stages, instead of producing a 3D object consistent with both the distribution and the input. We propose GenSfM, which integrates image-based 3D reconstruction with a native 3D diffusion model. In particular, we use a multi-modal diffusion transformer to jointly denoise the shape latent and per-view pose latents under shared self-attention, grounding generated geometry in the observed views while using object-level priors to infer unobserved regions. A compact 3D-native UV-volume tokenization keeps the joint sequence small as views are added, and a pose-aware stage refines geometry and texture by sampling per-voxel image features at the recovered camera poses. On Toys4k and GSO, for $4$ uncalibrated views, GenSfM improves novel-view PSNR by $3.2$-$4.5$ dB and reduces median Chamfer distance by $2.8$-$3.8\times$ over a strong native-3D generation baseline, while recovering input-view poses over $96\%$ Acc@$5^\circ$ without any external pose-estimation backbone, post-hoc optimization, or known intrinsics. Project page: https://dotb1ue.github.io/GenSfm/.

</details>


### [218] [EARS: Evidence-Aware Retrieval via Sound and Motion for Budgeted Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35536)
*Shih-Yao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、免查询的前端EARS，用光流运动与音频能量给帧排序并做时序非极大值抑制，在稀疏事件视频的低帧预算下显著提升证据定位与VLM定位精度，但在静音类基准上略逊于均匀采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM只观测K帧时，均匀帧采样容易漏掉稀疏事件视频中的短时证据，需要在帧预算受限条件下优先保留含证据的帧。

Method: EARS（Evidence-Aware Retrieval via Sound and motion）：免训练、免查询的前端；以光流运动与音频能量构造证据分数，再施加时序非极大值抑制以获得帧间多样性；单套配置跨两个基准通用。

Result: 证据分数本身帧级AP为0.39；仅用于选帧时，EARS结合时序多样性达到定位AP 0.94，均匀采样为0.88。更新后的匹配研究中K=1时EARS在XD子集F1为0.660，居中的Uniform为0.614（配对区间包含零）；早期骨干研究采用不同协议。在多为静音的UCF-Crime上EARS略落后于Uniform。

Conclusion: EARS适用于低预算、显著证据与运动或音频对齐的视频；在密集叙事或隐蔽事件上，均匀采样或语义选帧更优，且廉价的模态统计无法可靠地在这两者间路由。新对照未复现原始留出集搜索，结论需谨慎推广。

Abstract: Uniform frame sampling can miss brief evidence in sparse-event videos when the VLM sees only K frames. We propose EARS (Evidence-Aware Retrieval via Sound and motion), a training-free, query-free front-end that ranks frames using optical-flow motion and audio energy, then applies temporal non-maximum suppression for diversity. The evidence score alone has frame-level AP 0.39; used only to select frames for the VLM, EARS with temporal diversity reaches localization AP 0.94, compared with 0.88 under Uniform sampling. In the updated matched study, at K = 1 EARS reaches XD-subset F1 0.660 versus 0.614 for centered Uniform (the paired interval includes zero); earlier backbone studies use separate protocols. On mostly-silent UCF-Crime, however, EARS slightly trails Uniform. A single configuration is used across both benchmarks; the new controls do not repeat the original held-out search. EARS therefore targets low-budget videos whose salient evidence aligns with motion or audio; on dense narratives or stealthy events, uniform or semantic selection remains preferable, and cheap modality statistics cannot reliably route between them. Code: https://github.com/mikeslin-ir/EARS

</details>


### [219] [Metric Scale for Insta360 Structure-from-Motion from the Camera's Embedded IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35554)
*Davide Cuttini,Diego Di Tommaso,Alessio Antoniacomi,Luca Simonetti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用Insta360 X5/X6内置1kHz IMU替代智能手机AR位姿来恢复双鱼眼SfM的度量尺度，通过对整段测量轨迹做线性视觉-惯性对齐来估计尺度，并与LiDAR、标定靶、公开基准和产线数据进行了系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双鱼眼360相机SfM只能恢复到相似变换，度量尺度需外部来源；现有方案依赖手机ARKit/ARCore位姿，不够自包含且精度有限（1.94% MAE）。作者希望验证相机自带IMU能否作为独立、更优的尺度来源。

Method: 对X5/X6的IMU做噪声密度、相机-IMU同步、加速度计尺度因子、零偏稳定性等标定；从数据中估计相机-IMU旋转与外参及帧时序；在整段survey的SfM位姿上做线性视觉-惯性对齐求尺度；发现共享重力与偏置的标准形式会高估尺度3.4%，改为按连续段独立估计后平均以消除偏差。另做窗口构造消融与单目度量深度网络对比。

Result: 9组与同装LiDAR对比：惯性尺度MAE 0.74%，手机尺度1.94%；打印标定靶上二者均在目标1%不确定度内；公开基准30个序列（尺度1/40、1、40）平均误差-0.07%、标准差1.28%；50组仅以手机为参考的产线数据：两者标准差2.6%，惯性尺度平均低0.9%，差异随手机锚点范围增大，且无法归因于任一参考。

Conclusion: 消费级360相机内置IMU可作为自包含且优于手机AR位姿的度量尺度来源；分段独立估计并平均可消除共享偏置引起的系统性高估，方法在多场景下精度稳定。

Abstract: Structure-from-motion (SfM) from the dual-fisheye video of a consumer 360-degree camera recovers geometry only up to a similarity transform; in the pipeline considered here, metric scale is obtained from smartphone ARKit or ARCore poses captured alongside the camera. This paper evaluates a self-contained alternative: the IMU embedded in the Insta360 X5 and X6, recorded at 1 kHz. The sensor is characterised (noise density, camera-IMU synchronisation, accelerometer scale factor, bias stability), the camera-IMU rotation and frame timing are estimated from the data, and the scale is recovered by a linear visual-inertial alignment over the SfM poses of a whole survey. The standard formulation, with gravity and accelerometer bias shared across the survey, overestimates the scale by 3.4% on average; estimating each contiguous segment independently and averaging removes the bias. On nine surveys with a co-mounted LiDAR scanner the inertial scale deviates from the LiDAR by 0.74% in mean absolute error, against 1.94% for the smartphone scale; on printed fiducial markers both agree within the 1% uncertainty of the target. On the reference trajectories of thirty sequences of a public benchmark, brought to scales of 1/40, 1 and 40, the estimator recovers the metric scale with a mean error of -0.07% and a standard deviation of 1.28%. On fifty production surveys where the smartphone is the only reference, the two scales agree to a standard deviation of 2.6%, the inertial scale being 0.9% lower on average; the difference does not depend on window construction, grows with the extent of the smartphone anchor, and cannot be attributed to either reference. An ablation over window construction and a comparison with monocular metric-depth networks complete the study.

</details>


### [220] [Copying As Defense: Protecting Images from Editing By Eliciting Model Copying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35566)
*Salma Abdel Magid,Sanghyuk Chun,Eric Slyman,Kushal Kafle,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"复制即防御"(CaD)：通过在早期Transformer块最大化目标token对参考图的注意力质量，使DiT图像编辑器退化为复现输入而非执行编辑，编辑成功率从81.4%降至24.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于DiT的上下文图像编辑器（如Flux Kontext）可被滥用于恶意或未经同意的图像篡改。现有主动防御通过降低输出对受保护参考图的注意力来干扰编辑，但这会让模型转而依赖文本指令与自生成token，仍能产出一个弱接地的新图像，无法真正阻止编辑。作者发现反向策略更有效：与其让模型忽略参考图，不如诱导它复制参考图。

Method: 通过受控注意力干预实验，发现仅在少量早期块中将目标token注意力集中于参考图即可使编辑行为崩溃，模型倾向于有损复现输入。据此提出CaD：在早期Transformer块中最大化逐token的目标-参考注意力质量；由于注意力行经过归一化，提升参考注意力自然压制文本与生成画布的注意力分配。仅约束早期块同时允许截断反向传播路径，降低优化开销。

Result: 在多种编辑器与基准上，CaD将平均编辑成功率从无保护图像的81.4%降至24.0%（相对下降70.6%），相较最强基线DeContext（43.0%）再降低44.3%相对值；每步优化速度约为同类方法的1.8–2.0倍。

Conclusion: 防御思路应从"阻断参考信息"转向"劫持注意力使其复制参考"，早期块注意力引导是高效且有效的主动防御机制，兼具更强防护效果与更低优化成本。

Abstract: Image editors built on diffusion transformers (DiTs), such as Flux Kontext, make it possible to turn a single photograph into a convincing manipulated image, including harmful and non-consensual edits. Proactive defenses aim to prevent such manipulations by adding an imperceptible perturbation before an image is shared. Existing defenses for DiT-based in-context editors make the output attend less to the protected reference image. But this leaves the model free to follow the edit instruction using the text and its own generated tokens, often producing a new, weakly grounded image rather than preventing the edit. We find that the opposite strategy is more effective: instead of leading the model to ignore the reference, we encourage it to copy it. Through controlled attention interventions, we show that concentrating target-token attention on the reference image in only a few early blocks is sufficient to collapse the editing behavior, causing the model to reproduce its input, often as a lossy copy, rather than execute the requested transformation. Based on this insight, we introduce Copying-as-Defense (CaD), which maximizes per-token target-to-reference attention mass in early transformer blocks. Since attention rows are normalized, increasing reference attention suppresses the attention allocated to the text and generated canvas. Restricting the objective to early blocks also lets us truncate the backpropagation path, keeping optimization cheap. Across editors and benchmarks, CaD reduces average edit success from 81.4% on unprotected images to 24.0%, a 70.6% relative reduction, and by a further 44.3% relative to DeContext, the strongest baseline (43.0%). CaD is also roughly 1.8-2.0x faster per optimization step than comparable methods.

</details>


### [221] [Hyperspectral Unmixing using a Structured Linear Mixing Model with Additive Spectral Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35580)
*Salah Eddine Brezini,Yannick Deville,Moussa Sofiane Karoui,Fatima Zohra Benhalouche,Abdelaziz Ouamri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文针对线性高光谱解混中的光谱变异性（SV）问题，基于结构化加性变异性线性混合模型（SAV-LMM）提出两种NMF解混算法，并设计考虑SV的初始化方案，在合成与真实数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际高光谱图像受大气效应、纯材料本征差异及光照/地形变化影响，存在光谱变异性（类内变异性）。标准基于LMM的解混方法忽略这些现象，会使误差在整个解混流程中传播扩散，因此需要能显式建模SV的解混方法。

Method: 采用参数化模型SAV-LMM，通过在标准LMM中加入两个加性项来刻画SV；基于非负矩阵分解（NMF）设计两种解混算法，优化融合了针对SV的额外惩罚项的目标函数；并提出一种考虑SV的改进初始化策略。

Result: 在合成数据集与真实数据集上的实验表明，与所对比的现有先进方法相比，所提算法在含SV的高光谱图像线性解混中更有效。

Conclusion: 显式建模光谱变异性并结合相应惩罚与初始化的NMF解混框架，可有效缓解SV带来的误差传播，提升线性高光谱解混性能。

Abstract: Linear hyperspectral unmixing aims at retrieving pure material spectra present in an observed scene and their associated abundance fractions in each pixel. In most practical situations, hyperspectral images are affected by spectral variability (SV) also known as intra-class variability, which is caused by different physical phenomena including atmospheric effects, intrinsic variability of the pure materials and illumination or topographic changes. Nevertheless, the standard hyperspectral unmixing methods that rely on the Linear Mixing Model (LMM) do not consider these phenomena. Thus, neglecting them will spread errors throughout the linear hyperspectral unmixing process. To avoid this problem, this paper proposes two hyperspectral unmixing algorithms dedicated to a parametric model called Structured Additive Variability LMM (SAV-LMM). This mixing model handles SV in an additive manner by incorporating two terms into the standard LMM. Moreover, the designed algorithms are based on Nonnegative Matrix Factorization (NMF) and optimize a cost function which integrates additional penalization accounting for SV. Furthermore, a novel improved initialization scheme, which takes SV into account, is proposed. Experiments conducted on both synthetic and real datasets show the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms for linearly unmixing hyperspectral images with SV compared to the tested state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond Distribution Matching: Self-Supervised Representation Forcing for Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35594)
*Junyi Chen,Haiyu Zhang,Zhoujie Fu,Tengfei Wang,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self Flow Forcing (SF²)，在DMD框架中加入预测式表征损失，通过异质噪声水平制造师生信息不对称，实现自监督表征对齐，缓解少步蒸馏中的时空质量退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型少步蒸馏中，DMD仅依赖输出空间目标，缺乏对内部表征的显式正则；当蒸馏步数急剧压缩时，纯输出监督不足，学生模型在细粒度空间结构与连贯时序动态上均出现退化。

Method: 在DMD框架内引入预测性表征损失且不改变原输出目标：给学生与EMA教师施加不同噪声水平，形成信息不对称，迫使学生从更严重的损坏输入预测教师内部表征，从而把自监督表征对齐融入分布匹配；无需外部特征提取器。

Result: 大量实验表明SF²有效抑制时序闪烁，并持续提升空间保真度与运动质量；机制与架构无关，兼容双向与因果模型，额外计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 通过在分布匹配中加入基于信息不对称的表征级自监督对齐，可在不引入对抗训练与外部特征提取器的前提下显著改善少步视频蒸馏的时空质量，具备良好的通用性与低开销特性。

Abstract: Few-step distillation is essential for deploying modern video diffusion models. Among existing approaches, Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) has emerged as a highly promising paradigm by achieving strong output-space fidelity without adversarial training, while remaining flexible across various student architectures. We observe, however, that DMD fundamentally relies on an output-space objective, lacking explicit regularization on the model's internal representations. As the distillation step count aggressively shrinks, this output-only supervision becomes insufficient, causing the distilled student to degrade in both fine-grained spatial structures and coherent temporal dynamics. To address this limitation, we introduce Self Flow Forcing (SF$^2$), which incorporates a predictive representation loss into the DMD framework without altering its output objective. By providing the student and an Exponential Moving Average (EMA) teacher with heterogeneous noise levels, we establish an information asymmetry. This formulation forces the student to predict the teacher's internal representations from a more corrupted input, effectively integrating self-supervised representation alignment into distribution matching. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SF$^2$ effectively mitigates temporal flickering and consistently improves both spatial fidelity and motion quality. Furthermore, the proposed mechanism is architecture-agnostic - seamlessly supporting both bidirectional and causal models - and requires no external feature extractors, introducing minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [223] [Synthetic Deformable Tubular Benchmark: A mechanically annotated dataset for evaluating deformable 3D reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35621)
*Íñigo Cirauqui-Viloria*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开源工具包LumenMorph及合成可变形管状基准数据集（5,580张RGB图像），提供相机位姿、几何、深度、变形场、持续性材质对应与有限元记录等真值，并给出SfM、光流与几何位移插值三类评测示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形3D重建的评测需要同时关联相机运动、表面变形、材质对应与图像外观的真值，而现有资源难以提供这种受控且完整的多模态标注，因此需要一个可控的合成参考基准。

Method: 构建开源工具包LumenMorph用于生成、标注和评测管状场景；基准在六类场景族上交叉三种表面运动条件与动/静相机，另含四类场景族中仅对应动相机有限元动作的外观匹配变体；通过公开文档化接口提供位姿、几何、深度、变形场、持久材质对应及离散有限元记录，并以三角形身份与材质权重支持跨帧表面点追踪及三维轨迹查询；另用独立实现的几何与力学检查验证标注与生成模型的一致性。

Result: SfM、光流及基于给定几何的位移插值实验表明，该资源可区分配准覆盖率与几何精度，揭示相机运动与表面运动的影响，并检验力学先验的收益及其失配问题；数据完整性、固定环境可重复性与保留预测分数回放具有各自独立的验证范围。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了受控的合成参考，公开读取接口、评测契约与可配置生成器支持复用与扩展；作者明确其不宣称解剖或临床意义上的真实性。

Abstract: Evaluating deformable 3D reconstruction requires ground truth linking camera motion, surface deformation, material correspondence and image appearance. We introduce the Synthetic Deformable Tubular Benchmark, generated with LumenMorph, an open-source toolkit for creating, annotating and evaluating tubular scenes. The release contains 5,580 RGB images. Its baseline crosses three surface-motion conditions with moving and stationary cameras in six scene families; matched appearance variants cover only moving-camera finite-element actions in four families. Documented public and evaluation interfaces expose camera poses, geometry, depth, deformation fields, persistent material correspondences and discrete finite-element records. Persistent triangle identities and material weights let users track the same surface location across frames and query its three-dimensional trajectory. Separately implemented geometric and mechanical checks assess annotation consistency with the generating models. Structure-from-motion, optical-flow and supplied-geometry displacement-interpolation studies demonstrate how the resource distinguishes registration coverage from geometric accuracy, exposes camera- and surface-motion effects, and tests the benefits and mismatch of mechanical priors. Public readers, evaluation contracts and the configurable generator support reuse and extension. Data integrity, fixed-environment repeatability and retained-prediction score replay have distinct verification scopes. The benchmark provides a controlled synthetic reference without claiming anatomical or clinical realism.

</details>


### [224] [SeeRF: Learning to See Beyond Fields of View via Generative Image Registration and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35637)
*Xunpeng Yi,Yibing Zhang,Qinglong Yan,Yifan Lu,Han Xu,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生成式配准-融合框架SeeRF，通过微分同胚配准+扩散模型，突破多模态传感器视场不重叠的限制，实现完整场景级信息融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态传感器存在成像视角差异与视场不匹配，现有融合方法只能在共享视场内整合信息，导致融合结果包含模态特有区域与融合伪影，难以完整表征场景结构与语义信息。

Method: 1) 基于微分同胚平稳速度场的形变生成策略；2) 视觉基础模型引导的特征提取与由粗到细渐进式多模态配准模块，实现跨模态流估计并生成有效视场掩码；3) 将配准模块与生成式扩散模块紧耦合进行多模态补全与融合，引入跨模态控制编码模块及控制残差特征编解码机制提供引导。

Result: 在多个公开数据集与真实场景上的大量实验表明该方法性能优越，验证了其有效性、先进性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 借助扩散模型的先验建模能力，SeeRF克服了共享视场限制，可对非重叠视场区域进行有效补全与自然融合，实现完整的场景级多模态融合。代码将开源于 https://github.com/XunpengYi/SeeRF。

Abstract: Due to the inherent discrepancies in imaging viewpoints and field-of-view mismatches among multi-modal sensors, existing fusion methods have long struggled to achieve complete scene-level information integration beyond the limitation in sensor fields of view. Consequently, the generated fusion results often contain modality-specific regions and fusion artifacts, limiting the comprehensive representation of scene structures and semantic information. To address this challenge, we propose a generative registration-fusion framework, termed SeeRF. Specifically, it first introduces a deformation generation strategy based on the diffeomorphic stationary velocity field, combined with visual foundation model-guided feature extraction and a coarse-to-fine progressive multi-modal image registration module, to achieve accurate cross-modal flow estimation and generate valid field-of-view masks. Subsequently, the registration module is tightly coupled with the generative diffusion module for multi-modal completion and fusion, where the cross-modal control encoding module and the control residual feature encoder-decoder mechanism provide effective guidance. By leveraging the powerful prior modeling capability of generative diffusion models, the proposed framework overcomes the limitations imposed by shared fields of view and enables effective completion and natural fusion of non-overlapping field-of-view regions. Extensive experiments on multiple public datasets and real-world scenarios demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance, validating its effectiveness, superiority, and robustness. The code will be available at https://github.com/XunpengYi/SeeRF.

</details>


### [225] [Printed and Scanned: Exposing the Fragility of Single-Image Morphing Attack Detectors under Print-Scan Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35682)
*Diogo J. Paulo,Hugo Proença,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了SYN-MAD22的打印-扫描版本（300/600 DPI，两台扫描仪），发现仅在数字域训练的8个先进S-MAD模型在打印-扫描退化下平均EER升高三倍以上，揭示了数字基准性能与现实证件签发流程鲁棒性之间的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸变形攻击威胁证件签发与自动边检，现有S-MAD算法主要在数字域基准（如SYN-MAD22）上评测；但部分国家真实护照签发流程要求申请人照片先打印再重新扫描，该步骤引入半色调、重采样、色彩空间与传感器噪声，可能掩盖S-MAD依赖的高频伪影，导致基准结果与真实部署不符。

Method: 将SYN-MAD22中全部真实与变形图像以300 DPI和600 DPI打印并重新扫描，构建打印-扫描版数据集；使用该数据集评测8个仅在数字数据上训练的先进S-MAD模型；并用第二台扫描仪重复采集以评估跨设备鲁棒性。

Result: 在数字域接近SOTA的检测器在打印-扫描条件下平均等错误率（EER）上升超过三倍，且在两台扫描设备上呈现一致的定性退化趋势。

Conclusion: 数字域基准性能无法代表对打印-扫描退化的鲁棒性，此前被低估的该差距对人脸变形检测的实际部署具有关键影响，需在含打印-扫描环节的数据上重新评估与训练S-MAD模型。

Abstract: Face morphing attacks, in which the biometric traits of two or more identities are blended into a single face image, pose a severe threat to identity document issuance and automated border control. Consequently, a large body of work has proposed Single-image Morphing Attack Detection (S-MAD) algorithms and benchmarked them on datasets such as SYN-MAD22. However, the majority of these benchmarks operate exclusively in the digital domain, which contrasts with the real-world passport issuance pipeline in some countries, which still requires an applicant-supplied photograph to be printed and subsequently re-scanned before it is embedded in the identity document or fed into an automated verification system. This print-scan step introduces halftoning, resampling, color-space, and sensor noise that are absent from digital pipelines, and may mask the high-frequency artifacts that S-MAD models rely on. In this work, we introduce a print-scan version of the SYN-MAD22 dataset, obtained by printing and re-scanning all bona fide and morphed images at 300 and 600 DPI, and we use it to evaluate eight state-of-the-art S-MAD models trained exclusively on digital data. We additionally repeat the acquisition with a second scanner to assess cross-device robustness. Our results show that detectors that achieve near state-of-the-art performance in the digital domain suffer a more than threefold increase in average equal error rate, with the same qualitative degradation observed across both scanning devices, exposing a critical, previously under-reported gap between digital-benchmark performance and robustness to print-scan degradation.

</details>


### [226] [OmniShowcase: Omnidirectional Subject-Consistent Video Generation from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35684)
*Junyeong Ahn,Geonwoo Kim,Kinam Kim,Gihwi Kim,Hoonjin Jung,Hyojin Jang,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OmniShowcase：用全自动 3D 资产数据合成管线与表面法向图几何条件，实现多视角参考图像驱动的 360° 主体视频生成，达到主体一致性与 3D 一致性 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期主体到视频（S2V）方法仅以单张参考图作为条件，当相机视角变化暴露出输入中不可见的表面时，需隐式推断主体的外观与几何，效果受限。多参考视角方法虽提供更多信息，但未显式建模连接这些观测的 3D 结构；且现有训练数据不能保证视角变化下保持同一底层 3D 主体。

Method: 两个核心组件：(i) 全自动数据合成管线，沿多样相机轨迹生成训练视频，视角变化由底层主体几何驱动，可覆盖完整 360° 环绕；(ii) 几何条件方法，向视频模型提供由参考视角重建的转台式表面法向图（turntable of surface-normal maps），无需与生成帧做时间对齐。构建 Showcase-4K 数据集：基于 3D 资产、含 12 种相机运动、88 种轨迹配置，并附重建得到的表面法向条件；同时提出带源网格与渲染相机参数的留出评测集 ShowcaseBench。

Result: 实验显示在主体身份一致性与 3D 一致性上达到 SOTA，同时保持有竞争力的视频质量与运动表现。

Conclusion: 通过显式引入与生成帧解耦的几何条件（表面法向图）配合几何驱动的自动化数据合成，可有效支撑大视角乃至 360° 环绕的主体视频生成，兼顾身份保真与三维一致性。

Abstract: Generating videos of a specific subject from a few reference images remains challenging when the camera reveals surfaces that are not visible in the input. Early subject-to-video (S2V) methods condition on a single reference image and must therefore infer the subject's unseen appearance and geometry as the viewpoint changes. To address this limitation, recent methods extend S2V to multiple reference views, providing more information about the subject from different viewpoints. However, they do not explicitly specify the 3D structure that connects these observations. In addition, existing training data do not guarantee that viewpoint changes preserve the same underlying 3D subject. We present OmniShowcase, built on two components: (i) a fully automated data synthesis pipeline that produces training videos along diverse camera trajectories, with viewpoint variation driven by underlying subject geometry, even spanning full 360-degree orbits; and (ii) a geometry-conditioning method that provides the video model with a turntable of surface-normal maps reconstructed from the reference views without requiring temporal alignment with the generated frames. We construct Showcase-4K, a corpus of 3D-asset-based videos covering twelve camera motions across 88 trajectory configurations with reconstruction-derived surface-normal conditioning, and ShowcaseBench, a held-out evaluation set with the source meshes and camera parameters used to render each video. Experiments show state-of-the-art subject identity and 3D consistency while maintaining competitive video quality and motion. Project page: justin4ai.github.io/OmniShowcase

</details>


### [227] [MatAnyone-E: Sparse Memory Propagation for Efficient High-Resolution Video Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35724)
*Shuyun Wang,Shangchen Zhou,Peiqing Yang,Yuekun Dai,Xin Yu,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatAnyone-E 通过"低分辨率传播定位不确定区域 + 局部高分辨率记忆匹配 + 轻量解码器"的稀疏化策略，在保持 MatAnyone 2 抠像质量的同时实现约 7.4 倍推理加速，并将 4K 峰值显存降至原来的 7.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视频抠像需保留发丝、软透明等细节并保证时序一致性。基于记忆的方法虽质量高，但随分辨率提升，全图特征与历史记忆的匹配开销急剧增大（计算量与显存均难以承受），因此需要将计算聚焦于真正需要精细处理的位置。

Method: 1) 先进行低分辨率传播，识别需要精细检查的不确定区域，并筛选与之最相关的记忆候选；2) 仅在这些区域用所选记忆执行高分辨率匹配，其余位置复用低分辨率匹配结果，从而同时减少需精细匹配的位置数与记忆检索量；3) 基于实测运行时瓶颈设计轻量解码器，并通过蒸馏使其复现原解码器的特征表示，降低高分辨率蒙版生成成本。

Result: 抠像质量与 MatAnyone 2 相当；估计约 7.4 倍推理加速；4K 下峰值 GPU 显存仅为 MatAnyone 2 的 7.6%；在单张 NVIDIA H200 上启用编译推理后达到 1080p 73.07 FPS、4K 28.62 FPS。

Conclusion: 将记忆匹配计算稀疏化到不确定区域、并以蒸馏轻量解码器替代原解码器，可在不损失抠像完整性与质量的前提下显著提升高分辨率视频抠像的效率与可部署性。

Abstract: High-resolution video matting requires preserving fine details, such as hair strands and soft transparency, while maintaining consistency across frames. Memory-based methods achieve strong matting quality by matching current-frame features with information stored from earlier frames, but this process becomes increasingly costly as resolution grows. We present MatAnyone-E, an efficient extension of MatAnyone 2 that focuses computation where it is most needed. Our approach first performs low-resolution propagation to identify uncertain regions that require closer inspection and the memory candidates most relevant to them. It then performs high-resolution matching only for these regions using the selected memory, while reusing low-resolution matching results elsewhere. This strategy reduces both the number of locations requiring detailed matching and the amount of memory searched, while preserving the information needed to produce a complete matte. We further reduce the cost of generating high-resolution mattes with a lightweight decoder designed around measured runtime bottlenecks and trained to reproduce the original decoder's representations. Experiments show that MatAnyone-E maintains matting quality comparable to MatAnyone 2 while achieving an estimated 7.4x inference speedup and reducing peak GPU memory usage to 7.6% of the usage of MatAnyone 2 at 4K. With compilation-enabled inference on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU, it reaches 73.07 FPS at 1080p and 28.62 FPS at 4K.

</details>


### [228] [VidEdit3D: Flexible 3D Gaussian Editing with Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35756)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Tong Zhao,Juntao Xu,Wenhao Rao,Yiwen Chen,Yiwei Wang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VidEdit3D，将 3D 场景编辑重构为基于轨迹的视频编辑任务，利用视频扩散模型的时空先验，结合轨迹引导渲染（TGR）、流量对齐视频编辑（FAVE）与区域选择性 3DGS 精修（RS-GS），在保持多视角一致性的同时实现结构性编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 3DGS 的文本引导 3D 编辑方法在"多视角一致性保持"与"允许结构性修改"之间存在关键权衡；图像编辑器具难以对非常规视角物体施加预期编辑。

Method: 1) 以轨迹形式将 3D 场景编辑重构为视频编辑任务，借相机运动与视频时间动态的类比，利用预训练 VDM 的时空先验；2) TGR 选取关键帧并构建符合 VDM 输入要求的平滑视频片段；3) FAVE 为免训练框架，含 ECG 机制，在共享潜状态对比源/编辑图像条件，并采用调度式源图像放缩来强化对编辑参考的遵循；4) RS-GS 将几何与外观更新严格限制在目标基元上，减少背景属性泄漏。

Result: 实验表明，相比基线方法具有更强的指令对齐能力与更好的多视角一致性。

Conclusion: 借助视频扩散模型的时空先验与选择性 3DGS 精修，可在免训练条件下实现兼具结构修改能力与跨视角一致性的文本引导 3D 场景编辑。

Abstract: Recent advancements in text-guided 3D editing, particularly with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), have shown promise but still lag behind the flexibility of 2D editing tools. Existing methods face a critical trade-off between preserving multi-view consistency and allowing structural modifications. Image-based editors can also struggle to apply the intended edit to objects viewed from unusual angles. To address these limitations, we introduce VidEdit3D, a framework that reformulates 3D scene editing as a trajectory-based video editing task. By drawing a parallel between camera movement in static 3D scenes and temporal dynamics in video, we leverage the robust spatiotemporal priors of pre-trained Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) to encourage semantic and structural coherence across views. Specifically, we propose Trajectory-Guided Rendering (TGR) to select informative keyframes and construct smooth video clips aligned with VDM input requirements. To propagate edits from an anchor view to these clips, we develop Flow-Aligned Video Editing (FAVE), a training-free framework incorporating an Editing Consistency Guidance (ECG) mechanism. ECG contrasts source and edited image conditions at a shared latent state, using scheduled source-image amplification to strengthen adherence to the edited reference while maintaining original scene context. Finally, we introduce Region-Selective 3DGS Refinement (RS-GS) to strictly confine geometric and appearance updates to target primitives, reducing background attribute leakage. Experiments demonstrate stronger instruction alignment and better multi-view consistency than the baselines.

</details>


### [229] [Does VGGT Know What It Does Not Know?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35806)
*Adityan Thirumani Srinivasan,David Nordström*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Frozen VGGT features linearly probe pose failures (AUC 93.5%) better than its own confidence, revealing latent failure-awareness in two-view fusion layers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建模型（如VGGT）单次前向即回归相机与几何，但对预测位姿不报告任何不确定性——错误的相对位姿与正确的无从区分，下游无法判断是否应弃权。作者追问：冻结的模型内部是否"知道自己何时错了"。

Method: 构建HardMatch基准（1,000对困难的刻意挑选图像对，VGGT在35%上失败）；在VGGT冻结特征上训练线性探针预测位姿失败，并与VGGT自身的点图置信度、以及使用VGGT全部输出的分类器对比；在held-out类别上评估ROC-AUC，并零样本迁移到PhotoTourism和ScanNet-1500的旋转失败；通过层间定位确定信息来源。

Result: 线性探针在held-out类别上AUC达93.5%，高于点图置信度79.7%与全输出分类器77.7%；探针能捕获VGGT自信但实际失败的样本；使下游系统可对不信任的图像对弃权；无需重训即迁移到PhotoTourism与ScanNet-1500的旋转失败，且超出共视性可解释的范围。

Conclusion: 该失败知识不存在于逐图编码器中，而是在融合双视图的层中间计算得出，第二模型π³同样如此。结论：VGGT对其自身失败所掌握的信息远多于其所报告的。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models such as VGGT regress cameras and geometry in a single forward pass, but they report no uncertainty for the camera poses they predict: an incorrect relative pose looks no different from a correct one. We ask whether the frozen model nonetheless knows when its pose is wrong. On HardMatch, a benchmark of 1,000 deliberately difficult image pairs on which VGGT fails on 35% of pairs, a linear probe on VGGT's frozen features predicts pose failure on held-out categories with ROC-AUC 93.5%, compared with 79.7% for VGGT's own point-map confidence and 77.7% for a classifier trained on all of VGGT's outputs. The probe catches failures VGGT is confident about, lets a downstream system abstain on the pairs it distrusts, and transfers without retraining to rotation failures on PhotoTourism and ScanNet-1500, beyond what co-visibility explains. This knowledge is absent from the per-image encoder: it is computed partway through the layers that fuse the two views, and the same holds for a second model, $π^3$. In essence, we find that VGGT knows more about its own failures than it reports.

</details>


### [230] [Back to Basics: A Skill-Centric Approach to Generalist Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35823)
*Minh Pham-Dinh,Md Farhan Ishmam,Hernan Yupanqui,Reaz Hassan Joarder,Kenneth Marino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MiniWeb：65站点/15领域微型真实Web环境，369项人工标注任务与37个交互原语（7技能族）；提出MiniWebGen自动生成技能中心训练数据，Qwen3.5-4B在3.1K轨迹微调后单原语成功率+24.9pp（13个未见站点）；再以MiniWebAgent（执行器+规划器）提升全任务成功率19.4pp，并零训练迁移至WebArena-Lite(+9.3pp)、Online-Mind2Web(+16.1pp)、WebVoyager(+20.0pp)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器智能体评测只报告任务成功与否，无法诊断"哪项技能失败"，训练也多依赖大规模端到端轨迹，缺乏技能级针对性练习与可迁移的紧凑配方。

Method: 构建MiniWeb仿真环境（65个互联站点、15领域、369任务、37个可复用交互原语归为7个技能族）用于技能级诊断；提出MiniWebGen从MiniWeb自主生成经验证、技能中心的训练轨迹；用3.1K验证轨迹微调Qwen3.5-4B作为执行器；MiniWebAgent将执行器与规划器组合，规划器把完整任务分解为已习得的技能级步骤。

Result: 十种开放权重智能体中拖拽与手势族最弱（最优<24%）；微调后单原语成功+24.9pp（13个未见站点），各技能族均有增益；MiniWebAgent在未见MiniWeb站点全任务成功+19.4pp，外部零训练迁移WebArena-Lite +9.3pp、Online-Mind2Web +16.1pp、WebVoyager +20.0pp。

Conclusion: 短程技能轨迹不直接转化为全任务成功，需"先直接练习可复用技能、再组合求解陌生任务"的两阶段配方；该配方为紧凑浏览器智能体提供了简洁可复现路径。

Abstract: Browser agents do not just need more practice. They need to practice the right things. We introduce MiniWeb, a realistic miniature of the web comprising 65 interconnected websites across 15 domains, where agents navigate, search, and transact as people do. Its 369 human-annotated tasks cover everyday workflows and label 37 reusable interaction primitives grouped into 7 skill families, allowing us to diagnose which skills fail rather than only whether a task succeeds. Benchmarking ten open-weight agents, we find that drag & gesture is consistently the weakest family, with no agent solving more than 24% of its instances. To address this gap, we introduce MiniWebGen, a framework that autonomously generates verified, skill-centric training data from MiniWeb. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-4B on just 3.1K verified trajectories improves single-primitive success by 24.9pp across 13 unseen websites, with gains across every skill family. However, these trajectories teach short-horizon interactions, which do not directly translate to full-task success. We build on this result with MiniWebAgent, which pairs the trained executor with a planner that decomposes full tasks into the skill-level steps it learned to perform. This approach improves task success by 19.4pp points on unseen MiniWeb sites and transfers to external benchmarks, with gains of 9.3pp on WebArena-Lite, 16.1pp on Online-Mind2Web, and 20.0pp on WebVoyager, without training on their trajectories. We provide a simple recipe for compact browser agents: practice reusable skills directly, then compose them to solve unfamiliar tasks.

</details>


### [231] [Seeing Through Distractions: Context-Stable Attribution via the Least Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35827)
*Sai Ganesh Nagarajan,Toshinori Yamauchi,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将"在完整真实图像上加入反而降低目标类分数、且不改变预测标签"的特征形式化为"上下文干扰特征"(contextual distractor)，在弱平均边际单调性假设下，推导了半值与最小核心(least core)之间差距的定量界与充分条件，并证明将半值权重移向更大上下文会缩小有符号差距，最后以受控图像分类实验和Salient ImageNet评估验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值等半值方法广泛用于机器学习特征归因，但在计算机视觉中存在一类被忽视的情形：某特征加入完整真实图像后虽不改变预测标签，却会降低目标类分数。此类"上下文干扰特征"的归因行为及其与博弈论解概念（半值、最小核心）之间的一致性缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 将上述特征定义为contextual distractor；在semivalues与least core两种解概念下考察其归因；引入弱平均边际单调性(weak average marginal monotonicity)假设，基于"采样到更小上下文的概率"推导半值—最小核心差距的定量上界与充分条件；进一步分析半值权重向更大上下文偏移对有符号差距的影响。

Result: 给出了半值与最小核心差距的定量边界及充分条件；证明将半值权重移向更大上下文会减小有符号差距；受控图像分类实验与Salient ImageNet上的评估为上述理论提供了有力的实证支持。

Conclusion: 上下文干扰特征的存在会使半值归因与最小核心解产生系统性偏差，且该偏差可由上下文采样概率刻画并可调控；这为视觉特征归因中解概念的选择与解释可靠性提供了理论依据与实验证据。

Abstract: Shapley-value-based explanations are widely used for feature attribution in machine learning. However, in computer vision, a feature may reduce the target-class score when added to the complete genuine image, even when the predicted label remains unchanged. We formalize such features as contextual distractors and investigate their attributions under semivalues and the least core. Under a weak average marginal monotonicity assumption, we derive quantitative semivalue--least-core gap bounds and sufficient conditions based on the probability of sampling smaller contexts. We further show that shifting semivalue weights toward larger contexts decreases the signed gap. Finally, controlled image-classification experiments and evaluations on Salient ImageNet provide strong empirical support for our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [232] [OSWAM: An Effective World Action Model with One-Step Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35850)
*Guowen Zhang,Liyi Chen,Xu Hu,Pengfei Wang,Jingfan Chen,Chenhang He,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OSWAM通过构造imagination tokens初始化扩散过程并提供物理先验，实现单步高质量动作生成，在自动驾驶与具身控制中取得与数十步直接策略WAM相当或更优的性能，并获约3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接策略世界动作模型（WAM）虽解耦了动作生成与显式未来视觉预测以提升推理效率，但仍需数十步去噪才能达到有竞争力的动作质量，带来显著延迟，难以实时部署；而简单减少去噪步数会使多步非线性去噪轨迹坍缩为粗粒度更新，大幅降低动作质量。

Method: 作者认为问题根源在于随机噪声初始化缺乏结构化动作先验，迫使模型依赖迭代去噪恢复连贯动作序列。据此构造imagination tokens对扩散过程进行初始化，提供物理先验与动作条件表征；并引入带未来条件化的特权信息蒸馏，增强未来感知的动作生成，从而支持仅一步扩散完成动作生成。

Result: 在自动驾驶和具身控制任务上，OSWAM取得与需数十步的直接策略WAM及Joint-WAM相当甚至更优的结果；相较直接策略WAM基线约3倍加速，在NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU上支持实时编译推理，延迟低至40ms。

Conclusion: 以结构化动作先验初始化扩散过程可有效将去噪压缩至单步而不损失动作质量，OSWAM实现了高效、实时的世界动作模型动作生成。

Abstract: Direct-policy World Action Models (WAM) have demonstrated the benefits of decoupling action generation from explicit future visual prediction for efficient inference in autonomous driving and embodied control. However, they still require tens of denoising steps to achieve competitive action quality, incurring substantial latency and hindering real-time deployment. Simply reducing the number of denoising steps will collapse the nonlinear multi-step denoising trajectory into coarse updates, substantially degrading action quality. To reduce the denoising steps while enabling real-time action generation, we present an effective world action model with one-step action generation, namely OSWAM, for autonomous driving and embodied control. We argue that it is the absence of structured action priors in random noise initialization that forces direct-policy WAMs to recover coherent action sequences through iterative denoising. Hence, we construct imagination tokens to initialize the diffusion process, providing physical priors and action-conditioned representations. We further introduce privileged information distillation with future conditioning to enhance future-aware action generation. As a result, OSWAM can efficiently and effectively generate high quality actions in just one diffusion step. Our experiments demonstrate that OSWAM achieves comparable or even better results in both autonomous driving and embodied control than direct-policy WAM and Joint-WAM that require tens of steps. It obtains approximately $3\times$ speedup over direct-policy WAM baselines and supports real-time compiled inference with latency as low as 40ms on an NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU. The project page is available at https://gwenzhang.github.io/OSWAM/

</details>


### [233] [FL4N: Feed-forward Latent Model for 4D Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35854)
*Eunbeen Hong,Hyeonseo Yu,Honggyu An,Jooho Son,Hyunah Ko,Soowon Son,Chaehyun Kim,Jahyeok Koo,Jisoo Kim,Junhwa Hur,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FL4N，首个前馈式潜在4D新视角合成模型，将时空统一为单一潜在表示，以静态潜在先验残差建模动态，实时渲染并达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目前馈方法依赖显式4D高斯表示，易出现时间不一致与严重渲染伪影；且动态训练数据有限。

Method: 摒弃显式4D基元，将4D场景先验编码为跨时空统一潜在表示；核心思想是在预训练静态潜在先验之上以残差学习时序动态；引入轻量动态编码器，利用静态场景特征将物体运动与相机视差解耦；仅以图像合成目标训练。

Result: 在仅用图像合成目标训练下达到SOTA的4D新视角合成质量，且支持实时渲染；所学潜在表示可泛化至真实世界视频，并解耦视点与时间。

Conclusion: 以潜在残差范式替代显式4D表示，可在有限动态数据下高效实现高质量、实时、可泛化的4D新视角合成。

Abstract: 4D novel view synthesis (NVS) aims to render dynamic scenes across viewpoints and timestamps, which requires jointly inferring 3D structure and temporal dynamics. Existing monocular feed-forward methods often rely on explicit 4D Gaussian representations, which are prone to temporal inconsistency and severe rendering artifacts. We present FL4N, the first feed-forward latent 4D NVS model. Instead of relying on explicit 4D primitives, FL4N encodes a 4D scene prior into a unified latent representation across space and time. Our core insight is that learning temporal dynamics as residuals on top of a pretrained static latent prior enables effective 4D modeling with limited dynamic training data. To this end, we introduce a lightweight dynamic encoder that leverages static scene features to disentangle object motion from camera-induced parallax. Trained solely with an image synthesis objective, FL4N achieves state-of-the-art 4D NVS quality while rendering in real time. Its learned latent representation generalizes to real-world videos and disentangles viewpoint from time.

</details>


### [234] [Do vision models know that three is three?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35857)
*Ankit Sonthalia,Arnas Uselis,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉编码器是否以统一方向编码跨"视觉域"(regime)的抽象概念转换，系统检验了46个视觉模型的线性表征假设(LRH)，发现"碎片化线性"现象：局部线性强而全局线性缺失，且强局部线性模型可逐步摆脱碎片化，实现跨域线性迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性表征假设(LRH)在语言模型中成立(概念变化对应嵌入空间方向)，但视觉中同一概念可呈现在视觉上不相关、极少共现的"域"(如数字图像与手势图像在像素空间几乎无共性)。作者希望检验视觉模型是否仍以统一线性方向编码底层抽象概念，而非仅在单一域内成立。

Method: 在46个跨架构、训练目标与规模的视觉编码器上，对10个概念族(含数感、年龄、物体身份、MIT-States属性)进行线性方向探测；对每个域内及跨域的概念转换进行统计显著性检验，量化局部与全局线性度，并测量域间方向的一致性与线性探针跨域迁移能力，分析局部-全局线性相关性与尺度效应。

Result: 几乎普遍地在各测试模型、各域内都能找到统计显著的概念方向；但两个不同域并不总共享同一概念方向，多个模型对同一概念转换学到多个分散在不同域的线性结构(碎片化线性)。局部-全局线性度呈显著正相关(τ∈[0.38,0.89]，覆盖所有概念)，强局部线性模型逐渐摆脱碎片化；当全局线性出现时，概念方向可作为线性探针跨域迁移。

Conclusion: 视觉模型中线性表征是"局部成立、全局未必"的：概念方向与域绑定，但碎片化并非固有问题，随局部线性增强而缓解并可实现跨域迁移，为视觉表征的可解释性与概念泛化提供新视角。

Abstract: Do vision models encode the transition $2{\to}3$ the same way whether it appears in handwritten digits or in hand gestures, and do they "understand" that clean${\to}$dirty is the same transformation regardless of whether it is applied to a kitchen or a carpet? The linear representation hypothesis (LRH) answers this for language models: concept changes correspond to directions in (language) embedding space. In vision, the same concept can manifest in visually unrelated "regimes" that rarely co-occur: images of digits and hands do not share much in common in pixel space. To understand whether vision models still encode the underlying abstract concepts, we study 46 vision encoders across architectures, training objectives, and scales, on 10 concept families including numerosity, age, object identity, and MIT-States attributes. Within each regime, we find statistically significant concept directions near-universally across our tested models. However, two distinct regimes do not always share the same concept direction. Several models learn multiple linear structures for the same concept transition, split across regimes. We call this phenomenon "fragmented linearity": strong local linearity coexisting with near-absent global linearity. Fragmentation is, however, not fixed: models with stronger local linearity progressively escape it (local-global linearity correlated with $τ\in [0.38, 0.89]$ across all concepts), and where global linearity does emerge, concept directions can transfer as linear probes across regimes.

</details>


### [235] [VibeVideoBench:Evaluating Multimodal Agents on Code-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35864)
*Muxi Diao,Wenbin Xu,Lele Yang,Xuyang Ma,Yuzhe Chen,Jiankang Chen,Kaiyu Jiang,Changyi Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Hongyi Fu,Jianxiong Wang,Tianke Zhang,Haonan Fan,Yingxin Li,Bingyi Li,Xu Wang,Xuanyu Zheng,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Wentao Zhang,Weiran Xu,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VibeVideoBench，首个面向"代码驱动视频生成"的多模态智能体评测基准，含239个中英文自包含任务、层级化证据式评测，覆盖9个前沿智能体系统共2151次评测，揭示该任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散视频模型虽能生成画面惊艳的片段，但无法可靠渲染精确公式、正确图表、规则一致的博弈或与画面事件同步的旁白。新兴替代路线是由多模态智能体编写代码逐帧渲染并合成音频，但缺乏针对该范式的系统评测基准。

Method: 构建VibeVideoBench：(1) 239个中英文自包含任务，跨12类（数据叙事、音乐驱动视频、课堂讲解、论文讲解等），要求智能体在原生harness中、沙箱内一小时内交付带声MP4；(2) 层级化证据式评测，包括交付规范的程序化检查、约1.3万条任务专属rubric检查（可追溯至对应需求）、以及5个质量维度（由三个VLM评审团打分并用盲测人类偏好验证）。

Result: 评测9个前沿智能体系统共2151次rollout：GPT-6 Astra与Claude Opus 5.5明显领先，但最强系统仍在约四分之一任务上未满足核心要求；视频是否交付与质量同等重要，四分之三的失败视频源于最终渲染启动过晚、运行过慢或根本未启动；评审团在系统排序上与彼此及人类偏好一致，但对单个视频的一致性仅为中等。

Conclusion: 代码驱动视频生成对当前前沿智能体仍是开放难题，瓶颈兼具"能否交付"与"质量"两面，且交付失败主因是时间与渲染流程管理；VLM评审在系统级排名上可靠、视频级判断尚需谨慎，为后续智能体与评测方法研究提供基准。

Abstract: Diffusion video models produce striking clips, but they cannot reliably render an exact formula, a correct chart, a ruleconsistent game or narration synchronized to on-screen events. A different route is emerging: multimodal agents that write code to render every frame and synthesize every sound. We introduce VibeVideoBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents on code-driven video generation. Its 239 self-contained tasks in English and Chinese span 12 categories, from data stories and music-driven pieces to classroom lessons and paper explainers; each asks an agent, running in its native harness inside a sandbox, to deliver a finished MP4 with sound within one hour. Evaluation is hierarchical and evidence-based: program checks of the delivery spec, about 13k task-specific rubric checks traced to the requirements they enforce, and five quality dimensions anchored per task, judged by three VLM judge panels and validated with blind human preferences. Across nine frontier agent systems and 2,151 scored rollouts, the benchmark is far from solved: GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 lead clearly, yet even the best system fails a core requirement on about a quarter of tasks. Whether a video arrives at all matters as much as its quality, and three quarters of the lost videos are final renders started too late, run too slowly or never started. The judges agree with each other and with human preferences on system rankings, but only moderately on individual videos.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [AdShot: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Video Advertisement Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33377)
*Wen Xie,Om Rastogi,Sai Siddhartha Vivek Dhir Rangoju,Gijs Overgoor,Yakov Bart,Sarah Ostadabbas*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出AdShot基准，用于评测多模态大模型在广告剪辑中的镜头选择能力：含823对专业剪辑广告（30秒源片与15秒成片）、194个品牌、17个行业，并标注镜头边界与内容特征；对13个纯视频MLLM与2个音视频MLLM在精度/召回/F1、时长约束与内容保留上评测，发现架构比规模更重要、时长平均绝对误差5.39秒且普遍超时、模型存在系统性内容偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告主需为同一广告制作多种时长版本以适配预算与受众偏好，该过程人工密集且成本高。现有多模态大模型虽擅长通用视频理解，但其完成专门化编辑任务（如为广告裁剪选取镜头）的能力尚未被探索，且缺少相应评测基准。

Method: 构建AdShot基准：823对经专业剪辑的广告视频对（30秒源片对应15秒成片），覆盖194个品牌、17个行业，提供镜头级边界与内容特征（动作、情绪、信息、影像）标注。评测13个纯视频MLLM与2个音视频MLLM（7B–72B），指标包括镜头选择准确率（Precision、Recall、F1）、时长约束（15秒）与内容保留，并设置音频消融实验。

Result: (1) 模型架构比参数规模更关键，较小模型常可匹敌甚至超越更大模型；目标广告镜头数为6–10时表现最佳；(2) 模型时长平均绝对误差为5.39秒，多数倾向超出15秒目标，但仍能显著降低人工剪辑成本；(3) 所有模型呈现系统性内容偏好，倾向选取信息丰富与情绪导向的镜头，而低估动作与影像类镜头。

Conclusion: MLLM在广告镜头选择上已具备一定实用价值但仍存在时长控制与内容偏好偏差；作者公开基准以推动多模态AI与计算广告交叉方向的研究。

Abstract: Advertisers routinely create multiple duration variants of ads to meet marketing budgets and viewer preferences, a labor-intensive and costly process. While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at general video understanding, their ability to perform specialized editorial tasks remains unexplored. We introduce AdShot, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on shot selection for ad clipping. It contains 823 professionally edited ad pairs (30-sec sources and 15-sec edits) across 194 brands and 17 industries, annotated with shot-level boundaries and content features (action, emotion, information, imagery). We evaluate 13 state-of-the-art video-only MLLMs and 2 audio-visual MLLMs (7B-72B parameters) on shot selection accuracy (Precision, Recall, F1), duration constraints (15s), and content preservation, including an audio ablation. We find: (1) model architecture matters more than scale, with smaller models often matching or exceeding larger ones; performance is strongest for target ads with 6-10 shots; (2) models exhibit a mean absolute duration error of 5.39s, with most tending to overshoot the 15s target, though they can still substantially reduce human editing effort; (3) all models exhibit systematic content biases, favoring information-rich and emotion-focused shots while under-selecting action and imagery. We publicly release our benchmark to facilitate research at the intersection of multimodal AI and computational advertising.

</details>


### [237] [MMFact: Simplifying Multimodal Misinformation Detection with Shared Resources and Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34162)
*Yuchen Miao,Zijun Wang,Mingtai Zhang,Xiaoyi Chen,Sihan Deng,Xianglin Ding*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: MMFact 是一个多模态虚假信息检测的共享资源与工具集合，整合 47 个检测器实现与基线、3 个多模态数据集，以及语义特征、OCR、图像描述、外部证据等预处理材料，并提供模块化训练评估框架，以实现更公平的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态虚假信息检测方法依赖各自不同的预处理流程与辅助资源，导致实验对比难以解释、公平性存疑。作者希望消除预处理差异带来的混淆变量，让研究者聚焦于检测方法本身的开发与评估。

Method: 构建统一资源共享平台：汇集 47 个检测器实现与基线、3 个多模态数据集，并统一准备多类材料（语义特征、OCR、图像描述、外部证据）；兼容方法可直接共用同一批材料；同时提供模块化训练与评估框架，在保留各方法特有设计的同时统一实验流程。

Result: 通过共用预处理材料，减少了预处理引入的变异，使不同方法之间能够进行更公平的比较；模块化框架支持在统一实验规程下保留方法特定设计。

Conclusion: 将预处理材料视为共享研究资源可有效降低实验噪声并提升可比性，MMFact 作为工具性贡献帮助研究者专注于检测方法的开发与评估。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation detection methods often rely on different preprocessing pipelines and auxiliary resources, making experimental comparisons difficult to interpret. We present MMFact, a collection of shared resources and tools that simplifies this research workflow. It brings together 47 detector implementations and baselines, three multimodal datasets, and prepared materials ranging from semantic features to OCR, image descriptions, and external evidence. Compatible methods can use the same materials, reducing variation introduced by preprocessing and supporting fairer comparisons. A modular training and evaluation framework preserves method-specific designs while providing common experimental procedures. By treating prepared materials as a shared research resource, MMFact helps researchers focus on developing and evaluating detection methods.

</details>


### [238] [Multimodal Concert Video Editing: System Design and Exploratory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35816)
*Lennart Schiweck,Adrian Kempf,Michelle Retig,Moritz Feucht,Tim Mangliers,Benjamin Himpel*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出一个模块化多机位音乐会自动剪辑框架，结合乐器活动、可见乐器与图像清晰度，并采用节拍计时与概率化机位选择，在完整镜头区间上做可解释打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机位音乐会剪辑需在音乐相关性、画质与切换时机之间协调决策，现有方法缺乏模块化、可解释的整合框架。

Method: 融合多轨音频的乐器活动、画面中可见乐器与图像清晰度等特征；以节拍为基准确定切换时机；用概率化机位选择；采用可解释评分模型在完整镜头区间映射各组件到机位选择；并进行组件测试与四机位音乐会的探索性专家研究。

Result: 组件测试与四机位音乐会的探索性专家研究显示，各剪辑变体获得混合评价，表明仅有局部视听相关性不足以保证整体剪辑质量。

Conclusion: 需将局部视听相关性扩展为语境与序列级准则；该架构为测试此类扩展及研究组件行为对完整剪辑质量的影响提供基础。

Abstract: Editing multi-camera concerts requires coordinated decisions about musical relevance, visual quality and cut timing. We present a modular framework that combines instrument activity from multitrack audio, visible instruments and image sharpness with beat-based timing and probabilistic camera selection. An interpretable scoring model links these components to the choice of views over complete shot intervals. Component tests and an exploratory expert study on a four-camera concert yield mixed assessments across the editing variants. The findings motivate extending local audiovisual relevance with contextual and sequence-level criteria. The architecture provides a basis for testing these extensions and studying how component behaviour affects the quality of complete edits.

</details>
